  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望厘清开放费米子系统中纠缠/负度谱能隙何时闭合、以何种顺序出现，以及这些时间临界性是否先于几何退相干时间（负度开始下降的时刻）发生，并区分高斯性与对称性在决定临界性判据中的作用。

Method: 作者对比了两类开放动力学：仅在平均意义上保持费米宇称（弱对称）因而保持态为高斯态的损耗与增益通道，与在每次跳跃中都保持宇称（强对称）但使态变为非高斯的密度退相位。对退相位情形，在链的键项对易的特殊点上精确求解了非高斯动力学。

Result: 在高斯态中，子系统的纠缠能隙闭合恰好发生在其两个宇称扇区权重相等时；负度能隙闭合则发生在此条件对子系统或其补集成立时，因此负度临界性从不晚于纠缠临界性。均匀平衡的损耗与增益仅按比例缩小两个宇称扇区，对任意二次哈密顿量都保留两种临界性。在开放Kitaev链中，弱失衡损耗与增益使临界性发生偏移，偏移量由切口处的占据数决定；强速率下仅在奇数个近邻格点跨越半填充时才允许在弛豫时间附近发生闭合。弱退相位使两种临界性延迟，并将其移至宇称平衡之前；当退相位速率接近临界阻尼时，两种临界性都退向无穷大时间；在强退相位下，负度远在任一能隙开始收缩之前就开始下降。

Conclusion: 决定时间临界性判据的是高斯性而非对称性。平衡损耗/增益对临界性无影响，失衡则使其偏移，且负度临界性始终不晚于纠缠临界性。退相位作为非高斯机制在弱、临界与强三种区间表现出截然不同的行为：延迟、退向无穷远、以及在能隙收缩前负度先下降。

Abstract: When the two largest eigenvalues of an entanglement or negativity spectrum meet, the gap of the corresponding entanglement or negativity Hamiltonian closes while entropies stay smooth, an event we call a temporal criticality. We ask when such criticalities occur in open fermionic systems, in which order, and whether they precede the geometric decoherence time, at which the negativity starts to fall. We compare loss and gain, which respect fermion parity only on average (weak symmetry) and keep the state Gaussian, with density dephasing, which respects it in every jump (strong symmetry) and makes the state non-Gaussian. We find that Gaussianity, rather than the symmetry, fixes the criterion for a criticality. In a Gaussian state the entanglement gap of a subsystem closes exactly when its two parity sectors carry equal weight, and the negativity gap closes exactly when this happens for the subsystem or its complement, so the negativity criticality never comes after the entanglement one. Uniform balanced loss and gain only shrink the two parities and leave both criticalities in place at every rate, for any quadratic Hamiltonian. In an open Kitaev chain, weak imbalanced loss and gain shift the criticalities by amounts set by the occupations at the cut, and at strong rates they allow a closing near the relaxation time only when an odd number of neighboring sites pass half filling. For dephasing we solve the non-Gaussian dynamics exactly at the point where the bond terms of the chain commute. Weak dephasing delays both criticalities and, away from the chain ends, moves them ahead of the parity balance, where the parity rules of Gaussian states would place them. As the dephasing rate approaches critical damping, both criticalities recede to infinite time, and at strong dephasing the negativity starts to fall long before either gap begins to shrink.

</details>

Abstract: Analog quantum computing with neutral atom computers is rapidly evolving into a promising way to perform combinatorial optimization tasks at scale. Autonomous aerial vehicles (drones) are increasingly used for surveillance over a given territory. In this paper, we propose an innovative framework integrating classical machine learning forecasts and quantum annealing to find the best combination of tours for a given surveillance criterion. The risk maps produced by machine learning are used to find possible surveillance tours, while a quantum-assisted heuristic chooses the best combination, producing a full surveillance plan. By modelling our decision problem for drone paths as a Team Orienteering Problem (TOP) with additional constraints, we construct a new heuristic using quantum annealing to find good solutions. We apply this framework to wildfire prevention, where finding tours most likely to detect a starting fire has clear benefits. We study the effect of larger quantum resources on heuristic performance and compare those results to the use of classical resources.

### [80] [Efficient Quantum Monte Carlo through Cluster Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03893)
*Jorge Sánchez-Segovia,Álvaro M. Alhambra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种在"连通集团空间"中直接采样的马尔可夫链算法，用蒙特卡洛取代簇展开的确定性枚举，从而在收敛区域内规避符号问题，并获得多项式时间复杂度的FPRAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡洛方法在一般量子多体系统中受符号问题阻碍；簇展开虽能在收敛区域内提供自由能等的收敛表示并导出确定性多项式算法，但需穷举连通集团，开销达 (Nε⁻¹)^O(logΔ)。在长程系统中 Δ≡N，导致准多项式时间，效率严重受限。

Method: 引入马尔可夫链算法，直接在连通集团构成的抽象空间（而非物理构型空间）中进行蒙特卡洛采样，替代确定性枚举。由于采样发生在抽象集团空间，收敛区域内不存在传统的指数级符号问题。作者对一般聚合物模型与量子配分函数分别给出算法与复杂度分析。

Result: 对一般聚合物模型，建立了运行时间为 Õ(N⁴ε⁻³) 的 FPRAS；对温度 β≤β_sampling=O(1) 的量子配分函数，复杂度改进为 Õ(N^{3+1/D}ε⁻³)，其中 D 为晶格维度。该框架可直接应用于相互作用 ∝1/r^α 的长程量子系统，包括 α>2D 的 2-local 模型。

Conclusion: 通过将簇展开的枚举替换为抽象集团空间中的采样，算法在收敛区域内规避符号问题，并将长程系统原本的准多项式开销降至多项式，为量子多体及长程相互作用体系提供了高效可证的多项式时间近似方案。

Abstract: The sign problem remains a major obstacle to extending the success of Monte Carlo methods to generic quantum many-body systems. A powerful alternative relies on cluster expansions, which provide convergent representations of free energies and related quantities, with applications ranging from tensor network contractions to quantum dynamics and circuits. Within their convergence regime, these expansions often yield deterministic polynomial-time algorithms. However, such methods require exhaustive enumeration of connected clusters, leading to a computational overhead of $(N\varepsilon^{-1})^{\mathcal{O}(\logΔ)}$, where $N$ is the system size, $\varepsilon$ the desired precision, and $Δ$ the interaction-graph degree. This becomes particularly severe in long-range systems, where $Δ\equiv N$, resulting in quasi-polynomial runtimes. Here, we introduce a Markov chain algorithm that samples cluster expansions directly in the space of connected clusters, replacing deterministic enumeration by Monte Carlo sampling. Because sampling occurs in an abstract cluster space rather than a physical configuration space, the conventional exponential sign problem is absent within the convergence regime. For general polymer models, we establish an FPRAS with runtime $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(N^4\varepsilon^{-3})$. For quantum partition functions at temperatures $β\leqβ_{\mathrm{sampling}}\equiv\mathcal{O}(1)$, this improves to $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(N^{3+1/D}\varepsilon^{-3})$, where $D$ is the lattice dimension. The framework directly applies to long-range quantum systems with interactions $\propto1/r^α$, including $2$-local models with $α>2D$.

</details>

TL;DR: 该文用拟阵组织跨度程序优化，分离查询依赖数据与程序依赖结构，用于组织对手上下界并给出R10闭包及递归自组合的查询复杂度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨度程序是设计量子查询算法的通用方法，其最优代价与一般对手界一致。作者希望将这一优化中的依赖数据与程序结构分离，从而更清晰地组织对手上下界，并研究特定查询问题的精确刻画与递归构造。

Method: 引入源拟阵与程序拟阵：源拟阵记录查询依赖、目标确定与分离数据，程序拟阵记录目标及带响应标签输入元素之间的依赖。对二元查询集合，证明全局拟阵-跨度程序最优值等于 Adv^{±}_{Q}(f)。对固定正则程序拟阵，利用投射唯一性将实表示优化归约为正元素权重；将正负代价表示为加权基比，导出精确1、2、3和约简，并沿Seymour分解进行组合式求值。

Result: 对带点R10闭包给出精确证书 3.9087553 < Adv^{±}_{Q_R}(f_R) < 3.9301；自然R10程序优化代价为5，另一个正则程序代价至多 sqrt(87/5)。递归自组合得到N比特输入族：随机查询复杂度 Ω(N^{0.7324867...})，显式量子算法使用 O(N^{0.6500178...}) 次查询，且 Q(F)=Θ(N^α)，其中 0.6204277... < α < 0.6229063...

Conclusion: 拟阵化组织有效统一了跨度程序优化与对手界，并通过加权基比与和约简提升可计算性；R10闭包证书与递归自组合进一步给出显式的随机/量子查询复杂度分离与指数区间。

Abstract: Span programs provide a general method for designing quantum query algorithms, and for Boolean coordinate queries their optimum cost agrees with the general adversary bound. We develop a matroidal organization of this optimization that separates dependence data determined by the query problem from the dependence structure chosen by a span program. The resulting source matroid records query dependence, target determination, and separation data, while a program matroid records dependencies among the target and response-labeled input elements of a candidate program.
  When a query problem admits a source matroid, we use its structure to organize adversary upper and lower bounds. For separating binary query sets in this setting, the global matroid-span-program optimum equals $\mathrm{Adv}^{\pm}_{\mathcal Q}(f)$. For a fixed regular program matroid, projective uniqueness reduces optimization over real representations to positive element weights. We express positive and negative costs as weighted basis ratios and derive exact $1$-, $2$-, and $3$-sum reductions, giving a compositional evaluation of the fixed-weight objective along a Seymour decomposition.
  For pointed $R_{10}$ closure, exact certificates give $3.9087553<\mathrm{Adv}^{\pm}_{\mathcal Q_R}(f_R)<3.9301$. The natural $R_{10}$ program has optimized cost $5$, while another regular program has cost at most $\sqrt{87/5}$. Recursive self-composition yields a family on $N$ input bits with randomized query complexity $Ω(N^{0.7324867\ldots})$, an explicit quantum algorithm using $O(N^{0.6500178\ldots})$ queries, and $Q(F)=Θ(N^α)$ for $0.6204277\ldots<α<0.6229063\ldots$.

</details>

Method: 对任意QLDPC码进行形变，引入额外辅助量子比特，使一组稳定子的乘积等价于目标逻辑Clifford算子的测量；证明形变后的Clifford稳定码仍为LDPC码，并分析其距离与容错性质。

Result: 证明形变码保持LDPC特性；码距在仅损失与物理比特数无关的常数因子下得以保持；Clifford测量对数据量子比特误差与测量误差的容错距离随原码距离线性增长。

Conclusion: 该框架可用于在算法相关子程序中实现逻辑非-Clifford门，并在量子稳定子码中制备魔态，为非-Clifford操作的容错实现提供了通用途径。

Abstract: Non-Clifford operations required for universal quantum computation can be implemented by measuring logical Clifford operators. We present a generalized framework to fault-tolerantly measure transversal Clifford logical operators in quantum low-density parity check (QLDPC) codes. Our protocol takes any QLDPC code and deforms the code across additional ancillary qubits such that a product of stabilizers measures the desired logical Clifford operator. We prove that the deformed, Clifford-stabilized code remains LDPC and that its distance is preserved up to a constant factor independent of the number of physical qubits. We further prove that the Clifford measurement has a fault distance against data qubit and measurement errors that grows linearly with the distance of the original code. We apply our methods to implement logical non-Clifford gates in algorithmically relevant subroutines and to prepare magic states in quantum stabilizer codes.

</details>

Abstract: The decay of out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) to a small residual value signals the scrambling of quantum information. This behavior is generic: it occurs in both chaotic and interacting integrable many-body systems, and therefore cannot by itself distinguish between them. In this paper we analytically study the late-time saturation value of a closely related observable, operator purity, and show that its scaling with system size robustly distinguishes chaotic from integrable systems. Our approach is based on the Mazur bound, which provides concrete scaling estimates and makes explicit why the locality of integrable charges matters. We support our findings with numerical simulations of one-dimensional spin chains.

TL;DR: 本文研究Pauli Correlation Encoding (PCE)中压缩比对可表示性与解恢复的影响，发现压缩比增大会因Pauli可观测量间的代数闭包关系引入可表示性约束；通过最大独立集基准测试揭示拟设设计、压缩比与解质量之间的关键权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G/6G网络复杂度提升带来组合优化需求，量子优化虽有前景但受限于量子比特数、噪声与可扩展性。PCE作为比特高效的优化框架通过多项式变量压缩应对这些问题，但压缩对可表示性及解恢复的影响尚不明确，因此需要系统研究。

Method: 提出假设：压缩比升高会因编码Pauli可观测量间的代数闭包关系带来可表示性约束。以最大独立集（MIS）为基准问题，评估多种PCE拟设设计与不同压缩比，并考察电路重复次数的影响。

Result: 拟设选择对解质量与可行性均关键，两类拟设家族始终优于其他方案，逼近比接近0.9；增加电路重复次数收益有限甚至可能降低性能；减少压缩（增加可用量子比特数）显著提升原始解质量，而后处理性能相对稳定。

Conclusion: 可表示性效应在压缩PCE编码中起重要作用，为可扩展量子优化中压缩、拟设设计、解码与解质量之间的关系提供了新见解。

Abstract: The increasing complexity of 5G and emerging 6G wireless networks has intensified the need for scalable optimization techniques capable of addressing computationally challenging combinatorial problems. While quantum optimization offers a promising alternative, current near-term quantum hardware remains limited by qubit availability, noise, and scalability constraints. Pauli Correlation Encoding (PCE) has emerged as a qubit-efficient optimization framework that addresses these challenges through polynomial variable compression. However, the impact of compression on representability and solution recovery remains poorly understood. In this work, we investigate the hypothesis that increasing compression ratios introduce representability constraints arising from algebraic closure relationships among encoded Pauli observables. Using Maximum Independent Set benchmark problems, we evaluate multiple PCE ansatz designs and compression ratios. We find that ansatz selection is critical to both solution quality and feasibility, with two ansatz families consistently outperforming alternatives and achieving approximation ratios approaching 0.9. Furthermore, increasing circuit repetitions provides limited benefit and can degrade performance. Most notably, reducing compression by increasing the number of available qubits significantly improves raw solution quality, while post-processed performance remains comparatively stable. These findings suggest that representability effects play an important role in compressed PCE encodings and provide new insight into the relationship among compression, ansatz design, decoding, and solution quality in scalable quantum optimization.

</details>

Conclusion: 该方案借助里德堡集体激发与外部驱动场，实现了带宽可原位调谐的微波-光相干转换，缓解了传统转换器的效率-带宽权衡，为混合量子网络提供可行路径。

Abstract: Coherent microwave-to-optical transduction is essential for hybrid quantum networks. Typical transducers, however, have bandwidths set by fixed device parameters and face efficiency-bandwidth tradeoffs. We propose a cavity-based microwave-to-optical transducer mediated by a collective Rydberg excitation, where external driving fields tune the conversion bandwidth in situ from narrowband, spectrally selective operation to broadband operation without modifying cavity parameters. Under impedance-matching conditions, the system operates as a continuous-wave phase-preserving linear transducer, maintaining both near-unit efficiency and quantum-limited added noise throughout the tunable bandwidth range.

Abstract: Despite its remarkable empirical success and predictive power, quantum theory is riddled with mysteries. The latter result complements the Local Friendliness no-go theorem, which challenged the absoluteness of experimentally obtained measurement outcomes. In Part II of this thesis, we turn to black hole puzzles and the interplay of quantum theory and gravity. We present a paradox that unifies extended Wigner's friend arguments with black hole puzzles, strengthening the cloning and firewall paradoxes. After discussing subtleties in these paradoxes, we next present an analysis combining elements of quantum theory with gravity, analyzing the detector response near a superposed black hole using elements of quantum reference frames.

Abstract: What makes one quantum process more complex than another? For stationary, memoryful quantum space series admitting finite-dimensional, primitive causal models, we show that a memory-minimal model can be chosen to generate the outputs isometrically, without a discarded environment. The resulting canonical matrix product state representation determines both the minimum memory dimension and the minimum memory entropy. The latter is precisely the entanglement entropy across the past-future bipartition, giving entanglement an operational interpretation as irreducible memory for causal generation. Within this class, we further establish a vanishing fundamental entropy rate, the absence of causal asymmetry, and infinite quantum Markov order. These results establish an operational framework for quantifying intrinsic quantum structure, revealing deep connections between causal generation, complexity, memory, entropy, and entanglement.

Abstract: Algorithms which prepare states via the variational principle assume that closeness in energy implies closeness in state; we formalise how this assumption can fail by showing that a ground-state energy estimate converged to within $ε$ of the true value is compatible with observable expectation values in error by up to $\sqrt{2εχ_O}$ at leading order, where $χ_O$ is the static susceptibility of the observable. As a result, the observable error can be orders of magnitude larger than the energy error, a mechanism we call non-linear error amplification, and because a variational algorithm cannot guarantee the correctness of these properties from energy alone, energy does not necessarily certify an observable. In light of our findings, we examine three algorithms: the variational quantum eigensolver on the transverse-field Ising chain, the density-matrix renormalisation group on a pair of weakly coupled hydrogen chains, and Krylov quantum diagonalisation on lithium fluoride. In each case we demonstrate that a well-converged energy can leave observables uncertified.

The walls are rigid, so perturbation theory in the transverse field $g=Γ/J_0$ can be carried out for each configuration separately, and we take it to order $g^8$. Quantum fluctuations select $\langle 3\rangle$ ($\uparrow\uparrow\uparrow\downarrow\downarrow\downarrow$) at order $g^2$, because spins next to a wall gain the most energy from virtual flips. The structures $\langle 23\rangle$, $\langle 223\rangle$ and $\langle 2223\rangle$ then open between $\langle 3\rangle$ and the antiphase $\langle 2\rangle$ at orders $g^4$, $g^6$ and $g^8$, in the order of the Fisher-Selke sequence of the classical three-dimensional model. A second layer suppresses each successive phase more strongly, beyond the stiffening of the local fields. We test the two widest phases with stochastic series expansion quantum Monte Carlo, comparing the free energies of the competing ordered states by thermodynamic integration, since annealing freezes in the modulation that forms at the ordering transition. The simulations reproduce the $\langle 3\rangle$ wedge to about 1%, and in a pre-registered test $\langle 23\rangle$ is stable at six of seven points, with the seventh resolved by post-hoc runs.

Abstract: In Part I, we introduced a general framework for constructing logical gates in quantum CSS codes from cohomology invariants, focusing on cup products and their generalizations from topological to qLDPC codes. In particular, we produced a family of $C^{k-1}Z$ logical gates that we called copy-cup on $k$ copies of the same quantum code implemented by finite-depth circuits. Here, we vastly extend this framework by introducing twists incorporating code automorphisms, higher twisted cohomology operations, and folding operations in relative cohomology. The framework includes operations lifting coefficients from $\mathbb{Z}/2$ to $\mathbb{Z}/2^k$, enabling all diagonal logical gates such as single-qubit rotations $\operatorname{R}(2π/2^k)$. Composing these operations with maps that select cohomology classes yields addressable logical gates. Examples include addressable $T$ gates on three- and four-tori and gates in qLDPC codes beyond topological ones. We introduce cohomology operations, such as the higher twisted Pontryagin powers, that to our knowledge have not previously appeared in the mathematics literature. These operations arise from $E_\infty$-structures on cochains, with explicit constructions using cyclic resolutions and the surjection operad. We expect these results to be of independent interest in homotopy theory. Our formalism applies much more broadly to chain complexes carrying suitable operadic structures, and we expect this operadic perspective to uncover further connections between homotopy theory and fault-tolerant quantum gates.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该工作为研究非阿贝尔sw-SSB提供了系统框架，并为构造非阿贝尔拓扑码的解码器奠定基础。结果表明非阿贝尔情形下强对称性仅非局域实现、破缺后仅剩中心Z(G)与弱对称性，中心序化的阈值随层级升高不降；S3与SU(2)的数值结果支持上述预测。

Abstract: We develop an operational approach to strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (sw-SSB) of a non-Abelian symmetry group G. We formulate it as the decoding task of distinguishing two strongly symmetric states that differ by a pair of inserted irreps, after both are corrupted by strongly symmetric decoherence. For finite G, this task is dual to distinguishing logical states of the quantum double D(G) under pure-charge noise. We derive a hierarchy of statistical mechanics models describing optimal decoding from the fusion outcomes measured on successively larger nested regions. As for maximum-likelihood decoders of Abelian codes the measured irreps act as quenched disorder obeying a Nishimori-like condition. Strikingly, because local irreps do not determine how they fuse globally, the strong $G\times G$ symmetry is realized only nonlocally, and irreps trivially charged under Z(G) (the center of G) can hide inside noise-generated ones. As a result, the strong symmetry spontaneously breaks down to Z(G), times the weak symmetry. The only transition at nonzero error rate is then an ordering of the center, whose threshold cannot decrease as the hierarchy is ascended. This conclusion holds at any finite level of the hierarchy for non-nilpotent groups. We confirm these predictions with tensor-network calculations for $S_3$ and SU(2). At the first level, the $S_3$ model, whose center is trivial, shows no transition, and the fidelity correlations of all irreps provably decay to non-zero values. The SU(2) model exhibits a $Z_2$ transition consistent with the Nishimori-Ising universality class, where the fidelity correlations of spin-$1/2$ irreps become long-range. The threshold rises from the first to the second level of the hierarchy by $16\%$. Our results provide a systematic framework for studying non-Abelian sw-SSB and for constructing decoders for non Abelian topological codes.

Abstract: Extreme values of a physical system are rare by definition, yet they can govern some of its most consequential and anomalous behavior. Their sparsity makes them particularly costly to identify in large data sets. Quantum computation offers a potential way to reduce this cost. The challenge is that quantum amplitudes cannot simply be read, copied, compared, or marked using conventional search oracles. Here we introduce an algorithm for searching amplitude-encoded quantum signals that exploits their underlying structure. Through a nonlinear amplitude transformation, the algorithm identifies and certifies a global extremum while exponentially reducing the effective search space for structured signals. We establish a polylogarithmic query complexity in the data size $N$, together with global completeness, efficient classical verification, and a measurable criterion for quantifying exploitable structure. We demonstrate the framework using computational turbulence data. Applied to line cuts of velocity gradients from a $1024^3$ simulation containing more than one billion grid points, the algorithm consistently isolates the extreme tail and reduces the median search space to $O(\log N)$. The clustered structure characteristic of turbulence further accelerates the search. These results establish a direct connection between physical structure and quantum advantage in extreme-value search. For accessibility, the main text emphasizes the central ideas and results, while the theoretical derivations are provided in the Appendices.

</details>

Conclusion: 该变分乘积态框架为经典自旋动力学的量子扩展提供了统一且几何清晰的途径，在保留局部量子效应与排除格点间纠缠的近似下，兼具理论澄清与数值可行性。

Abstract: We introduce a distinct route for extending classical atomistic spin dynamics into the quantum regime for arbitrary spin $s$, based on a variational product-state \textit{ansatz} that includes both pure and mixed local states. By construction, intersite entanglement -- expected to remain negligible even on very short time scales in many real materials -- is excluded, while local quantum effects are retained through the nonclassical structure of $s\geq 1$ density operators, and nonlinear dynamics arise from both the mean-field interdependence of the local states and the state-dependent dissipative terms. This framework clarifies the variational and geometric origin of the inequivalence between quantum Landau-Lifshitz and Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert dynamics, provides a criterion for their equivalence under a common time rescaling, establishes connections with the classical theory through two qualitatively distinct limits ($s\to\infty$ and a large number of quantum spins $N$), and allows realistic $\mathcal{O}(N)$ simulations of quantum spin dynamics in materials.

</details>

Abstract: We study the emergent randomness in projected ensembles generated by dual-unitary circuits. Previous work has established that this projected ensemble of states forms a quantum state design in the limit where the bath size is large, but the rate of convergence at finite bath size has not been rigorously established. Under three inverse-polynomial assumptions, local permutation mixing, a bound on recurrent backward motion, and contraction at the measured boundary, we fill this gap and prove that a bath of polynomial size suffices to form approximate $k$-designs. In establishing our results, we develop a microscopic transport interpretation of emergent randomness in the projected ensemble, in which deviations from local permutation operators appear as defects, and make connections with the theory of quantum walks.

Abstract: We develop a quantum input-output theory for open photonic systems based on macroscopic quantum electrodynamics using the first-order electromagnetic Green's function. The resulting relation contains a port-to-port scattering matrix and a Langevin-noise contribution from material absorption that together preserve the bosonic output commutation relations. Using finite-difference time-domain simulation, we calculate the emitter-modified transmission for a nanophotonic cavity and inverse-designed coupler, providing a direct route from computed electromagnetic response to a quantum input-output model.

Abstract: A small excitation gap makes a quantum dot a sensitive probe of a weak electric field, but the charge detector that reads the probe can also excite it. We ask how much of this gap enhancement survives continuous readout, for two interacting electrons tuned through a confinement-driven crossover in a gate-defined quantum dot, using a weak-coupling, stationary two-level model. The answer depends on the energy structure of the detector, not only on its noise. A quantum point contact measures more strongly at higher bias, but once the bias energy exceeds the gap it also excites the probe. When this back-action dominates the probe's own relaxation, the optimised mean-current information rate grows only as the inverse of the gap. A sensor quantum dot with a narrow level instead reaches full measurement strength at a bias below the gap, where, for a non-interacting detector and to second order in the coupling, it cannot excite the probe at zero temperature. This energy-filtered readout keeps the inverse-square gap dependence of the quantum Fisher information, down to a gap set by the detector's level width and the electron temperature. Through such a detector the interacting pair gives about three times the information rate of a single electron with the same gap and detector, compared with 1.6-1.7 in the quantum Fisher information, for both contact and Coulomb-type interactions. These different gap dependences, and the different optimal biases of the two detectors, can be tested by bias and gate sweeps on suitably tuned charge-sensed double dots.

Abstract: Neutral atom-based quantum computers have the ability to dynamically reshape the qubit register geometry between experimental runs. This capability enables different types of problems to be mapped directly onto the hardware, significantly enhancing the efficiency of solving, for instance, complex optimization and machine learning use-cases. However, this flexibility requires finding atom positions to implement target pairwise interactions, inducing a difficult nonconvex optimization for setting up the quantum machine. In this work, we introduce a new force-directed method that targets interactions to encode the input problem on the quantum computer. Numerical results show that our new method can improve embedding feasibility and solution quality by orders of magnitude, and remains reliable on the largest instances.

*Francesco Debortoli,Fabian Grusdt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于可移动量子杂质（具可寻址内态）的编织非阿贝尔任意子的协议，通过Ramsey干涉测量R、F矩阵元，并以Ising与Fibonacci任意子为例展示任意子统计的直接体现，进一步构建将飞行量子比特信息编码进拓扑保护量子存储的编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔任意子的操控是拓扑量子计算与拓扑保护量子存储的基础，但如何在实验上直接测量刻画非阿贝尔统计的R、F矩阵元并将其用于量子信息存储仍缺乏具体可行方案。作者旨在提供一个可实验实现的量子杂质协议，直接揭示任意子统计，并桥接常规飞行比特与拓扑量子存储。

Method: 利用具有可寻址内部自旋态的可移动杂质作为探针，构建Ramsey干涉测量方案：让杂质与任意子耦合、移动并完成编织，从而在干涉相位与条纹中读取R、F矩阵元。以Ising与Fibonacci任意子为具体案例，展示非阿贝尔统计如何在干涉信号中直接显现；并基于该框架设计一个“常规到拓扑”量子比特编码器。

Result: 提出了可实验测量R、F矩阵元的编织协议；在Ramsey干涉仪中直接呈现Ising与Fibonacci任意子的非阿贝尔统计特征；并给出将常规飞行量子比特的量子信息转移到拓扑保护量子存储中的编码器方案。

Conclusion: 量子杂质结合Ramsey干涉是探测与利用非阿贝尔任意子统计的有效且可实验实现的途径，既能测量R、F矩阵元，又能实现从飞行量子比特到拓扑量子存储的信息编码，为拓扑量子计算提供实验路径。

Abstract: The manipulation of non-Abelian anyons is a foundational ingredient for topological quantum computing and underlies topologically protected quantum memories. Here we analyze a quantum-impurity-based protocol for braiding non-Abelian anyons. Exploiting mobile impurities with addressable internal spin states, we provide a concrete protocol to experimentally measure the matrix elements of the $R$ and $F$ matrices that characterize the non-Abelian statistics of anyons. In particular, focusing on the case of Ising and Fibonacci anyons, we illustrate how the anyonic nature of the excitations emerges directly in the Ramsey interferometer we propose. Building on this framework, we further introduce a conventional-to-topological qubit encoder to transfer quantum information from conventional flying qubits into a topologically protected quantum memory.

</details>


### [101] [Gate-Based Quantum Simulation of Finite-Temperature Open-System Dynamics: A Post-Processing Reconstruction Approach for Collision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04075)
*Kazunari Hashimoto,Chikako Uchiyama*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种无需制备热态辅助比特的方法：对一类U(1)对称的系统-辅助比特相互作用，有限温开放系统动力学可由纯辅助比特（正交基态）分支的模拟数据经经典后处理重构，同一数据可复用于不同温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在门基量子计算机上模拟开放系统动力学是研究耗散量子现象的途径。碰撞模型通过系统与辅助比特的序贯碰撞灵活表示环境，但有限温下通常需反复制备热态辅助比特，带来线路开销和门误差，因此希望绕过显式热态制备。

Method: 针对广泛的一类U(1)对称系统-辅助比特相互作用，利用纯辅助比特制备于正交基态进行模拟，将目标温度仅通过经典后处理引入，把各纯分支数据线性组合以重构有限温动力学。

Result: 证明有限温动力学可由纯辅助比特分支数据重构；温度仅作为经典后处理参数，同一组纯分支数据可复用于不同温度而不需额外量子处理；从而避免显式热态制备，降低辅助比特制备开销及制备引入的误差。

Conclusion: 该方案为门基量子计算机上高效模拟有限温开放系统动力学提供了资源更省、误差更低的途径，通过经典后处理替代量子热态制备，提升温度扫描类任务的实用性。

Abstract: Simulating open-system dynamics on gate-based quantum computers offers a route to exploring dissipative quantum phenomena through programmable quantum processing. Collision models provide a direct and flexible framework by representing the environment through sequential system--ancilla collisions. At finite temperatures, however, they typically require repeated preparation of thermal ancillas, adding circuit overhead and gate errors. Here, we show that, for a broad class of $U(1)$-symmetric system--ancilla interactions, finite-temperature dynamics can instead be reconstructed from simulations with pure ancillas in orthogonal basis states. The target temperature enters only through classical post-processing of the resulting pure-branch data, allowing the same data to be reused for different temperatures without additional quantum processing. Bypassing explicit thermal-state preparation reduces ancilla-preparation overhead and preparation-induced errors.

</details>


### [102] [Exact Finite-Channel Schur Moments of the Wigner-Smith Matrix with a Tunnel Barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04170)
*Israel F. Araujo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究破缺时间反演对称性、含M个等效开放通道及均匀隧道势垒（反射概率R）的混沌腔Wigner-Smith时间延迟矩阵，证明非理想耦合下的Schur矩可分解为理想耦合的逆Laguerre值与由CUE Schur多项式平均给出的势垒因子，并给出有限通道下的精确时间延迟矩公式及对半经典猜想的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非理想耦合下Wigner-Smith矩阵的联合矩阵分布已有精确随机矩阵结果，但Schur矩的显式有限通道公式研究不足；同时半经典展开无法捕捉某些指数小项，需要严格有限通道结果予以刻画。

Method: 利用Schur矩因子化：将非理想Wigner-Smith矩阵的Schur矩写成理想耦合逆Laguerre值与势垒因子之积，势垒因子为CUE中Schur多项式的平均；通过双重Cauchy生成函数将其化为有理符号的Toeplitz行列式，纳入CUE特征多项式比值的经典理论；对单行分拆使用精确CUE比值公式导出生成函数，并给出单列分拆的精确有限和与M×M方阵内的互补对称性。

Result: 对单行分拆得到所有势垒系数及n≤M范围内全部有限时间延迟矩的封闭生成函数；精确比值公式分解为半经典可见部分与正比于R^{M+1}的额外贡献，后者对1/M的任意代数阶均不可见；对第二矩，该贡献精确复现已知随机矩阵⟨τ_W^2⟩中半经典展开缺失的指数小项；同时给出单列分拆的精确有限和与Schur平均的互补对称性。

Conclusion: 新贡献不在于经典的CUE比值恒等式本身，而在于将其与精确非理想Wigner-Smith映射结合，并由此提取有限通道时间延迟公式及对半经典Schur矩猜想的修正。

Abstract: We consider the Wigner--Smith time-delay matrix of a chaotic cavity with broken time-reversal symmetry, a finite number $M$ of equivalent open channels, and a uniform tunnel barrier of reflection probability $R$. Exact random-matrix results for nonideal coupling are known at the level of the joint matrix distribution, whereas explicit finite-channel formulas for Schur moments are much less developed. We show that every Schur moment of the nonideal Wigner--Smith matrix factorizes into its ideal-coupling inverse-Laguerre value and a barrier factor given by a circular unitary ensemble (CUE) average of a Schur polynomial. A dual-Cauchy generating function reduces the latter to a Toeplitz determinant with a rational symbol, placing the barrier problem within the classical theory of exact CUE characteristic-polynomial ratios.
  For one-row partitions, specializing the exact CUE ratio formula gives a closed generating function for all barrier coefficients and therefore all finite delay-time moments in the allowed range $n\le M$. The exact ratio formula decomposes into a sector visible to the semiclassical expansion and an additional contribution proportional to $R^{M+1}$, which is invisible to every algebraic order in $1/M$. For the second moment, this contribution exactly reproduces the exponentially small term in the known random-matrix expression for $\langleτ_W^2\rangle$ that is absent from the semiclassical expansion. We also derive an exact finite sum for all one-column partitions and an exact complement symmetry for Schur averages inside the $M\times M$ square. The new contribution therefore lies not in the underlying CUE ratio identities, which are classical, but in combining them with the exact nonideal Wigner--Smith mapping and extracting the resulting finite-channel time-delay formulas and corrections to semiclassical Schur-moment conjectures.

</details>


### [103] [A Practical Introduction to VQE: Methods, Challenges, and Applications in Physics and Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06448)
*Jannis Ehrlich,Felix Rupprecht,Konstantin Lamp,Elias Walter,Alejandro D. Somoza,Manuel Enns,Christian Elsässer,Birger Horstmann,Daniel F. Urban*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述变分量子特征求解器（VQE）在量子模拟中的各关键组件，面向初学者提供结构化的实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQE已成为近中期量子硬件上处理电子结构与材料科学问题的核心混合量子-经典方法，但物理、化学与量子信息领域的研究者在应用时面临问题编码、拟设构建、经典优化与测量策略等多重算法选择，需一份可及性强的入门指引。

Method: 以结构化综述形式梳理VQE各组件，结合作者早期实践经验，讨论影响实际性能的因素（噪声特性、可用的误差缓解技术、硬件相关电路考量），并辅以物理与量子化学示例。

Result: 归纳出对新手尤为相关的要点，并总结了实际工作流程中常见挑战，为基于VQE的量子模拟提供可及入口。

Conclusion: 本文旨在支持研究者在方法与底层硬件持续演进过程中做出知情决策，提供面向VQE量子模拟的入门参考。

Abstract: The Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) has become a central hybrid quantum-classical method for addressing electronic structure and materials science problems on near-term quantum hardware. As researchers across physics, chemistry, and quantum information engage with VQE, they encounter a wide range of algorithmic choices, including problem encoding, ansatz construction, classical optimization, and measurement strategies to choose from. This article provides a structured overview of these components and discusses practical factors that influence VQE performance in real settings, such as noise characteristics, available error-mitigation techniques, and hardware-dependent circuit considerations. Drawing partly on our early experiences, we highlight aspects that may be especially relevant for newcomers and outline common challenges observed in practical workflows. Our aim is to offer an accessible entry point for researchers approaching VQE-based quantum simulation and to support informed decision-making as the method and underlying hardware continue to evolve, which we support by discussion of examples from physics and quantum chemistry.

</details>


### [104] [Parallelizing Dissipative Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04176)
*Sudatta Hor,Nathan Wiebe,Yongshan Ding*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种耗散量子算法的并行化方案，通过同时实现Lindbladian的跳跃算子来降低电路深度，利用几何局域性使跳跃算子保持空间局域，从而将每步电路深度指数级降低，并在100量子比特一维基准系统中实现53倍的查询深度缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散量子算法受限于Lindbladian跳跃算子的顺序实现，导致电路深度过高，难以在近期量子计算硬件上实用。因此需要一种降低电路深度的方法。

Method: 本文分析了一种并行化方案，用于同时实现多个跳跃算子。利用几何局域性证明各跳跃算子保持空间局域，从而支持并行执行，指数级降低每时间步的电路深度。

Result: 数值实验表明，在100量子比特的一维基准系统上，查询深度减少了53倍。该方案在电路深度上带来指数级降低，整体上实现多项式级改进。

Conclusion: 降低的电路深度使耗散量子算法在近期量子计算中更具实用性。

Abstract: Inspired by classical Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods, dissipative quantum algorithms are an emerging class of methods for preparing quantum states on a quantum computer. They operate by simulating a dissipative process governed by a mathematical operator known as the Lindbladian. The performance of these algorithms can be limited by the sequential implementation of the Lindbladian's constituent jump operators, which leads to high circuit depth. To address this issue, this work analyzes a parallelization scheme to implement jump operators simultaneously. We leverage the structure of geometric locality to show that individual jump operators remain spatially localized. This exponentially reduces the circuit depth per time step, resulting in an overall polynomial improvement. Numerical experiments demonstrate a 53x reduction in query depth for a 1D benchmark system of 100 qubits. The reduced circuit depth makes dissipative algorithms more practical for near-term quantum computing.

</details>


### [105] [Reduced Cost Quantum Kernel Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04190)
*Daniel R. Germain,Benjamin Goldweber,Kayla J. Rodriguez,Kristen Rhinehardt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究单类异常检测作为非变分量子核模型的高效训练范式,通过训练集缩减与Nyström低秩近似,在保持异常判别性能的同时将量子核训练成本降低数千倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子核方法在近期量子机器学习中前景可观,但训练成本受限于Gram矩阵构建的二次缩放以及估计单个核值的测量开销。作者希望寻找一种资源高效的训练范式来缓解这一问题。

Method: 采用ULB信用卡欺诈检测数据集,评估三种量子特征映射在不同训练集规模与量子比特数下的表现;使用幂律饱和模型刻画性能随数据的边际收益;检验核值是否存在指数集中现象;并引入Nyström近似将核构建从O(N^2)降为O(Nm),结合下采样策略。

Result: 幂律饱和模型显示随训练数据增加性能收益显著递减,500样本模型保留了约90–93%的渐近平均精度(AP);在所考察量子比特范围内未观察到严重指数集中;Nyström近似仅用5个landmarks即保留94–97%的精确核AP。对ZZ-QSVR,从4000降至500训练样本并施加5-landmark近似,核评估次数从约800万降至2485,减少超过3200倍,AP从0.812降至0.737,训练成本从约13.4亿美元降至约417,480美元。

Conclusion: 训练集缩减与低秩近似可显著降低训练非变分量子核异常检测器所需的资源,同时保留有意义的异常判别性能,为近-term量子机器学习提供了资源高效的运行区间。

Abstract: Quantum kernel methods are promising for near-term quantum machine learning, but their training costs are driven by the quadratic scaling of Gram-matrix construction and the measurement cost of estimating individual kernel values. In this work, we investigate one-class anomaly detection as a resource-efficient operating regime for training non-variational quantum kernel models. Using the ULB Credit Card Fraud Detection dataset, we evaluate three quantum feature maps across training-set sizes and qubit counts. Power-law saturation models reveal strong diminishing returns with increasing training data, with 500-sample models retaining approximately 90--93\% of their fitted asymptotic average precision (AP). We further observe no severe exponential concentration among the evaluated feature maps over the investigated qubit range, thereby avoiding the exponentially increasing measurement requirements associated with that regime. Finally, Nyström approximation reduces kernel construction from $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ to $\mathcal{O}(Nm)$ evaluations, with QSVR retaining 94--97\% of its exact-kernel AP using only five landmarks. For ZZ-QSVR, downsampling from 4,000 to 500 training samples and applying a five-landmark approximation decreases the number of required kernel evaluations from approximately $8.0$ million to $2,485$, a reduction of more than 3,200-fold, while AP decreases from $0.812$ to $0.737$. Under the minimum IonQ pricing assumption considered here, this corresponds to an illustrative reduction in quantum kernel training cost from approximately \$1.34 billion to \$417{,}480. These results demonstrate that training-set reduction and low-rank approximation can substantially reduce the resources required to train non-variational quantum kernel anomaly detectors while retaining meaningful anomaly-discrimination performance.

</details>


### [106] [Engineering logical gates with irrep surface codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06775)
*Yabo Li,Zijian Song,Vieri Mattei,Naren Manjunath,Apoorv Tiwari,Tyler D. Ellison*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"不可约表示曲面码"(irrep surface codes, ISCs),设计一类二维拓扑量子纠错码,能够横向(transversally)实现指定的逻辑门集合,并通过晶格手术(lattice surgery)协议与常规曲面码计算集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算通常依赖一组固定的逻辑原语,实现其他所需操作往往需要消耗大量资源。作者希望系统性地定制拓扑码,使其原生支持特定逻辑门,从而降低实现目标操作的资源开销。

Method: 引入ISCs,即工程化的二维拓扑码,可横向实现预设逻辑门集合。所设计的门只需满足两个条件:(i)生成有限群(受Eastin-Knill定理约束,至多相差全局相位);(ii)不可约,即任何与门集对易的算符必正比于恒等算符。该框架可容纳任意有限对角门群及任意可逆经典门,因其均可嵌入更大的不可约有限门群,并支持超出Clifford层级任意有限层的逻辑门。此外,作者开发晶格手术协议,在曲面码与满足两类充分条件之一的ISC之间转移编码信息。

Result: 该框架可涵盖任意有限对角门群、任意可逆经典门,以及Clifford层级之外的逻辑门;并给出在曲面码与ISC间转移编码信息的晶格手术协议(需满足两类充分条件之一)。

Conclusion: 这些结果建立起一套系统化方法,可将拓扑码定制到所需逻辑操作,并集成进常规容错量子计算流程。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation typically relies on a fixed set of logical primitives, while realizing other desired operations can require substantial resources. We introduce irrep surface codes (ISCs), two-dimensional topological quantum error-correcting codes engineered to transversally implement a prescribed set of logical gates. The engineered gates only need to satisfy two conditions: (i) they generate a finite group, which, up to global phases, is forced by the Eastin-Knill theorem, and (ii) they are irreducible, meaning that any operator commuting with the set of gates must be proportional to the identity. This accommodates arbitrary finite groups of diagonal gates and arbitrary reversible classical gates, since each can be embedded into a larger irreducible finite group of gates. It also enables logical gates outside every finite level of the Clifford hierarchy. To incorporate these operations into surface-code computations, we further develop lattice-surgery protocols to transfer the encoded information between surface codes and ISCs obeying either of two sufficient conditions. Together, these results establish a systematic method for tailoring topological codes to desired logical operations and integrating them into conventional computations.

</details>


### [107] [Rewire and reset are all you need: Minimal resources for manipulating non-Abelions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06845)
*Chiu Fan Bowen Lo,Rohith Sajith,Anasuya Lyons,Ashvin Vishwanath,Ruben Verresen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作确定了量子双模型中任意子编织与融合所需的最少电路资源：编织可由无辅助比特的序列化酉电路实现，融合则被证明无法纯酉完成，但仅需一个量子比特重置即可实现"输入无关"融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子的编织与融合是拓扑量子计算的基础，但现有电路方案通常依赖辅助比特（ancilla）和测量，资源开销较大。作者希望确定在一般有限群的量子双模型中执行这些任意子操作所需的最少电路资源，并厘清辅助比特在编织与融合中的必要性。

Method: 对于编织，作者通过形变任意子所依附的底层图，构造顺序执行的、无辅助比特的酉电路；并进一步提出"图重连"（graph rewiring）这一图解演算，为单个双量子比特门赋予几何解释，并追踪其对稳定子（stabilizer）的作用。对于融合，作者先证明一个一般性不可行定理，排除在不引入辅助比特情况下对未知任意子类型进行纯酉融合的方案，随后给出基于量子比特重置的融合协议。

Result: 编织可由无辅助比特的序列化酉电路实现，其资源由底层图的形变刻画。融合方面得到一个no-go定理：在不引入辅助比特时，无法用纯酉电路融合未知类型的任意子；而仅使用一个量子比特重置即可实现这种"输入无关"的融合。因此，非阿贝尔任意子（non-Abelions）的编织与融合在资源上并不需要辅助比特。

Conclusion: 与普遍认知相反，辅助比特资源对于非阿贝尔任意子的编织和融合并非必需。该结果对纠错实验具有启示：所提出的融合协议凸显了"重置"这一被低估的纠错原语，可在不付出辅助比特与测量所需的时空开销的情况下实现纠错。

Abstract: Braiding and fusing anyons are fundamental to topological quantum computation, yet existing circuit protocols often employ ancillas and measurements. We determine the minimal circuit resources for performing such anyon operations in quantum double models of generic finite groups. Our approach braids anyons through a sequential unitary circuit without ancillas. Intuitively, this is obtained by deforming the underlying graph on which the anyons live. More generally, we introduce graph rewiring, a diagrammatic calculus that assigns a geometric interpretation to individual two-qudit gates and tracks their action on stabilizers. For fusion, we prove a general no-go theorem excluding purely unitary protocols for fusing unknown anyon types without introducing ancillas. Remarkably, we show such "input-agnostic" fusion is enabled by simply using a qudit reset. Hence, contrary to folklore, ancilla resources are not needed to braid or fuse non-Abelian anyons (non-Abelions). This work has implications in experimental efforts in error correction: our fusion protocol highlights reset as an underutilized primitive for error correction without the spacetime-overhead of ancillas and measurements.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum Sensing Applications in Consumer Electronics: Emerging Methods and Near-Term Use Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04230)
*Sounak Bhowmik,Himanshu Thapliyal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从平台与应用的框架出发，综述了量子传感在消费电子领域的近期实用性，涵盖四种关键平台及多类应用场景，指出其更可能通过升级混合经典-量子系统中的测量部件而落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费电子在尺寸、功耗与噪声方面对经典传感方法构成限制，量子传感作为一种切实可行的手段，有望提升消费电子的传感能力，因此需要评估其近期应用潜力。

Method: 采用平台与应用的框架进行综述：先概述通用传感协议，再讨论与消费电子相关的四种关键量子传感平台——氮-空位（NV）色心、原子蒸气池、里德堡原子传感器和光子量子传感器；并结合导航定位、医疗健康与可穿戴、射频与无线系统、物联网及成像等案例研究，分析其工程优势。

Result: 量子传感并非直接取代现有器件，而更可能通过升级混合经典-量子系统中的特定测量部件在近期获得应用；当前最明确的机会在于弱场探测、紧凑型射频接收以及在噪声环境下保持鲁棒的传感。

Conclusion: 量子传感在消费电子中的近期定位是作为混合系统的测量组件升级方案，重点方向为弱场探测、紧凑射频接收与抗噪传感。

Abstract: Quantum sensing is emerging as a practical means of improving sensing capabilities in consumer electronics, particularly where size, power, and noise constrain classical approaches. In this paper, we reviewed the near-term applicability of quantum sensing using a platform-and-application framework. Within this framework, we outlined the general sensing protocol and discussed the working of the four key quantum sensing platforms relevant to consumer electronics: nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers, atomic vapor cells, Rydberg atomic sensors, and photonic quantum sensors. Through case studies involving navigation and positioning, healthcare and wearables, radio-frequency (RF) and wireless systems, the Internet of Things (IoT), and imaging, we examined the tangible engineering advantages offered by quantum sensing. Rather than replacing devices outright, quantum sensing is more likely to earn its place in the near-term by upgrading specific measurement components within hybrid classical-quantum systems. Right now, the clearest opportunities are in weak-field detection, compact RF reception, and sensing that remains robust in noise.

</details>


### [109] [Optimal phase control of a measurement-assisted quantum refrigerator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04237)
*Jinkai Liu,Xian He,Jizhou He,Jianhui Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过对测量方位角相位α进行随机化开环控制（每次事件独立从分布P(α)采样），在保持事件率、极角和单事件布居转移概率不变的前提下，可消除稳态相干并最大化制冷功率；当且仅当⟨e^{iα}⟩_P=0时达到有效布居转移率上界，用两个等权、相位差π的相位即可实现，代表性参数下制冷功率提升约32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三能级量子冰箱中，倾斜的非选择性测量虽能关闭上跃迁，但会生成相干，相干反馈到布居上并抑制冷却流（制冷电流）。因此需要寻找一种控制手段来消除该相干的有害反馈、提升制冷性能。

Method: 对测量的方位角相位α实施随机化开环控制：每次测量事件独立地从概率分布P(α)中采样α，同时固定事件率、极角和单事件布居转移概率。直接布居转移与相位无关，而相干源带有因子e^{-iα}。通过精确消去稳态相干，得到有效布居转移率k_eff[P]的界0≤k_eff[P]≤A_m，其中A_m为裸直接混合率。

Result: 当固定测量同时耦合布居与相干时，上界A_m可被达到，当且仅当⟨e^{iα}⟩_P=0。最小实现方案为两个等权重、相位相差π的相位。由于制冷窗口内冷电流随k_eff严格单调增加，该条件在本文考虑的独立随机相位分布中最大化稳态制冷功率。对一组代表性参数，相位循环相比固定相位测量使固定能隙制冷功率提升约32%，而提取冷热量与供给测量能量之比保持不变。

Conclusion: 随机化相位（相位循环）是一种有效手段：在满足⟨e^{iα}⟩_P=0时，可在生成器层面消除布居—相干源及其回流，同时保留完整直接混合，从而在不改变热力学效率比的前提下显著提高量子冰箱的稳态制冷功率。

Abstract: A tilted nonselective measurement can close the upper transition of a three-level quantum refrigerator, but it also generates coherence that feeds back on the populations and suppresses the cooling current. We formulate randomized open-loop control of the measurement azimuthal phase \(α\), sampled independently at each event from a probability distribution \(P(α)\), while keeping the event rate, polar angle, and single-event population-transfer probability fixed. Direct population transfer is phase independent, whereas the coherence source carries the factor \(e^{-iα}\). Exact elimination of the stationary coherence gives \(0\leq k_{\rm eff}[P]\leq A_m\), where \(k_{\rm eff}[P]\) is the effective population-transfer rate between the upper levels and \(A_m\) is the bare direct mixing rate. When the fixed measurement couples populations and coherence, the upper bound is attained if and only if \(\langle e^{iα}\rangle_P=0\), where \(\langle\cdot\rangle_P\) denotes an average over the phase distribution. The minimal realization uses two equally weighted phases separated by \(π\): the population-to-coherence source and its return to the populations then vanish at the generator level, while the full direct mixing remains. Because the cold current increases strictly with \(k_{\rm eff}\) in the refrigeration window, the same condition maximizes the stationary cooling power over the independent random-phase distributions considered here. For one representative parameter set, phase cycling increases the fixed-gap cooling power by about \(32\%\) relative to a fixed-phase measurement, while leaving the ratio of extracted cold heat to supplied measurement energy unchanged.

</details>


### [110] [Q-MERGE: Parallelising Quantum State Preparation for Large-Scale Classical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04247)
*Archie Butterworth,Jens Renders,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 Q-MERGE，一种将大规模目标态分块、并行制备并经 SELECT-SWAP 与测量相干合并的振幅编码态制备框架，在保持对段级制备方法无关的同时，实现电路深度与量子比特数的可调权衡，并在真实数据集与离子阱硬件上验证其高保真与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子处理经典数据依赖将经典数据集高效映射到量子态振幅上，但大规模振幅编码态的制备是量子计算的主要瓶颈。现有方法在扩展到大态时面临电路深度或量子比特资源的严重限制。

Method: Q-MERGE 将大目标态划分为 M 个 n 量子比特段，独立并行制备各段，再通过 SELECT-SWAP 操作与测量相干合并为单一振幅编码态。该框架对段级制备方法不敏感，可复用现有技术处理更小子问题，并提供电路深度与量子比特数之间的可调权衡。借助 mid-circuit measurement 与 preparation-register reuse，将所需 ancilla 从 O(Mn) 降至 O(n)。

Result: 在真实 128×256 超声数据集上，Q-MERGE 达到 1.066×10^-8 的 infidelity，而相同底层方法的直接制备为 3.413×10^-1，提升约七个数量级。在 Quantinuum System Model H2 离子阱量子计算机上完成实验验证，并用 shadow-overlap tomography 验证制备态。对 Haar 随机态的数值分析显示在 M 高达 10^7 段时仍保持一致的 success probability。

Conclusion: Q-MERGE 通过分块制备与相干合并有效缓解大规模振幅编码态的制备瓶颈，显著降低 ancilla 开销并提供深度—比特权衡；实验与数值结果支持其面向海量经典数据集编码的可扩展性。

Abstract: Quantum processing of classical data fundamentally relies on efficiently mapping classical datasets onto the amplitudes of quantum states. Preparing large amplitude-encoded states, however, remains a major bottleneck in quantum computing. In this paper, we introduce a novel state-preparation framework we call Q-MERGE, that addresses this bottleneck by partitioning a large target state into $M$ $n$-qubit segments, preparing these segments independently and in parallel, and then coherently combining them into a single amplitude-encoded state using SELECT-SWAP operations and measurement. Q-MERGE is agnostic to the segment-level preparation method, allowing existing techniques to be applied to smaller subproblems while providing a tunable trade-off between circuit depth and qubit count. With mid-circuit measurement and preparation-register reuse, the required ancilla qubits can be reduced from $\mathcal{O}(Mn)$ to $\mathcal{O}(n)$. Applied to a real-world \(128\times256\) ultrasound dataset, Q-MERGE achieves an infidelity of $1.066\times10^{-8}$, compared with $3.413\times10^{-1}$ for direct preparation using the same underlying method, a seven-order of magnitude improvement. We demonstrate its feasibility experimentally on the Quantinuum System Model H2 trapped-ion quantum computer and validate the prepared state using shadow-overlap tomography. Numerical analysis of Haar-random states indicates consistent success probability for up to $M=10^7$ segments, supporting the scalability of Q-MERGE for encoding massive classical datasets.

</details>


### [111] [Amenable groups with nearly exponential sofic profile, and quantum channels that need nearly linear memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04271)
*Seth Douglas,Nidhal Mghirbi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个有限呈示的初等可均群，其某个有限片段的 sofic profile（Cornulier 定义）为 exp(r^{1+o(1)})，接近计数方法可探测的上限；该群嵌入 Brin 群 3V，由此导出精确量子信道存在性的对偶结论，并给出其记忆-纯度权衡与 Stinespring 膨胀的精确刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可均群在多大程度上"远离有限"是一个核心问题：群的每个有限片段都能被有限集的置换模仿，而模仿所需的点数（sofic profile）刻画了这种逼近代价。作者希望构造 sofic profile 尽可能大（接近计数上限）的有限呈示可均群，并借此揭示群论与量子信道资源理论之间的深层联系。

Method: 构造一个"灯夫"群，但灯放在配置上而非点上：Houghton 群移动三条射线的点，有限支撑仿射映射作用于其二进制配置，每个配置上放置一个 S_3 的拷贝。该群中 N 个点承载 2^N 盏灯，而任意两盏灯可通过每次至多涉及三个点的移动汇聚。群嵌入 Brin 群 3V。关键工具是"单轮定理"：设备单次使用后，其记忆携带该群的近似表示。

Result: 构造的有限呈示初等可均群具有有限片段，其 profile 为 exp(r^{1+o(1)})；3V 亦含具有近指数 profile 的有限片段。导出的 C^{873} 上量子信道：使用 n 次、每次在下一输入到达前释放输出的设备，需约 n/B 量子比特记忆（允许消耗 B 比特纯度），精确设备可达至多对数因子；最优交换率下最少记忆约 sqrt(n)，纯度同阶。该信道可因子化且属于有限最大混合浴信道的闭包，但任何以精度 u 模仿它的此类浴所需维度为 exp(u^{-1+o(1)})，而最小 Stinespring 膨胀以维度 130 的纯环境精确实现它。

Conclusion: 该工作以组合群论构造近乎最优 sofic profile 的可均群，并通过单轮定理将其转化为量子信道的一次性与多次性使用之间的资源不对称：信道"便宜用一次、昂贵用多次"，其记忆-纯度权衡与膨胀维度被精确量化，体现了群近似表示与量子信道复杂度之间的对偶。

Abstract: How far from finite can an amenable group be? Every finite piece of an amenable group can be imitated by permutations of a finite set, and Cornulier's sofic profile counts how many points such an imitation needs at accuracy $1/r$. We build a finitely presented elementary amenable group with a finite piece whose profile is $\exp(r^{1+o(1)})$, close to the most our counting method can ever detect. The construction is a lamplighter with its lamps on configurations rather than on points. Houghton's group moves the points of three rays, finitely supported affine maps act on their binary configurations, and a copy of $S_3$ sits on every configuration. Then $N$ points carry $2^N$ lamps, yet any two lamps can be brought together by moves that each involve at most three points. The group embeds in Brin's group $3V$, so $3V$ too has a finite piece of nearly exponential profile. The group also gives an explicit quantum channel on $\mathbb C^{873}$ that is cheap to use once and expensive to use many times. A device that applies it $n$ times, releasing each output before the next input arrives, needs about $n/B$ qubits of memory when it may spend $B$ bits of purity, and exact devices achieve this up to polylogarithmic factors. With exchange at the optimal rate, the least memory is about $\sqrt n$, attained with purity of the same order. The channel is factorizable and lies in the closure of channels with finite maximally mixed baths, yet any such bath that imitates it to accuracy $u$ needs dimension $\exp(u^{-1+o(1)})$, although its minimal Stinespring dilation implements it exactly with a pure environment of dimension $130$. The engine is a one-round theorem: after a single use, the memory of any such device carries an approximate representation of the group.

</details>


### [112] [Polynomial Separation between One-Way and Two-Way LOCC in Discrimination of Maximally Entangled States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04288)
*Youngrong Lim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：在任意局部维度 d 下，任意 k 个两两正交的最大纠缠态集合，只要 d ≥ Ck⁴(log2k)^ν，就可用 A→B→A 双向 LOCC 协议完美区分；由此得到单向与双向 LOCC 之间的多项式分离，并推出九份拷贝足以在足够大的局部维度下区分任意正交最大纠缠态集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单向 LOCC 在 d≥4 时甚至无法区分四个正交最大纠缠态，说明单向能力存在本质局限。本文旨在量化单向反馈（单条经典消息）能带来多大提升，并刻画双向 LOCC 可完美区分的正交最大纠缠态数量随局部维度 d 的增长关系，从而建立单向与双向 LOCC 之间的复杂度分离。

Method: 设计 A→B→A 三阶段协议：Alice 先做测量并发送一条反馈消息给 Bob，同时保持态的精确正交性；Bob 据此缩小候选集合至单元素或一对正交态；Bob 再细化自己的测量结果并将信息回传，使 Alice 能够完成最终区分。通过构造性分析给出协议在 d ≥ Ck⁴(log2k)^ν 条件下的完美区分保证。

Result: 证明任意 k 个两两正交最大纠缠态在 d ≥ Ck⁴(log2k)^ν（C,ν 为普适常数）时可被 A→B→A 协议完美区分。等价地，单条反馈消息可将始终可区分候选数从常数提升至 Ω(d^{1/4}/(log d)^{ν/4})，从而给出单向与双向 LOCC 的多项式分离。推论：在足够大的局部维度下，九份拷贝即可完美区分任意正交最大纠缠态集合。

Conclusion: 单向 LOCC 的区分能力受限，而仅增加一条反馈消息即可实现从常数到 d 的多项式级提升。本文刻画了双向 LOCC 完美区分的维度阈值，确立了单向与双向 LOCC 之间的多项式分离，并表明九份拷贝在充分大的局部维度下足以完成任意正交最大纠缠态集合的完美双向 LOCC 区分。

Abstract: One-way local operations and classical communication (LOCC) can fail to distinguish four orthogonal maximally entangled states in every local dimension $d\ge4$. We show that every set of $k$ pairwise orthogonal maximally entangled states in $\mathbb C^d\otimes\mathbb C^d$ is perfectly distinguishable by an $A\to B\to A$ protocol whenever $d\ge Ck^4[\log(2k)]^ν$, for universal constants $C,ν>0$. A single feedback message therefore raises the number of candidates that can always be distinguished from a constant to $Ω\!\left(d^{1/4}/(\log d)^{ν/4}\right)$, establishing a polynomial separation between one-way and two-way LOCC. In our protocol, Alice preserves exact orthogonality while reducing overlaps between Bob's conditional subspaces. Bob reduces the candidates to a singleton or an orthogonal pair and refines his outcome so that Alice can complete the discrimination. As a corollary, nine copies suffice for perfect two-way LOCC discrimination of any orthogonal maximally entangled set in all sufficiently large local dimensions.

</details>


### [113] [The cost of each side condition in a gauged logical measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04306)
*Shuoming An,Fusheng Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文剖析了 Williamson–Yoder 关于 gauging 测量容错性保证所依赖的四个附带条件,证明它们权重不等:首末轮完美性条件承担了时间维度的容错距离,去掉它会使故障距离坍缩为 1;而扩张条件并不决定结果。两个分量在含参双变量自行车码与 Bacon–Shor 测量上被精确计算并在证明助手中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑测量读出受保护信息,其能承受的噪声决定了量子计算可吸收的错误量。Gauging 通过在被测量子比特构成的图上、每条边放置一个额外量子比特来构造逻辑测量,将全局算符转化为局域奇偶校验。其容错性分为两部分:所留代码的距离,以及故障可隐藏的轮数。Williamson 与 Yoder 证明两者均不低于代码距离,但该保证依赖四个被视为整体的附带条件,而这些条件从未被单独定价。本文旨在揭示这些条件并不等价,从而为阈值估计提供精确依据。

Method: 作者通过构造性反例与精确计算,逐一检验四个附带条件的作用。特别地,考虑一种探测器比较相邻轮次的模型,分析去掉"首末轮必须完美"这一看起来仅属记账性质的条件后的后果;并在同一组量子比特上、均处于覆盖范围之外的两条路径上比较扩张条件的影响。随后在含参双变量自行车码与 Bacon–Shor 测量上精确计算距离与轮数两个分量,并在证明助手中完成形式化验证。

Result: 对于每种代码与每种轮数,去掉首末轮完美条件后,故障距离(即未被察觉却翻转读出的最小权重故障)被压平为 1,所测实例上完整协议随之崩溃。扩张条件(即不允许小集合量子比特被封闭)并不决定结果:同一组量子比特上的两条路径,均处于覆盖范围之外,却分别给出距离 1 与 2。在"比较轮次即全部规则"的情形下轮数是紧的;而完整协议比轮数提前一轮达到该距离。

Conclusion: 四个附带条件并非同等重要:首末轮完美性这一看似记账性的要求实际承担了时间维度的容错距离,而扩张条件对结果不起决定作用。两分量在具体实例上被精确计算并形式化验证。阈值估计会消耗此类数值,因而附带条件是可以被定价的,这为更精细的容错分析提供了依据。

Abstract: A logical measurement reads out protected information, and the noise it survives sets how much a quantum computation can absorb. Gauging builds one from a graph on the measured qubits, one extra qubit on each edge, turning a global operator into local parity checks. Its tolerance splits in two: the distance of the code left behind, and the rounds a fault can hide in. Williamson and Yoder proved both components stay at or above the code's distance. The guarantee rests on four side conditions taken as a package, none of them priced. Here we show they are not worth the same: the demand that the first and last rounds be perfect, which reads like bookkeeping, carries the temporal part. Dropping it flattens the fault distance, the least weight of a fault that passes unseen and flips the readout, to one for every code and round count, in a model whose detectors compare adjacent rounds; the full protocol collapses on the instance measured. The expansion condition, that no small set of qubits be sealed off, does not decide the outcome: two paths on the same qubits, both outside the covered regime, give distances one and two. The round count is tight where comparing rounds is the whole rule; the full protocol reaches the distance a round early. Both components are computed exactly on a gauged bivariate bicycle code and a Bacon--Shor measurement, inside a proof assistant. Threshold estimates consume such numbers; side conditions can be priced.

</details>


### [114] [Lindblad equation for the quantum Rabi model in the deep strong coupling regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06417)
*Daniel Holst,Patrick Potts,Peter Samuelsson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在微扰深强耦合区为开放量子Rabi模型导出了Lindblad形式的量子主方程，并与Bloch-Redfield方程进行了基准对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子Rabi模型在深强耦合区（谱接近简并且近似谐振）缺乏简洁、物理一致的主方程描述，需要构造Lindblad形式以刻画耗散动力学。

Method: 在Schrieffer-Wolff变换框架下，结合secular近似与singular-coupling近似，推导微扰深强耦合区的Lindblad形式主方程；并与Bloch-Redfield方程进行对比基准测试。

Result: 所推导方程与Bloch-Redfield方程在多个可观测量的时间演化及稳态性质上表现出极好的一致性；在特定极限情形下获得了可解析的结果。

Conclusion: 该Lindblad主方程可为深强耦合区开放量子Rabi模型的耗散动力学提供有效且物理一致的描述，并支持极限情形的解析分析。

Abstract: We derive a quantum master equation in Lindblad form for the open quantum Rabi model in the perturbative deep strong coupling regime, where the spectrum is close to degenerate and harmonic. The Lindblad form is obtained by combining secular and singular-coupling approximations, working in a Schrieffer-Wolff transformed frame. We benchmark our equation against a Bloch-Redfield one, analyzing the time-evolution and steady state properties of several observables and find excellent agreement. Analytical results are obtained in limiting cases.

</details>


### [115] [Generalized Dunkl Quantum Systems with Energy-Dependent Interactions: Exact Solvability and Thermodynamic Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04322)
*B. Hamil,R. Boudjouraf,A. Benchikha,M. Merad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一类由含能量相关相互作用的广义Dunkl–Schrödinger方程描述的可精确求解量子系统，引入含传统反射项与导数-反射耦合项的双参数Dunkl算符扩展，导出修正Heisenberg代数、连续性方程与守恒概率流，并以能量相关谐振子为例求出Laguerre多项式形式的精确解及热力学量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dunkl变形与能量相关相互作用各自已被广泛研究，但两者结合的框架尚缺乏系统的可解模型与一致的概率诠释。作者希望构建一个兼具反射对称性与能量依赖性的广义形式体系，以扩展可精确求解量子模型的范围，并考察其对能谱与热力学性质的调控能力。

Method: 引入含传统反射贡献与额外导数-反射耦合的双参数Dunkl微分算符扩展，得到修正Heisenberg代数；由此导出连续性方程、守恒概率流与广义归一化条件。以能量相关谐振子为具体应用，解析求得以关联Laguerre多项式表示的精确本征函数与能谱；再用Euler–Maclaurin求和公式计算正则配分函数，并导出内能、熵与比热的解析表达式，进行数值分析。

Result: 获得了能量相关谐振子的精确本征函数与能谱解析式；Wigner参数、形变参数与能量依赖参数的联合效应产生宇称劈裂、非线性能谱与非均匀能级间距，同时保持精确可解性。导出了配分函数、内能、熵与比热的解析表达式，数值分析表明广义Dunkl形变与能量相关相互作用可有效调控系统的谱结构与热学特性。

Conclusion: 广义Dunkl形变与能量相关相互作用为具有反射对称性的精确可解量子模型提供了新的可调控自由度，扩展了此类模型的范围，并为谱结构与热力学行为的联合调控提供了理论工具。

Abstract: This work investigates a class of exactly solvable quantum systems described by a generalized Dunkl--Schrödinger equation with energy-dependent interactions. A two-parameter extension of the Dunkl differential operator is introduced, incorporating both the conventional reflection contribution and an additional derivative-reflection coupling. The resulting formalism leads to a modified Heisenberg algebra, from which the continuity equation, conserved probability current, and generalized normalization condition are derived for energy-dependent potentials. As an application, an energy-dependent harmonic oscillator is studied in detail. Exact analytical expressions for the eigenfunctions and energy spectrum are obtained in terms of associated Laguerre polynomials. The combined effects of the Wigner parameter, the deformation parameter, and the energy-dependence parameter are shown to produce parity splitting, nonlinear energy spectra, and nonuniform level spacing while preserving exact solvability. The thermodynamic properties of the model are then examined through the canonical partition function using the Euler--Maclaurin summation formula. Analytical expressions for the partition function, internal energy, entropy, and specific heat are derived and analyzed numerically. The results demonstrate that the generalized Dunkl deformation and energy-dependent interaction provide effective control over both the spectral and thermal characteristics of the system, extending the class of exactly solvable quantum models with reflection symmetry.

</details>


### [116] [A Cheat-Sensitive Primitive for Destination-Private Quantum Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04359)
*S. F. E. Oliviero,S. Onofri,V. Giovannetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种极简的两次调用路由原语：用户Alice在多个候选客户端中建立纠缠链路，同时通过相干编码的隐藏顺序机制检测服务提供者Bob是否窥探了目标地址。协议证明了路由请求可携带物理层面的作弊检测，并能以非零概率发现显式的目的地读取攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络依赖中间节点分发纠缠，但这些节点可能获知哪些用户正在被连接，从而带来隐私泄露风险。因此需要一种机制，在完成路由请求的同时检测中间节点是否读取了目标地址。

Method: 借鉴量子隐私查询的隐藏顺序机制，构造两次调用的路由原语：一次调用包含直接请求，另一次包含目标地址与空地址的相干叠加，Alice对两次调用的顺序保密。被选中客户端持有的私有匹配量子比特在有利的Bell测量结果后恢复相干测试态，使Alice无需Bob任何额外操作即可验证返回的查询。

Result: 该协议表明路由请求可以携带物理作弊测试，并以非零概率检测出显式的目的地读取攻击。作者明确限定其结论范围：尚未给出定量的信息-扰动权衡、可组合安全性，也未证明输出在任意攻击下未被测量。此外还刻画了精确的完美通过极限，其中Bob的剩余态在私有匹配态平均后与目的地无关。

Conclusion: 本工作提出了一个初步但方向明确的量子路由隐私检测原语，展示了将物理作弊测试嵌入路由请求的可行性，同时坦承其在安全性证明上的局限，为后续定量权衡与可组合安全研究留下开放问题。

Abstract: Quantum networks will rely on intermediate nodes to distribute entanglement, but the same nodes may learn which users are being connected. We introduce a minimal two-call routing primitive in which a user, Alice, establishes an entangled link with one of several clients while testing whether the provider, Bob, has disturbed a coherently encoded request. The construction adapts the hidden-order mechanism of quantum private queries: one call contains a direct request, the other a coherent superposition of the desired and null addresses, and Alice keeps their order secret. A private matching qubit held by the selected client restores the coherent test state after a favorable Bell-measurement outcome, so that Alice can test the returned query without any further operation by Bob. The claim is deliberately limited. The protocol shows that a routing request can carry a physical cheating test, and it detects an explicit destination-reading attack with nonzero probability. It does not yet provide a quantitative information-disturbance tradeoff, composable security, or certification of an unmeasured output against arbitrary attacks. We also characterize the exact perfect-pass limit, in which Bob's residual state, averaged over the private matching states, is independent of the destination.

</details>


### [117] [Structural Conditions for Distributed Quantum Advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04393)
*Sabina Drăgoi,María Gragera Garcés,Lirandë Pira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式量子优势的三项必要条件——可负担的"编织"（knitting）、剪切后仍存活的经典困难性，以及变分电路可求解的梯度——并证明其随子电路增长/接口宽度的标度规律，作为筛选准则应用于十八类电路族，且在IBM处理器上验证了跨两个受场扰动环面码片区的单门编织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路切割（circuit cutting）可将量子计算运行于比电路本身更小的处理器上，但其代价是经典"编织"成本随被切门数指数增长。仅保证成本可负担并不足以构成量子优势，因为若被编织的子电路本身经典易解，则整体仍可经典模拟。因此需要明确分布式量子优势所需满足的条件，并据此筛选有潜力的电路族。

Method: 作者形式化提出分布式量子优势的三项要求：（1）可负担的编织；（2）经典困难性在切割后仍保留；（3）变分电路需具备可分辨的梯度。随后证明两个标度结论：当子电路在有界接口之后增长时编织成本保持可负担，而当固定宽度子电路数量增多时成本呈指数增长。将这些要求转化为筛选准则，并对十八类电路族进行系统评估；最后以经典可验证的原理性证明，在IBM Nighthawk处理器上对两个受场扰动的环面码片区执行连接单门编织，覆盖至多142个自旋。

Result: 理论结果表明，有限局域深度且接口有界的电路是可同时满足三项要求的一类设置，但其困难性仅在worst case下成立。筛选十八类电路族后识别出这一可行区域。实验方面，在IBM Nighthawk上完成单门编织，最多涉及142个自旋；重构结果在至多98量子比特规模下，能够分辨出接触关联（contact correlation）并超越独立执行的表现。

Conclusion: 可负担的编织是分布式量子优势的必要而非充分条件，需同时满足经典困难性的保留以及变分梯度可解性；有限局域深度、有界接口的电路族是有希望的候选，但其平均情形困难性尚未确立，仅为worst case。实验验证了跨环面码片区的编织在原理上可行，为后续在可证明困难性场景下的分布式量子优势研究提供了筛查框架与实证基础。

Abstract: Circuit cutting runs a quantum computation on processors smaller than the circuit, at the price of classical knitting whose cost grows exponentially with the number of cut gates. Keeping this cost affordable is necessary but not sufficient for an advantage, since knitting classically easy subcircuits is itself classically easy. We formulate three requirements for distributed quantum advantage under a stated budget: affordable knitting, classical difficulty that survives the cut, and, for variational circuits, resolvable gradients. We prove that under stated assumptions knitting stays affordable when subcircuits grow behind a bounded interface and becomes exponentially expensive when fixed-width subcircuits multiply. We turn the requirements into screening criteria and apply them to eighteen circuit families. We identify finite local-depth circuits with bounded interfaces as a setting in which the requirements can coexist, with hardness established only in the worst case. As a classically verifiable proof of principle, we knit one gate joining two field-perturbed toric-code patches on an IBM Nighthawk processor for up to 142 spins, and confirm that the reconstruction can at least resolve the contact correlation beyond independent execution for up to 98 qubits.

</details>


### [118] [Imaginarity as a resource in Shor's algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04443)
*Linlin Ye,Chuanfa Wu,Zhaoqi Wu,Shao-Ming Fei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从量子信道"虚性"（imaginarity）视角研究顺序版Shor阶寻找算法，将过程分解为实对角因子与残余相位信道，建立了残余虚性与单块成功概率的联系，给出理想与噪声情形下成功概率的精确值及上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shor阶寻找算法的顺序实现中，成功概率的资源来源尚缺乏细化的刻画。作者希望通过量子信道的虚性这一量子资源度量，揭示信道资源行为与算法成功概率之间的定量关系，从而为理想与噪声情形下的顺序量子算法提供更精细的解释。

Method: 固定模乘酉算子的一个本征分支与目标输出路径，将顺序过程分解为实对角因子与残余相位信道；对理想残余相位信道分析三种迹范数诱导的虚性度量；结合分支概率推导成功概率的精确表达式，并对噪声残余相位信道进行推广分析。

Result: 对理想的残余相位信道，三种迹范数诱导的虚性度量彼此相等；在"好"输出路径上，较小的残余虚性对应较高的单块成功概率。结合各分支概率得到顺序阶寻找协议的精确理想成功概率，并给出以残余相位信道虚性表示的下界与上界；进一步推广至噪声信道，得到噪声成功概率的上下界。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了信道虚性在Shor算法中的作用，为理想与噪声顺序量子算法中的量子资源行为提供了精细化视角。

Abstract: We investigate the sequential version of Shor's order-finding algorithm from the perspective of the imaginarity of quantum channels. By fixing an eigenbranch of the modular multiplication unitary and a target output path, the process is effectively decomposed into a real diagonal factor and a residual phase channel. We find that for the ideal residual phase channel, the three trace-norm induced imaginarity measures are all equal. On good output paths, a smaller residual imaginarity corresponds to a higher single-block success probability. By combining the branch-wise probabilities, we obtain the exact ideal success probability of the sequential order-finding protocol and derive lower and upper bounds in terms of residual phase-channel imaginarity. We further extend the analysis to noisy residual phase channels, and obtain the lower and upper bounds on the noisy success probability. These results clarify the role of imaginarity of channels in Shor's algorithm and provide a refined perspective on quantum-resource behavior in ideal and noisy sequential quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [119] [Optimal Interferometer Geometry for Gravitationally Induced Quantum Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04471)
*Alireza Maleki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析引力诱导量子纠缠方案中干涉仪几何构型的关键作用，发现几何优化可显著增强引力诱导相移，并指出原有近似会严重损害预测精度与实验可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力实验验证因普朗克尺度不可及而极为困难，近期提出利用两个大质量粒子间的引力诱导量子纠缠作为引力量子性的经验证据，但需提升其可探测性。

Method: 以量子干涉测量技术为基础，分析干涉仪几何构型对引力诱导纠缠产生的影响，并进行几何优化与相移计算评估。

Result: 几何优化能大幅增强引力诱导相移，改善实验探测前景；同时表明既有方案所用的标准近似会严重限制预测精度并危及实验可行性。

Conclusion: 干涉仪几何构型对纠缠生成具有决定性作用，几何优化与更精确的处理方法对引力诱导纠缠的实验检验至关重要。

Abstract: The quest for a quantum theory of gravity remains a central challenge in modern physics, driven by the goal of unifying general relativity and quantum mechanics. Direct experimental access to quantum gravitational effects is notoriously difficult due to the inaccessibility of the Planck scale. Recent proposals, however, suggest that gravitationally induced quantum entanglement between two massive particles, realized through advanced quantum interferometric techniques, could provide empirical evidence for the quantum nature of gravity. We demonstrate that the geometrical configuration of an interferometer plays a decisive role in entanglement generation. Our analysis reveals that geometric optimization can substantially enhance the gravitationally induced phase shift, thereby improving the prospects for experimental detection of gravity-mediated entanglement. Moreover, we show that standard approximations employed in prior proposals may severely limit predictive accuracy and compromise experimental feasibility.

</details>


### [120] [Symmetry resolved entanglement in Schwinger pair production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04513)
*Wajid Joyia,S. M. Al-Marzoug,Amaria Javed*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用 U(1) 电荷对称分辨技术，将施温格效应产生的成对模式纠缠分解到各电荷扇区，给出恒定电场下每对模式的闭式结果，揭示电场通过重新分配统计权重和输运扇区间纠缠来组织纠缠的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施温格效应中的粒子对产生会生成量子纠缠，但通常的总纠缠度量或粒子数分布无法显示纠缠在电荷空间中具体驻留于哪些扇区，以及电场如何移动这些纠缠。作者希望通过引入电荷对称分辨，提供比总量度量更精细的物理图像。

Method: 以 QED 的 U(1) 电荷为对称群，对纯双分划约化使用对称分辨纠缠熵（symmetry resolved entanglement entropy），对混合粒子-反粒子对使用电荷失衡分辨负性（charge imbalance resolved negativity）。将动量模式的约化密度矩阵按电荷扇区分解，并在恒定电场下对每一种模式配对求闭式解，所有场依赖由单对产生概率 |β|^2=e^{-πμ} 控制。

Result: 对粒子-粒子、反粒子-反粒子对，各电荷扇区内的纠缠熵与电场无关，电场只重新分配各扇区的统计权重；对混合粒子-反粒子对，被占据扇区额外带有一个场依赖熵。真空产生的粒子对最为丰富：其分辨负性分布在三个失衡扇区，随电场增大从一个扇区输运到下一个，仅在临界场时三者贡献相等。跨越固定动量双分划的纠缠则冻结在初始值，因为对产生守恒扇区电荷。

Conclusion: 对称分辨把总纠缠度量细化为扇区分辨图像，暴露出施温格机制纠缠在电荷空间中的位置以及电场如何移动它，提供了超越总纠缠度量与粒子数分布的信息。

Abstract: We study the charge resolved structure of the pairwise mode entanglement produced by the Schwinger effect. Using the $U(1)$ electric charge of quantum electrodynamics, we decompose the entanglement of the reduced density matrices of momentum modes into individual charge sectors, applying the symmetry resolved entanglement entropy to the pure bipartition reductions and the charge imbalance resolved negativity to the mixed particle-antiparticle pair. For a constant electric field we obtain closed form results for every mode pairing, with all field dependence controlled by the single pair production probability $|β|^2=e^{-πμ}$. For the particle, particle and antiparticle antiparticle pairs the entanglement entropy within each charge sector is field independent, so that the field acts only by redistributing statistical weight among the sectors; for the mixed particle antiparticle pair the populated sector additionally carries a field dependent entropy. The vacuum created pair is the richest case: its resolved negativity is distributed over three imbalance sectors and is transported from one sector to the next as the field increases, the sectors contributing equally only at the critical field. The entanglement across a fixed momentum bipartition, by contrast, is frozen at its initial value because pair creation conserves the sector charge. Symmetry resolution thus refines the total entanglement measures into a sector resolved picture, exposing where in charge space the entanglement of the Schwinger mechanism resides and how the electric field moves it, information beyond that contained in the total measures or in particle number distributions.

</details>


### [121] [The collapse time of weak measurement on $N$ entangled sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04556)
*Truong-Son P. Van,David W. Snoke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了在N个备选初态叠加中，局域弱测量坍缩到单一占据位点所需时间。对于等初始占据概率和固定置信阈值1-δ，证明T_N/ln N→1/2（L^1意义下），时间以特征测量时间τ_m为单位，并纠正了此前提出的双对数渐近标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清弱测量诱导坍缩的渐近时间标度，纠正此前文献中提出的双对数渐近标度错误。

Method: 基于PRA 111, 032217 (2025)模型，在等初始占据概率、固定置信阈值1-δ条件下，对局域弱测量坍缩时间进行解析证明（L^1收敛），并结合有限测量数值拟合。

Result: 证明T_N/ln N→1/2在L^1意义下成立；首个达到1-δ的位点即为最终结果的概率恰为1-δ；纠正双对数渐近标度；有限范围数值拟合无法确定渐近常数修正或精确的有限N规律。

Conclusion: 弱测量坍缩时间的正确渐近标度为T_N∼(1/2)ln N，与先前双对数预测相悖；有限N行为仍无精确律。

Abstract: We determine the time required for local weak measurements to collapse to one occupied site in an initial superposition of $N$ alternatives in the model of PRA 111, 032217 (2025). For equal initial occupation probabilities and a fixed confidence threshold $1-δ$, we prove $T_N/\ln N\to1/2$ in $L^1$, with time measured in units of the characteristic measurement time $τ_m$ of each detector. We also connect the asymptotic stabilization theorem of that work to a finite measurement: the first site to reach $1-δ$ is the eventual outcome with conditional probability exactly $1-δ$. The result corrects the previously proposed double-logarithmic asymptotic scaling. The finite-range numerical fit does not determine a constant asymptotic correction or a precise finite-$N$ law.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum Submodular Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04560)
*Yonggang Jiang,Xiaoming Sun,Penghui Yao,Zekun Ye,Jialin Zhang,Zhijie Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非负次模函数最大化的量子查询复杂度，包括无约束情形与单调函数的基数约束情形。在精确可逆数字价值神谕模型下，无约束问题可用 O_ε(log n) 查询达到 (1/2-ε)-近似，相比经典 Ω(n/log n) 下界呈指数分离；基数约束问题以 Õ_ε(min{√n, n/k}) 查询达到 (1-1/e-ε)-近似，并证明了逼近阈值处的量子下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次模函数最大化在经典计算下需要大量查询，其经典查询复杂度已被充分研究并存在若干下界。作者希望探明量子计算能否在该问题上突破经典查询复杂度壁垒，明确量子加速的适用范围与极限。

Method: 在精确可逆数字价值神谕模型下设计量子算法：针对无约束最大化给出期望 (1/2-ε)-近似算法；针对基数约束 k 的单调次模最大化给出有界误差的 (1-1/e-ε)-近似算法；同时构造量子上界对应的下界证明（利用多项式方法等导出指数级查询下界）。

Result: 无约束：O_ε(log n) 查询达 (1/2-ε)-近似，与经典 Ω(n/log n) 形成指数分离。基数约束：Õ_ε(min{√n, n/k}) 查询达 (1-1/e-ε)-近似；k=o(n) 时相对经典至少有至多对数因子的二次加速；k=cn（c<1-1/e-ε）时为 O_{ε,c}(log n)，与经典 Ω(n/log n) 指数分离。下界：无约束超过 1/2+ε 需 exp(Ω(ε²n)) 查询，k/n≤ε 时超过 1-1/e+ε 需 exp(Ω(ε²k)) 查询。

Conclusion: 量子计算在次模函数最大化的特定近似阈值下可实现指数级或二次加速，但在超过 1/2（无约束）和超过 1-1/e（小基数约束）的近似阈值处，量子计算无法带来指数加速，存在指数级查询下界。

Abstract: We study the quantum query complexity of maximizing a non-negative submodular function, considering both the unconstrained setting and, for monotone functions, a cardinality constraint $k$ on an $n$-element ground set. In the exact reversible digital value-oracle model, our unconstrained algorithm achieves an expected $(1/2-\varepsilon)$-approximation using only $O_\varepsilon(\log n)$ queries. In contrast, any classical randomized algorithm that attains a fixed expected ratio above $1/4$ requires $Ω(n/\log n)$ queries (Li, Feldman, Kazemi, and Karbasi, 2022), establishing an exponential separation in query complexity. For cardinality-constrained maximization, we give a bounded-error quantum algorithm that achieves a $(1-1/e-\varepsilon)$-approximation using $\widetilde O_\varepsilon(\min\{\sqrt n,n/k\})$ queries. When $k=o(n)$, our algorithm achieves at least a quadratic speedup up to logarithmic factors over classical randomized algorithms (Mirzasoleiman, Badanidiyuru, Karbasi, Vondrák, and Krause, 2015; Peng and Rubinstein, 2025). Moreover, when $k=cn$ for any fixed rational $c<1-1/e-\varepsilon$, the query complexity reduces to $O_{\varepsilon,c}(\log n)$, yielding an exponential separation from the classical $Ω(n/\log n)$ lower bound (Li, Feldman, Kazemi, and Karbasi, 2022). We further prove quantum lower bounds of $\exp(Ω(\varepsilon^2n))$ queries for achieving a ratio beyond $1/2+\varepsilon$ without constraints, and $\exp(Ω(\varepsilon^2k))$ queries for exceeding $1-1/e+\varepsilon$ when $k/n\le\varepsilon$. These barriers demonstrate that quantum computation offers no exponential speedup at these approximation thresholds.

</details>


### [123] [Geometry-Controlled Squeezed-Waveguide Enabled Quantum Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04562)
*Luis D. Zambrano-Palma,Yusef Maleki,M. Suhail Zubairy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究一维波导中多发射体量子电池在宽带压缩真空库驱动下的性能，揭示波导几何（发射体位置）通过调控集体与相位敏感耦合来控制稳态的非经典关联及非被动性，从而实现ergotropy的强调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用非经典资源提升量子电池性能是量子技术的重要目标，而波导几何如何调控电池性能与热力学特性尚需明确。

Method: 将多个发射体耦合到一维波导，并由宽带压缩真空库驱动；通过集体耗散与相位敏感关联分析单、双、三发射体构型的稳态与ergotropy。

Result: 单发射体：压缩库沉积有限能量但态仍被动，ergotropy为零。双发射体：相位敏感库关联产生相干，可将稳态驱动至完全非被动区。三发射体：集体热动力学本身支持有限ergotropy，压缩进一步提高可提取能量占比。由于集体与相位敏感耦合依赖发射体位置，几何可直接控制非经典关联。

Conclusion: ergotropy可被强烈调制而存储能量变化微弱；在双发射体构型中，仅几何即可在被动稳态与全部储能可幺正提取的稳态之间切换，表明波导几何是量子电池非被动性的直接控制手段。

Abstract: Harnessed nonclassical resources to enhance the performance of quantum batteries is an important goal of quantum technology. In this work, we investigate multi-emitter batteries coupled to a one-dimensional waveguide driven by a broadband squeezed-vacuum reservoir, and determine how the waveguide geometry controls the battery performance and and their thermodynamic features. We show that for a single emitter, the squeezed reservoir deposits finite energy while the state remains passive, yielding zero ergotropy. For two emitters, however, phase-sensitive reservoir correlations generate coherence and can drive the steady state into a fully nonpassive regime. For three emitters, collective thermal dynamics already supports finite ergotropy, while squeezing further increases the extractable component of the stored energy. Crucially, because the collective and phase-sensitive couplings depend on the emitter positions, geometry provides direct control over the nonclassical correlations and, consequently, over the nonpassivity of the steady state.
