The ergotropy can therefore be strongly modulated while the stored energy changes only weakly, and in the two-emitter configuration geometry alone can switch the battery between a passive steady state and one in which all stored energy is unitarily extractable.

</details>


### [124] [Decoy-state optical quantum information processing with coherent states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04581)
*Wenyuan Wang,H. F. Chau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于相干态与阈值探测器的线性回归方法，结合随机/结构化采样，模拟单光子源和光子数分辨探测器，在Fock态统计预测精度和探测资源消耗上显著优于传统诱骗态线性规划方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效高速单光子源与光子数分辨（PNR）探测器在实验上仍难以实现，限制了量子信息处理任务的开展。现有基于线性规划和笛卡尔积网格采样的诱骗态后处理方法精度有限且探测资源消耗大，亟需一种更高效、更精确的替代方案。

Method: 利用相位随机化相干态和阈值探测器，结合诱骗态后处理来模拟单光子源与PNR探测器。提出一种简单的线性回归方法，基于相干态探针的可观测统计量预测Fock态统计，并配合随机或结构化采样策略选择探针设置。该方法适用于固定希尔伯特空间维度的未知酉电路，也可扩展至非酉量子信道（如有损、非线性或任意信道）。

Result: 相比传统基于线性规划和笛卡尔积网格的诱骗态后处理，该方法在精度上大幅提升且探针资源显著减少。对于固定尺寸希尔伯特空间维度的未知酉电路效果尤佳：仅用32个输入/输出探针设置即可准确模拟线性光学中的4光子干涉，用256个探针设置即可模拟6光子干涉。同时适用于非酉量子信道。

Conclusion: 该方法使得仅依靠易获取的相干光源和阈值探测器即可实现多种应用，如小规模线性光学量子计算电路的表征，以及量子传感/计量器件的表征。

Abstract: Photonic qubits play an important role in quantum information processing. However, to date, efficient high-speed single photon sources and photon number resolving (PNR) detectors are still difficult to make. Here, we use phase-randomized coherent states and threshold detectors, combined with decoy-state post-processing, to simulate single photon sources and PNR detectors and perform various quantum information processing tasks. We propose a simple linear regression method to predict Fock state statistics based on observable statistics from coherent-state probes, combined with random/structured sampling of probe settings. Compared with traditional decoy-state post-processing based on linear programming and Cartesian product grid sampling, our method enables drastically improved accuracy and much reduced probe resources. It works particularly well for unknown unitary circuits of interest with fixed-size Hilbert space dimensions. For instance, it can accurately simulate 4-photon interference in linear optics with just 32 input/output probe settings and 6-photon interference with 256 probe settings. Moreover, it also works for non-unitary (such as lossy, nonlinear, or arbitrary) quantum channels. This enables a wide variety of applications that can be implemented with easily attainable coherent light sources and threshold detectors, such as characterization of small-scale linear optical quantum computing circuits or quantum sensing/metrology devices.

</details>


### [125] [Variational Quantum Attention for Molecular Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04588)
*Yu-Cheng Lin,Yu-Chao Hsu,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种边感知变分量子注意力机制（QGAT），将接收原子、邻接原子与化学键共同编码为量子注意力态，用于分子图学习；在五项分子性质与生物活性预测任务上与GATv2相当，并在BBBP上于六种线路拟设中均稳定提升，案例研究还显示其注意力归因行为与经典机制存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测是计算药物发现的核心，图神经网络通过消息传递学习邻域原子环境的权重，但变分量子线路如何改变分子图中的注意力行为尚不清楚。

Method: 提出边感知变分量子注意力机制，由接收原子、邻接原子和连接键共同决定量子注意力状态；在五个分子性质与生物活性预测任务上与匹配的经典注意力基线GATv2进行对比，并在Verubecestat BACE1抑制剂系列上开展归因分析，同时进行线路消融实验。

Result: QGAT性能与GATv2相当，在BBBP上于全部六种评估的线路拟设中均有一致提升；在BACE1系列中QGAT取得高于GATv2的Spearman相关，并对若干与报道SAR一致的结构变化给出正向归因；线路消融表明性能依赖线路设计。

Conclusion: 变分量子注意力可作为可行的分子注意力参数化替代方案，并表现出区别于匹配经典评分器的、依赖线路与化学体系的行为；但需更广泛验证以确定这些差异在不同化学系列与靶点间的普适性。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction is central to computational drug discovery, where graph neural networks learn to weight neighboring atomic environments during message passing. Yet it remains unclear how variational quantum circuits alter learned attention behavior in molecular graphs. We introduce an edge-aware variational quantum attention mechanism for molecular graph learning, in which the receiving atom, neighboring atom, and connecting bond jointly determine the quantum attention state. Across five molecular property and bioactivity prediction tasks, QGAT achieves competitive performance relative to GATv2, with a consistent improvement on BBBP across all six evaluated circuit ansatzes. We further compare how the quantum and classical attention scores weight molecular structure beyond accuracy. In the Verubecestat BACE1 inhibitor series, QGAT achieves a higher Spearman correlation than GATv2 and assigns positive attributions to several structural changes consistent with reported structure-activity relationships (SARs). This case study shows that the two attention mechanisms can exhibit different prediction and attribution behavior across structurally related BACE1 analogues, while broader validation is required to determine how consistently these differences generalize across chemical series and targets. Circuit ablations further show that performance depends on the circuit design. Together, these results show that variational quantum attention can serve as a viable alternative molecular attention parameterization while inducing circuit- and chemistry-dependent behavior distinct from a matched classical scorer.

</details>


### [126] [Efficient Quantum State Identity Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04597)
*Shih-Han Hung,Ming-Hsien Tsai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子态身份判定问题：给定 n 个未知纯量子态的副本，判断是否任意两态保真度至少 1-ε，或存在一对保真度至多 ε。该问题放宽了此前的"全同或正交"承诺。作者给出两种量子算法，分别在电路深度和样本复杂度上具互补保证，并证明匹配的样本复杂度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作多考虑"全同或正交"的强承诺，条件过于严格、难以刻画实际中"近似相同"的量子态情形。本文希望放宽该承诺，允许保真度处于 ε 与 1-ε 之间的中间区间，从而更一般地研究量子态身份判定所需的最优资源，特别是样本复杂度与电路深度之间的权衡。

Method: 对任意固定 ε∈(0,1/4)，提出两种算法。(1) 基于并行 SWAP 测试的常数深度算法，使用 O(log(n/δ)log(2/δ)) 份每个输入态副本，成功率至少 1-δ。(2) 利用 Schur 变换降低样本复杂度至 O(log(n/δ))，核心分析是将 Jucys–Murphy 元素与 Schur 采样相联系。同时通过下界论证证明任何失败概率不超过 δ≤1/2-c（c>0 常数）的量子算法至少需要 Ω(log(n/δ)) 份副本。

Result: 两种算法在固定 ε 下分别实现：常数电路深度、样本复杂度 O(log(n/δ)log(2/δ))；以及通过 Schur 采样达到 O(log(n/δ)) 的样本复杂度。下界为 Ω(log(n/δ))，因此第二种算法在 n 与 δ 的依赖上均达到最优。

Conclusion: 放宽全同或正交承诺后，量子态身份判定问题在固定 ε 下具有 Θ(log(n/δ)) 的最优样本复杂度；Schur 变换配合 Jucys–Murphy 元素分析是达到该最优界的关键，而并行 SWAP 测试则提供常数深度的互补方案。

Abstract: We study the following quantum state identity problem: given access to copies of $n$ unknown pure quantum states, the goal is to determine whether every pair has fidelity at least $1-\varepsilon$ or some pair has fidelity at most $\varepsilon$. This problem relaxes the identical-or-orthogonal promise considered in previous work. For any fixed overlap parameter $\varepsilon\in(0,1/4)$, we present two quantum algorithms with complementary guarantees on circuit depth and sample complexity. The first runs in constant depth using parallel swap tests and takes $O(\log(n/δ)\log(2/δ))$ copies of each input state to succeed with probability at least $1-δ$. The second uses the Schur transform to reduce the sample complexity to $O(\log(n/δ))$, through an analysis relating Jucys--Murphy elements to Schur sampling. We also prove a matching sample complexity lower bound of $Ω(\log(n/δ))$ for any quantum algorithm with failure probability at most $δ\le 1/2-c$, for any constant $c>0$, establishing that the second algorithm achieves the optimal dependence on both $n$ and $δ$ for fixed $\varepsilon$.

</details>


### [127] [Multi-Yoked Surface Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04613)
*Yuga Hirai,Yasunari Suzuki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出多级链式表面码（multi-yoked surface codes），通过多级级联与新型外码（chain codes）及可移动工作区的格点手术分配方案，在保持平面近邻连通性的同时显著提升编码密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面码是容错量子计算的主要候选方案，具备平面近邻连通性、高阈值与高效解码等优势，但其编码率低，在万亿次操作（teraquop）区域每个逻辑量子比特需要约一千个物理量子比特。已有的 yoked surface codes 仅通过单级级联将密度提升两到三倍，仍有进一步提升编码密度的需求。

Method: 1）将 yoking 思想推广到多级级联，提出 multi-yoked surface codes；2）提出名为 chain codes 的外码族，通过链接种子码的副本，使其能够检测下一级码的块错误，从而利用下级所有逻辑量子比特并在少数级联层内实现高编码率；3）设计补丁分配方案，使用可移动工作区通过格点手术执行 yoking 校验，并采用分层 yoking 间隔——底层校验频繁执行、高层校验较少执行，避免单个大距离外码所需的长 yoking 间隔。

Result: 作为一个基准，构造了采用 $[[256,166,8]]$ 外码的三级 yoked surface code，并在 SI1000 噪声模型下利用表面码解码器的软信息进行数值评估。结果显示逻辑错误率随内码距离指数抑制，在 teraquop 区域，三级 yoked surface code 比未 yoked 的表面码密度约高 4 到 4.5 倍。

Conclusion: 多级级联的链式表面码在保持平面近邻连通性和容错优势的同时，有效提升了编码密度，为构建更高效的容错量子计算架构提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The surface code is the leading candidate for fault-tolerant quantum computation owing to its planar nearest-neighbor connectivity, high threshold, and efficient decoding, but its low encoding rate demands on the order of a thousand physical qubits per logical qubit in the teraquop regime. Yoked surface codes improve the density by a factor of two or three by concatenating a high-density parity check code on top of the surface code while retaining planar nearest-neighbor connectivity. In this work, we generalize this idea to multiple levels of concatenation and introduce multi-yoked surface codes. Our construction rests on two new ingredients. First, we propose a family of outer codes called chain codes, built by chaining copies of a seed code that detects the block errors of the code one level below; the resulting codes use all logical qubits of the lower level and achieve a high encoding rate within a few concatenation levels. Second, we design a patch allocation in which movable workspaces perform the yoking checks by lattice surgery with a hierarchy of yoking intervals, so that lower-level checks are performed frequently while higher-level checks are performed less often, which avoids the long yoking intervals that a single large-distance outer code would require. As a benchmark, we construct a three-yoked surface code with a $[[256,166,8]]$ outer code and evaluate it numerically under the SI1000 noise model using soft information from the surface code decoder. The logical error rate is suppressed exponentially in the inner code distance, and in the teraquop regime the three-yoked surface code is about 4 to 4.5$\times$ denser than the unyoked surface code.

</details>


### [128] [Exact Clifford Optimality for Two-Sided Locally Randomized Classical Shadows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04621)
*Xiaotian Nie,Tao Zhang,Yadong Wu,Linghui Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于经典阴影（classical shadows）架构下测量多体关联的统计成本，证明由两个集体泡利旋转构成的电路 $U_*$ 在给定测量架构中达到最小平方阴影范数，其渐近最优值为 $(4/3)\cdot(9/5)^k$，将 Wu 等人的指前因子从 $2$ 改进为 $4/3$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠测量能够降低学习多体关联的统计成本。作者希望在特定测量架构下寻找能最小化阴影范数（即统计成本）的最优可控幺正电路，从而在保持指数基最优的前提下进一步优化指前因子。

Method: 针对指定 $k$ 量子比特区域上的全支撑泡利串，采用经典阴影架构：在作用于该区域的可控幺正两侧各加一层独立、均匀随机的乘积单量子比特 Clifford 层，随后进行计算基读出。作者在该架构下解析求解平方阴影范数的最小值，并给出解析闭式解与有限尺寸有理数证书。

Result: 证明两集体泡利旋转电路 $U_*$ 对所有 $k\geq1$ 在所有 Clifford 可控幺正选择中达到精确最小平方阴影范数：奇数 $k$ 时为 $C_k^{\rm opt}=4\cdot 9^k/(3\cdot 5^k+3^k-6)$，偶数 $k$ 时为 $C_k^{\rm opt}=4\cdot 9^k/(3\cdot 5^k-3^k+2)$；渐近最优为 $(4/3)\cdot(9/5)^k$，确立 Wu 等人的指数基最优并将指前因子由 $2$ 改进为 $4/3$。对 $1\leq k\leq6$，精确有理数证书表明同一最优值在整个幺正群 $\mathrm U(2^k)$（含所有非 Clifford 幺正）上成立。

Conclusion: 有限尺寸结果支持如下猜想：在同一测量架构内，$U_*$ 对任意 $k$ 在所有幺正变换中均最优。该工作刻画了该架构下的最优阴影范数，并改进了已有指前因子。

Abstract: Entangling measurements can reduce the statistical cost of learning many-body correlations. We study Pauli strings with full support on a specified $k$-qubit region using classical shadows with two independent, uniformly random product single-qubit Clifford layers around a controllable unitary acting on that region, followed by computational-basis readout. In this architecture, we prove that a circuit $U_*$ of two collective Pauli rotations attains the exact minimum squared shadow norm over all Clifford choices of the controllable unitary for every $k\geq1$, namely $C_k^{\rm opt}=4\cdot 9^k/(3\cdot 5^k+3^k-6)$ for odd $k$ and $C_k^{\rm opt}=4\cdot 9^k/(3\cdot 5^k-3^k+2)$ for even $k$. The asymptotic optimum $(4/3)\cdot(9/5)^k$ establishes Wu et al.'s exponential base as optimal and improves their prefactor from $2$ to $4/3$. For $1\leq k\leq6$, exact rational certificates prove that the same optimum holds over the entire unitary group $\mathrm U(2^k)$, including all non-Clifford unitaries, within the same measurement architecture. These finite-size results motivate the conjecture that $U_*$ is optimal over all unitaries for arbitrary $k$ within this architecture.

</details>


### [129] [Bounding Two-Way Average Communication Cost of Simulating Quantum Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04648)
*Kai-Siang Chen,Gelo Noel M. Tabia,Bo-An Tsai,Swati Kumari,Yeong-Cherng Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从非局域博弈导出输入平均通信代价的下界，适用于变长、全交互双向协议；对并行博弈给出显式有限量子关联，其精确模拟代价超过任意给定有限预算，并对并行Magic-Square关联证明n log2(3/2)比特下界及渐近紧的单向协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝尔非局域关联无法用无通信的局域隐变量模型复现，经典通信代价成为非局域性的自然量化指标。尽管固定有限贝尔场景中有限通信总足以模拟任意关联，但确定精确模拟给定非局域关联所需的最小通信量仍具挑战性，尤其在平均代价设定下。

Method: 从非局域博弈推导输入平均通信代价下界，允许变长、完全交互的双向协议；将下界应用于并行博弈以获得显式有限量子关联；对并行Magic-Square关联构造达到渐近最优速率的一向通信协议；提出由确定性关联构造的下界层级及对应上界。

Result: 并行博弈下界给出精确模拟代价超过任意预设有限输入平均通信预算的显式有限量子关联；n个并行Magic-Square获胜关联的精确模拟输入平均代价下界为n log2(3/2)比特，并由一向协议渐近达到；n份CHSH博弈最大获胜量子关联的精确模拟输入平均下界约为0.04627n比特。

Conclusion: 所提下界层级与基于选定通信代价内确定性关联的互补上界在最终层恢复精确输入平均代价；若在较低层两者一致，其公共值即为精确输入平均代价，无需枚举所有通信策略。

Abstract: Bell nonlocal correlations cannot be reproduced by local hidden-variable models without communication, making classical communication cost a natural quantitative measure of nonlocality. Although finite communication always suffices to simulate any correlation in a fixed finite Bell scenario, determining the minimum amount needed to (exactly) simulate any given nonlocal correlation remains challenging, especially in the average-cost setting. Here, we derive lower bounds on input-average communication cost from nonlocal games, allowing variable-length, fully interactive two-way protocols. When applied to parallel games, these bounds yield explicit finite quantum correlations for which the exact simulation cost exceeds any prescribed finite input-average communication budget. For $n$ parallel Magic-Square-winning correlations, we prove a lower bound of $n\log_2(3/2)$ bits and give a one-way communication protocol that attains this rate asymptotically. For the quantum correlation maximally winning the $n$-copy of the Clauser--Horne--Shimony--Holt nonlocal game, we obtain an input-average lower bound of approximately $0.04627n$ bits for exact simulation. Finally, we formulate a hierarchy of lower bounds and complementary upper bounds using deterministic correlations up to chosen communication costs. Both recover the exact input-average cost at their final levels, and if the bounds agree at lower levels, their common value yields the exact input-average cost without needing to consider all communication strategies.

</details>


### [130] [Correlations decide a shallow-circuit advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04666)
*Zijian Gong,Zhaobin Lyu,Jingjing Hu,Dengfeng Li,Shuoming An*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅用经典样本即可认证"量子计算优势"的高效检验方法：检验所读取的内容由经典电路的比特与比特对关联模式决定，而非某种距离度量。核心是一条不等式，其第二项由Lean 4机器验证的塌缩定理约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机声称能产生经典计算机无法复现的样本，最尖锐的版本是：给定每次运行一个纠缠态的常数深度量子电路，对比浅层少输入门、有限随机性的经典电路。然而此前没有任何高效检验能从纯经典样本出发认证这一断言，因此需要建立这样的检验理论。

Method: 基于一条不等式构造检验：任何产生样本的机器与目标的距离不超过其误标比例加上"串的另一半"（标签之外的比特串）偏离均匀分布的程度。利用Lean 4形式化验证的塌缩定理逐次约束第二项（逐次固定采样器随机输入比特的一个设置）。检验四项自然检查均被证明失效；第五项检查读取同样的关联，针对有限输入门类精确有效。标签检验被证明在其容差内样本最优（差一个对数因子）；第五项检查在"有界钉住剩余类"上不仅有效而且可靠。

Result: 四项自然检查可证明地失败；第五项检查能捕捉它们无法企及的远离目标构造，并在有限输入门类上精确。标签检验在其容差内样本最优（差对数因子）。实验的瓶颈是保真度接近0.99的43比特纠缠资源态，而非读出。

Conclusion: 认证量子优势的检验取决于经典类的单比特与比特对关联模式。留下两个具名开放问题；若第一个有肯定答案，则可将保证扩展到完整类别。

Abstract: Quantum computers are claimed to produce samples ordinary classical computers cannot reproduce. The sharpest such claim known pits constant-depth quantum circuits, given one entangled state per run, against shallow classical circuits of few-input gates and limited randomness. No efficient test was known that certifies such a claim from classical samples alone. Here we show that what such a test must read is decided by the classical class's pattern of single-bit and pair correlations, not by a distance. The test rests on one inequality: any machine producing the samples sits no further from the target than the fraction it mislabels plus the deviation of its string half, the bits beyond the label, from uniform. A collapse theorem, machine-checked in the Lean~4 proof assistant, confines that second term, one setting of the sampler's random input bits at a time. Four natural checks provably fail; a fifth, reading those same correlations, catches a far-from-target construction they cannot reach, and is exact on the class of few-input gates. The label test is proven and sample-optimal in its own tolerance up to a logarithmic factor; the fifth check is sound, not only effective, on the bounded pinned-residue class, pairwise-uniform samplers whose residue, the string's weight modulo the prime, is pinned by few seeds and whose seed sharing is bounded. What binds an experiment is the $43$-qubit entangled resource state at fidelity near $0.99$, not the readout. What remains are two named open problems; a positive answer to the first would extend the guarantee to the full class.

</details>


### [131] [Quantum-State Oblivious Transfer over Quantum Erasure Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04667)
*Remi A. Chou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子擦除信道上的量子态不经意传输（QOT），在信息论安全框架下给出有限分组长度的逆定理与可达性界，并在诚实但好奇设定下使两者匹配；恶意设定下构造结合交互式哈希与接收检测测试，性能达到相同容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态不经意传输（QOT）是量子密码学中的核心原语，但此前缺乏在量子擦除信道下、无初始纠缠且仅使用认证经典通信时，信息论安全的有限分组长度刻画与容量结果。本文旨在填补这一空白，并统一处理诚实但好奇与恶意两类敌手模型。

Method: 模型设定：Alice持有两个量子系统，Bob希望恢复其中一个；双方通过独立信道使用与认证公开经典通信交互，无预共享纠缠。针对擦除信道，分别分析诚实但好奇与恶意行为。方法上：推导有限分组长度的逆定理（converse）与可达性（achievability）界；恶意设定构造将交互式哈希（interactive hashing）与对Bob是否收到所选信道输出的测试相结合，并把每个输入分布到多个量子比特上，使擦除能对整个输入（即使输入与外部参考系纠缠）实现隐藏；进一步推导固定误差下的渐近展开。

Result: 在诚实但好奇设定下，有限分组长度逆定理界与可达性界相匹配；恶意设定下由上述构造给出可达性界，并证明在固定误差渐近展开下，恶意与诚实但好奇两种设定具有相同的容量。

Conclusion: 量子擦除信道上的量子态不经意传输可在无初始纠缠条件下实现信息论安全；诚实但好奇与恶意两种安全模型具有相同容量，表明恶意攻击不降低该任务的渐近性能。

Abstract: We study quantum-state oblivious transfer over the quantum erasure channel under information-theoretic security guarantees. Specifically, Alice holds two quantum systems, Bob wishes to recover one of them, and the parties have access to independent channel uses and authenticated public classical communication, with no initial shared entanglement. We consider both honest-but-curious and malicious behavior. We establish finite-blocklength converse and achievability bounds, which match in the honest-but-curious setting. The malicious setting construction combines interactive hashing with tests of whether Bob received selected channel outputs, and distributes each input across several qubits so that erasures conceal an entire input from a dishonest receiver, even when the inputs are entangled with an external reference. We also derive fixed-error asymptotic expansions and show that both malicious and honest-but-curious settings have the same capacity.

</details>


### [132] [Stabilizer codes over general phase spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04694)
*Sayan Chakraborty,Victor V. Albert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一类通用稳定子码理论，其稳定子群由互易的qudit Pauli、谐振子位移和平面转子位移构成，稳定子群在量子相空间中形成广义格以保证有限逻辑维度；通过格论（辛对偶格）工具系统构造了不可分解的振荡子-转子、转子-qudit、振荡子-转子-qubit码以及部分提升Golay码得到的振荡子-qubit码，并给出逻辑算符、Clifford-Gaussian门、码维度、错误子空间向量丛、距离、最优解码与量子权重枚举量及MacWilliams恒等式，证明有限能量码字的编码在误差随阻尼参数倒数指数小时为等距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将稳定子码从离散Pauli/qudit、GKP连续变量码统一到qudit、谐振子（oscillator）与平面转子（planar rotor）混合的量子相空间框架，并借助Rieffel的非交换几何结果，构造无法被广义Clifford变换分解为独立子系统的混合连续-离散稳定子码，同时继承格论中距离、解码与权重枚举量的成熟工具。

Method: 以量子相空间中的广义格（辛格）作为稳定子群，混合qudit Pauli、振荡子位移与转子位移；从辛对偶格解析推导逻辑算符，从格对称性导出Clifford-Gaussian门；用稳定子格的余体积（covolume）确定码维度，将伴随子空间组织成向量丛；将方法解释为“稳定子码与GKP码的级联（concatenation）+格论结果+解级联（unconcatenation）”；引入由Golay码坐标一半上应用Construction A得到部分提升Golay码所对应的振荡子-qubit码；用阻尼参数刻画有限能量码字。

Result: 构造出振荡子-转子码、转子-qudit码、振荡子-转子-qubit码等不能被广义Clifford变换分解的码，以及由部分提升Golay码得到的振荡子-qubit码；给出逻辑算符的解析形式、Clifford-Gaussian门的格对称来源、码维度等于稳定子格余体积、伴随子空间的向量丛结构；建立距离、最优解码与量子权重枚举量，并统一恢复Pauli、qudit与GKP版本及已知MacWilliams恒等式；构造的有限能量码字使编码为等距，误差随阻尼参数倒数指数小。

Conclusion: 该理论为离散与连续变量量子纠错码提供了统一的格论/非交换几何框架，并证明混合振荡子-转子-qudit稳定子码可实现有限维度、不可分解且可有效解码，同时以有限能量形式逼近理想编码等距。

Abstract: We develop a theory of stabilizer codes whose stabilizer groups consist of commuting qudit Paulis, oscillator displacements, and/or planar-rotor displacements. We consider codes whose stabilizer groups form generalized lattices in quantum phase space, a condition that guarantees a finite logical dimension. We construct oscillator-rotor, rotor-qudit, and oscillator-rotor-qubit codes that cannot be decomposed into separate subsystems by generalized Clifford transformations. We also introduce an oscillator-qubit code arising from a partially lifted Golay code, where Construction A is applied to half of the Golay code coordinates. We derive analytical forms of logical operators from the symplectic dual lattice, determine Clifford-Gaussian gates from lattice symmetries, show that the code dimension equals the covolume of the stabilizer lattice, and organize the syndrome subspaces into a vector bundle. Our technique builds on earlier non-commutative geometric results by Rieffel, but it can often be interpreted simply as concatenating a given stabilizer code with a Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) code, applying conventional lattice-theoretic results, and unconcatenating. We formulate distances, optimal decoding, and quantum weight enumerators, recovering the Pauli, qudit, and GKP versions together with known MacWilliams identities. We construct finite-energy codewords whose encoding is an isometry up to an error exponentially small in the inverse damping parameter.

</details>


### [133] [Sum-of-squares compilation of matrix-model dynamics for NISQ and fault-tolerant hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04731)
*Ayanendu Dutta,Nabanita Sarkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作针对BFSS/BMN矩阵模型势$V=-\frac{g^2}{4}\sum_{I,J}Tr[X_I,X_J]^2$，通过Walsh谱下界证明现有相位多项式Trotter步在$\{CNOT,CZ,CPhase,R_{ZZ},SWAP,X,R_z\}$门类中接近最优，并利用平方对易子项的精确分解构造出NISQ（Hadamard，$\Theta(d^2N^3Q^3)$双量子比特门）与容错（$T$，$\Theta(d^2N^3Q^2)$ T门）两类新电路，显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SU(N)矩阵模型势（BFSS/BMN家族）的热大N态对偶于黑洞，其模拟至关重要；现有相位多项式编译成本约$O(d^2N^4Q^4)$每Trotter步，且在该门类中已接近最优。作者希望突破此极限，找到更高效的量子电路实现。

Method: 1) 证明对一般Trotter步，Walsh支撑$m_{\min}$同时下界旋转数与双量子比特数（覆盖所有含辅助比特的电路）；2) 构造达到$1.01$--$1.10\,m_{\min}$的奇偶网络；3) 利用$V$分解为平方对易子项之和：NISQ方案将每个平方项加入Fourier寄存器并在其中平方（Hadamard）；容错方案采用可逆算术，每个平方项注入一次相位梯度（$T$门），无任意角旋转。

Result: NISQ电路在原生$R_{ZZ}$下，从SU(2)的$Q=6$、SU(5)的$Q=2$起，双量子比特门数少于所有奇偶网络；T门数与面码时空分别最高优于精度匹配的Hamming-weight-phasing基线$11.9\times$和$25.5\times$（除最小点外所有构建点）。无分解的消融实验成本最高多$21\times$，并在17点中15点败于基线，表明优势源自分解而非算术。两种构造均适应含费米子的mini-BMN超对称形变，态矢模拟器上完整Trotter演化复现精确乘积公式至$2\times10^{-12}$。

Conclusion: 现有Trotter步在特定门类中已达近最优，改进必须引入该类外的新门。精确对易子平方分解提供了突破路径，所构建的NISQ与容错电路均在门数上严格优于基线，且优势来源于分解本身；方法可扩展至含费米子的超对称形变，并经数值验证达到极高精度。

Abstract: The SU$(N)$ matrix-model potential $V=-\tfrac{g^2}{4}\sum_{I,J}Tr[X_I,X_J]^2$, the interaction of the BFSS/BMN family, whose thermal large-$N$ states are dual to black holes, is diagonal in the coordinate basis and is compiled in the literature as a phase polynomial with $O(d^2N^4Q^4)$ rotations per Trotter step. That cost is close to optimal for its gate class: we show that for a generic Trotter step the Walsh support $m_{\min}$ lower-bounds both the rotation count and the two-qubit count of every circuit over $\{CNOT,CZ,CPhase,R_{ZZ},SWAP,X,R_z\}$, ancillas included, and we build a parity network attaining $1.01$--$1.10\,m_{\min}$. Improvement therefore requires a gate outside that class, and the exact factorisation of $V$ into a sum of squared commutator entries supplies one in each era. For NISQ, Hadamard: each squared entry is added into a Fourier register and squared there, $Θ(d^2N^3Q^3)$ two-qubit gates. For fault tolerance, $T$: reversible arithmetic with one phase-gradient injection per squared entry, $Θ(d^2N^3Q^2)$ T gates and no arbitrary-angle rotation. Both are built and verified. With a native $R_{ZZ}$ the NISQ circuit uses fewer two-qubit gates than every parity network from $Q=6$ at SU(2) and $Q=2$ at SU(5); the oracle beats a precision-matched Hamming-weight-phasing incumbent at every point built except the smallest, by up to $11.9\times$ in T count and, at equal width, $25.5\times$ in surface-code spacetime. An ablation with the same arithmetic but no factorisation costs up to $21\times$ more and loses to that incumbent at $15$ of $17$ points, so the factorisation, not the arithmetic, produces the win. Both constructions survive the supersymmetric deformation to mini-BMN with fermions, and complete Trotter evolutions on a statevector simulator reproduce the exact product formula to $2\times10^{-12}$.

</details>


### [134] [Topology-independent quantum advantage in communication networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04734)
*Ankush Pandit,V. N. S. Meghanath Ashtakala,Leon George Padayatty,Debashis Saha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"拓扑无关量子优势"，即量子协议在相同通信约束下、对任意经典资源分配与通信拓扑均优于所有经典协议，并通过三方等值任务、三/四方经典关联多胞体刻画及若干面不等式予以证明与应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有量子通信优势通常建立在固定通信网络之上，结论依赖具体拓扑与资源分配。作者希望确立一种不依赖网络配置、更稳健的量子优势形式，从而在未知或可变网络结构中依然成立。

Method: 首先构造三方等值任务（两个发送方持三值输入，无输入接收方判断是否相等）作为示例；随后建立拓扑无关通信的一般框架，完整刻画最小三方情形下的经典关联多胞体并求其面不等式；最后将框架推广至四方网络，刻画相应经典通信结构并寻找任务。

Result: 三方等值任务展示了拓扑无关量子优势；三方经典关联多胞体的面不等式给出多个此类优势实例，其中一个可实现非酉量子信道的半器件无关认证，另一个可在无网络配置先验假设下认证顺序量子网络拓扑；四方网络中亦识别出对所有容许经典拓扑均有量子优势的通信任务。

Conclusion: 拓扑无关性是一种真实且稳健的量子通信优势形式，为网络中的量子优势提供了与拓扑和资源分配无关的判定与认证工具。

Abstract: Quantum communication can outperform classical communication in a variety of information-processing tasks, but such advantages are typically established for a fixed communication network. Here we introduce topology-independent quantum advantage, in which a quantum protocol outperforms every classical protocol subject to the same communication constraint, irrespective of how the classical communication resource is distributed among the parties or which communication topology is employed, even when the parties may share unlimited classical randomness. We first demonstrate this phenomenon through a simple three-party equality task: two senders receive ternary classical inputs, while an input-free receiver determines whether the inputs are equal. We then formulate a general framework for topology-independent communication and completely characterize the corresponding classical correlation polytope in the minimal three-party scenario. Its facet inequalities reveal several instances of topology-independent quantum advantage. Remarkably, one of these inequalities enables the semi-device-independent certification of a nonunitary quantum channel, while another certifies a sequential quantum network topology without any prior assumption about the network configuration. Finally, we extend the framework to four-party networks, characterize the relevant classical communication structures, and identify a communication task exhibiting quantum advantages against all admissible classical topologies. Our results establish topology independence as a genuine and robust form of quantum advantage in communication networks.

</details>


### [135] [Record consistency under repeated readout and apparatus composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04788)
*D. M. Theshan N. Weerasinghe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究"边际无信号"不足以保证装置在重复读出、网络复合与复用下保持联合记录律，提出条件记录判据并用四类模型刻画其边界，结合有限资源障碍与数值模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立结果概率上的无信号约束无法判定装置在重复读出、网络复合或复用场景下是否保持其可及记录的联合分布律，因此需要识别上述扩展所必需附加的条件。

Method: 采用条件记录判据，构造并分析四个显式模型：包裹柯西回溯源的连续/非连续读出、有限角带宽泄漏界、两源贝尔结点上的修正矩阵非负秩、高斯控制器与共享经典噪声固定单态；并证明有限资源障碍、构造正有限控制器近似。

Result: 两次条件独立连续读出迫使角度无关响应，三次读出在无连续性时亦然；有限角带宽给出定量泄漏界而仅连续性无一致稳定性；修正矩阵在每个偶均匀角网格上满非负秩；高斯控制器可抵消分支质量依赖但留下可区分联合记录并给出精确噪声与律变折衷；共享经典噪声可复现变宽孤立结果族但保留与刷新控制器产生不同时间统计；获得有限资源障碍及正有限控制器近似，8.3百万次独立模拟支持模型特定预测。

Conclusion: 将装置扩展到更丰富记录与干预时，边际无信号必须伴随额外条件（如连续性、有限带宽、资源有限性）才能保证联合记录律的保持，这些条件界定了无信号论证的适用范围。

Abstract: No-signalling constraints on isolated outcome probabilities do not determine whether an apparatus preserves the joint law of its accessible records under repeated readout, network composition, or reuse. We study these extensions through a conditional-record criterion and four explicit models. For a wrapped-Cauchy retrocausal source, protection under two conditionally independent continuous readouts forces an angle-independent response; three readouts suffice without continuity. A finite angular-bandwidth assumption gives a quantitative leakage bound, whereas continuity alone gives no uniform stability. At a specified two-source Bell junction, the required correction matrix has full nonnegative rank on every even uniform angular grid. A Gaussian controller can cancel branch-mass dependence while leaving a distinguishable joint record, for which we derive exact noise and law-change tradeoffs. Finally, a fixed singlet with shared classical noise reproduces the variable-width isolated outcome family, but retained and refreshed controllers generate different temporal statistics. We prove a finite-resource obstruction and construct positive finite-controller approximations. Numerical studies comprising 8.3 million independent simulated histories support the model-specific predictions. Together, the results identify conditions that must accompany marginal no-signalling when extending an apparatus to richer records and interventions.

</details>


### [136] [Competing pathways in polaritonic chemistry and general harmonic environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04817)
*Jonas Vinther,Leonardo A. Cunha,Johannes Flick*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立极化子化学的量子动力学框架，用张量网络HEOM方法计算振动态间概率流，揭示振动强耦合如何通过改变各跃迁速率、重塑动力学瓶颈并重新分配反应通量来调控竞争反应路径的支化比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验表明分子与腔模的强光-物质耦合能显著改变化学反应速率与产率，但已有多种机制假说，仍缺乏对"振动强耦合如何改变反应动力学"的清晰微观理解，尤其缺乏对多步竞争反应网络中各跃迁如何被腔调制并传播的定量工具。

Method: 采用普适的量子动力学框架分析极化子化学中的竞争反应路径，考虑含多个相关跃迁频率的反应坐标与一般谐振环境；扩展基于张量网络的层级运动方程（HEOM）方法以计算振动态间的概率流，使用简洁的有效谱密度表述并将马尔可夫浴模受控压缩为系统级跳跃算符；据此对态到态概率流进行分解，追踪腔诱导的单步跃迁变化如何通过多步反应网络传播；并处理反应坐标、旁观模与腔环境的耦合情形。

Result: 局域跃迁修正可改变动力学瓶颈，并在竞争路径间选择性重新分配反应通量；在不可逆条件下改变支化比，而在可逆区域对平衡产率影响不大。对反应坐标、旁观模与腔环境耦合的情形，出现由杂化衰减通道间干涉导致的结构化、非对称速率谱。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了理解振动强耦合下路径选择性化学反应性的机制性框架，说明腔效应主要通过逐跃迁改性并经由反应网络的通量再分配发挥作用，而非简单改变整体产率。

Abstract: Recent experiments suggest that strong light-matter coupling between molecules and cavity modes can significantly modify chemical reaction rates and yields. Despite multiple proposed mechanisms, a clear microscopic understanding of how vibrational strong coupling alters reaction kinetics remains incomplete. Here, we employ a general quantum-dynamical framework for analyzing competing reaction pathways in polaritonic chemistry by considering reaction coordinates with multiple relevant transition frequencies and general harmonic environments. We extend on a tensor-network formulation of the hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) method to compute probability currents between vibrational states, leveraging a concise formulation for effective spectral densities and a controlled compression of Markovian bath modes into system-level jump operators. This allows us to decompose the resulting state-to-state probability currents, revealing how cavity-induced changes in individual transitions propagate through multistep reaction networks. We find that these local modifications can alter kinetic bottlenecks and selectively redistribute reaction flux between competing pathways, changing branching ratios under irreversible conditions without substantially affecting the equilibrium yield in the reversible regime. Finally, for coupled reaction-coordinate, spectator-mode, and cavity environments, we find structured, asymmetric rate profiles arising from interference between hybridized decay channels. Together, these results provide a mechanistic framework for understanding pathway-specific chemical reactivity under vibrational strong coupling.

</details>


### [137] [Sidewall spacer passivated epitaxial NbN/AlN/NbN Josephson junctions for superconducting qubit applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04820)
*Koki Honda,Daiki Kurihara,Hiroki Kutsuma,Hirotaka Terai,Taro Yamashita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种侧壁间隔层钝化的外延NbN/AlN/NbN约瑟夫森结制备工艺，减少非晶介质体积与工艺复杂度，在0.3–10⁴ A/cm²临界电流密度范围内获得高质量结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Al/AlOₓ/Al结的集成需求限制了性能；外延NbN/AlN/NbN结因晶态隧道势垒和NbN高转变温度而具吸引力，但其集成通常依赖含非晶层间介质的复杂工艺，会损害量子比特相干时间。因此需降低非晶介质体积并简化工艺。

Method: 采用侧壁间隔层钝化的外延NbN/AlN/NbN结制备工艺；通过改变AlN隧道势垒厚度调控临界电流密度；利用缓冲氢氟酸处理对比处理前后的电流-电压特性以评估湿法刻蚀影响。

Result: 所制结具有约5.6 mV的大能隙电压和低的亚能隙漏电流；在0.3至10⁴ A/cm²的广泛临界电流密度范围内均可制备高质量结；缓冲氢氟酸处理前后I-V特性对比表明湿法刻蚀未降低结的电学特性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了外延约瑟夫森结的可扩展制备工艺，为高相干氮化物超导量子比特提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: Epitaxial NbN/AlN/NbN Josephson junctions are promising alternatives to conventional Al/AlO$_x$/Al junctions for superconducting qubits due to their crystalline tunnel barriers and the high transition temperature of NbN. However, their integration into quantum circuits typically requires complex fabrication processes involving amorphous interlayer dielectrics, which can degrade qubit coherence times. To address these issues, we developed sidewall spacer passivated epitaxial NbN/AlN/NbN junctions using a fabrication process that reduces both the volume of amorphous dielectric material and fabrication complexity. The fabricated junctions exhibited a large gap voltage of approximately 5.6 mV and low subgap leakage currents. By varying the thickness of the AlN tunnel barrier, high-quality junctions were fabricated over a wide range of critical current densities ${\it J}_c$, from 0.3 to 10$^4$ A/cm$^2$. In addition, the comparison of the current-voltage characteristics before and after buffered hydrofluoric acid treatment indicated that the wet etching process did not degrade the electrical characteristics of the junctions. These results establish a scalable fabrication process for epitaxial Josephson junctions and provide a promising platform for high-coherence nitride-based superconducting qubits.

</details>


### [138] [Epitaxial NbN-junction-based self-shunted superconducting flux qubit with high anharmonicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04821)
*Tokunoshin Uchida,Shingo Nozaki,Daiki Kurihara,Hiroki Kutsuma,Shotaro Shirai,Atsushi Noguchi,Hirotaka Terai,Taro Yamashita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种基于全外延生长的NbN/AlN/NbN约瑟夫森结的自分流超导磁通量子比特（SSFQ），实现了797 MHz的高非谐性与13–25 μs的长寿命，同时显著缩小器件尺寸，为可扩展超导量子比特提供了新材料与器件平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电容分流（C-shunt）磁通量子比特需要大面积分流电容，占用较大面积，不利于规模化集成；因此需要一种超紧凑、强非谐且长寿命的量子比特方案。

Method: 采用全外延生长的NbN/AlN/NbN约瑟夫森结制备自分流超导磁通量子比特（SSFQ），利用其自身结电容实现分流，无需额外的并联大电容。

Result: NbN结SSFQ获得797 MHz的高非谐性，器件面积相较含大分流电容的C-shunt磁通量子比特大幅减小；寿命达13–25 μs，与NbN基C-shunt磁通量子比特及Al基磁通量子比特（结面积小10倍以上）相当或更优。

Conclusion: 结果表明，大面积外延NbN基约瑟夫森结既可用于SSFQ，也可用于合并元件transmon，为可扩展超导量子比特及大规模超导量子计算机提供了新的器件选择与材料平台。

Abstract: We report a demonstration of a self-shunted superconducting flux qubit (SSFQ) as an ultracompact, strongly anharmonic, and long-lifetime qubit by using the full epitaxially grown NbN/AlN/NbN Josephson junctions. The NbN-junction-based SSFQ achieved a high anharmonicity of 797 MHz as well as drastically reduced footprint of the qubit compared to capacitively shunted (C-shunt) flux qubits with a large shunt capacitor. The lifetime of the SSFQ reached 13 - 25 $μ$s, which is comparable to or relatively higher than that of NbN-based C-shunt flux qubits and Al-based flux qubits with more than 10 times smaller junctions. This indicates that the large epitaxial NbN-based junction can be adopted for SSFQ as well as merged-element transmon with the relatively large junctions. The results presented in this work will offer a new choice of the scalable qubits as well as the material platform toward the realization of large-scale superconducting quantum computers.

</details>


### [139] [Entanglement-of-formation potentials for feedback-assisted classical communication and randomness distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04831)
*Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立基于纠缠生成的势函数框架，为反馈辅助经典通信与单向随机性蒸馏给出更紧的容量上界，并揭示双向蒸馏可严格超越单向极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为反馈辅助经典通信和单向共享随机性蒸馏寻找统一且更紧的容量上界，并厘清单向与双向随机性蒸馏的本质差异。

Method: 为无初始共享纠缠的有限维量子信道构造纠缠生成势；证明精确的摊销（amortization）定理，界定通信实验室间消息信息与剩余纠缠之和的增量；由此导出基于混合凸包（mixed-convex-roof）表达式的经典反馈容量上界，并给出对应态的有限分组长度上界。

Result: 确定了无噪声信道与纠缠破坏信道的 flagged mixture 容量；改进了已发表的去极化信道逆定理；对振幅阻尼信道精确求值；振幅阻尼 Choi 态给出精确基准，满秩广义振幅阻尼 Choi 态展示出有效改进；并用两轮弱测量协议证明双向随机性蒸馏严格超过所有非平凡 qubit isotropic 态的单向精确速率。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了通信与随机性界共享的纠缠论方法；同时表明单向态势不能作为一般的双向逆定理，保留的量子关联使双向蒸馏能够超越单向极限。

Abstract: We develop entanglement-of-formation potentials for feedback-assisted classical communication and one-way randomness distillation. For a finite-dimensional quantum channel without initial shared entanglement, an exact amortization theorem bounds the increase of message information plus entanglement remaining across the communicating laboratories. This proves a classical-feedback capacity bound using a mixed-convex-roof expression previously studied by Winter and Yang in the context of potential capacities. We determine the capacity of flagged mixtures of noiseless and entanglement-breaking channels, improve a published depolarizing-channel converse, and evaluate the new bound exactly for the amplitude damping channel. A state analogue gives a finite-blocklength upper bound on the net shared randomness obtainable with one-way classical communication. Amplitude-damping Choi states provide exact benchmarks, while a full-rank generalized amplitude-damping Choi state demonstrates a useful improvement where we do not evaluate the operational rate exactly. We also distinguish one-way from two-way randomness distillation: a two-round weak-measurement protocol strictly exceeds the exact one-way rate of every nontrivial qubit isotropic state. The protocol preserves quantum side information during its first decoding and extracts additional randomness in the reverse direction. This shows why the one-way state potential is not a general two-way converse. Together, these results establish a common entanglement-based approach to sharper communication and randomness bounds, while revealing how retained quantum correlations enable two-way distillation to surpass one-way limits.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum codes in the Lee metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04834)
*Jinkang Guo,Yiqiu Han,Aranya Chakraborty,Shubham P. Jain,Tianhao Liu,Victor V. Albert,Andrew Lucas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出离散小位移噪声的量子编码框架，将量子比特错误建模为低权X/Z型Pauli位移并用Lee度量刻画，发展了Z_q上稳定子码的Lee度量编码理论，分析了Lee权扩散、Z_4码量子化及Lee-LDPC码距离上界，并讨论了螺旋重复码的自校正性质及其超图积构造的二维量子码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量系统中的小相空间位移噪声具有低权结构，而核自旋噪声近似满足这一结构并可由Lee度量捕捉。经典Lee度量编码理论已较成熟，但缺乏对应的量子扩展，且传统Hamming度量下的编码在物理量子比特资源利用上可能不够高效，因此需要建立Lee度量下的量子编码形式化框架。

Method: 1. 对Z_q上的稳定子码建立量子Lee度量编码理论，分别使用X、Z分量的联合与分离Lee度量，其中量子比特的联合度量将Y错误计为双重；2. 研究Clifford门下的Lee权扩散，并通过Gray映射将Z_4上的码量子化；3. 对量子CSS Lee-LDPC码证明Lee距离上界；4. 在局部Metropolis动力学下分析经典q元螺旋重复码的存储时间，并通过超图积构造局部二维量子码。

Result: 对量子比特，联合Lee度量可设计出比传统Hamming度量码用更少物理量子比特即可检测和纠正X、Z错误的码；通过Gray映射量子化Z_4码，得到具有二重横向非量子Clifford门的码；证明n个量子dit上的量子CSS Lee-LDPC码的Lee距离不超过O(n)且与q无关，表明大q量子dit的内部Hilbert空间无法用于构造高Lee距离码；在局部Metropolis动力学下，某些经典q元螺旋重复码在固定温度、q随系统长度增大时具有指数长的存储时间，可理解为有限温度下的自发对称破缺，即使在一维局部模型中亦然；其超图积给出继承Z错误自校正的局部二维量子码，但X错误自校正仍为开放问题。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典Lee度量编码理论拓展到量子稳定子码框架，揭示了Lee度量下量子码在资源效率和横向门方面的优势，同时给出大q量子dit高Lee距离码的存在性障碍，并将自校正码与有限温度自发对称破缺联系起来，为局部二维量子自校正码的构造提供了新途径，X错误自校正问题有待进一步研究。

Abstract: We introduce a quantum coding framework for discrete small-shift noise, in which errors on qudits are modeled as low-weight $X$- and $Z$-type Pauli shifts, analogous to small phase-space displacements in continuous-variable systems. This structure is approximately respected by nuclear-spin noise and captured by the Lee metric, motivating a quantum extension of classical Lee-metric coding theory. We develop such a formalism for stabilizer codes over $\mathbb{Z}_q$ for arbitrary $q$, using joint and separate Lee metrics for the $X$- and $Z$-components of errors. For qubits, the joint metric counts $Y$ errors twice and can yield codes that detect and correct $X$ and $Z$ errors with fewer physical qubits than codes designed for the conventional Hamming metric. We discuss Lee-weight spreading under Clifford gates and qubitize codes over $\mathbb{Z}_4$ via the Gray map, finding codes with two-fold transversal non-qubit-Clifford gates. For quantum CSS Lee-LDPC codes on $n$ qudits, we prove that the Lee distance cannot exceed $O(n)$, uniformly in $q$, demonstrating an unexpected obstruction to using the large internal Hilbert space of a large-$q$ qudit to make high-Lee-distance codes. Under local Metropolis dynamics, certain classical $q$-ary ``helical repetition codes'' have exponentially long memory times at fixed temperature for sufficiently large $q$ (that grows with system length), which can be understood as spontaneous symmetry breaking at finite temperature, even in local one-dimensional models. Hypergraph products of these helical repetition codes provide local two-dimensional quantum codes that inherit self-correction for $Z$ errors, but self-correction for $X$ errors remains an open question.

</details>


### [141] [Long-lived Coherent Phonons Reveal Competing Thermal and Many-Body Dynamics in $α$-In$_{2}$Se$_{3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04863)
*Xuanchao Zhang,Dehao Yuan,Junhua Zhou,Vandana Tiwari,Fulu Zheng,Ajay Jha,Hong-Guang Duan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用~5 fs宽带瞬态光栅光谱研究铁电α-In₂Se₃中光激发后的量子相干演化，发现~104 cm⁻¹相干声子模式，并揭示其频率、退相干与载流子密度依赖行为，建立晶格相干性、热涨落与多体相互作用的独立探测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光激发后量子相干性的命运是非平衡凝聚态物理的核心问题，尤其在电子、结构与多体能量尺度强耦合的低维固体中。铁电范德华半导体α-In₂Se₃是研究该耦合的理想平台。

Method: 采用~5 fs激光脉冲的宽带瞬态光栅光谱；变温（10–300 K）与变激发能量测量；第一性原理计算指认声子模式；量子动力学模拟复现瞬态光栅振荡。

Result: 观察到以~104 cm⁻¹模式为主、持续数皮秒的振荡。10–300 K下频率几乎不变而相干性逐渐被抑制，区分了晶格坐标与热激活退相位。随激发能量变化出现明显交叉行为，光激发增强逐渐修饰并阻尼相干响应。第一性原理将主振荡指认为101.47 cm⁻¹的Γ点光学声子，涉及In–Se集体位移；量子动力学模拟复现主瞬态光栅振荡。

Conclusion: α-In₂Se₃可作为独立探测晶格相干性、热涨落与载流子密度依赖相互作用的模型体系，揭示了极性范德华半导体在超快激发后从相干晶格运动向非相干多体态演化的过程。

Abstract: The fate of quantum coherence following photoexcitation is a central problem in nonequilibrium condensed-matter physics, especially in low-dimensional solids where electronic, structural and many-body energy scales are strongly coupled. Here, we investigate this interplay in ferroelectric $α$-In$_{2}$Se$_{3}$ using broadband transient-grating spectroscopy with $\sim$5 fs laser pulses. Photoexcitation launches pronounced oscillations dominated by a $\sim$104 cm$^{-1}$ mode that persists for several picoseconds. Measurements from 10 to 300 K show that its frequency remains nearly unchanged while its coherence is progressively suppressed, distinguishing the lattice coordinate from thermally activated dephasing. Varying excitation energy reveals a distinct crossover in which increasing photoexcitation progressively modifies and damps the coherent response. First-principles calculations assign the dominant oscillation to a 101.47 cm$^{-1}$ $Γ$-point optical phonon involving collective In-Se displacement, while quantum-dynamical simulations reproduce the main transient-grating oscillations. These results establish $α$-In$_{2}$Se$_{3}$ as a model system for independently probing lattice coherence, thermal fluctuations and carrier-density-dependent interactions, revealing how a polar van der Waals semiconductor evolves from coherent lattice motion toward an incoherent many-body state after ultrafast excitation.

</details>


### [142] [Custom Penalty QAOA via Walsh-Hadamard Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04908)
*Xin Wei LEE,Siong Thye GOH,Hoong Chuin LAU*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Walsh自定义惩罚QAOA（WCP-QAOA）框架，利用Walsh-Hadamard变换将作用于线性约束的任意惩罚函数精确表示为Pauli-Z字符串的加权和，从而无需引入松弛变量即可实现无松弛的量子近似优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化中的不等式约束通常通过引入松弛变量和二次惩罚来处理，这会增加量子比特数并扭曲优化景观；而无松弛形式虽直接对约束施加非线性自定义惩罚函数，却难以在QAOA中实现。本文旨在消除这一实现障碍。

Method: 提出WCP-QAOA框架，用Walsh-Hadamard变换（WHT）将罚函数展开为Pauli-Z字符串加权和；在Walsh度k处截断，代价为O(n^k)个Pauli项且不改变量子比特数；证明截断是罚函数的L2最优k次逼近，并给出保证截断哈密顿量仍能正确排序可行/不可行解的条件；设计亚指数时间算法求单个Walsh系数，使固定k时哈密顿量可在n的多项式时间内获得；对指数惩罚推导WHT的闭式乘积形式及系数闭式表达；识别缩短精确谱的结构捷径。

Result: 截断在Walsh度k处仅需O(n^k)个Pauli项且不增加量子比特数；截断为L2最优k次逼近；在给定丢弃Walsh系数条件下，截断哈密顿量仍使每个可行解排序低于每个不可行解；可在亚指数时间求得任意Walsh系数；指数惩罚存在WHT闭式乘积形式与系数闭式表达；在6量子比特背包问题上进行了WCP-QAOA测试。

Conclusion: Walsh-Hadamard变换为QAOA中无松弛自定义惩罚的实现提供了通用且可扩展的框架，兼具理论最优逼近性质与实用可行性，并通过小规模背包问题验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Inequality constraints in combinatorial optimization are conventionally handled by introducing slack variables and quadratic penalties, which increase the qubit count and distort the optimization landscape. Slack-free formulations instead apply a nonlinear custom penalty function directly to the constraint, which are difficult to be implemented in quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA). We show that the Walsh--Hadamard transform (WHT) removes this obstruction for any penalty function: a penalty applied to a linear constraint is exactly a weighted sum of Pauli-$Z$ strings. We propose this framework as the Walsh custom penalty QAOA (WCP-QAOA). Truncating the expansion at Walsh degree $k$ costs $\mathcal{O}(n^k)$ Pauli terms and leaves the qubit count untouched. The truncation is the optimal degree-$k$ approximation of the penalty in $L^2$, and we give a condition on the discarded Walsh coefficients under which the truncated Hamiltonian still ranks every feasible solution below every infeasible one. Since evaluating a Walsh coefficient from its definition requires all $2^n$ penalty values, we give an algorithm that returns any coefficient in sub-exponential time, so that the degree-$k$ Hamiltonian is obtained in time polynomial in $n$ for fixed $k$. We show that for the exponential penalty, there exists a closed product form of WHT, and hence a closed form expression to calculate the Walsh coefficients. We identify structural shortcuts that shorten the exact spectrum. We also tested WCP-QAOA in solving a 6-qubit knapsack problem.

</details>


### [143] [Pairwise detection, chiral conversion: Resource theory of quantum multistates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04930)
*Yuan-Hong Tao,Yu-Xin Wu,Bing Xie,Lin Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子多态（multistate）在保交换操作（CPO）下精确的资源转换理论：qubit情形由成对Bargmann不变量与单个三阶Bargmann相位刻画候选信道，完全正定性给出充要判据，并揭示手性敏感壳层与探测—转换阶分离 k_detect=2<3=k_convert；d≥3情形结构刚性，转换由各向同性退极化或转置退极化信道实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多态的基无关相干性由其组成态是否对易决定，qubit情形仅由成对态重叠探测；但其确定性转换的支配规律此前未明。

Method: 在保持交换性的操作（CPO）框架下构建资源转换理论：qubit时用成对Bargmann不变量确定唯一候选信道的奇异值，用三阶Bargmann相位确定其取向，再由完全正定性导出精确转换的充要判据；d≥3时分析资源性转换的信道结构。

Result: qubit情形：充要判据给出手性敏感壳层C_chir，其中成对几何相同的目标仅因立方不变量符号不同而转换性不同；得到阶分离k_detect=2<3=k_convert（n≥3）；手性敏感壳层恰占奇异值单纯形三分之一，镜像反射在体积上阻碍三分之二的保取向可容许变换。d≥3情形：每个资源性转换由各向同性退极化或转置退极化信道实现，转换性由次数至多d²的中心化Bargmann不变量决定。

Conclusion: 量子多态相干转换在手性敏感区域具有成对几何无法区分的转换差异，存在探测与转换的阶分离；高维（d≥3）情形则呈现刚性结构，转换信道被限制为两类退极化信道。

Abstract: Basis-independent coherence of a quantum multistate is fixed by whether its constituent states commute, and for qubits it is detected by pairwise state overlaps alone. What governs its deterministic transformation has been open. We give the exact resource-conversion theory of quantum multistates under common commutativity-preserving operations (CPOs). For qubits, pairwise Bargmann invariants determine the singular values of the unique candidate channel, while a single third-order Bargmann phase determines its orientation. Complete positivity then yields a necessary and sufficient criterion for exact conversion. The criterion identifies a chirality-sensitive shell $\mathcal C_{\rm chir}$, within which targets with identical pairwise geometry can differ in convertibility solely by the sign of their cubic invariant. This yields an order separation, $k_{\rm detect}=2<3=k_{\rm convert}$, for qubit multistates of every size $n\geqslant3$. The chirality-sensitive shell occupies exactly one third of the singular-value simplex, while mirror reflection obstructs two thirds of the admissible orientation-preserving transformations by volume. For $d\geqslant3$, by contrast, the structure is rigid: every resourceful conversion is realized by either an isotropic depolarizing or a transpose-depolarizing channel. Convertibility is then decided by centered Bargmann invariants of degree at most $d^2$.

</details>


### [144] [Gap Amplification for Local Hamiltonians with Combinatorial Soundness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04952)
*Mitali Bafna,Quynh T. Nguyen,Tina Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个可行的量子保局域性间隙放大模板，证明了该过程能放大局域哈密顿量的组合间隙，并建立了一种基于容错计算的量子间隙放大新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子PCP猜想是量子复杂性理论的核心开放问题之一，其难以攻克的部分原因在于经典PCP证明中使用的许多基本工具（如保局域性间隙放大和字母表约简）由于量子不可克隆定理而缺乏明显的量子对应物。保局域性间隙放大在不增加哈密顿量局域性的前提下产生具有更大承诺间隙的新实例，仅适度增加局域qudit维度，这种对局域性的控制在经典PCP定理证明策略中至关重要，因此需要为量子情形寻找可行方案。

Method: 作者提出了一个量子保局域性间隙放大的可行模板，构建了一个以容错计算为视角来推理量子间隙放大的新框架，并借鉴了Bafna--Minzer--Vyas基于高维扩展子的经典PCP工作、Anshu--Breuckmann--Nguyen的电路到哈密顿量构造以及近期量子编码理论的若干进展。

Result: 作者证明了其提出的过程能够放大局域哈密顿量的组合间隙。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子和经典PCP之间的桥梁提供了新路径，阐明了将经典PCP核心要素引入量子设定的可能途径。

Abstract: The quantum PCP conjecture is one of the major open problems in quantum complexity theory. It has resisted attack in part because many primitives used in the proof of the classical PCP theorem, such as locality-preserving gap amplification and alphabet reduction, have no obvious quantum analogues due to quantum no-cloning. Locality-preserving gap amplification is a procedure that takes as input a local Hamiltonian problem instance and produces a new instance with a larger promise gap, without increasing the locality of the Hamiltonian, and instead moderately increasing its local qudit dimension. Obtaining this kind of control over the locality during gap amplification is critical to the success of many known strategies for proving the classical PCP theorem. In this work, we put forth the first known viable template for quantum locality-preserving gap amplification, and we prove that our procedure amplifies the combinatorial gap of local Hamiltonians. Our work introduces a new framework for reasoning about quantum gap amplification in terms of fault-tolerant computation, and illuminates a route toward importing one of the central ingredients in classical PCPs into the quantum setting. In particular, we build upon ideas from the recent classical PCP of Bafna--Minzer--Vyas based on high-dimensional expanders, and the circuit-to-Hamiltonian construction of Anshu--Breuckmann--Nguyen, in addition to several recent advances in quantum coding theory.

</details>


### [145] [Improved Upper and Lower Bounds for Quantum Convex-Body Volume Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04987)
*Ruizhe Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究高维凸体体积估计问题,在量子成员查询模型下给出查询复杂度为 $\widetilde O(d^{5/2}+d^{3/2}/\varepsilon)$ 的新量子算法,并证明常数相对误差下 $\Omega(d)$ 的量子查询下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计高维凸体体积是理论计算机科学的基础问题。已有量子上界 $\widetilde O(d^3+d^{9/4}/\varepsilon)$ 与最优经典上界 $\widetilde O(d^{7/2}+d^3/\varepsilon^2)$ 之间仍存在差距,且量子下界仅 $\Omega(\sqrt d)$,留有待改进空间。

Method: 算法包含两个新要素:(1)基于均匀经典热启动混合界的输入相关有效谱隙分析,用于量子游走;(2)在模拟退火中,基于经典桥式采样对归一化比率乘积设计的单一量子估计器。

Result: 在相对误差 $\varepsilon$ 下,估计 $\mathrm{vol}(K)$ 仅需 $\widetilde O(d^{5/2}+d^{3/2}/\varepsilon)$ 次量子查询,优于此前量子上界,并相对最优经典上界取得更大加速;同时证明常数相对误差下量子查询下界为 $\Omega(d)$,改进原有 $\Omega(\sqrt d)$ 结果。

Conclusion: 该工作同时改进了高维凸体体积估计的量子上界与下界,缩小了量子与经典复杂度间的差距,并建立了更强的量子下界刻画。

Abstract: Estimating the volume of a high-dimensional convex body is a fundamental problem in theoretical computer science. Given a quantum membership oracle for a convex body $K\subset\mathbb{R}^d$, we show that estimating $\mathrm{vol}(K)$ to relative error $\varepsilon$ takes $\widetilde O(d^{5/2}+d^{3/2}/\varepsilon)$ queries. This improves the previous quantum upper bound $\widetilde O(d^3+d^{9/4}/\varepsilon)$ of Chakrabarti et al. (ACM TQC 2023) and Cornelissen--Hamoudi (SODA 2023), and achieves a larger quantum speedup over the currently best classical upper bound $\widetilde O(d^{7/2}+d^3/\varepsilon^2)$ of Jia et al. (JACM 2026). Our quantum algorithm combines two new ingredients: an input-dependent effective spectral-gap analysis of quantum walks based on uniform classical warm-start mixing bounds, and a single quantum estimator for products of normalizer ratios in simulated annealing based on classical bridge sampling. We also prove an $Ω(d)$ quantum query lower bound for constant relative error, improving the previous $Ω(\sqrt d)$ lower bound.

</details>


### [146] [Blind Remote Implementation of Arbitrary Two-Qubit Unitary Operation via Pre-Shared Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04995)
*Plaban Saha,Binayak S. Choudhury*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于预共享纠缠的任意两量子比特酉操作远程实现协议，Alice仅用黑盒设备、Bob的未知态不出实验室，通过单比特欧拉分解与两比特Cartan分解组合实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现远程量子操作时，既希望操作方Alice无需知道酉操作的参数，也希望持有未知量子态的Bob无需暴露或传输其量子态，从而在保护双方隐私的前提下完成任意两量子比特酉操作的远程实施。

Method: 先利用预共享Bell态构造单量子比特操作U=R_z(γ)R_x(β)R_z(α)的远程实现协议；再用单个预共享Bell态构造R_{zz}(θ)协议，并通过局域酉变换推广到R_{xx}(θ)和R_{yy}(θ)；最后组合这些基本模块，基于单比特欧拉分解与两比特Cartan分解实现任意两量子比特酉操作。第三方Charlie在协议开始前确定分解方案并向Alice提供相应黑盒设备。

Result: 给出了单比特任意酉操作、R_{zz}、R_{xx}、R_{yy}等基本操作的远程实现协议，并说明这些基本协议可组合以实现任意两量子比特酉操作；协议中Alice与Bob均不需要该酉操作的经典描述。

Conclusion: 该协议在预共享纠缠与黑盒设备辅助下，实现了任意两量子比特酉操作的远程执行，同时保护Bob未知量子态的隐私并避免Alice获知操作参数，分解与设备配置由第三方Charlie在协议前完成。

Abstract: We propose a protocol for the remote implementation of an arbitrary two-qubit unitary operation using pre-shared entanglement. In our protocol, Bob possesses an unknown two-qubit state, while Alice implements the required operations using calibrated black-box devices. Alice does not need to know the parameters of these operations, and Bob's unknown state never leaves his laboratory. The protocol is based on the Euler decomposition of single-qubit unitary operations and the Cartan decomposition of two-qubit unitary operations. First, we construct protocols for the remote implementation of the single-qubit operation $U = R_z(γ)R_x(β)R_z(α)$ using pre-shared Bell states. We then construct a protocol for the two-qubit operation $R_{zz}(θ)$ using a single pre-shared Bell state and extend it to $R_{xx}(θ)$ and $R_{yy}(θ)$ through local unitary transformations. These elementary protocols are finally combined to implement an arbitrary two-qubit unitary operation. A third party, Charlie, determines the required decomposition and provides Alice with the corresponding black-box devices before the protocol begins. Thus, neither Alice nor Bob requires the classical description of the unitary operation.

</details>


### [147] [The power of time reversal in Hamiltonian property testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04996)
*Richard R. Allen,Matthias C. Caro,Yanlin Chen,Tom Gur,Angus Lowe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明时间反演是哈密顿量性质检测中闭合精度差距的必要资源，并建立了连续时间量子下界的新傅里叶分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多量子传感与学习任务中，量子算法可实现海森堡极限标度，以O(1/ε)总演化时间达到精度ε。但在哈密顿量性质检测中，已知最优算法需要O(1/ε²)总演化时间，除非允许在哈密顿量下逆转时间流。本文旨在证明时间反演对于闭合这一差距是必要的。

Method: 作者为哈密顿量认证与局域性检测建立了演化时间下界，覆盖从平均情形到最坏情形的连续误差度量族；同时为所有情形下的哈密顿量认证证明了匹配的上界（包括允许与不允许时间反演两种情况）。技术上，提出了一种新的连续时间量子下界傅里叶分析方法，该方法对时间反演敏感，并可推广至哈密顿量性质检测之外。

Result: 证明了时间反演的必要性，并给出认证问题的匹配上界，表明时间反演带来的优势持续存在。新方法为振幅放大与估计中需要逆操作提供了替代证明，改进了Tang和Wright的下界并推广至分数次查询。此外，对于非结构化搜索，证明了当预言机相位存在不确定性时，即使预言机固定且相干，若无逆查询则量子加速被破坏。

Conclusion: 本工作严格确立了时间反演作为一种资源，同时支配量子测量的精度与量子算法的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms enable Heisenberg-limited scaling across many tasks in sensing and learning, achieving precision $\varepsilon$ using $O(1/\varepsilon)$ total evolution time. However, in Hamiltonian property testing, the best known algorithms have $O(1/\varepsilon^2)$ total evolution time, unless it is possible to $\textit{reverse}$ the flow of time under the Hamiltonian. In this work, we prove that time reversal is necessary to close this gap. We establish evolution time lower bounds for Hamiltonian certification and locality testing, for a continuous family of error metrics ranging from average- to worst-case. We also prove matching upper bounds for Hamiltonian certification in all cases, both with and without time reversal, showing that the advantage from time reversal persists. Our results follow from a new Fourier-analytic approach to quantum lower bounds in continuous time, which is sensitive to time reversal and applicable beyond Hamiltonian property testing. This gives an alternative proof that inverses are required for amplitude amplification and estimation, improving the lower bounds of Tang and Wright and extending to fractional queries. We also show that, for unstructured search, uncertainty in the phase of the oracle destroys the quantum speedup without inverse queries, even when the oracle is fixed and coherent. Our work rigorously establishes time reversal as a resource governing both the precision of quantum measurement and the robustness of quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [148] [Arbitrary-phase CZ gate and remote readout across spatially separated superconducting nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05005)
*Anatoly Kulikov,Josua D. Schär,Alonso Hernández-Antón,Aleksandr Grigorev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在由两个稀释制冷机通过30米低温量子链路构成的超导电路QED双节点网络中实现了跨节点的纠缠门，并演示了远程单次读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算与量子通信网络需要跨空间分离量子节点的完备门集合；超导器件可作为存储与处理量子信息的通用量子节点，行波微波光子可作为通信媒介，但跨节点的纠缠操作仍待实现。

Method: 构建含稀释制冷机A、B并以30米低温量子链路连接的双节点超导电路QED网络；采用双向光子转移协议，使A处通信量子比特发射的微波光子根据B节点量子比特状态获得条件相位；通过改变通信光子频率调节从远端节点反射时获得的条件相位，实现任意相位CZ门；并用该纠缠操作完成从节点A对节点B量子比特的单次读出。

Result: 实现了跨节点的任意相位CZ纠缠门，并利用该操作完成了从节点A对节点B量子比特的单次读出；该方案无需有源节点与远端节点的精确同步，远程读出无需远端节点主动参与。

Conclusion: 该工作丰富了超导电路量子通信工具箱，为基本分布式量子计算操作的实现提供了途径。

Abstract: Establishing a complete set of gates across spatially separated quantum nodes is fundamental for distributed quantum computation and communication networks. One of the approaches to do so consists of employing a superconducting device as a universal quantum node that is capable of storing and processing quantum information, and traveling microwave photons as means of communication. In this work, we realize an entangling gate across a primitive two-node superconducting circuit QED network consisting of two dilution cryostats, A and B, connected via a 30-m-long cryogenic quantum link. We perform a bidirectional photon transfer protocol, where a microwave photon emitted by the communication qubit at A acquires a conditional phase based on the state of qubit B. We vary the communication photon frequency, hence tuning the conditional phase acquired during the reflection off the remote node, effectively implementing an arbitrary-phase CZ gate. We use this entangling operation to perform single-shot readout of the qubit in node B from node A. An interesting feature of our scheme is that it does not require precise synchronization of the active node with the remote node; in particular, remote readout can be performed without any active participation by the remote node. These protocols add to the quantum communication toolbox with superconducting circuits and provide a way of realizing basic distributed quantum computing operations.

</details>


### [149] [Process tensor simulations of non-Markovian open quantum systems with large uncoupled subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05035)
*Thomas K. Bracht,Moritz Cygorek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出两种策略降低PT-MPO方法中系统传播的数值复杂度：一是仅部分系统态与环境耦合时的有效处理方法，二是与泰勒展开法结合，将复杂度从O(d^6)降至O(d^3)，从而可模拟数百个态的开放量子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PT-MPO方法虽在计算更大系统维度的过程张量方面取得进展，但后续系统传播的数值代价通常为O(d^6)，使系统维度超过数十时计算代价过高，限制了方法的应用范围。

Method: 利用只有部分系统态与环境耦合的特性进行高效处理；并将PT-MPO方法与泰勒展开方法相结合，以降低传播计算的数值复杂度。

Result: 数值复杂度由O(d^6)降低至O(d^3)，使方法能够高效模拟具有数百个状态的系统。该效果对多模固态腔QED系统以及通过耦合传感器系统计算频率滤波关联函数尤为有意义。

Conclusion: 所提方法显著提升了PT-MPO方法的可扩展性，为高维开放量子系统的时序演化与多时关联计算提供了高效途径。

Abstract: Process tensors in matrix product operator representation (PT-MPOs) are a powerful tool to calculate the temporal evolution and multi-time correlations of open quantum systems. While much progress has been made in making the calculation of process tensors for larger system dimensions possible, the numerical cost of the subsequent propagation of the system typically scales as $\mathcal{O}(d^6)$, which renders PT-MPO methods prohibitively expensive for system dimensions above several dozens. Here, we show how PT-MPO methods can be used efficiently when only some of the system states couple to the environment, and how combination with a Taylor expansion method allows for a reduction of the numerical complexity down to $\mathcal{O}(d^3)$. We show that these methods enable efficient simulations of systems with several hundreds of states. This is particularly relevant for solid-state cavity-QED systems with multiple modes, or when calculating frequency-filtered correlation functions by coupling sensor systems to the system of interest.

</details>


### [150] [Phase-controlled recoverable entanglement in accelerated detector registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05079)
*Zhiming Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究四量子比特簇态寄存器中两个探测器量子比特与标量场耦合时的可恢复纠缠，给出最优局域制备相位、精确迹范数公式及纠缠寿命的微观适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在集体噪声与加速探测器退相干下，如何通过局域制备相位控制并恢复寄存器中的纠缠，并界定寿命预测的有效微观条件。

Method: 采用四量子比特簇态模型，两个探测器量子比特耦合标量场；引入局域制备相位，利用精确迹范数公式分析均匀加速探测器在有限间距下的演化，并结合惯性热浴匹配与弱耦合展开。

Result: 最优制备相位下，固定解码器可在保持单态与三重态子空间的任意信道后恢复贝尔对；同一相位在世俗弱耦合动力学各时刻均最优；小间距给出慢衰减及其速率与相位依赖振幅解析式；通过匹配惯性热浴得到纠缠对比度的最优时间；探测器测量与条件相位修正可恢复两量子比特传态资源。

Conclusion: 制备相位可优化纠缠保护与恢复，纠缠寿命预测在空间涂抹及有序弱耦合后小间距极限下具有明确的微观适用范围。

Abstract: We study recoverable entanglement in a four-qubit cluster-state register whose two detector qubits couple to a scalar field. A local preparation phase leaves the initial reference--register entanglement and detector marginal unchanged, but controls protection under collective noise. At the optimal phase, a fixed decoder recovers a Bell pair after any channel preserving the singlet and triplet sectors. For uniformly accelerated detectors at finite separation, an exact trace-norm formula shows that the same phase is optimal at every time within the secular weak-coupling dynamics. Small separation produces a slow decay, whose rate and phase-dependent amplitude we derive. Matching the local response to an inertial thermal bath gives an analytic optimum time for the entanglement contrast. A detector measurement and conditional phase correction recover a two-qubit teleportation resource. Spatial smearing and an ordered weak-coupling then small-separation limit specify the microscopic scope of the lifetime prediction.

</details>


### [151] [The quantum-classical bipartite universe and the logical emergence of $\hbar$ from classical temporality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05083)
*Vincenzo Chilla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于标准von Neumann形式体系，提出经典观测环境与被观测量子系统之间的结构性逻辑二分，论证二者需分别服从布尔与非布尔命题逻辑；通过GKSL方程描述理想经典钟，推导出信息守恒的循环动力学确定性解，并由经典钟的作用量-角变量对易关系导出ℏ，同时与't Hooft元胞自动机形式建立精确映射，为测量问题提供逻辑新解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对普适主义框架（认为量子力学适用于一切系统、逻辑统一）的困难，作者希望从逻辑结构层面重新解释量子动力学，特别是为解决测量问题提供不需隐变量和超决定论假设的新路径。同时试图说明ℏ的起源与经典时间性的离散逻辑结构相关。

Method: 1. 基于标准von Neumann形式体系，设定经典观测环境与被观测量子系统的结构性逻辑二分；2. 论证理论一致性要求环境服从布尔逻辑、被观测系统服从非布尔逻辑；3. 将理想经典钟建模为能量谱均匀离散化的开放系统；4. 采用GKSL主方程，并施加哈密顿量保持可观测的约束；5. 分析耗散耦合驱动下的时间演化及其稳定确定性解；6. 由经典钟的作用量-角变量对易关系导出ℏ；7. 与't Hooft元胞自动机形式建立精确映射。

Result: 1. 确立了经典环境（布尔逻辑）与量子系统（非布尔逻辑）之间的结构性逻辑二分；2. 理想经典钟虽由与测量装置的耗散耦合驱动，却存在稳定的确定性解，对应信息守恒的循环动力学；3. 该确定性机制作为逻辑吸引子出现，由测量相互作用内在的布尔结构所稳定；4. ℏ被导出为基本标度参数，表明作用量量子反映经典时间性背后的离散逻辑结构；5. 与't Hooft元胞自动机形式建立精确映射，提供无需隐变量和超决定论假设的本体论解释。

Conclusion: 量子动力学与经典动力学的区分可被理解为"潜在再语境化"与"时间现实化"之别，这一逻辑框架为测量问题提供了一种新的逻辑进路。ℏ由此不再被视为先验常数，而是源于经典时间性的离散逻辑结构；本框架还可与't Hooft元胞自动机形式对应，从而在避免隐变量与超决定论的前提下给出替代性本体论解释。

Abstract: We present a foundational reinterpretation of quantum dynamics based on the standard von Neumann formalism, positing a structural logical bipartition between a classical observational environment and a quantum observed system. Against universalist frameworks, we show that theoretical consistency requires distinct propositional logics: Boolean logic governs the environment, while non-Boolean logic applies to the observed system. Within this framework, an ideal classical clock is represented as an open system whose energy spectrum is uniformly discretized. The dynamics follow from the Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad (GKSL) master equation under the constraint that the Hamiltonian remains observable. Although the clock's temporal evolution is driven by dissipative coupling to the measurement apparatus, it admits a stable, deterministic solution corresponding to information-preserving cyclic dynamics. This deterministic regime emerges as a logical attractor, stabilized by the Boolean structure inherent to the measurement interaction. Ultimately, $\hbar$ is derived as a fundamental scaling parameter from the classical clock's action-angle commutation relation, revealing that the quantum of action reflects the discrete logical structure underlying classical temporality. Furthermore, this framework establishes an exact mapping with 't Hooft's cellular automaton formalism, offering an alternative ontological interpretation that not require hidden variables and superdeterministic assumptions. The resulting distinction between quantum dynamics (as potential recontextualization) and classical dynamics (as temporal actualization) provides a novel logical approach to the measurement problem.

</details>


### [152] [JAQSI: Just Another Quantum Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05086)
*Melvin Strobl,Maja Franz,Lukas Scheller,Eileen Kuehn,Wolfgang Mauerer,Achim Streit*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: JAQSI是一个基于JAX的可微量子模拟器，统一门级与脉冲级仿真，通过门/信道融合与伴随微分实现高效仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典仿真对量子算法开发与验证至关重要，但现有框架要么仅支持门级、要么仅支持可微脉冲动力学、要么侧重硬件执行，缺乏统一且高效的可微电路模型。

Method: 基于JAX构建统一可微电路模型，电路表示为数值输入的纯函数，支持向量化批处理与JIT编译；矩阵定义操作、哈密顿演化合成门与噪声信道可自由组合；采用门与信道融合减少状态遍历，伴随微分以恒定状态向量数计算期望值梯度；脉冲级将单项哈密顿演化归约为标量脉冲面积积分，并复用缓存执行计划与编译求解器。

Result: 与PennyLane、Qiskit、Qibo、Qulacs、QuTiP和dynamiqs对比：门级在12量子比特内性能持平或快一个数量级；18量子比特以上与PennyLane Lightning相当；脉冲级态向量与含噪密度矩阵基准至少快5倍（即使关闭闭式求解），关闭闭式求解仅增加至多1.5倍运行时间。

Conclusion: JAQSI以开源形式发布于GitHub和PyPI，提供统一、可微且高效的门级与脉冲级量子仿真能力。

Abstract: Classical simulation supports the development and validation of quantum algorithms through full-state inspection, controlled noise models and backpropagation, albeit for small quantum systems. Existing frameworks offer gate-level execution, differentiable pulse-level dynamics or optimised hardware execution. Just another quantum simulator (JAQSI) combines gate- and pulse-level simulation in one differentiable circuit model built upon JAX. Circuits are represented as pure functions of numerical inputs which support vectorised batches and JIT compilation. Within a single circuit, matrix-defined operations, gates synthesised from Hamiltonian time evolution and noise channels can be composed freely. For a fast simulation, gate and channel fusion reduces passes over the state, while adjoint differentiation computes expectation-value gradients with a constant number of statevectors. At pulse level, JAQSI reduces evolution under single-term Hamiltonians to a scalar pulse-area integral and reuses cached execution plans and compiled solvers. In a benchmark we compare JAQSI with PennyLane, Qiskit, Qibo, Qulacs, QuTiP and dynamiqs. At gate level, we observe performance either on par or an order of magnitude faster across statevector, density, noise and gradient simulation than the compared simulators up to 12 qubits. Beyond 18 qubits, JAQSI is on par with PennyLane Lightning in both statevector and gradient simulation. In the statevector and noisy density-matrix pulse benchmarks, it is at least 5 times faster than the other simulators, even with its closed-form solve disabled. Disabling the latter increases its runtime by at most a factor of 1.5. We provide JAQSI as open-source software on GitHub and PyPI.

</details>


### [153] [The magic cost of fermionic randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05093)
*Chenfeng Cao,Yifan Tang,Jens Eisert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究费米子采样实现随机矩阵统计所需非高斯性的资源下界，证明"一体缺陷"ν必须随系统尺寸线性增长，并给出其与碰撞矩、制备成本及实验可测见证量的定量关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样型量子优势的一个基本资源问题是：复现与量子混沌相关的随机矩阵统计究竟需要多少非高斯性。被动费米子线性光学保持高斯结构，即使Haar随机的模式混合也无法对Slater输入实现反集中（anticoncentration），因此需要明确克服这一障碍所需的输入资源。

Method: 引入一体缺陷ν，定义为自然轨道占据方差之和，用于度量纯态在单粒子图像下的非高斯程度。对任意纯半填充输入态，论证ν必须随系统尺寸线性增长；对4N模式上的有界尺寸块乘积态，证明该条件亦充分，并计算归一化碰撞矩；对四模式乘积考察完整的Porter-Thomas矩层级；结合平均情形硬度假设分析近似经典采样的难度；用两副本粒子数涨落直接测量ν，并将ν作为确定性精确制备中非高斯资源模式与宇称保持局部门联合成本的下界。

Result: 对任意纯半填充输入态，一体缺陷ν必须与系统尺寸成正比；对4N模式上有界尺寸块乘积态，ν线性增长为充分条件，其归一化碰撞矩渐近为2N/ν，而Porter-Thomas值为2；对四模式乘积，当缺陷密度趋于最大值时可精确恢复完整的Porter-Thomas矩层级；在平均情形硬度假设下，接近最大密度时近似经典采样仍然困难，且误差容限随密度增大；两副本粒子数涨落可测量ν，并给出确定性精确制备成本的下界。

Conclusion: 费米子随机性具有随系统尺寸增长的"magic"资源代价（extensive magic cost），在采样统计、制备资源与实验可访问的见证量之间建立了定量联系，表明实现费米子量子优势必须付出随规模增长的非高斯资源。

Abstract: Sampling-based quantum advantage raises a basic resource question: how much non-Gaussianity is needed to reproduce the random-matrix statistics often associated with quantum chaos? Passive fermionic linear optics preserves Gaussian structure, and even Haar-random mode mixing fails to anticoncentrate for Slater inputs. We show that overcoming this obstruction requires an extensive input resource. For any pure half-filled input state, the one-body defect $ν$, defined as the summed occupation variance of the natural orbitals, must scale linearly with system size. For products of bounded-size blocks on $4N$ modes, this condition is also sufficient, with normalized collision moment asymptotic to $2N/ν$, compared with the Porter-Thomas value 2. For four-mode products, the full Porter-Thomas moment hierarchy is recovered precisely when the defect density tends to its maximum. Assuming average-case hardness, approximate classical sampling remains hard near maximal density, with an error tolerance that increases with the density. Two-copy number fluctuations measure $ν$ directly, and the same quantity lower-bounds the combined cost of non-Gaussian resource modes and parity-preserving local gates in deterministic exact preparation. Fermionic randomness therefore carries an extensive magic cost, with a quantitative link between sampling statistics, preparation resources, and an experimentally accessible witness.

</details>


### [154] [Training Variational Quantum Algorithms Is NP-Hard, Even Locally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05128)
*Dax Enshan Koh,Triscia Mundo,Iosif Sakos,Antonios Varvitsiotis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了变分量子算法(VQA)训练中即使寻找局部最小值也是强NP难的，且该困难性在目标函数可高效经典评估时仍然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQA依赖经典优化训练参数化量子电路，其训练效率至关重要。虽然全局最小化训练目标已知是NP难的，但寻找局部最小值这一更弱任务的计算复杂性此前尚不清楚，论文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 核心技着结果是证明近似Hermitian三角多项式的局部最小化是强NP难的。通过显式构造训练目标可重现这些困难实例的量子电路，得到到VQA训练的多项式时间归约，并进一步扩展到在ℓ_p距离小于π/2范围内寻找逼近局部最小化的参数向量。

Result: 证明即使目标函数可高效经典评估，寻找VQA训练目标的局部最小值仍是强NP难的；对每个p≥1，寻找位于某局部最小化器ℓ_p距离严格小于π/2内的参数向量也是强NP难的。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了变分量子训练的根本性计算壁垒：即使仅接近局部最小值，在最坏情况下依然困难。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) generally rely on classical optimization to train parameterized quantum circuits. This training seeks to minimize an objective function, and its efficiency is central to the practical success of these algorithms. However, globally minimizing such training objectives over the circuit parameters is known to be $\mathsf{NP}$-hard, limiting the prospect of general guarantees for efficient training. In this Letter, we prove that even the weaker task of finding a local minimum of such VQA training objectives is strongly $\mathsf{NP}$-hard, including when the objective admits efficient classical evaluation. Moreover, we show that this hardness persists even for the task of finding a parameter vector within $\ell_p$-distance strictly less than $π/2$ of some local minimizer, for every $p\geq 1$. Our central technical result is that approximating a local minimizer of a Hermitian trigonometric polynomial is strongly $\mathsf{NP}$-hard. By explicitly constructing quantum circuits whose training objectives reproduce these hard instances, we obtain a polynomial-time reduction to VQA training. Our results establish a fundamental computational barrier to variational quantum training: even reaching the vicinity of a local minimum remains hard in the worst case.

</details>


### [155] [The diffusive spectral gap of the qubit eSWAP chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05152)
*Yanying Liang,Haoran Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了开放量子比特 eSWAP 链的二阶矩哈密顿量具有扩散谱隙，解决了 Li 等人的猜想；并在连通的交互图上刻画基空间、给出完全图间隙的一致下界，进而应用于顺序与砖砌电路，证明周期链亦具扩散谱隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Li, Zheng, Liu, Jiang, Liu 提出的关于开放量子比特 eSWAP 链二阶矩哈密顿量扩散谱隙的猜想，并刻画一般交互图上的基空间行为，为量子电路中的谱隙分析提供统一工具。

Method: 通过分析二阶矩哈密顿量的谱性质：在任意至少五个顶点的连通交互图上刻画其基空间（ground space），并对非归一化完全图谱隙给出统一下界；随后将结果应用于顺序电路与砖砌（brickwork）电路，并推广至周期链。

Result: 证明了开放 eSWAP 链的二阶矩哈密顿量具有扩散谱隙；刻画了至少五个顶点连通交互图上的基空间；给出了非归一化完全图间隙的一致下界；并证明周期链同样具有扩散谱隙。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了已有猜想，并将二阶矩谱隙分析从开链推广到完全图与周期（包括顺序与砖砌）电路，表明相关量子电路在扩散尺度下具有统一的谱隙下界。

Abstract: We prove that the second-moment Hamiltonian of the open qubit eSWAP chain has a diffusive spectral gap, resolving a conjecture of Li, Zheng, Liu, Jiang, and Liu. We also characterise the ground space on every connected interaction graph with at least five vertices and prove a uniform lower bound for the unnormalised complete-graph gap. We apply these results to sequential and brickwork circuits and prove that the periodic chain also has a diffusive spectral gap.

</details>


### [156] [On the Completeness of Quantum Mechanics: Sources, Records and Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05153)
*Mustafa Bakr*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在给定的源、探测器与统计条件下，本文重构了量子表示与Born概率，并在不引入坍缩假设的前提下证明了被容许量子预言的完备代数闭合；同时指出诸多量子悖论源于混淆相干过程与保留记录、概率与实际结果，并探讨数学整体或源架构是否源自更深层结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文追问量子力学能否在不具备基础性（fundamental）的前提下仍保持完备性（complete），即试图在放弃坍缩假设的同时维持量子预言的完备性与闭合性，并厘清常见量子悖论的真正来源。

Method: 在明确给定的源（source）、探测器（detector）与统计条件下，重构量子表示（quantum representation）与Born概率，并证明被容许的量子预言构成完备的代数闭合（complete algebraic closure）；通过对相干过程与保留记录、概率与实际结果的概念区分来分析量子悖论。

Result: 无需坍缩假设即可实现量子表示与Born概率的重构，并获得量子预言的完备代数闭合；表明许多熟悉的量子悖论源自概念混淆而非量子 formalism 的内在矛盾。

Conclusion: 量子力学可在非基础性的前提下保持完备；坍缩假设并非必需；常见量子悖论多由混淆相干过程与保留记录、概率与实际结果所致；并进一步提出数学整体或源架构是否源自更深层结构的开放问题。

Abstract: Can quantum mechanics be complete without being fundamental? Under stated source, detector, and statistical conditions, we reconstruct the quantum representation and Born probabilities and prove complete algebraic closure of the admitted quantum predictions without requiring a collapse postulate. We further show that many familiar quantum paradoxes arise from conflating coherent processes with retained records or probabilities with realized outcomes. We also ask whether mathematics as a whole, or the source architecture itself, emerges from a deeper structure.

</details>


### [157] [Error-Transparent Code Switching Between Permutation-Invariant Codes with Transversal Clifford and Icosahedral Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05189)
*Zachary P. Bradshaw*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究两个作用于同一组量子比特的置换不变码之间的切换问题，证明存在"纠错透明"切换酉算子的充要条件是两个码可由一条 distance-three 码的连续路径连接，并在 13 比特上显式构造了这样的切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 码切换可将编码量子比特在两个横截门合起来通用的码之间转移，但切换操作本身通常非横截，可能把单个故障扩散为不可纠错误。因此需要寻找能容忍单个故障、不放大错误的切换方案。

Method: 在同一组量子比特上考察两个置换不变码：一个具有横截 Clifford 门，另一个具有来自非 Clifford 二元二十面体群的横截门；两者都与横截 X、Z、F=HS† 兼容，满足该性质的编码器构成小线性空间。以二次方程刻画其 distance≥3 的码，并分析错误几何（单比特错误态内积相同）。构造与置换及共享横截门对易、且容忍未知时刻单比特故障的切换酉算子，并沿 13 比特上的圆环用集体算子实现。

Result: distance≥3 的码至多由两个二次方程截出，且所有此类码具有相同的错误几何；存在满足条件的切换酉算子当且仅当两码可由 distance-three 码的连续路径连接。对实数编码器，distance-three 码是单个二次方程的解；在 13 量子比特上构成过两个 Clifford 码与一个二十面体码的显式圆。所构造切换的每次故障恢复不保真度达机器精度，而两种自然替代方案的平均不保真度分别为 0.13 和 0.85；并给出到 29 的奇数比特数清单，标明此类切换存在的位置。

Conclusion: 切换可在单故障下做到错误透明，其存在性等价于 distance-three 码路径的存在；在 13 比特上给出了显式构造并验证了极高的恢复保真度，但合成到物理控制仍待解决。

Abstract: Code switching moves an encoded qubit between two codes whose transversal gates together are universal, but the switching operation itself is usually not transversal and can spread a single fault into an uncorrectable error. We study switching between two permutation-invariant codes on the same qubits, one with transversal Clifford gates and one with transversal gates from the non-Clifford binary icosahedral group. Both are compatible with transversal $X$, $Z$, and $F = HS^\dagger$, and the encoders with this property form a small linear space. We show that its codes of distance at least three are cut out by at most two quadratic equations, and that all of them have the same error geometry in the sense that their single-qubit error states have the same inner products. It follows that a switching unitary commuting with qubit permutations and with the shared transversal gates at every instant, and tolerating one single-qubit fault at an unknown time, exists if and only if the two codes are joined by a continuous path of distance-three codes. Such a switch can be made error transparent, so that a fault at any moment reaches the target code as the same single-qubit error. For real encoders, the distance-three codes are the solutions of a single quadratic equation, and on thirteen qubits, they form an explicit circle through two Clifford codes and an icosahedral code. We construct the switching unitary along this circle as a collective operator, leaving its synthesis into physical control open, and verify that its recovery infidelity per fault is at machine precision, whereas two natural alternatives leave average infidelities of $0.13$ and $0.85$. A survey over odd qubit numbers up to $29$ lists where such switches exist.

</details>


### [158] [Universal and rigorous asymptotic convergence of standard macroscopic quantum electrodynamics to the modified Langevin noise formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05202)
*A. Ciattoni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明朗之万噪声形式化(LNF)的虚构真空吸收极限过程具有普适有效性：通过构造MLNF态与可观测量到LNF的映射，严格证明虚构吸收趋于零时LNF矩阵元精确收敛到MLNF对应量，从而涵盖任意量子散射过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LNF是无穷大有损介质宏观量子电动力学的成熟方法，但应用于有限物体时需给真空赋予随极限渐近消失的虚构吸收。已有对该奇异极限的论证主要局限于真空涨落与介质诱导相互作用（Casimir-Polder力、热辐射、量子发射体动力学等），严格排除了涉及外量子辐射散射的情形。因此需要证明该极限过程在包括任意量子散射在内的所有情形下普遍成立。

Method: 引入合适映射，将任意MLNF态与可观测量对应到添加虚构真空吸收的LNF中的实体；在此基础上严格证明当虚构吸收趋于零时，由该映射构造的每个LNF矩阵元精确收敛于其MLNF原像。

Result: 证明了LNF极限过程普遍有效：由映射构造的LNF矩阵元在虚构吸收趋于零时精确收敛于相应MLNF矩阵元，因此该极限过程确立了LNF对MLNF的渐近收敛，覆盖所有物理预言，包括任意量子散射过程。

Conclusion: LNF的虚构真空吸收极限对任意宏观系统与电磁过程（含外量子辐射散射）均成立；MLNF是无需极限过程的完整正则量子理论，对无界介质精确退化为LNF，而LNF通过该极限等价于MLNF。

Abstract: The standard Langevin noise formalism (LNF) is a well-established approach for macroscopic quantum electrodynamics in unbounded lossy media, and accordingly, its application to finite objects requires endowing the surrounding vacuum with a fictitious absorption that is taken to asymptotically vanish. Existing justifications for such a singular limiting procedure have been mainly restricted to phenomena governed by vacuum fluctuations and medium-induced interactions (Casimir-Polder forces, thermal radiation, quantum emitter dynamics, etc.), thereby strictly excluding scenarios that involve the scattering of external quantum radiation. Conversely, the modified Langevin noise formalism (MLNF) has recently been established as the complete canonical quantum theory for arbitrary macroscopic systems and electromagnetic processes, entirely bypassing the need for limiting procedures, treating incident quantum radiation fields as independent degrees of freedom, and exactly reducing to the LNF for unbounded media. In this work, we demonstrate that the LNF limiting procedure is universally valid by showing that it recovers any physical prediction of the MLNF across all regimes, including arbitrary quantum scattering processes. Specifically, we introduce a suitable mapping associating any MLNF state and observable with a corresponding entity within the LNF augmented by fictitious vacuum absorption.
