We then rigorously prove that, as the fictitious absorption tends to zero, every LNF matrix element constructed via this mapping exactly converges to its MLNF original, so that the limiting procedure establishes the asymptotic convergence of the LNF to the MLNF.

</details>


### [159] [Theory of the Edge-of-Chaos Advantage in Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05213)
*Shuaifan Cao,Wei Xia,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构建了可解析的量子储备池模型，严格分析其混沌区与可积点，揭示了"混沌边缘最优"的理论机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值研究普遍显示量子储备池计算在混沌边缘表现最优，但这一现象缺乏严格的理论解释，其背后记忆保持与非线性信息处理之间的权衡机制尚不明确。

Method: 构建显式量子储备池模型，对其混沌区与可积点分别进行严格分析。混沌区借助高斯幺正系综统计刻画记忆衰减；可积点以自由费米子模型描述，考察振幅编码与费米子两点关联的线性读出能力。

Result: 混沌区中高斯幺正系综统计导致记忆快速丢失，学习性能随任务所需存储时间指数衰减，限制了长程时间依赖的学习；可积点处证明振幅编码加两点关联线性读出无法学习一类耦合当前与历史输入的非线性任务，而引入相互作用可缓解该障碍。

Conclusion: 研究分别识别了混沌区的记忆保持局限与可积点的非线性处理局限，为混沌边缘优势提供了理论基础，并为面向时间学习的量子储备池设计提供指导。

Abstract: Numerical studies suggest that quantum reservoir computing performs optimally near the edge of chaos, a behavior commonly attributed to a balance between memory retention and nonlinear information processing. Here we develop an explicit quantum reservoir model and rigorously analyze its chaotic regime and integrable point. In the chaotic regime, we show that Gaussian-unitary-ensemble statistics lead to rapid memory loss: learning performance decays exponentially with the storage time required by a temporal task. This decay limits the ability of chaotic reservoirs to learn long-range temporal dependencies. At the integrable point, described by a free-fermion model, we prove that amplitude encoding and a linear readout of fermionic two-point correlations cannot learn a representative class of nonlinear tasks coupling present and past inputs. Introducing interactions alleviates this obstruction. By identifying distinct limitations on memory retention in the chaotic regime and nonlinear processing at the integrable point, our analysis provides a theoretical basis for the observed edge-of-chaos advantage and guides the design of quantum reservoirs for temporal learning.

</details>


### [160] [Efficient Fidelity Estimation of General Quantum Resource States via Clifford Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05222)
*Shunta Takahashi,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Bell-相干保真度估计（BCFE），仅用Clifford门和Pauli测量即可对任意纯态目标实现最优样本复杂度Θ(1/ε)，并在STAR架构表面码仿真中验证其资源效率显著优于DFE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保真度估计是评估量子资源态的基础工具。现有方法中，层析成像与直接保真度估计（DFE）在固定相对精度与置信度下最坏情况样本复杂度为O(1/ε²)；直接目标投影可达O(1/ε)，但通常需要非Clifford操作，其可靠性依赖于本身正被基准测试的资源态，形成循环依赖。因此需要一种既达到最优样本复杂度、又仅依赖Clifford门与Pauli测量的保真度估计方法。

Method: 提出Bell-相干保真度估计（BCFE）：仅使用Clifford门和Pauli测量，通过对单拷贝相干部分的统计误差进行显式控制，实现对任意纯态（可多比特）目标的保真度估计。并将BCFE应用于STAR（Space-Time Efficient Analog Rotations）架构中的模拟旋转资源态，在表面码下进行含电路级噪声的仿真评估。

Result: BCFE达到最优样本复杂度Θ(1/ε)。在表面码仿真中，对于相同的保真度估计标准误差，BCFE所需资源态数量比DFE最多减少约27倍；尽管额外的Bell测量操作引入电路级噪声，其估计值仍接近参考值。

Conclusion: BCFE为任意纯态目标提供了仅需Clifford门与Pauli测量的最优样本复杂度保真度估计方案，并在实际容错量子计算架构（STAR、表面码）中展示了显著的资源节约与噪声鲁棒性，支持在容错量子计算框架内实现高效的资源基准测试。

Abstract: Fidelity estimation is a fundamental tool for assessing quantum resource states across quantum information science. For small infidelity $ε$, tomography and direct fidelity estimation (DFE) have worst-case sample complexity $O(1/ε^2)$ at fixed relative accuracy and confidence. Direct target projections achieve $O(1/ε)$ but generally require non-Clifford operations whose reliability depends on the resource states that are typically the target of benchmarking. Here, we introduce the Bell--coherence fidelity estimation (BCFE) with optimal sample complexity $Θ(1/ε)$ using only Clifford gates and Pauli measurements. We prove that BCFE is applicable to arbitrary pure targets, possibly multi-qubit, with the statistical error of the single-copy coherence part explicitly controlled. To evaluate BCFE in a practical setting, we apply BCFE to analog rotation resource states in the candidate early-FTQC architecture called the Space-Time Efficient Analog Rotations (STAR) architecture. In the simulations using surface codes, BCFE is estimated to require up to approximately $27$ times fewer resource states than DFE for the same standard error in the infidelity estimate, while its estimates remain close to reference values despite circuit-level noise in the additional Bell measurement operations. These results support efficient resource benchmarking within fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>


### [161] [Complete Classification of Reversible Entanglement Transformations of Two Qutrits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05234)
*Wei Song,Xiao-Lan Zong,Ming Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对两-qutrit态之间的可逆LOCC转换给出了完整分类：两纠缠态可逆互换当且仅当它们的最小保留块在局部酉等价意义下相同，并给出基于部分转置负本征空间与至多三次矩阵零检验的构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Alice与Bob在LOCC下将一个共享混合态转换为另一个、且能完全恢复原态这一可逆转换问题：在此类可逆转换中究竟哪些量可以改变，此前缺乏完整刻画。

Method: 考虑单拷贝的精确确定性LOCC变换，允许有限协议及本文规定的可数协议；利用local flags将可分部分替换为局部制备而保留纠缠块的思想，证明互转两纠缠态（非局部酉等价）必具此分解，并借助部分转置的负本征空间与矩阵零检验确定最小保留块。

Result: 给出两-qutrit态可逆转换的完整分类：两态可逆互换当且仅当其未归一化的最小保留块局部酉等价；两部分可由局部测量区分，纠缠块权重相同且归一化后局部酉等价，每个纠缠态有唯一最小保留块；对负部分转置态，最小块可由其部分转置的负本征空间及至多三次矩阵零检验求得。

Conclusion: 可逆LOCC转换中不变的是归一化纠缠块（局部酉等价），而可分部分可通过local flags被局部制备替换；最小保留块是刻画两-qutrit可逆转换的完备不变量。

Abstract: Suppose Alice and Bob can transform one shared mixed state into another by local operations and classical communication, and can also recover the first state from the second. What can change in such a conversion? We give a complete classification of reversible conversions between two-qutrit states. We consider exact deterministic LOCC transformations of a single copy, allowing finite protocols and the countable protocols specified here. Local flags are known to allow a separable part to be replaced by local preparation while an entangled block is retained. We prove that such a decomposition is necessary for two mutually convertible entangled states that are not locally unitarily equivalent. The two parts are distinguishable by local measurements. The entangled blocks have the same weight and are locally unitarily equivalent after normalization. Each entangled state has a unique smallest retained block. Two entangled states are reversibly convertible if and only if these unnormalized blocks are locally unitarily equivalent. For a state with negative partial transpose, the smallest block can be found using the negative eigenspace of its partial transpose and at most three matrix zero tests.

</details>


### [162] [Origin and classification of fractional dynamical topology in non-Hermitian quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05262)
*Zimiao Zhang,Li Chen,Weilun Jiang,Heng Shen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非厄米两带模型中的动力学量子相变（DQPT），揭示动力学拓扑序参量的半整数跳变源于复能谱的不连续性，并与淬火哈密顿量的稳态相图内在关联，同时给出DQPT的分类，并推广至多带与二维模型，形成分数化动力学拓扑信号的统一理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学量子相变为刻画非平衡临界现象提供了基本框架，近来被推广到非厄米系统。半整数跳变的动力学拓扑序参量在厄米与非厄米系统中均可出现，但其在非厄米情形下的起源仍不清楚，亟需厘清其物理机制。

Method: 以两带非厄米模型为对象研究DQPT，分析复能谱的不连续性；考察其与淬火哈密顿量稳态相图的内在联系；对DQPT的发生进行分类；并将分析推广到多带哈密顿量与二维模型。

Result: 证明半整数（分数）跳变源自复能谱的不连续性；表明该行为与淬火哈密顿量的稳态相图内在关联；给出DQPT发生的分类；在多带与二维模型中同样获得一致结论，从而统一解释了分数化动力学拓扑信号。

Conclusion: 非厄米系统中动力学拓扑序参量的分数跳变可由复能谱不连续性解释，并与稳态相图相联系，该框架可推广至多带与高维情形，为分数化动力学拓扑现象提供统一理解。

Abstract: Dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPT) provide a fundamental framework for characterizing nonequilibrium critical phenomena, and have recently been extended to non-Hermitian systems. Half-integer jumps of the dynamical topological order parameter can arise in both Hermitian and non-Hermitian systems, yet their origin in non-Hermitian settings remains unclear. Here, we investigate DQPTs in a two-band non-Hermitian model and show that these fractional jumps originate from the discontinuity in the complex energy spectrum. We demonstrate that this behavior is intrinsically linked to the steady-state phase diagram of the quench Hamiltonian, and also classify the occurrence of DQPT. We further extend our analysis to the multiband Hamiltonian and the two-dimensional model, providing a unified understanding of fractional dynamical topological signatures.

</details>


### [163] [Quantum n-coloring is undecidable for every n $\ge$ 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05280)
*Christian Bo Kidmose-Frederiksen,Alfred Leth Nielsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子n-着色问题的不可判定性从n=3推广到所有n≥3，通过一个基本归约实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已知量子n-着色问题仅对n=3不可判定，作者希望将这一不可判定性结果推广到所有n≥3的情形。

Method: 构造归约：令G'=(G□K_3)∨K_{n-3}（笛卡尔积与连接运算），证明G是量子3-可着色的当且仅当G'是量子n-可着色的。

Result: 证明了G量子3-可着色与G'量子n-可着色等价，从而将量子n-着色的不可判定性从n=3扩展到所有n≥3。

Conclusion: 量子n-着色问题对所有n≥3均不可判定。

Abstract: The quantum $n$-coloring problem, given graph $G$, asks whether there exists a perfect quantum strategy for the $n$-coloring game of $G$. Previously it was known that quantum $n$-coloring is undecidable only for $n=3$. We extend this to all $n\geq 3$ with one elementary reduction. Letting $G'=(G\square K_3)\lor K_{n-3}$, we show that $G$ is quantum $3$-colorable if and only if $G'$ is quantum $n$-colorable.

</details>


### [164] [Fast Random Compilers for Time-Dependent and Time-Independent Lindbladian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05288)
*Youngjin Seo,Dhrumil Patel,Hyukjoon Kwon,Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作为Lindbladian动力学模拟开发了一阶与二阶随机采样算法，涵盖时间无关与时间依赖两种情形，其中二阶构造达到ε^{-1/2}精度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qDRIFT型随机量子模拟已扩展至Lindbladian动力学，但高阶随机编译器主要针对Hamiltonian模拟。本文旨在将高阶随机编译推广至Lindbladian模拟，以改进精度依赖。

Method: 基于一阶qDRIFT型Lindbladian模拟，为具有有限局部分解的时间无关Lindbladian引入qSWIFT型二阶修正；并为一般时间依赖Lindbladian构造一阶和二阶连续时间采样算法，进一步扩展至局部Hamiltonian与耗散生成元的线性组合输入模型，使随机采样器可由局部演化而非完整Lindbladian表述。

Result: 对λt≥1（λ为Hamiltonian系数与耗散率之和），二阶近似以r=O(λ²t²/√ε)个时间片达到归一化diamond范数误差至多ε，改进了qDRIFT型一阶近似的ε^{-1}精度依赖；时间依赖情形的二阶构造在时间片数上同样达到ε^{-1/2}的目标精度依赖。

Conclusion: 二阶修正映射无需为CPTP，因此用于估计可观测量期望值而非确定性制备输出态。该工作将高阶随机编译成功推广至Lindbladian模拟并显著改善精度依赖。

Abstract: Randomized product formulas provide simple and flexible methods for quantum simulation. While qDRIFT-type randomized simulation has been extended to Lindbladian dynamics, higher-order randomized compilers have primarily been developed for Hamiltonian simulation. In this work, we develop first- and second-order randomized sampling algorithms for Lindbladian simulation in both the time-independent and time-dependent settings. Building on first-order qDRIFT-type Lindbladian simulation, we introduce a qSWIFT-type second-order correction for time-independent Lindbladians with a finite local decomposition. For $λt \geq 1$, where $λ$ is the sum of the Hamiltonian coefficients and dissipative rates, the resulting approximation achieves normalized diamond-norm error at most $\varepsilon$ using $r=O(λ^2 t^2/\sqrt{\varepsilon})$ time slices, improving the $\varepsilon^{-1}$ precision dependence of the first-order qDRIFT-type approximation. We then develop first- and second-order continuous-time sampling algorithms for general time-dependent Lindbladians, with the second-order construction likewise achieving an $\varepsilon^{-1/2}$ dependence on the target precision in the number of time slices. We further extend the time-dependent construction to a linear-combination input model over local Hamiltonian and dissipative generators, allowing the randomized sampler to be formulated in terms of local evolutions rather than the full time-dependent Lindbladian. Because the second-order corrected maps need not be CPTP, we use them to estimate observable expectation values rather than to deterministically prepare output states.

</details>


### [165] [Demonstration of a Structured Agentic Workflow for Applied Quantum Computing Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05304)
*Dikshant Dulal,Maxence Grandadam,Maciej Koch-Janusz,Mykola Maksymenko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子智能体操作系统（QAOS），以非线性工作流图组织异构多智能体节点，控制上下文与算力流动，将复杂量子研发项目分解为受项目图、方法学与验证步骤约束的沙箱化任务；在质子转移量子化学原型任务上验证，QAOS 能正确构建最小模型并实现 SQD、FCI、DFT 求解，而基线智能体（Claude Sonnet 5 + Claude Code）会静默忽略显式指令且运行间波动大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件进步使量子计算应用于科学问题成为可能，但应用量子计算研究横跨领域知识、算法、理论物理、器件物理及量子与经典计算方法，所需专业知识的广度与项目固有复杂度对单个研究者乃至通用AI系统都构成挑战，需要结构化、可控的智能体研究系统来支撑此类研发项目。

Method: QAOS 围绕异构多智能体节点构成的非线性工作流图构建，对上下文与计算资源进行受控流动；将复杂研究项目分解为沙箱化的多智能体任务，执行时受项目图约束、任务相关方法学指导以及必要的验证步骤制约。在量子化学原型任务中，要求其为质子转移问题建立最小模型，并用基于采样的量子对角化（SQD）实现求解，同时配合 FCI（精确对角化）与密度泛函理论（DFT）等经典参考方法。结果与基线智能体（Claude Sonnet 5 搭配 Claude Code 框架）的求解方案进行对比。

Result: 在质子转移量子化学原型任务中，QAOS 完成了最小模型搭建及 SQD、FCI、DFT 求解方案。与基线智能体相比，基线会静默忽略显式指令，且多次运行结果差异很大；QAOS 则表现出更好的指令遵循性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 结构化、图约束、带验证机制的异构多智能体系统（QAOS）能有效应对量子与计算研发项目的复杂性，在指令遵循和结果一致性上优于通用基线智能体，为量子计算应用研发提供了可用的智能体化研究范式。

Abstract: Quantum hardware advances now make it practical to explore quantum approaches to scientific problems. Applied quantum computing research sits at the intersection of domain knowledge, algorithms, theoretical physics, device physics, and quantum and classical computational methods. The breadth of expertise required and the inherent project complexity challenge not only any individual researcher, but also generic AI systems. Here, we introduce the Quantum Agentic Operating System (QAOS), a structured agentic research system designed to address this challenge for quantum and computational R&D projects. QAOS is built around a nonlinear workflow graph of heterogeneous multi-agent nodes, with a controlled flow of context and computational resources. It decomposes a complex research project into sandboxed multi-agent tasks executed under constraints from the project graph, task-relevant methodologies, and necessary verification steps. We demonstrate QAOS on a quantum chemistry prototyping task, in which it is asked to set up a minimal model for a proton-transfer problem and to implement its solution using sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD), together with classical reference methods such as FCI (exact diagonalization) and density functional theory (DFT). We compare the QAOS solution to those obtained with baseline agents (Claude Sonnet 5 with the Claude Code harness). In contrast to QAOS, the baseline agents silently ignore explicit instructions, and the runs show large variability.

</details>


### [166] [Lifted surgery for non-Abelian two-block group-algebra codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05307)
*Tushar Pandey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将"提升手术"(lifted surgery)从阿贝尔群代数码推广到非阿贝尔群的两块群代数码，刻画其块仿射自同构并证明非交换性带来的测量增益至多等于最大阿贝尔子群的指数；构造出增益为2（距离至13）与增益为3（至[[336,26,12]]）的具体码，并通过合并距离引理与电路级仿真验证非阿贝尔gadget在可靠性不劣的前提下将测量轮数减少2–3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 码手术是量子LDPC码中测量逻辑算子的常用手段，但代价为O(d)轮综合征提取。提升手术利用平移对称性，在一个合并码中一次性测量逻辑算子轨道，从而摊薄该代价。然而此前的提升手术仅适用于阿贝尔群代数码。本文的动机是追问：若把提升手术推广到非阿贝尔群的两块群代数码，非交换性能否使一个合并码测量到比其任一阿贝尔对称群更多的逻辑算子，从而获得额外增益。

Method: 1) 将提升手术推广至非阿贝尔群上的两块群代数码；2) 对该类码的块仿射自同构(block-affine automorphisms)进行完整分类，并以全自同构群作为衡量标准重新评估增益；3) 证明增益上界为最大阿贝尔子群在群中的指数；4) 给出"合并距离引理"(merged-distance lemma)，用以判定gadget能否保持码距；5) 构造具体码例并做电路级仿真，采用Relay-BP译码比较非阿贝尔与阿贝尔gadget的可靠性及所需轮数。

Result: 在完整块仿射自同构群的衡量下，多数表面上的非阿贝尔增益消失；增益被证明至多等于最大阿贝尔子群的指数。构造出精确距离达13、增益为2的码，其中包括一个合并码即可读出全部逻辑量子比特的实例；并给出A_4、S_4、SL(2,3)乘积群上最优逻辑增益为3的码，最大达到[[336,26,12]]。合并距离引理给出了保持码距的简明条件。电路级仿真中，在可分辨的错误率范围内，非阿贝尔gadget的可靠性与所替代的阿贝尔gadget相当或更优（在统计误差内），同时所需综合征提取轮数减少2–3倍。

Conclusion: 非交换性本身并不能带来超越阿贝尔子群指数上界的测量增益：在以完整块仿射自同构群衡量时，非阿贝尔情形下的增益受限于最大阿贝尔子群的指数，多数表观增益随之消失。尽管如此，在可达的增益范围内，非阿贝尔提升手术在保持甚至改善可靠性的同时显著降低测量轮数，是一种实用的开销优化手段；而合并距离引理为判断码距是否保持提供了便捷判据。

Abstract: Code surgery measures a logical operator of a quantum LDPC code with $O(d)$ rounds of syndrome extraction. Lifted surgery amortises this cost on Abelian group-algebra codes by measuring an orbit of logical operators under a translation symmetry in one merged code. We extend it to two-block group-algebra codes over non-Abelian groups and ask whether non-commutativity lets one merged code measure more operators than any Abelian group of its symmetries. We classify the block-affine automorphisms of these codes and find that, measured against this full group, most apparent non-Abelian gains disappear. We prove that the gain is at most the index of a largest Abelian subgroup. We find codes with exact distance up to $13$ where the gain is two, including codes in which one merged code reads out every logical qubit, and codes over products of $A_4$, $S_4$ and SL(2,3) with gain three for a best logical, up to $[[336,26,12]]$. A merged-distance lemma gives a simple condition for the gadget to preserve the code distance. In circuit-level simulations with Relay-BP decoding, at the error rates we can resolve, the non-Abelian gadget is as reliable as, or more reliable than, the Abelian gadgets it replaces within statistical error, while using two to three times fewer rounds.

</details>


### [167] [Shallow Quantum Circuits for Geometrically Local Lindblad Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05362)
*Zheyu Shen,Yusen Wu,Xiao Yuan,Yukun Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了在常数维格点上模拟时间无关、有限范围 Lindblad 动力学的量子算法，深度为 O(T·polylog(nT/ε))，门数为 O(nT·polylog(nT/ε))；核心创新是通过共享酉扩张保留环境信息，使 HHKL 空间分解适用于开放系统，并证明了环境态的指数占据界。同时给出了Ω(nT)门数、Ω(T)深度以及Ω(log n/log log n)深度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放系统动力学支配量子多体系统中的退相干、输运和工程耗散，但已有的 Lieb-Robinson/HHKL 型空间分解和高效量子模拟结果主要针对酉动力学。将这类高效模拟扩展到非酉的 Lindblad 耗散通道存在本质困难：酉动力学中使用的可逆重叠修正对耗散通道没有直接对应物，因为信息会不可逆地丢失到环境中。

Method: 针对 n 个格点、常数维、有限范围、有界局域强度、允许非对易跳跃算符的时间无关 Lindblad 动力学，将环境保留在共享酉扩张中，使从系统丢失的信息保持相干可用，从而把空间修正变为可逆操作。证明分解所生成环境态的指数占据界，以实现紧凑表示和对局域因子的相干模拟。每次短时演化被归约为对数直径区域上常数个并行层，使用最近邻门和多对数辅助密度。下界方面，构造时间无关、纯耗散的量子信息输运过程，针对具有局域量子与经典通信的线路证明门数和深度下界。

Result: 上界：对 T≥1、金刚石范数误差≤ε，算法深度 O(T·polylog(nT/ε))，门数 O(nT·polylog(nT/ε))。下界：对局域量子与经典通信线路，期望门数 Ω(nT)，深度 Ω(T)，在时间尺度不超过格点直径时成立，与上界在时间和体积的主导依赖上仅差多对数因子；固定时间且误差足够小时，深度下界为 Ω(log n/log log n)，表明随格点增大，达到全局精度可能需要增加深度。

Conclusion: 该工作将 HHKL 空间分解推广到开放量子系统，给出了有限范围 Lindblad 动力学的高效量子模拟算法，并配以匹配的（近似）无条件最坏情况下界，说明其时间与体积依赖在主导阶上是最优的；同时揭示了固定时间下全局精度对深度的对数增长需求。

Abstract: Open-system dynamics govern decoherence, transport, and engineered dissipation in quantum many-body systems. We propose a quantum algorithm for simulating time-independent, finite-range Lindblad dynamics with bounded local strength on a constant-dimensional lattice of $n$ sites, allowing noncommuting jump operators. For time $T\ge1$ and diamond-norm error at most $ε$, it has depth $O(T\operatorname{polylog}(nT/ε))$ and gate count $O(nT\operatorname{polylog}(nT/ε))$. These results extend the HHKL spatial decomposition to open systems. The main obstacle is that the reversible overlap corrections used for unitary dynamics have no direct analogue for dissipative channels. We overcome this by retaining the environment in a shared unitary dilation, keeping information lost from the system coherently available and making the spatial corrections reversible. We prove exponential occupation bounds for the environmental states generated by the decomposition, allowing compact representation and coherent simulation of local factors on the inputs that arise. The construction reduces each short-time evolution to a constant number of parallel layers on regions of logarithmic diameter, using nearest-neighbour gates and polylogarithmic auxiliary density.
  We also prove unconditional worst-case lower bounds using time-independent, purely dissipative processes that transport quantum information. For circuits with local quantum and classical communication, the lower bounds are $Ω(nT)$ for the expected gate count and $Ω(T)$ for depth on time scales up to the lattice diameter. They match the upper bounds' leading time and volume dependence up to polylogarithmic factors. At fixed time and sufficiently small fixed error, we further prove a depth lower bound of $Ω(\log n/\log\log n)$, showing that global accuracy can require increasing depth as the lattice grows.

</details>


### [168] [Minimax Quantum State Tomography in a Known Trace-Distance Ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05374)
*Yonglong Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究未知量子态位于已知参考态 $\rho$ 的迹距离球（半径 $R$）内时的量子态层析问题。对任意集体测量，作者确定了极小极大期望风险和置信半径（在通用常数意义下），并给出拷贝复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子态层析中，当先验信息表明未知态接近某个已知参考态时，可利用该结构降低样本复杂度。本文旨在刻画这种局部（迹距离球内）层析问题的极小极大最优风险与样本复杂度，且结果需对维度、中心谱、半径和样本量一致成立。

Method: 下界方面，采用加权迹范数见证（weighted trace-norm witness），在平方根扰动族中保留所有谱尺度。上界方面，对一种改进的现有基于纯化的过程（purification-based procedure）进行直接的谱分析与集中分析。

Result: 记 $\Gamma = d(\mathrm{Tr}\sqrt{\rho})^2 + d^2 R$。对 $d\ge2$、$0<R\le1$、$n\ge1$ 个副本，期望风险为 $\Theta(\min\{R,\sqrt{\Gamma/n}\})$。在失败概率 $0<\delta\le1/3$ 下，极小极大置信半径为 $\Theta(\min\{R,\sqrt{(\Gamma+\log(1/\delta))/n}\})$。当 $\varepsilon\le c_0 R$（$c_0>0$ 为通用常数）时，拷贝复杂度为 $\Theta((\Gamma+\log(1/\delta))/\varepsilon^2)$。

Conclusion: 论文完整刻画了迹距离球内量子态层析的极小极大最优风险、置信半径与拷贝复杂度，结果对维度、中心谱、半径和样本量一致，且上下界在通用常数意义下匹配。

Abstract: We study quantum state tomography when the unknown state lies in a trace-distance ball of radius $R$ around a known reference state $ρ$. For arbitrary collective measurements, we determine the minimax expected risk and confidence radius up to universal constants, uniformly over the dimension, center spectrum, radius, and sample size. Write $Γ=d(\mathrm{Tr}\sqrtρ)^2+d^2R$. For $d\ge2$, $0<R\le1$, and $n\ge1$ copies, the expected risk is $Θ(\min\{R,\sqrt{Γ/n}\})$. At failure probability $0<δ\le1/3$, the minimax confidence radius is \[
  Θ\!\left(\min\left\{R,\sqrt{\frac{Γ+\log(1/δ)}{n}}\right\}\right). \] For $\varepsilon\le c_0R$ with a universal $c_0>0$, the copy complexity is $Θ((Γ+\log(1/δ))/\varepsilon^2)$. For the lower bound we use a weighted trace-norm witness to retain all spectral scales in a square-root perturbation family. For the upper bound, we give a direct spectral and concentration analysis of a modified existing purification-based procedure.

</details>


### [169] [On State Distinguishing Without Inverse Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05389)
*Emma Wang,Joseph Carolan,Andrew M. Childs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究仅正向访问（无逆操作）时量子态区分问题的查询复杂度：即使两个态均已知，也需 $\Omega(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 次正向查询；但在连续时间变体中，用量子 Zeno 动力学可仅用 $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$ 总正向演化时间完成区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态区分在标准模型下需 $\Theta(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 份拷贝，而拥有酉态制备 oracle 及其逆可提升至 $\Theta(\varepsilon^{-1})$ 次查询。现实中 oracle 可能是不可逆的物理过程，因此研究仅有正向访问的中间情形具有重要意义。Tang 和 Wright 已证明已知态与未知接近态的区分需 $\Omega(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 次正向查询，本文旨在加强该下界并探索连续时间变体中是否能保持二次优势。

Method: 下界方面，采用直接的 hybrid 论证，避免使用此前工作中的高级 compressed oracle 技术，证明即使两个态均已知，区分它们仍需 $\Omega(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 次正向查询。上界方面，针对连续时间变体的态区分问题，利用量子 Zeno 动力学将演化限制在已知的二维子空间内，设计出维度无关的算法。

Result: 证明了即使两个量子态均已知，仅正向访问时区分它们仍需要 $\Omega(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 次正向查询，加强了 Tang 和 Wright 的结果；同时给出了一个维度无关的算法，在连续时间变体的态区分问题中仅需 $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$ 的总正向演化时间。

Conclusion: 对于标准态区分问题，无逆访问的正向查询无法获得二次加速；但对于自然的连续时间类比问题，量子 Zeno 动力学使得二次优势在仅有正向访问时依然成立。

Abstract: In the quantum state distinguishing problem, an algorithm is given copies of a state $|ψ\rangle$ or $|φ\rangle$ separated by trace distance $\varepsilon$, and tasked with deciding which is the case. It is a standard result that $Θ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ copies are required, while having access to a unitary state preparation oracle and its inverse improves this to $Θ(\varepsilon^{-1})$ queries. We study the intermediate case with forward-only access, capturing scenarios where the oracle is a physical process that cannot be reversed. Tang and Wright recently showed that distinguishing a known state from an \emph{unknown} close state requires $Ω(\varepsilon^{-2})$ forward queries in high dimension; we strengthen this result by proving the same bound even when both states are known, as in the state distinguishing setting. Moreover, our lower bound uses a direct hybrid argument, avoiding advanced compressed oracle techniques used in the prior work. In contrast, we give a dimension-independent algorithm solving a continuous-time variant of state distinguishing with only $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$ total forward evolution time. The algorithm uses quantum Zeno dynamics to confine evolution to a known two-dimensional subspace. Thus, a quadratic advantage survives without inverse access for a natural continuous-time analogue of state distinguishing.

</details>


### [170] [Performance Analysis of Multiparty GHZ-State Quantum Conference Key Agreement over Optical Fiber Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05448)
*Dipanjan Bera,Ramniwas Meena,Neel Kanth Kundu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于GHZ态和N-BB84协议的量子会议密钥协商（QCKA）系统在有限密钥下的性能，将双体擦除-泡利信道扩展以纳入探测器暗计数，推导了误码率闭式表达式，并通过优化参数估计概率提升了密钥率与最大安全距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GHZ态是量子会议密钥协商（QCKA）的天然资源，但其在光纤中传输时会因传输损耗、偏振退相干和探测器缺陷而退化。现有研究对N方GHZ-based QCKA在有限密钥条件下的性能刻画不足，尤其是将探测器暗计数纳入信道模型后的误码率特性及参数优化问题尚待深入。

Method: 采用N-BB84协议研究N方GHZ-based QCKA系统的有限密钥性能，将双体擦除-泡利信道扩展以纳入探测器暗计数来建模每条光链路，刻画该量子信道对共享GHZ态的影响并推导误码率的闭式表达式；随后将这些误码率纳入有限密钥密钥率框架，采用Brent无导数法优化参数估计概率。

Result: 解析结果表明，局部比特误码率仅依赖于透射率和暗计数，而全局相位误码率在全部N-1条链路上累积，因此随距离和参与方数量增加而退化更快。数值结果显示，光纤距离和网络规模增大均降低可达密钥率；相比固定分配基线，优化后的参数估计概率一致地提升密钥率并延长最大安全距离，与渐近极限的比较量化了残余的有限密钥惩罚。

Conclusion: 该工作为N方GHZ-based QCKA提供了包含探测器暗计数的有限密钥性能分析框架，揭示了全局相位误码率随距离与用户数的复合退化特性，并证明通过优化参数估计概率可有效提升密钥率与安全距离，为实际量子会议网络的参数设计提供指导。

Abstract: Quantum conference key agreement (QCKA) enables multiple spatially separated users to establish a common key with composable information-theoretic security by exploiting multipartite quantum correlations. Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) states are a natural resource for this task, but their distribution over optical fiber is degraded by transmission loss, polarization decoherence, and detector imperfections. We investigate the finite-key performance of an $N$-party GHZ-based QCKA system using the $N$-BB84 protocol, where each optical link is modeled by extending the bipartite erasure--Pauli channel to incorporate detector dark counts. We characterize the impact of this quantum channel on the shared GHZ state and derive closed-form expressions for the error rates. The analytical results reveal that the local bit-error rate depends only on transmissivity and dark counts, while the global phase-error rate compounds over all $N-1$ links and therefore degrades faster with both distance and the number of parties. These error rates are integrated into a finite-key secret-key-rate (SKR) framework, in which we optimize the parameter-estimation probability via Brent's derivative-free method. Numerical results show that increasing fiber distance and network size reduce the achievable SKR. Furthermore, the optimized parameter-estimation probability consistently increases the SKR and extends the maximum secure distance relative to a fixed-allocation baseline, with comparison to the asymptotic limit quantifying the residual finite-key penalty.

</details>


### [171] [Game of Nons: Non-Gaussian Nonunitary Environments with Nonlinear Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05454)
*Artem Karasev,Alexander Teretenkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个微扰框架，用于处理与二次型玻色环境（二次哈密顿量、线性 Lindblad 算符）二次耦合的系统，适用于初始态为非高斯、非平稳的单模和多模环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖环境初始态为高斯或平稳的假设，难以处理非高斯、非平稳的环境，因此需要一种更一般性的微扰处理工具。

Method: 通过在倍增算符空间中的线性演化与特征函数表示相结合，将有序多时间环境关联函数用初始特征函数的导数来表达，并据此递归构造任意有限阶的时间无卷积（time-convolutionless）生成元。

Result: 以通过双光子相互作用耦合到阻尼模式的量子比特为例，初始 Fock 态与热态即使具有相同的一阶和二阶场矩，也会因四阶矩不同而产生不同的二阶生成元。

Conclusion: 该框架可将环境关联函数与微扰生成元统一处理，能够刻画非高斯初始态的高阶矩对系统动力学的影响，具有较强的普适性。

Abstract: We develop a perturbative framework for systems coupled quadratically to bosonic environments with quadratic Hamiltonians and linear Lindblad operators. The method applies to both single-mode and multimode environments whose initial states may be non-Gaussian and nonstationary. By combining linear evolution in a doubled operator space with a characteristic-function representation, we express ordered multitime environmental correlation functions in terms of derivatives of the initial characteristic function. These correlation functions enter a recursive construction of the time-convolutionless generator at arbitrary finite order. We illustrate the method with a qubit coupled to a damped mode via a two-photon interaction. Initial Fock and thermal states with identical first and second field moments produce different second-order generators through their fourth moments.

</details>


### [172] [Optimization Geometry of QAOA and Variational Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05524)
*Vojtěch Novák,Ivan Zelinka,Silvie Illésová,Swagatam Das,Martin Beseda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种可视化与刻画变分量子算法目标函数的方法，通过采样方向的局部极小值数量与局部搜索终点的质量差异两个性质，比较BFGS多起点与jSO，用于指导优化器选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法将哈密顿量、拟设和参数化选择转化为经典非凸优化问题，但目标函数的几何结构如何影响优化器行为尚不清晰，需要可解释的诊断方式来连接模型构造与优化器选择。

Method: 以VQE和QAOA为研究对象，区分目标函数的两个性质：沿采样方向遇到的局部极小值数量，以及局部搜索终点之间的质量差异。将BFGS作为局部优化器，jSO作为自适应差分进化代表，并在相同函数评估预算下允许BFGS多起点，形成更强的基线。考察挫败度、电路深度、参数绑定、混合局域性和非线性重参数化对目标函数的影响。

Result: 增加独立电路深度会提高采样局部极小值数量，但并未使全局搜索更有效；参数绑定则同时产生更多重复局部结构和更大的局部搜索终点质量差异，在此情形下自适应差分进化优于函数评估匹配的多起点BFGS。

Conclusion: 局部极小值数量本身并不能决定全局搜索是否有利，关键在于不同吸引域是否通向质量相近或差异显著的目标值。该结果提供了连接模型构建、目标函数几何、经验诊断与优化器选择的实用工作流。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms turn choices of Hamiltonian, ansatz, and parameterization into a classical nonconvex optimization problem. We study how this objective function can be visualized and characterized in ways that help explain optimizer behavior. We distinguish two properties of the objective: the number of local minima encountered along sampled directions and the differences in quality among local-search endpoints. We then ask how a local optimizer, represented by BFGS, compares with adaptive differential evolution, represented by jSO. Rather than comparing jSO with a single local run, we allow BFGS multiple starts within the same function-evaluation budget. This gives local search repeated opportunities to explore different basins and provides a stronger baseline for asking when a global evolutionary solver is useful. We study these questions using VQE and QAOA, focusing on how frustra- tion, circuit depth, parameter tying, mixed locality, and nonlinear repa- rameterization change the Hamiltonian expectation-value objective seen by the classical optimizer. Increasing independent circuit depth raises the sampled local-minimum count without making global search more effective. Parameter tying, by contrast, produces both more repeated local structure and much larger differences in quality among local-search endpoints, and in this regime adaptive differential evolution outperforms function-evaluation-matched multistart BFGS. The comparison shows that the number of local minima alone does not determine whether global search is advantageous: the important distinction is whether different basins lead to similarly good solutions or to substantially different objective values. These results provide a practical workflow for connecting model construction, objective- function geometry, empirical diagnostics, and optimizer choice.

</details>


### [173] [Optimal and Verifiable Quantum Advantages in Communication Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05585)
*Ryan Anselm,Michelle Ding,Dar Gilboa,Sabee Grewal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在通信复杂度上建立了搜索问题的最优量子-经典分离：提出"Pelagic Fourier Fishing"全搜索问题，量子单向n量子比特可解而随机双向需Ω(2^n)比特；并构造可多项式验证的变体及一族Clifford层级相关的分离，最后讨论近期实验应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子与经典在通信复杂度上的分离，尤其是搜索问题的分离，是理解量子计算优势的重要方向。作者希望建立最优的量子-经典通信分离，并使其具备可验证性以适配近期实验。

Method: 引入全搜索问题 Pelagic Fourier Fishing；给出可多项式时间验证的变体；构造一族高效可验证的全搜索问题，用量子协议中Alice以Clifford层级第d层单个酉变换制备消息、Bob进行Clifford测量，实现量子单向与随机单向通信的分离。

Result: Pelagic Fourier Fishing 存在n量子比特量子单向协议，而任意随机双向协议需Ω(2^n)比特；其可验证变体同样量子单向n量子比特，而随机单向需Ω(2^n)比特。还得到对每个固定d≥2，量子单向与随机单向之间n对Ω_d(n^d)的分离，且该分离在此消息限制下渐近最优；d=2时Alice消息为稳定子态、Bob测量为Clifford，无magic却达到最优二次量子优势。

Conclusion: 论文建立了搜索问题上的最优量子-经典通信分离，并说明这些分离可适配近期量子优势实验，使所展示的优势既无条件成立又可高效验证。

Abstract: We establish optimal quantum-classical separations in communication complexity for search problems. We introduce a total search problem called Pelagic Fourier Fishing and show that it admits an $n$-qubit quantum one-way protocol, whereas every randomized two-way protocol requires $Ω(2^n)$ bits of communication. We then introduce a variant of this problem whose solutions can be verified in polynomial time. This variant also admits an $n$-qubit quantum one-way protocol, while every randomized one-way protocol requires $Ω(2^n)$ bits of communication.
  We also construct a family of efficiently verifiable total search problems achieving an $n$ versus $Ω_d(n^d)$ separation between quantum one-way and randomized one-way communication for every fixed $d \ge 2$. In the quantum protocol, Alice prepares her message using a single unitary from the $d$th level of the Clifford hierarchy, and Bob performs a Clifford measurement. This separation is asymptotically optimal under this restriction on Alice's message. For $d = 2$, Alice's message is a stabilizer state and Bob's measurement is Clifford, so the protocol uses no magic, yet achieves an optimal quadratic quantum advantage.
  Finally, we discuss how these separations can be adapted to near-term quantum advantage experiments in which the demonstrated advantage is both unconditional and efficiently verifiable.

</details>


### [174] [Autonomous Quantum Memories from Classical Stochastic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05589)
*Nadav Drechsler,Yuval Oreg,Dorit Aharonov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了CSS型量子记忆在Pauli噪声下与经典记忆之间的一般等价性，并据此严格证明了4D环面码结合Toom规则在局部Pauli噪声下构成量子记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机来自两方面：一是容错量子计算需要能抵抗现实噪声的量子记忆；二是关于自主多体系统中量子记忆何时能够出现这一基本问题。

Method: 通过两个关联的概率过程，建立CSS型量子记忆在Pauli噪声下与经典记忆的等价性。异步情形下，针对由Lindbladian生成的量子动力学，识别出一类基于syndrome的Lindbladian，使量子记忆问题等价于连续时间经典马尔可夫动力学中的记忆问题；同步情形下，引入基于syndrome的概率元胞自动机（SPCA），建立其经典记忆与局部量子信道下CSS型量子记忆的一一对应。

Result: 作为应用，首次严格证明了在局部Pauli噪声下，采用Toom规则的4D环面码在误差低于常数阈值时构成量子记忆，并同时针对同步PCA型量子动力学和Floquet Lindbladian给出证明。更一般地，证明了SPCA中的经典记忆可在Floquet Lindbladian动力学下提升为量子记忆；能否在时不变Lindbladian下实现仍为开放问题。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了关联经典与量子记忆的一般框架，阐明了全自主量子记忆的条件，并有望指导低维可证自主量子记忆的构造。

Abstract: The study of quantum memories out of thermal equilibrium is motivated both by the need for memories that withstand realistic noise in fault-tolerant quantum computers and by the fundamental question of when quantum memory can emerge in autonomous many-body systems. We establish a general equivalence between CSS-based quantum memories subject to Pauli noise and classical memories via two associated probabilistic processes. In the asynchronous setting, where the quantum dynamics is generated by a Lindbladian, we identify a broad class of syndrome-based Lindbladians for which the quantum memory problem is equivalent to memory in continuous-time classical Markov dynamics. In the synchronous setting, we introduce syndrome-based probabilistic cellular automata (SPCAs) and establish a one-to-one correspondence between their classical memories and CSS-based quantum memories under local quantum channels. This framework provides a systematic route for deriving and constructing quantum memories from classical probabilistic processes, and recovers known results, including the fact that the 4D toric code is a quantum memory in the presence of thermal noise.
  As an application, we give the first rigorous proof that the 4D toric code with Toom's rule is a quantum memory in the presence of local Pauli noise, provided the error is below a constant threshold. We prove this both for the corresponding synchronous, PCA-type quantum dynamics and for a Floquet Lindbladian. More generally, we show that classical memory in an SPCA can be lifted to a quantum memory under Floquet Lindbladian dynamics; whether this can be done with a time-independent Lindbladian remains open. Our results provide a general framework for relating classical and quantum memory, shed light on the conditions for fully autonomous quantum memories, and may guide the construction of provable autonomous quantum memories in low dimensions.

</details>


### [175] [Erasure of entanglement-locked energy in multi-cell quantum batteries through decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05592)
*Haniyeh Hajizade,Vahid Ameri,Alidad Askari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子电池中由纠缠关联所"锁住"的能量（correlation ergotropy），通过对两个耦合三能级单元的充电轨迹分析，揭示了锁定能量由真正的纠缠而非相干集体动力学携带，并给出纯双单元电池的精确闭式表达及其在退相干、冷阻尼、热占据下的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过泵浦充电器为量子电池充电时，储存能量中有相当一部分被锁在组成单元之间的双体关联中，这部分"关联能"无法通过任何局部单单元操作提取，只能依靠集体操作回收。作者希望厘清这部分锁定能量的物理来源（是真正的纠缠还是单纯的相干集体动力学），并为其建立资源论层面的刻画。

Method: 跟踪两个耦合三能级单元的充电轨迹，将 correlation ergotropy 与对数负度（logarithmic negativity）进行对比；对纯双单元电池推导了精确、基无关的闭式表达式，该表达式仅依赖于约化单元的排序本征值和单元能级；并比较等强度相干非纠缠驱动、退相干与退相位、冷阻尼以及热占据对锁定效应的影响。

Result: correlation ergotropy 与对数负度紧密同步，表明锁定能量由真正的双体纠缠携带，而非仅由相干集体动力学产生；等强度的相干非纠缠驱动完全不会产生这种锁定。在纯双单元电池中，锁定能量具有精确的基无关闭式，仅由约化单元排序本征值与能级决定；锁定比例在 Schmidt 单纯形的可分离角点为零，在对称最大纠缠态处升至1，且在局域幺正变换下不变。退相干与退相位会破坏关联但并不释放能量而是将其抹除；冷阻尼不破坏该效应，热占据则抑制之。

Conclusion: 本研究为量子电池中的关联提供了资源论式的解读，厘清了真正纠缠与相干集体动力学之间的界限，并指出了关联充电与集体提取协议在实验上可实现的条件下，提取必须是集体的且须在关联存续期间完成。

Abstract: Charging a quantum battery through a pumped charger can lock a substantial part of its stored energy inside the bipartite correlations that grow between the constituent cells, and this \emph{correlation ergotropy}, being the energy that no local single-cell operation can reach and that only collective operations can recover, is the object of the present study. Following the charging trajectory of two coupled three-level cells, we find that the correlation ergotropy closely tracks the logarithmic negativity, which shows that the locked energy is carried by genuine bipartite entanglement rather than by coherent collective dynamics alone, and that an equal-strength coherent non-entangling drive produces no such locking at all. For a pure two-cell battery we establish an exact, basis-independent closed form in which the locked energy depends only on the sorted eigenvalues of the reduced cell and the cell energy levels, revealing that the locked fraction rises from zero at the separable corners of the Schmidt simplex to unity at the symmetric maximally entangled state and remains invariant under local unitaries. Decoherence and dephasing, which destroy the correlations, do \emph{not} release this energy but erase it, so extraction must be collective and must take place while the correlations persist, while cold damping leaves the effect intact and thermal occupation suppresses it. These results give a resource-theoretic reading of correlations in quantum batteries, sharpen the line between genuine entanglement and coherent collective dynamics, and point to experimentally accessible conditions for correlated-charge and collective-extraction protocols.

</details>


### [176] [A solvable quantum many-body problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05593)
*John Lekner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出以粒子间距平方和开方定义的集体坐标 R，构造指数型多体波函数 e^{-λR}，证明其恰为 N 粒子体系在 1/R 型势下薛定谔方程的解析解，并给出具有确定角动量、平移不变的转动本征态集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体薛定谔方程一般难以解析求解，尤其在相互作用形式非平凡的体系中。作者希望寻找一类可严格求解的 N 体模型：既保留粒子间相互作用（用集体坐标 R 描述），又具备平移不变性与良好对称性，从而为多体关联效应和转动性质提供精确可对照的基准解。

Method: 引入集体坐标 R = sqrt(Σ_{i<j} r_ij²)（粒子间距离平方和的开方）。取波函数形式为 exp(-λR)（指数依赖全系统集体变量），代入 N 粒子薛定谔方程并化简，验证当势能为 V ∝ 1/R 时方程成立。进一步按平移不变要求分离质心运动，并构造角动量算符的本征态组合。

Result: 该指数型波函数确为 1/R 势下 N 体薛定谔方程的精确解；并由其生成一组平移不变、角动量确定的转动本征态，对应可解析标记的角动量量子数。

Conclusion: 1/R 型集体势与 exp(-λR) 波函数构成一类可精确求解的 N 体模型，所得解具有平移不变性与明确角动量结构，可为多体关联、转动谱及近似方法检验提供解析基准。

Abstract: A many-body wavefunction exponential in R, where the square of R is the sum of the squares of the interparticle distances, is shown to be the solution of Schrodingers equation for a potential proportional to the reciprocal of R, for N particles. A set of translationally invariant rotational eigenstates of angular momentum is found.

</details>


### [177] [Frame-dependent coherence of Gaussian quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05623)
*Nicolae Cotfas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用所有正则相干态构成的紧框架替代Hermite-Gauss基来描述高斯态，导出了混合高斯态框架依赖相干性的显式公式，并构造了快速收敛的离散量子态序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量高斯态的相干性通常基于Hermite-Gauss基描述，但该描述较为复杂。作者希望利用更一般的紧框架（所有正则相干态）来简化高斯态的描述，并获得相干性的显式表达。

Method: 采用所有正则相干态构成的紧框架描述Gaussian态；推导混合高斯态框架依赖相干性关于协方差矩阵的行列式与迹的显式公式；构造离散变量量子态序列以逼近混合高斯态；离散情形下以所有离散相干态的紧框架度量相干性。

Result: 得到了混合高斯态框架依赖相干性的显式公式，表示为协方差矩阵行列式与迹的函数；对每个混合高斯态构造出随Hilbert空间维度增大（15及以上）在纯度与相干性上快速收敛的离散变量量子态序列。

Conclusion: 基于相干态紧框架的描述比Hermite-Gauss基更简洁，可实现高斯态相干性的显式刻画，并能用高维离散量子态高效逼近连续变量高斯态。

Abstract: A Hilbert space is usually described using an orthonormal basis, but a more general description can be defined using a tight frame. In the case of continuous-variable Gaussian states, the coherence is usually described using the Hermite-Gauss basis. We present an alternative description using the tight frame of all canonical coherent states. The description of Gaussian states in terms of coherent states is simpler than in terms of Hermite-Gauss basis, and this allows us to obtain an explicit formula describing the frame-dependent coherence of a mixed Gaussian state as a function of the determinant and the trace of covariance matrix. For each mixed Gaussian state, we construct a sequence of discrete-variable quantum states rapidly convergent to it (as concern the purity and coherence) when the dimension of Hilbert space becomes large enough (15 or more). In the discrete case, the coherence is measured with respect to the tight frame of all discrete coherent states.

</details>


### [178] [Ground state energy estimation and preparation by the truncated power method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05627)
*Savero Lukianto Chandra,Daochen Wang,Carolyn Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文识别出GLHP的一个新经典可解区域：当真实基态的ℓ1范数多项式有界时，截断幂方法（TPM）可以以与量子算法多项式相当的运行时间求解GLHP，从而实现去量子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GLHP在误差较小时是BQP完全的，误差较大时经典可解。作者希望找到另一类经典可解的机制，探索量子优势的边界。

Method: 分析经典算法——截断幂方法（TPM，Kirby等人提出）在GLHP上的运行时间，并将其与量子算法进行参数对比。

Result: TPM的运行时间在所有相关参数上与GLHP量子算法多项式相当，仅多出一个‖ψ‖₁²的额外因子。

Conclusion: 若‖ψ‖₁²多项式有界，则TPM可去量子化GLHP的量子算法，表明ℓ1范数多项式有界是GLHP经典可解的一个正交区域。

Abstract: The Guided Local Hamiltonian Problem (GLHP) is the problem of ground state energy estimation and preparation given a guiding state having good overlap with the true ground state. The problem is known to be BQP-complete for small error but is classically tractable for larger error. In this work, we identify an orthogonal regime in which the problem is classically tractable, namely when the true ground state $ψ$ has polynomially bounded $\ell_1$-norm. In particular, we show that the Truncated Power Method (TPM), a classical algorithm introduced by Kirby et al. in the quantum context, has a runtime that is polynomially comparable to quantum algorithms for GLHP in all relevant parameters, except for an extra factor of $\|ψ\|_1^2$. In other words, if $\|ψ\|_1^2$ is polynomially bounded, then TPM dequantizes quantum algorithms for GLHP.

</details>


### [179] [Transformer-Based Time-Series Inference of Lindblad Dynamics in Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05647)
*Julian Guam,Jianqing Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种原始时间序列Transformer，直接输入Pauli期望值轨迹，联合学习含时/未知的哈密顿量与耗散率，在多耗散通道和测量噪声下实现高精度重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统中，当系统哈密顿量与耗散率部分未知或显式含时时，传统解析反演与系统辨识方法不可行；已有的Transformer方法依赖手工统计特征且局限于理想化条件。

Method: 构建raw time-series Transformer，直接输入⟨σx(t)⟩、⟨σy(t)⟩、⟨σz(t)⟩的完整轨迹，利用自注意力机制建模时间依赖；架构扩展以联合学习未知哈密顿量参数、处理多耗散通道并在真实测量噪声下鲁棒运行，全程无需人工特征工程。

Result: 在所有测试场景中均取得一致的高重建精度，并消除手工特征工程。

Conclusion: 为现实开放量子系统中的量子环境感知提供了可扩展、鲁棒且通用的框架。

Abstract: The Lindblad master equation is the standard framework for describing the non-unitary evolution of open quantum systems, where environmental interactions induce dissipation and decoherence. When both the system Hamiltonian and the dissipation rates are partially unknown or explicitly time-dependent, traditional analytical inversion and system-identification techniques become intractable. Recent works have demonstrated that Transformer-based models can infer unknown dissipation rates from observable time series, yet these approaches typically rely on hand-crafted statistical features under idealized and highly restricted conditions. Here we advance the paradigm by introducing a raw time-series Transformer that directly ingests the full trajectories of Pauli expectation values $\langleσ_x(t)\rangle$, $\langleσ_y(t)\rangle$, and $\langleσ_z(t)\rangle$, thereby fully exploiting the self-attention mechanism for temporal modeling. The architecture is further extended to jointly learn unknown Hamiltonian parameters, handle multiple dissipation channels, and operate robustly under realistic measurement noise. Across all tested scenarios the model achieves consistently high reconstruction accuracy while eliminating manual feature engineering. This provides a scalable, robust, and versatile framework for quantum environment sensing in realistic open quantum systems.

</details>


### [180] [Universal Improvement of Channel Fidelity via Entanglement Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05669)
*Xinan Chen,Eric Chitambar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究利用纠缠辅助的编解码策略，使n个非相同信道的编码后信道在最坏情况保真度上优于所有给定信道，且该策略具有普适性与最优性，并可推广至经典信道与一般量子资源理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道通信通常假设多次使用相同信道，但实际中退相干会使各次信道使用不再相同。因此需要研究在信道非相同条件下，如何通过编码解码提升通信性能。

Method: 提出一种纠缠辅助的编码与解码策略，将n个非相同信道组合为一个新信道；在给定信道初始纠缠保真度满足温和假设的条件下实现普适提升；并进一步扩展到经典信道（借助共享随机性）以及一般量子资源理论。

Result: 所得信道的worst-case fidelity高于所有n个给定信道；该策略对退极化信道达到最优，并且本质上是唯一的普适策略；经典信道中共享随机性可实现正确传输概率的普适增强；在一般量子资源理论中给出此类普适资源提升可能性的必要条件和充分条件。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，即使各次信道使用非相同，纠缠辅助编解码仍可普适且最优地提升信道保真度，并可推广到经典信道与一般量子资源理论，为量子资源操控提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Communication using quantum channels generally requires encoding and decoding on many identical uses of a quantum channel. In practical settings, decoherence may severely limit our ability to do so, potentially rendering each channel use nonidentical. Given $n$ nonidentical channels, we present an entanglement-assisted encoding and decoding strategy that yields a channel with higher worst-case fidelity than all of the $n$ given channels. The strategy is universal, in the sense that the improvement holds regardless of what channels are given, as long as they satisfy mild assumptions on their initial entanglement fidelities. Our protocol is optimal when the given channels are depolarizing channels, and is essentially the unique one that is universal. This idea can also be extended to classical channels, where shared randomness between the sender and the receiver allows universal enhancement of the probability of correct transmission. We then consider general quantum resource theories, providing a necessary condition and a sufficient condition for the possibility of such universal improvement of quantum resources.

</details>


### [181] [Momentum-Space Path Integral Approach to Non-Hermitian Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05702)
*Zhuo-Ting Cai,Wei Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在动量空间中构建了非厄米对称性破缺的半经典对偶表述，通过迹公式与量子化条件证明本征值的实/复性质由半经典轨道对称性决定，且量子化条件的自然形式取决于轨道拓扑（可收缩轨道两种表述等价，非可收缩轨道需边界项修正），并应用于有限非厄米晶格中布洛赫振荡的边界诱导谱跃迁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前基于坐标空间路径积分的量子-经典对应已建立，但缺乏动量空间的对偶表述；为完善非厄米对称性破缺的量子-经典对应框架并拓展其适用范围，需要发展动量空间表述。

Method: 在动量空间构建相应的迹公式与量子化条件，分析半经典轨道的对称性质与拓扑性质，并与坐标空间路径积分表述进行对比；以有限非厄米晶格中的布洛赫振荡为具体应用对象。

Result: 单个本征值的实/复性质由对应半经典轨道的对称性质决定；量子化条件的自然形式由轨道拓扑决定：相空间中可收缩周期轨道下坐标与动量表述等价，非可收缩轨道沿缠绕方向的量子化条件仍有效但其对偶形式需边界项修正；实空间缠绕轨道与布里渊区缠绕轨道分别自然选择坐标空间与动量空间量子化。在有限非厄米晶格中，跨越布里渊区的布洛赫振荡轨道保持相关对称性并给出实能级，而边界反射轨道形成对称相关对并产生复共轭本征值。

Conclusion: 本工作完善了非厄米对称性破缺的量子-经典对应框架，将其适用性拓展至更广泛的非厄米问题。

Abstract: A quantum-classical correspondence for non-Hermitian symmetry breaking has recently been established using coordinate-space path integrals, providing a semiclassical understanding of spectral transitions at the level of individual eigenstates. Here we develop its dual formulation in momentum space by constructing the corresponding trace formula and quantization condition. We show that the real or complex nature of individual eigenvalues is determined by the symmetry properties of the associated semiclassical orbits, as in the coordinate-space path-integral approach. Moreover, we demonstrate that the topology of semiclassical orbits determines the natural formulation of the quantization condition: the coordinate- and momentum-space formulations are equivalent for contractible periodic orbits in phase space, whereas for noncontractible orbits, the quantization condition along the winding direction remains valid, but its dual form must be corrected by a boundary term. In particular, real-space-winding and Brillouin-zone-winding orbits naturally select coordinate- and momentum-space quantization, respectively. As a nontrivial application, we investigate the boundary-induced spectral transition of Bloch oscillations in a finite non-Hermitian lattice, where Bloch-oscillation orbits winding across the Brillouin zone preserve the relevant symmetry and yield real energy levels, whereas boundary-reflected orbits form symmetry-related pairs and give rise to complex-conjugate eigenvalues. Our work completes the quantum-classical correspondence framework for non-Hermitian symmetry breaking, extending its applicability to a broader class of non-Hermitian problems.

</details>


### [182] [An efficient Hamiltonian-based quantum algorithm for characters of the symmetric group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05752)
*Dikshant Rathore,Leo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种更简单的对称群 S_n 特征态制备算法，基于哈密顿量演化序列，可在1D最近邻可重构量子比特上高效实现，门复杂度从 O(n) 到 Õ(n^{2.5}) 不等，优于原QFT方法的 Õ(n^3)，并推广至量子特征变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，对应对称群 S_n 特征表某一列的量子态（按特征值加权的不可约表示叠加）在合理复杂度假设下经典采样是困难的，而存在基于 S_n 上量子傅里叶变换的高效量子制备算法。但该原方法使用 QFT 子程序需要 Õ(n^3) 门，复杂度较高，且实现不够简便。因此作者希望给出更简单、门复杂度更低的替代算法。

Method: 作者使用一系列哈密顿量演化来制备特征态，并给出在1D最近邻门、可重构量子比特上的高效实现。通过领头阶、态依赖的 Trotter 误差分析估计门数；最坏情况界基于 QSVT 的哈密顿量模拟方法证明；并对基于 Trotter 的协议进行数值基准测试。此外，将协议推广为 S_n 上量子特征变换（QCT）的显式算法。

Result: 对于固定目标态误差，估计的充分总门数从 n-循环列的 O(n) 门，到近经典困难参数区域的 Õ(n^{1.75}) 门，再到最坏情况的 Õ(n^{2.5}) 门不等；原方法的 QFT 子程序使用 Õ(n^3) 门。数值基准表明 Trotter 估计偏保守。QCT 算法使用 Õ(n^{2.5}) 门和 O(n) 个量子比特。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了制备 S_n 特征态的替代简化算法，显著降低了门复杂度（相对原 QFT 方法），并推广至量子特征变换，同时讨论了其在对称orbifold共形场论纠缠熵中的应用。

Abstract: Consider a quantum state vector proportional to any given column of the character table of the symmetric group ($S_n$), corresponding to a superposition over the irreps weighted by the character values. It was recently argued that sampling from this state is classically hard under reasonable complexity-theoretic assumptions, while there exists an efficient quantum algorithm for preparing this character state using the quantum Fourier transform (QFT) over $S_n$ [arXiv:2501.12579]. We give an alternative, simpler algorithm to prepare this character state with a sequence of Hamiltonian evolutions, as well as an efficient implementation with reconfigurable qubits that uses only nearest-neighbor gates in 1D. The gate complexity, accounting for Hamiltonian simulation errors, depends on the choice of the column: For fixed target state error, the estimated sufficient total gate count ranges from as few as $O(n)$ gates for the $n$-cycle column, to $\widetilde O(n^{1.75})$ gates near the conjecturally classically hard parameter regime, to provably $\widetilde O(n^{2.5})$ gates in the worst case. In contrast, the QFT subroutine in the prior approach uses $\widetilde O(n^3)$ gates. Our gate count estimates come from leading-order state-dependent Trotter error analysis, and the proven worst-case bound relies on a QSVT-based Hamiltonian simulation method; numerical benchmarks of the Trotter-based protocol suggest that the estimates are conservative. Furthermore, we generalize our protocol to give an explicit algorithm for the quantum character transform (QCT) over $S_n$ using $\widetilde O(n^{2.5})$ gates and $O(n)$ qubits. An application to entanglement entropy of symmetric orbifold conformal field theories is also discussed.

</details>


### [183] [Certified Randomness Generation against Quantum Adversaries via Energy Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05766)
*Thomas A. Hahn,Efrat Gerchkovitz,Ester Yehuda,Rotem Arnon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究基于能量约束的prepare-and-measure量子随机数发生器(QRNG)，证明了针对经典与量子侧信息对手的单轮冯·诺依曼熵的近紧下界，并分析了其鲁棒性，为该框架下抗一般量子攻击的严格安全性证明提供了核心要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QRNG的安全性依赖于不同假设：设备无关QRNG需要非信号假设，单证明者QRNG需要后量子计算假设。基于物理能量约束的prepare-and-measure模型实现更简单、速率更优，但此前工作要么假设对手是经典的，要么仅给出较弱的条件最小熵界，缺乏对一般量子攻击的严格安全性证明。

Method: 在能量约束的prepare-and-measure QRNG模型中，针对同时持有经典和量子侧信息的对手，推导单轮冯·诺依曼熵的本质上紧的下界，并进一步分析该熵界的鲁棒性。

Result: 证明了单轮冯·诺依曼熵的近紧下界；表明在量子情形下使用冯·诺依曼熵而非最小熵可带来显著提升，在许多情况下几乎与经典界相匹配；并给出了熵界的鲁棒性分析。

Conclusion: 由于单轮冯·诺依曼熵是刻画QRNG密钥率的主要量，该下界为能量约束框架下针对一般量子攻击的严格安全性证明奠定了关键基础，同时揭示了冯·诺依曼熵在量子侧信息场景中的优越性。

Abstract: For quantum random number generators (QRNGs), strong security guarantees can be obtained under various assumptions. The most well-known assumptions are the non-signalling assumption (needed for device-independent (DI) QRNG) and post-quantum computational assumptions (needed for computationally bounded single-prover QRNG protocols). In this work, we study a third model: a simple prepare-and-measure QRNG whose security relies on physically motivated energy constraints. This physically motivated set-up allows for easier implementations of QRNGs and can supply better rates.
  So far, previous works that studied the energy-constrained model either assumed that the adversary is classical in their security proof or supplied relatively weak bounds on the certified randomness, by bounding the conditional min-entropy. In this work, we prove a new, essentially tight, lower bound on the single-round von Neumann entropy against adversaries holding both classical and quantum side-information. Our work reveals that working with the von Neumann entropy, rather than the min-entropy, in the quantum case can lead to a major improvement, nearly matching the classical bound in many cases. We further study the robustness of our entropy bound. Since the single-round von Neumann entropy is the main quantity of interest when characterizing the key rate of QRNGs, our bound establishes the main ingredient for rigorous security proofs against general quantum attacks in this framework.

</details>


### [184] [Quantum Complexity of Solving Linear Equations on Higher-Order Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05806)
*Caesnan M. G. Leditto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：在稀疏oracle模型与参数promise下，制备Hodge Laplacian线性系统的归一化最小范数解的量子态是BQP-hard，并结合高效量子算法得出该问题BQP-complete，从而为高阶网络分析中的量子优势提供最坏情形复杂性基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶网络（HONs）中单纯形模型涉及的Hodge Laplacian线性系统变量规模巨大（边、三角形乃至更高维单纯形），求解代价高昂。虽有近期量子算法针对该瓶颈，但与特定经典算法的比较无法判定该问题是否具备可证明量子优势的复杂性根基，因而需要建立其最坏情形复杂度刻画。

Method: 采用归约方法：在指定的稀疏oracle模型与参数promise下，构造一系列归约，将任意多项式时间有界误差量子计算映射为Hodge Laplacian线性系统；每个归约都能高效恢复前一个线性系统的归一化最小范数解；并给出从近似解态求解相应判定问题的高效量子算法。

Result: 证明了制备Hodge Laplacian线性系统归一化最小范数解的量子态是BQP-hard；结合高效量子算法，该问题为BQP-complete。同时表明量子线性系统问题（QLSP）的BQP-hardness在矩阵被限制为Hodge Laplacian时依然保持。

Conclusion: 该工作为评估高阶网络分析中的量子优势提供了最坏情形复杂性基础，说明即使矩阵限定为Hodge Laplacian，QLSP的BQP-hardness依然成立，从而赋予相关量子算法可证明的复杂性意义。

Abstract: Higher-order networks (HONs) represent interactions among groups of objects, supporting the study of collective behaviour that pairwise models can miss. In simplicial models of these networks, Hodge Laplacian linear systems provide a common mathematical framework for analysis, for example, for solving statistical ranking problems and investigating long-term stability in coupled oscillator systems. The large number of variables associated with edges, triangles, and higher-dimensional simplices can make these systems costly to solve. Recent quantum algorithms address this bottleneck. However, comparisons with particular classical algorithms do not decision output whether solving these linear system problems establishes a quantum complexity foundation for provable quantum advantage. We prove that preparing a quantum state encoding the normalized minimum-norm solution of Hodge Laplacian linear systems is $\mathsf{BQP}$-hard under the specified sparse oracle model and parameter promises. Our sequence of reductions maps an arbitrary polynomial-time bounded-error quantum computation to such linear systems. Each reduction allows efficient recovery of the normalized minimum-norm solution of the preceding linear system. Together with an efficient quantum algorithm for the associated decision problem from the approximate solution state, we show that this problem is $\mathsf{BQP}$-complete. These results provide a worst-case complexity foundation for evaluating quantum advantage in HON analysis and show that the $\mathsf{BQP}$-hardness of the quantum linear system problem (QLSP) persists even when the matrices are restricted to Hodge Laplacians.

</details>


### [185] [Spectral Analysis Beyond the Spectral Gap: Rapid Mixing of Quantum Gibbs Samplers in One Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05848)
*Hongrui Chen,Zhiyan Ding*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一般一维非对易局域自旋系统在任意固定正温度下量子 Gibbs 采样器具有快速混合，混合时间为 O(log^4(n)+log(1/ε))，突破了此前 O(n+log(1/ε)) 的界以及高温/弱相互作用限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 KMS 细致平衡量子 Gibbs 采样器的收敛时间为 O(n+log(1/ε))，快速混合仅在高温或弱相互作用下可得；谱隙所刻画的慢衰减率可能严重低估实际平衡速度，作者希望突破这些限制并解释该现象。

Method: 基于 CKG2023 的 KMS 细致平衡 Gibbs 采样器，利用"远离平衡的态必在低权重 Pauli 算子张成空间之外有显著分量"这一关键观察，在低权重 Pauli 算子空间外应用更强的耗散估计，构建超越谱隙的谱分析框架。分析经由 sandwiched Rényi-2 散度的指数收敛以及修正对数 Sobolev 不等式。

Result: 对 n 个格点系统，从任意初态收敛到迹范数误差 ε 的时间为 O(log^4(n)+log(1/ε))，优于此前的 O(n+log(1/ε))，并在高温与弱相互作用之外也建立了快速混合。衰减估计等价于 sandwiched Rényi-2 散度的指数收敛；结合谱隙还导出速率为 Ω(log^{-4}(n)) 的修正对数 Sobolev 不等式。

Conclusion: 该工作将弛豫分解为两阶段：坏初态的双指数收敛与近平衡态的指数收敛，从而说明谱隙可显著低估平衡速度，并提供了一个超越谱隙的快速混合证明框架。

Abstract: We prove rapid mixing of a quantum Gibbs sampler for general one-dimensional non-commuting local spin systems at every fixed positive temperature. In particular, for a system of $n$ sites, we show that the Kubo--Martin--Schwinger (KMS) detailed-balanced Gibbs sampler of~\cite{CKG2023} converges from any initial state to trace-norm error $ε$ in time $O(\log^4(n)+\log(1/ε))$. This improves the previous $O(n+\log(1/ε))$ bound and establishes rapid mixing beyond high-temperature and weak-interaction regimes. Our proof explains why the slowest decay rate, captured by the spectral gap, can substantially underestimate the speed of equilibration. The key insight is that a state far from equilibrium must have a substantial component outside the span of low-weight Pauli operators, where stronger dissipation estimates apply. This yields two stages of relaxation: a double-exponential convergence from a bad initial state, followed by exponential convergence near equilibrium. We turn this mechanism into a framework for proving rapid mixing through spectral analysis beyond the gap. Our decay estimate is equivalent to exponential convergence in sandwiched Rényi-2 divergence. Together with the spectral gap, it also implies a modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality with rate $Ω(\log^{-4}(n))$.

</details>


### [186] [Classical observable coalescence as a discriminating diagnostic for spectral degeneracies in non-Hermitian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05877)
*P. K. Nkaimbi,C. A. Mba,A. B. Moubissi,M. R. Kamsap,L. C. Fai,M. E. Ateuafack,M. N. Jipdi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"经典可观测量并合"判据，从物理构型直接识别例外点(EP)，并在非厄米Ising链中验证其可区分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EP识别依赖本征矢并合，需哈密顿量谱，在缺乏谱结构的经典平台中不可用，故需一种仅依赖经典可观测量的EP诊断方法。

Method: 对耦合J与交错虚场γ的非厄米Ising链，先做Heisenberg动力学的平均场约化，得到两支对称相关平衡分支；辅以精确Jordan-Wigner与Bogoliubov-de Gennes处理作对照，并设计耦合增益-损耗电路实现方案。

Result: 平衡分支在EP超曲面|γ|=|J|边界处融合，呈现√(J²−γ²)支点结构与双叶Riemann拓扑；γ=0,η=0处退化为diabolic point，无可观测量并合；精确方法在同一阈值确认本征矢并合。

Conclusion: 经典可观测量可作为非厄米临界性的实验可及标志，该判据具区分能力而非仅提示性，并可由增益-损耗电路实现。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) are conventionally identified through eigenvector coalescence, inaccessible in classical platforms lacking a Hamiltonian spectrum. We introduce classical observable coalescence as a diagnostic identifying EPs directly from physical configurations, and demonstrate it in a known non-Hermitian Ising chain with coupling $J$ and staggered imaginary field $γ$, chosen for verifiability against established quantum results. A mean-field reduction of the Heisenberg dynamics yields equilibrium branches organising into two symmetry-related families whose fusion at the boundary of the EP supersurface $|γ|=|J|$ exhibits the characteristic $\sqrt{J^2-γ^2}$ branch-point structure and two-sheeted Riemann topology. Crucially, the degeneracy at $γ=0,η=0 $ is shown to be a diabolic point, producing no observable coalescence, establishing the diagnostic as discriminating rather than merely suggestive. An exact Jordan-Wigner and Bogoliubov-de Gennes treatment confirms eigenvector coalescence at the same threshold, and a coupled gain-loss circuit realisation is proposed, establishing classical observables as experimentally accessible signatures of non-Hermitian criticality.

</details>


### [187] [Fusion-assisted decoding of non-Abelian topological order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05901)
*Rohith Sajith,Yabo Li,Zijian Song,Tsung-Cheng Lu,Carolyn Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出通过细化综合征集合（含解析非阿贝尔任意子融合结果的测量）可系统提升最大似然解码器性能，并以 \(D_4\) 量子双重模型在电荷噪声下的显式解码器与数值阈值为例进行验证，证明存在非零噪声阈值，单轮测量即可恢复整个逻辑量子比特（qudit）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ML 解码器在环面码及阿贝尔任意子表面码中已被充分理解并能达到信息论解码阈值。而支持横向或常深度非 Clifford 门、且在量子器件中可高效制备的非阿贝尔任意子码日益重要，但其解码面临新挑战：非阿贝尔任意子一般不能被确定性湮灭，逻辑算符也无法用阿贝尔同调分类。

Method: 通过细化综合征集合，引入能够解析非阿贝尔任意子融合结果的测量；针对电荷噪声下的 \(D_4\) 量子双重模型构造显式 ML 解码器，并将其映射到经典统计力学模型以进行阈值数值计算。

Result: 细化综合征可系统改善 ML 解码器性能；对 \(D_4\) 量子双重模型在电荷噪声下的数值结果显示存在非零噪声阈值，在该阈值以下，单轮测量即可恢复整个逻辑 qudit。

Conclusion: 非阿贝尔任意子码虽在解码上面临确定性湮灭与逻辑算符分类的困难，但通过细化综合征测量，ML 解码仍可获得非零阈值，并支持单轮测量恢复完整逻辑信息。

Abstract: Maximum likelihood (ML) decoders perform quantum error correction by measuring a set of syndromes, classically computing an optimal recovery based on these syndromes and the noise model, and then performing that recovery. ML decoders are well understood for the toric code and generalize straightforwardly to other surface codes with Abelian anyons, where they can saturate information-theoretic decoding thresholds. On the other hand, codes with non-Abelian anyons have become increasingly relevant because they can support transversal or constant-depth non-Clifford gates and admit efficient preparation protocols in modern quantum devices. However, they present new challenges for decoding: non-Abelian anyons cannot in general be deterministically annihilated, and the logical operators cannot be classified by Abelian homology. In this work, we show that refined syndrome sets, including measurements that resolve non-Abelian anyon fusion outcomes, systematically improve the performance of ML decoders. We demonstrate this concretely by constructing explicit decoders for the $D_4$ quantum double model under charge noise, and obtaining numerical results for thresholds via mappings to classical statistical-mechanics models. We show that there is a nonzero noise threshold below which the entire logical qudit can be recovered with a single round of measurements.

</details>


### [188] [Robust Diamond Quantum Sensing in Double-Quantum 4-Ramsey Interferometry by Dual-Feedback Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05907)
*Yuta Araki,Takeharu Sekiguchi,Yuji Hatano,Naota Sekiguchi,Zehan Li,Chikara Shinei,Masashi Miyakawa,Takashi Taniguchi,Tokuyuki Teraji,Hiroshi Abe,Shinobu Onoda,Takeshi Ohshima,Mutsuko Hatano,Takayuki Iwasaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过双反馈系统补偿双量子4-Ramsey干涉中的共模与差模频移，实现高灵敏、长期稳定的金刚石量子磁强计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测极弱磁场信号需长时间累积以提升信噪比，但热漂移与环境磁噪声引起的偏离最优工作频率严重制约传感系统的长期稳定性。

Method: 基于含失谐的哈密顿量推导信号对共模与差模失谐的解析解，明确DQ 4-Ramsey传感的鲁棒性极限；据此引入同步频率调制与双反馈方案，独立跟踪并补偿两类频移，同时维持最优磁场响应。

Result: 在2.8 K温度变化下实现稳定连续运行；在5-100 Hz范围内长时间（4小时）维持7.0 pT/Hz^1/2灵敏度，噪声底达64 fT/Hz^1/2；长期连续传感呈现预期的1/T^1/2标度关系，并避免幅度低估与波形失真。

Conclusion: 该工作为长期DQ 4-Ramsey金刚石磁强计提供了鲁棒方案，并展示了其在检测微弱生物磁信号方面的潜力。

Abstract: We developed a highly sensitive and long-term stable diamond quantum magnetometer by integrating a dual-feedback system that compensates for common- and differential-mode frequency shifts in double-quantum (DQ) 4-Ramsey interferometry. Detecting extremely weak magnetic-field signals requires extensive signal accumulation to enhance the signal-to-noise ratio. However, deviations from the optimal operating frequencies caused by thermal drift or environmental magnetic noise severely hinder the long-term stability of such a sensing system. To overcome the limitation, we first derived analytical solutions for the signal response to common- and differential-mode detunings based on a detuning-inclusive Hamiltonian to theoretically clarify the robustness limit in DQ 4-Ramsey sensing. Guided by this theoretical model, we introduced synchronized frequency modulation and a dual-feedback scheme to independently track and compensate for these frequency shifts while maintaining an optimal magnetic-field response. The proposed method enabled stable continuous operation against temperature variations of 2.8 K. By maintaining a magnetic field sensitivity of 7.0 pT/Hz^1/2 in the frequency range of 5-100 Hz for a prolonged duration of 4 hours, we achieved an extremely low noise floor of 64 fT/Hz^1/2. During prolonged continuous sensing, the system exhibited the expected 1/T^1/2 scaling with integration time T, and prevented both the underestimation of amplitude and waveform distortion. These results establish a robust approach for long-term DQ 4-Ramsey diamond magnetometry and demonstrate its potential for detecting weak biomagnetic signals.

</details>


### [189] [Evaluating the performance of QEC primitives on quantum processors at large width and depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05928)
*J. A. Montanez-Barrera; Kristel Michielsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于LR-QAOA的少样本QEC原语基准，将r随深度衰减映射为等效双比特退极化率λeff，在10台QPU上验证并与逻辑存储实验相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QEC依赖奇偶校验提取、MCM、复位、前馈与调度等原语，但现有评估要么孤立测试单个原语，要么依赖资源开销巨大的完整实验，缺乏低资源、可跨硬件比较的综合基准。

Method: 以固定线性参数的QAOA（LR-QAOA）构建MCM实现：按目标码的校验结构构造QAOA哈密顿量，使电路复现综合症提取的连接性与MCM模式；QAOA层数类比重复综合症提取轮数；由r随深度的衰减提取等效硬件误差并映射为双比特退极化率λeff；在IBM、IQM、Quantinuum的10台QPU上对比直接实现与MCM中介实现。

Result: 电路规模最高达2950次MCM操作；在Helios-1与H2-1上对表面码、三角色码、二元双循环qLDPC哈密顿量运行码结构基准，数据比特分别达81、91、48个，MCM操作达480次；在ibm_phoenix上实现表面码结构，发现基准响应与逻辑存储性能在不同QPU区域间存在相关性。

Conclusion: 该基准构建简便、资源需求低，可在完整逻辑存储实验之前用于比较不同硬件代际与QEC实现。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) relies on repeated parity extraction, mid-circuit measurement (MCM), reset, feed-forward, and scheduling, yet these primitives are usually assessed either in isolation or through resource-demanding experiments. We introduce a few-sample benchmark of QEC-relevant primitives based on an MCM implementation of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) with a fixed set of linear parameters (LR-QAOA). For a chosen code, the QAOA Hamiltonian is constructed from its check structure, such that the resulting circuit mimics the syndrome-extraction connectivity and MCM pattern while producing a direct algorithmic signal, the approximation-ratio r. The LR-QAOA depth, defined by the number of QAOA layers, plays a role analogous to the number of repeated syndrome-extraction rounds in a QEC experiment. From QPU executions, the decay of r with depth defines an effective hardware error, which we map to an equivalent two-qubit depolarizing rate λeff. We compare direct and MCM-mediated implementations across 10 QPUs from IBM, IQM, and Quantinuum, with circuits containing up to 2950 MCM operations. On Quantinuum's Helios-1 and H2-1, we run code-structured LR-QAOA benchmarks for surface-code, triangular color-code, and bivariate-bicycle qLDPC Hamiltonians up to 81, 91, and 48 data qubits, respectively, using up to 480 MCM operations. On IBM ibm_phoenix, we implement the surface-code structure and compare the LR-QAOA response with logical-memory experiments, observing a correlation between the benchmark and the logical performance across different regions of the QPU. Its construction and low resource requirements provide a practical benchmark for comparing hardware generations and QEC implementations before full logical-memory experiments are performed.

</details>


### [190] [Joint GKP Encoding for GHZ States Transmission over Bosonic Quantum MIMO Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05992)
*Seid Koudia,Selssabil Kadid,Symeon Chatzinotas,Mérouane Debbah*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在非酉量子MIMO信道中，通过有限能量GKP编码传输GHZ态的问题，提出联合晶格整形方案，并分析了不同CSI条件下的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多输入多输出（QuMIMO）信道存在相干混模、模式相关衰减和高斯噪声，传统乘积式GKP处理难以应对各向异性玻色噪声，需要研究如何利用联合GKP处理提升GHZ态在非酉信道中的保真度。

Method: 建立耦合模模型描述相干混模、模式相关衰减与高斯噪声；收发端结合可编程无源网格、量子极限MMSE增益、本地GKP恢复与联合经典伴随式解码；利用瞬时CSI、当前奇异值加信道统计、或仅统计信息优化复数Givens晶格。

Result: 仿真表明联合晶格整形优于乘积式GKP处理；在相干基漂移下，完整瞬时CSI能改善GHZ态保持，而奇异值条件设计与纯统计设计表现相近，因为二者均无法解析当前奇异帧。

Conclusion: 奇异帧几何是超出衰减知识之外的关键资源；联合GKP处理通过使逻辑晶格和接收机适应各向异性玻色噪声，而不仅是反转多模混模，从而实现性能提升。

Abstract: We study transmission of a Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) state over nonunitary quantum multiple-input multiple-output (QuMIMO) channels using finite-energy Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) encoding. A coupled-mode model describes coherent mixing, mode-dependent attenuation, and Gaussian noise. The transceiver combines programmable passive meshes, quantum-limited MMSE gain, local GKP recovery, and joint classical syndrome decoding. We optimize complex-Givens lattices using instantaneous channel-state information (CSI), current singular values with channel statistics, or statistics alone. Simulations show that joint lattice shaping outperforms product GKP processing. Under coherent-basis drift, full instantaneous CSI improves GHZ-state preservation, whereas singular-value conditioned and fully statistical designs perform similarly because neither resolves the current singular frames. These findings identify singular-frame geometry as a critical resource beyond attenuation knowledge where joint GKP processing adapts the logical lattice and receiver to anisotropic bosonic noise rather than merely inverting multimode mixing.

</details>


### [191] [Free fermion models with an operator entanglement growing as $O(\sqrt{t})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05995)
*Federico Tonetto,Jérôme Dubail*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究含随机时空依赖演化的一维守恒自由费米子体系中算子空间纠缠熵（OSEE）的时间演化，发现随机性使局域算子（含Jordan-Wigner弦）和演化算子的OSEE均以 O(√t) 增长，并通过映射到对称简单排他过程（SSEP）解析计算平均熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无随机性的自由费米子系统中，局域算子的OSEE至多呈对数增长；而随机性如何改变这一增长规律（以及演化算子的熵增长是线性还是被抑制）尚不清楚，作者希望刻画随机演化下OSEE的普适时间标度。

Method: 考察两类模型：布朗哈密顿量与砖墙matchgate线路。对含Jordan-Wigner弦的局域自旋算子及演化算子分别计算OSEE，覆盖所有Rényi指数；通过将量子模型映射到对称简单排他过程（SSEP）解析计算平均熵，并借助自平均假设（用数值支持）。此外还比较了保持空间平移不变的时间随机演化和淬火无序两种情形。

Result: 在含随机性的情形下，局域算子（含JW弦）与演化算子的OSEE均以 O(√t) 增长，与无随机性时演化算子预期的线性增长形成对比。保持平移不变的时间随机演化下OSEE同样按 √t 增长。淬火无序下，哈密顿量演化中局域算子（含JW弦）和演化算子的熵按 O(log log t) 增长，而砖墙matchgate线路中熵在所有时刻保持有界。

Conclusion: 随机性显著改变了自由费米子体系OSEE的增长规律：从无随机性的对数（局域算子）或线性（演化算子）增长变为 √t 增长；不同随机类型（时空随机、时间随机、淬火无序）给出不同标度，作者通过SSEP映射解析给出平均熵结果。

Abstract: We study the time evolution of the Operator Space Entanglement Entropy (OSEE) of one-dimensional number-conserving free fermions subject to a random space- and time-dependent evolution. It is known that, in free-fermion systems without randomness, the OSEE of \emph{local} operators grows at most \emph{logarithmically} in time. Here we show that, in the presence of randomness, the OSEE of local spin operators involving a Jordan-Wigner string grows as $\mathcal O(\sqrt t)$, for all values of the Rényi index. We establish this in two models: a Brownian Hamiltonian and a brick-wall matchgate circuit. We also consider the OSEE of the evolution operator, and we show that it also grows as $\mathcal O(\sqrt{t})$, in contrast with the linear growth generically expected without randomness. We analytically compute the average entropies of the two operators by mapping the quantum models to the symmetric simple exclusion process, relying on an assumption of self-averaging which we support numerically. We also consider two other types of randomness: random evolution in time that keeps spatial translation invariance, and quenched disorder. In the former case, we find that the OSEE also grows as $\sqrt{t}$. In the latter case, we find that the entropy grows as $\mathcal O( \log \log t)$ for Hamiltonian evolution both for local operators with a Jordan-Wigner string and for the evolution operator, whereas it remains bounded at all times in the brick-wall matchgate circuit.

</details>


### [192] [Metastability in Quantum Dynamics for Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06000)
*Yuchen Lu,Zherui Chen,Tongyang Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以量子朗之万动力学（QLD）为例，研究相干演化与耗散如何共同塑造亚稳态，刻画其延缓优化进程的机制并给出调控条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚稳态会使系统长期停留在中间状态，与真正收敛相分离。在用于优化的量子动力学中，这种平台期会延迟向非凸势全局极小点附近目标的推进，而其一般机制尚不清楚。

Method: 将目标函数编码进势能，以QLD为具体模型，精确求解两态模型，并基于隧穿频率、耗散强度与阻尼失配导出谱分离判据；进一步推广至多态模型分析阱间隧穿与阱内弛豫的分离。

Result: 得到两态模型的精确解与谱分离判据；证明足够弱的阱间隧穿可将慢布居转移与快阱内弛豫分离；并给出改变耗散结构以缩短逼近目标态时间的条件。双阱与多阱数值实验在固定目标与精度、计入控制耗时的前提下验证了改进。

Conclusion: 相干演化与耗散的相互作用是决定量子优化中亚稳态与收敛速度的关键，可通过调节隧穿与耗散结构来加速向目标态的逼近。

Abstract: Metastability separates apparent equilibration from true convergence: a dynamical system can remain near an intermediate state long before reaching its stationary state. In quantum dynamics used for optimization, such a plateau can delay progress toward a target concentrated near the global minimizers of a nonconvex potential. In this paper, we initiate the study of the general mechanisms by which coherent evolution and dissipation shape metastability, using quantum Langevin dynamics (QLD) as a concrete example [CLW+25], where the objective function is encoded in the potential and coherent transfer between wells interacts with tunable dissipation. To quantify this interaction, we solve a two-state model precisely and derive a spectral-separation criterion in terms of tunneling frequency, dissipation strength, and damping imbalance. Extending the analysis to multi-state models, we show that sufficiently weak tunneling between wells can separate slow population transfer from fast relaxation within each well. In addition, we establish conditions under which changing the structure of dissipation shortens the time needed to approach a prescribed target state. Numerical experiments on double-well and multi-well potentials demonstrate these improvements while keeping the target and accuracy fixed and accounting for the time spent on control.

</details>


### [193] [Polynomial neural surrogates for designing photonic quantum experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06032)
*Rohit Chaurasiya,Xuemei Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文为量子光学模拟器PyTheus开发了多项式神经网络代理模型，用于预测量子态并辅助量子实验的逆向设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理模拟器可预测实验构型生成的量子态，从而支持量子实验发现，但模拟器计算代价高，重复调用会限制对目标量子态实验的搜索效率。

Method: 受图完美匹配与态振幅之间关系的启发，设计具有多项式激活函数的物理启发式多项式神经代理模型，替代基于图的量子光学模拟器PyTheus；并分别为四、六、八光子系统训练独立代理模型。

Result: 相比标准多层感知机，代理模型以更少可训练参数取得更高预测精度；在GHZ、W和线性簇态的逆向设计中，较大系统上代理模型比直接用PyTheus优化更快。

Conclusion: 将底层物理规律融入神经代理模型，可为量子实验设计提供高效途径。

Abstract: Physics simulators can support the discovery of quantum experiments by predicting the states generated by experimental configurations. When these simulators are computationally expensive, repeated simulator calls can limit the search for experiments that generate a desired quantum state. Here, we develop a physics-inspired polynomial neural surrogate for PyTheus, a graph-based quantum-optics simulator, to predict quantum states and use it to design quantum experiments. Its polynomial activations are motivated by the relation between graph perfect matchings and the resulting state amplitudes. We train separate surrogate models for four-, six-, and eight-photon systems and show that they achieve higher prediction accuracy with fewer trainable parameters than standard multilayer perceptrons. We then use the trained surrogates for inverse design of GHZ, W, and linear-cluster states. For the larger systems, the surrogates also enable faster inverse design than direct optimization with PyTheus. These results suggest that incorporating the underlying physics into neural surrogates can provide an efficient approach to quantum experiment design.

</details>


### [194] [Materialised symmetries of 2D translationally invariant codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06036)
*Andrew Li,Stephen D. Bartlett,Andrew C. Doherty,Campbell K. McLauchlan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维平移不变(TI) CSS qLDPC码(如双变量自行车码)的物化对称性,将其对称空间的分解推广到无限晶格与有限周期/扭曲边界晶格,给出类平面波基,为基于匹配的解码器提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期近邻qLDPC码(如双变量自行车码等二维平移不变码)受到关注,其物化对称性可用于构造匹配型解码器,这类解码器可推广toric码的最小权完美匹配解码器,并具备优于BP等启发式解码器的渐近性能保证。然而,对称性的数学结构及其在平移下、在有限尺寸晶格限制下的性质尚未被充分研究。

Method: 作者将无限晶格上二维CSS TI码的对称空间分解为平移不变子空间,以类平面波形式写出显式对称基;然后通过将无限晶格基限制到与周期边界相容的基元,获得任意矩形周期晶格的对称基,并据此刻画对称空间随矩形维度的变化;最后通过实例说明扭曲边界下的对称基通常可较易确定而无需重新计算分解。

Result: 建立了二维CSS TI码对称空间的平移不变子空间分解与显式类平面波基;给出从无限晶格基到矩形周期晶格基的限制构造,并刻画对称空间随矩形尺寸的变化;实例表明扭曲边界上的对称基通常可直接确定;以gross码等为例展示了该框架的应用。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了二维平移不变qLDPC码对称性的数学结构与边界条件依赖性,为匹配型解码器的构造提供系统工具,并适用于gross码等实际码。

Abstract: There has been significant recent interest in near-term qLDPC codes as high-performance alternatives to surface and color codes. One such class of codes is 2-dimensional translationally invariant (TI) codes, such as bivariate bicycle codes, which share similar properties to topological codes. Fundamental objects in the study of such codes are the materialised symmetries, which can be used for the construction of matching-based decoders. These decoders generalise the minimum-weight perfect matching decoder for toric codes and provide similar asymptotic performance guarantees that heuristic decoders such as BP and its variants lack. Despite this, the mathematical structure of symmetries along with their properties under translation and restriction to finite-sized lattices has not been well-studied. We describe a decomposition of symmetry spaces of 2D CSS TI codes on infinite lattices into translation-invariant subspaces which allow us to write an explicit basis of symmetries in a plane-wave-like form. We then describe how to adapt this infinite lattice basis so that any corresponding rectangular periodic lattice basis can be obtained by restriction to basis elements compatible with the periodic boundaries. This allows us to describe how the symmetry space varies with different rectangular dimensions. We illustrate via examples that it is often straightforward in practice to determine a basis of symmetries on twisted boundaries as well, without needing to recompute the decomposition. We comment on the applications of this framework to matching-based decoders and provide examples of symmetries of such codes as the gross code.

</details>


### [195] [Comparing thresholds of XZZX and rotated surface codes under spatio-temporal correlated noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06059)
*Dhruv Koul*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了XZZX和旋转表面码在相关及时空噪声下的性能，采用最小权重奇偶因子（MWPF）解码器处理综合征超图，发现XZZX码的阈值随偏置比呈现非单调依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算研究通常假设独立同分布噪声，而相关误差与时空误差等复杂错误模型仍缺乏深入研究，这类噪声会为偏置定制码引入超边，使标准图解码器失效。

Method: 采用原生处理综合征超图、无需人工边分解的最小权重奇偶因子（MWPF）解码器，在电路级相关噪声模型下仿真XZZX码与旋转表面码的性能，并考察不同偏置比的影响。

Result: 在强Z偏置电路级相关噪声（η=100）下，旋转码阈值降至约0.36%，XZZX码降至约0.70%；XZZX码阈值随偏置比呈非单调依赖，在中等偏置（η≈10）处出现阈值最小值，因横向X、Y误差桥接对角弦、破坏其解耦对称性。

Conclusion: 偏置定制码的性能对时空相关噪声的几何结构高度敏感，采用具备超图感知能力的解码器至关重要。

Abstract: While independent and identically distributed noise is typically assumed in quantum computing research, complex error models such as correlated and spatio-temporal errors remain underexplored.
