In this paper, the performance of $\mathrm{XZZX}$ and rotated surface codes is evaluated under these complex noise models. Standard graph-based decoders struggle with the hyperedges generated by correlated errors on bias-tailored codes. To address this, we employ the Minimum Weight Parity Factor (MWPF) decoder, which natively processes syndrome hypergraphs without artificial edge decomposition. Our simulations show that under strong Z-biased circuit-level correlated noise ($η=100$), the threshold of the rotated code degrades to $\approx 0.36\%$, while the $\mathrm{XZZX}$ code drops to $\approx 0.70\%$. Furthermore, a distinct non-monotonic threshold dependence is observed for the $\mathrm{XZZX}$ code across different bias ratios. While the $\mathrm{XZZX}$ code effectively absorbs high-density Z-type hyperedges at extreme biases, it exhibits a threshold minimum at moderate biases ($η\approx 10$) because transverse X and Y errors actively bridge the diagonal strings, breaking the code's one-dimensional decoupling symmetry. These findings demonstrate that utilizing hypergraph-aware decoders is critical, as the performance of bias-tailored codes is highly sensitive to the geometric structure of spatio-temporal correlated noise.

</details>


### [196] [Quantum data loading from the learned shared structure of real signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06076)
*Pablo Herrero Gómez,Antonio Jimeno Morenilla,David Muñoz-Hernández,Higinio Mora Mora*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种"量子原生"数据加载器，只需一次性学习数据集的低维结构描述，即可用固定电路、每信号仅几个数字完成量子态制备，跨多个公开数据集达到最强结构化加载器的精度且门成本相当、参数更少；采样研究表明所需子集规模随信号增长保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从经典数据制备量子态的开销可能超过其服务的目标计算本身；现有加载器多为每个输入单独定制电路，成本随数据量增长而难以摊薄，因此需要能够"一次学习、复用"的加载方式。

Method: 利用真实数据集信号共享的结构，学习数据集的低维描述；据此构造量子原生加载器，用一组固定电路、每信号由少数几个数字设定即完成量子态制备。并在五个公开数据集、七个视图上，与最强结构化加载器在等门成本下比较精度与每信号参数数量；进而考察参数能否由随机子集推断，并开展预注册盲法复现。

Result: 在等门成本下达到最强结构化加载器的目标精度，且每信号所需数字少若干倍；这些数字可由随机子集推断，预注册盲法复现中，达到全信号精度10%以内所需子集规模在信号增大16倍时保持在预设裕度内不变，而结构化加载器所需规模持续增长。该加载器会拒斥无法表示的情形，覆盖的案例少于该基线，且不处理心电图数据。

Conclusion: 数据集的共享低维结构可一次学习并复用，使量子加载器在保持精度的同时显著降低每信号参数开销，并具备规模可扩展性与对不可表示输入的拒斥能力；但适用范围更窄，覆盖案例少于结构化基线且不涵盖心电图。

Abstract: Preparing quantum states from classical data can cost more than the computation they serve; most loaders tailor a circuit to each input. Here we show that the signals of a real dataset share structure that can be learned once and reused. Our quantum-native loader learns a low-dimensional description of a dataset and prepares every signal with one fixed circuit set by a few numbers. Across seven views of five public datasets it meets the targets of the strongest structured loader at equal gate cost with several times fewer numbers per signal. These numbers can be inferred from a random subset: in a preregistered blind replication the subset needed to come within ten per cent of full-signal accuracy stayed constant within a prespecified margin as signals grew sixteenfold, whereas the structured loader needed ever more. It declines what it cannot represent, covering fewer cases than that baseline and no electrocardiogram.

</details>


### [197] [Gaussian Universality and Its Breakdown in Tensor-Network Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06080)
*Shi-Tuan Wang,Zidu Liu,Li-Wei Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文用基于矩的方法，解析地给出了张量网络（重点为矩阵积态MPS）学习模型中高斯普适性出现与失效的精确条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程极限在描述过参数化机器学习模型中非常有效，但其在结构化张量网络架构中的有效性尚不明确，需要厘清其适用边界。

Method: 提出基于矩的分析方法，以矩阵积态为研究对象，分别考察大键维极限与大物理维极限，并区分局部观测量与全局观测量情形，给出高阶矩偏离的显式有限尺寸界。

Result: 在大键维极限下，局部与全局观测量模型均收敛为高斯过程，并有显式的有限尺寸高阶矩偏差界；在大物理维极限下，高斯普适性不再成立——局部观测量模型仍保持高斯过程行为，而全局观测量模型表现出持续的非高斯修正。

Conclusion: 张量网络学习中的高斯过程行为不仅由参数数量决定，还受架构缩放、观测量局域性以及谱性质的控制。

Abstract: Gaussian-process limits are powerful in describing overparameterized machine learning models, yet their validity in structured tensor-network architectures remains unclear. Here we analytically present a moment-based approach that identifies precise conditions for the emergence and breakdown of Gaussian universality in tensor-network learning models, with a focus on matrix product states. We prove that in the large bond dimension limit, the learning models with both local and global observables converge to Gaussian processes, with explicit finite-size bounds on higher-order moment deviations. Whereas in the large physical dimension limit, the Gaussian universality no longer persists: while the models with local observables retain Gaussian-process behavior, those global cases exhibit persistent non-Gaussian corrections. Our results reveal that Gaussian-process behavior in tensor-network learning is controlled not only by parameter number, but also by architectural scaling, observable locality, and the spectral properties.

</details>


### [198] [Collective Quantum State Preparation Is Nonadditive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06113)
*Lin Zhu,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang,Shenggen Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究加权Pauli控制下多副本量子态制备问题，推导单比特精确制备代价，发现制备代价严格不可加，集体协议可降低成本，并给出Rényi熵速度极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态制备是量子计算与控制的基本原语，但其资源代价通常仅针对单一目标态或在独立副本构造下分析，多副本情形及集体控制是否带来优势尚不明确。

Method: 在加权Pauli控制下（Pauli积旋转e^{iφP}按角度|φ|计费，与P的权重无关），推导单比特制备代价，并构造双T态的四脉冲集体协议；同时利用与维度无关的Rényi熵速度极限给出相干目标的下界与制备速率低于有限值时的指数保真度惩罚。

Result: 四脉冲集体协议以低于两个最优单副本协议的代价制备两个T态，证明代价严格不可加；识别出Bloch球上存在集体节省的有限区域，包括始终为乘积态的轨迹，表明瞬态纠缠并非优势的唯一机制；Rényi熵速度极限给出每个相干目标的广延下界及低于有限制备速率时的指数保真度惩罚。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了态合成的多副本几何：即使输出互不相关，仍可从集体控制中获益。

Abstract: Quantum state preparation is a basic primitive of quantum computation and control, but its resource cost is usually analyzed for one target state or under an independent-copy construction. Here we study the many-copy problem under weighted Pauli control, where a Pauli-product rotation $e^{iφP}$ is charged by its rotation angle $|φ|$, independently of the weight of $P$. We derive the exact single-qubit preparation cost and show that it is strictly nonadditive: a four-pulse collective protocol prepares two $T$ states at lower cost than two optimal one-copy protocols. We further identify finite regions of the Bloch sphere with collective saving, including trajectories that remain product throughout, showing that transient entanglement is not the only mechanism behind the advantage. Complementarily, a dimension-independent Rényi-entropy speed limit gives an extensive lower bound for every coherent target and an exponential fidelity penalty below a finite preparation rate. These results establish a many-copy geometry of state synthesis in which uncorrelated outputs can nevertheless benefit from collective control.

</details>


### [199] [Geometric Measure of Entanglement of Rank-2 Mixed Multiqubit States and its Estimation on a Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06127)
*N. A. Susulovska,Kh. P. Gnatenko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于几何纠缠度量与自旋关联的关系，推导了两维Hilbert子空间中rank-2混合多量子比特态几何纠缠的解析表达式，并针对任意多量子比特态与薛定谔猫态的等概率混合，构建了基于自旋关联的纠缠检测量子协议，通过AerSimulator与ibm_marrakesh量子计算机进行量化并结合量子计算误差分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合态的几何纠缠度量通常难以解析计算，且其与可观测物理量（如自旋关联）之间的定量联系尚不清晰；同时，缺乏在真实量子硬件上检测多量子比特混合态纠缠的可操作量子协议。

Method: 利用几何纠缠度量与自旋关联之间的关系，推导由任意多量子比特纯态构成、位于两维Hilbert子空间中的rank-2混合态几何纠缠的解析表达式；分析其对该混合中任意纯态参数的依赖（解析方法与量子计算相结合）；针对一类特殊混合态（任意多量子比特态与薛定谔猫态的等概率混合），构建基于自旋关联的几何纠缠检测量子协议，并在AerSimulator和ibm_marrakesh量子计算机上进行纠缠量化。

Result: 获得了rank-2混合多量子比特态几何纠缠的解析表达式，明确其与纯态参数及自旋关联的依赖关系；量子模拟结果复现了解析依赖关系；量子硬件测量结果在估计误差范围内与理论预测良好吻合，并给出了量子计算误差的影响分析。

Conclusion: 几何纠缠度量可通过自旋关联有效刻画并用于混合多量子比特态，所构建的量子协议能够在含噪量子设备上可靠检测此类纠缠，为混合态纠缠的实验量化提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The geometric measure of entanglement of mixed states is investigated based on its relation to spin correlations. We derive an analytical expression for the entanglement of rank-2 mixed states composed of arbitrary multiqubit pure states within a two-dimensional Hilbert subspace. The dependence of the entanglement of rank-2 mixed multiqubit states on the parameters characterizing arbitrary pure states in the mixture is studied both analytically and using quantum computing. For a particular class of mixed states, namely, equal-probability mixtures of an arbitrary multiqubit state and the Schrödinger cat state, we construct quantum protocols for detecting the geometric measure of entanglement based on its relation to spin correlations. We quantify the entanglement using both the AerSimulator and the ibm_marrakesh quantum computer. The effects of quantum-computing errors are also analyzed. The simulation results reproduce the analytical dependencies, while measurements on the quantum device demonstrate good agreement with theoretical predictions within estimated error bounds.

</details>


### [200] [When Equivalent Quantum Circuits Lose Synthesis Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06142)
*Boshuai Ye,Peng Liang,Arif Ali Khan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出"综合可用性"(synthesis availability)概念，即在量子编译流程的表示转换（序列化、OpenQASM交换、编译器转换、门级降级）中保留高层操作结构的能力，并通过Qiskit、TKET、Cirq实验系统研究其丢失现象，提出Verified机制恢复被请求的综合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子编译器需将QFT、多控X门等高层操作综合为门级电路，但跨编译阶段的表示变化（序列化、OpenQASM交换、编译器转换、门降级）虽保持计算等价，却可能抹除高层操作本身，导致接收方编译器无法应用其请求的综合方法。现有工作关注功能等价性，却忽视了后续综合选择所依赖的高层结构保留问题。

Method: 通过受控实验、代码仓库分析、项目测试捕获的电路以及慕尼黑量子工具包(MQT)流水线，在三款主流编译器Qiskit、TKET、Cirq中研究综合可用性。并提出Verified机制：在表示变化前记录高层操作，仅当校验确认记录仍与交付电路匹配时才重建这些操作，并评估其验证时间与误判情况。

Result: 序列化后若反序列化恢复高层操作则综合可用性得以保留；编译器转换仅在接收方支持该操作时才保留；在被评估的OpenQASM路径中均不保留。经OpenQASM 3直接往返后，全部360个交付条件下请求的综合均无效，尽管编译仍成功。在MQT流水线中，OpenQASM 2交换使八量子比特Grover电路的双量子比特门数增加37.2%。Verified在全部830次恢复运行中均恢复了被请求的综合，各操作族中位验证时间为2.0至638毫秒，能拒绝所有陈旧记录，但也会拒绝部分有效记录。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当后续综合选择依赖于所保留的高层结构时，仅保证功能等价性是不充分的；必须显式保存并在表示变化后重建高层操作，才能维持综合可用性，否则跨工具链的综合方法可能静默失效或导致电路质量下降。

Abstract: Quantum compilers synthesize high-level operations, such as the quantum Fourier transform and multi-controlled X gates, into gate-level circuits. Across compilation stages, a circuit may be serialized, exchanged as OpenQASM, converted between compilers, or lowered to gates. These representation changes can preserve computation while removing the high-level operation itself, leaving the receiving compiler unable to apply a requested synthesis method. We call this loss synthesis availability. We study synthesis availability in Qiskit, TKET, and Cirq through controlled experiments, repository analysis, circuits captured from project tests, and a Munich Quantum Toolkit pipeline. Synthesis availability survives serialization when deserialization restores the high-level operation and survives compiler conversion only when the receiving compiler supports that operation. It does not survive the evaluated OpenQASM routes. After a direct OpenQASM 3 round trip, requested synthesis has no effect in all 360 delivered conditions even though compilation succeeds. In the MQT pipeline, OpenQASM 2 interchange increases the two-qubit gate count by 37.2 percent for an eight-qubit Grover circuit. We also introduce Verified, which records high-level operations before a representation change and reconstructs them only when a check confirms that the record still matches the delivered circuit. Verified restores requested synthesis in all 830 recovery runs, with median verification times from 2.0 to 638 ms per operation family. The check rejects every stale record but also some valid records. These results show that preserving functional equivalence alone is insufficient when later synthesis choices depend on retained high-level structure.

</details>


### [201] [What Does It Cost to Simulate a Quantum Sentence Classifier? An Energy and Compute Perspective on Near-Term QNLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06176)
*Kishlay Kashyap,Sandipan Ganguly*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在SST-2二分类上系统测量VQC的模拟器计算代价：27种配置下VQC仅6/27优于匹配的经典基线，多随机种子后仅1例保持显著优势，而训练墙钟时间比经典拟合慢886–21,127倍（中位数3,158倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期QNLP实验多在经典模拟器上运行，模拟器开销属于该领域的实际计算负担，但现有准确率报告并不呈现这一成本；作者希望以可复现的可行性测量补齐"精度表之外的代价"这一信息缺口。

Method: 在SST-2二分类情感任务上训练变分量子分类器（VQC），使用PennyLane态矢量模拟器，构建27组受控配置网格：3种平衡训练集规模（N=200、500、1000）×3种量子比特数（4、6、8）×3种电路深度。每个VQC与共享同一PCA降维输入的逻辑回归匹配对比，并以完整TF-IDF逻辑回归作为未压缩参考；对初现优势的配置追加两个随机种子重跑，并在固定验证集上做配对检验。同时记录墙钟时间，并采集CodeCarbon能耗与CO2估计作为辅助指标。

Result: VQC在27个单种子对比中仅6个超过匹配基线；追加两个种子后，这6例中只有1例保持大于其配对的种子间波动的正平均优势，且固定验证集上的配对检验未能确立该优势。VQC训练墙钟时间比匹配的经典拟合慢886–21,127倍（中位数3,158倍）；量子比特从4增至8约使模拟器时间翻倍，在测试范围内单步成本可由参数量线性函数良好近似。CodeCarbon估计呈近乎恒定的约41 W功率，信息量基本不超出运行时间，故未据此构造能耗比。

Conclusion: 该研究范围有限（单一数据集、表示、ansatz、模拟器与CPU环境），属于可复现的可行性测量，而非对QNLP的总体性结论；其价值在于把模拟器成本与统计稳健性一并纳入评估，提示仅凭单种子精度表可能高估近期QNLP方法的实用优势。

Abstract: Near-term quantum natural language processing (QNLP) experiments often run on classical simulators, so simulator cost is part of the field's practical compute burden, yet accuracy tables do not show it. We measure that cost for a variational quantum classifier (VQC) on binary SST-2 sentiment classification, using PennyLane's state-vector simulator over a controlled grid of 27 configurations: three balanced training-set sizes (N = 200, 500, 1000), three qubit counts (4, 6, 8), and three circuit depths. Each VQC is compared with logistic regression on the same PCA-reduced input; full TF-IDF logistic regression gives an uncompressed reference. The VQC beats its matched baseline in 6 of 27 single-seed comparisons. After reruns at two further seeds, only 1 of these 6 keeps a positive mean advantage larger than its paired seed-to-seed variability, and paired tests on the fixed validation set do not establish it. VQC training is 886-21,127 times slower in measured wall-clock time than the matched classical fit (median 3,158 times); going from 4 to 8 qubits roughly doubles simulator time, and within the tested range per-step cost is well approximated by a linear function of the parameter count. CodeCarbon energy and CO2 estimates are secondary: they imply an almost constant power of about 41 W, so they add little beyond runtime, and we do not build an energy ratio from them. The study is narrow (one dataset, representation, ansatz, simulator, and CPU environment) and is a reproducible feasibility measurement, not a general verdict on QNLP.

</details>


### [202] [The superb supersinglet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06183)
*Gabriele Cobucci,Armin Tavakoli,Alexander Bernal,Shishir Khandelwal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究高维多体系统中专有的全反对称态（supersinglets）的纠缠性质及其量子信息应用。利用凸优化分析其对噪声的鲁棒性，发展基于集体自旋或随机测量的纠缠见证方法，并证明其可作为高维梯度传感的天然资源、实现海森堡标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维粒子构成的多体系统可承载量子比特系统无法实现的纠缠形式。全反对称态（supersinglets）因在任意集体局域幺正变换下保持不变而具有独特对称性，但其纠缠特性与量子信息应用价值尚未被系统揭示，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用凸优化刻画supersinglets的核心纠缠特征对噪声的鲁棒性；构建基于集体自旋测量或随机测量的纠缠见证方法，用于认证纠缠的深度与维度；并将supersinglets作为高维梯度传感资源，分析其标度行为。

Result: supersinglets的纠缠特征对噪声具有显著鲁棒性，在特定弛豫条件下明显超过若干典型纠缠态；所提见证方法可同时认证纠缠深度与维度；作为高维梯度传感资源，supersinglets可实现海森堡标度。

Conclusion: supersinglets是一类同时具备突出对称性与强纠缠、且对噪声极为鲁棒的多体纠缠态，兼具通用性与量子传感的实际潜力。

Abstract: Multipartite systems of high-dimensional particles can host forms of quantum entanglement inaccessible to qubit systems. A particularly fascinating class is formed by the fully antisymmetric states, also known as supersinglets, which possess a remarkable symmetry: they are invariant under arbitrary collective local unitaries. Here, we explore their entanglement properties and establish their quantum information applications. Using convex optimisation, we show that central entanglement features of supersinglets are remarkably robust to noise, substantially surpassing the robustness of several paradigmatic entangled states under certain relaxations. We then develop witness methods, based on collective-spin or randomised measurements, that certify both the depth and dimensionality of their entanglement. Finally, we identify supersinglets as a natural resource for high-dimensional gradient sensing and prove that they enable Heisenberg scaling. Together, these results reveal supersinglets as an exceptionally robust and versatile class of multipartite entangled states, combining striking symmetry and strong entanglement with concrete potential for quantum sensing.

</details>


### [203] [A robust framework for the comprehensive characterisation of parametric down-conversion sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06194)
*Denis A. Kopylov,Michael Stefszky,Christine Silberhorn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与器件无关的表征框架，将基于参量下转换（PDC）的压缩光源视为产生混合多模高斯态的黑箱，通过模式未分辨测量给出态纯度的下界，从而在无需精确器件模型的情况下评估真实压缩光源质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维量子光网络需要单模、纯的压缩态，而PDC源因光学元件不完美，实际输出态非纯且含内部模式结构。现有基于联合频谱强度或归一化二阶关联函数的表征技术均假设被探测态为纯态，导致对光源质量的评估不完整甚至产生误导。因此亟需一种不依赖纯态假设、也无需详细器件模型的表征方法。

Method: 作者针对信号场与闲频场可区分的PDC源，建立与器件无关的表征框架。将光源作为产生混合多模高斯态的黑箱处理，推导出态纯度的下界，并证明该下界可通过模式未分辨（mode-unresolved）的测量实验确定，无需对非线性器件及其外围光网络建立详细模型。

Result: 所提方法给出可实验测量的态纯度下界，能够对现实压缩光源质量进行整体性评估，突破了现有技术须假设纯态方能工作的限制。

Conclusion: 该框架为优化面向先进量子应用的压缩光源提供了实用基准，可实现不依赖具体器件模型的整体质量评估。

Abstract: Reliable verification of squeezed-light sources is essential for photonic quantum technologies. High-dimensional quantum optical networks demand single-mode, pure squeezed states which, in principle, can be generated through careful engineering of parametric down-conversion-based (PDC) sources. However, due to the imperfections of optical components, the generated state is never pure and commonly contains internal mode structure. Existing characterisation techniques, based on either joint spectral intensity or normalized second-order correlation functions, work under the assumption that the probed state is pure, and therefore can result in incomplete or misleading assessment of the source quality. Here, we develop a device-agnostic characterisation framework for PDC-based sources with distinguishable signal and idler fields. Treating the source as a black box producing a mixed multimode Gaussian state, we find a lower bound for the state purity, which can be experimentally determined via mode-unresolved measurements. Our approach enables a holistic assessment of realistic squeezed-light source quality without requiring a detailed model of the nonlinear device and its surrounding optical network, providing practical benchmarks for optimizing sources for state-of-the-art quantum applications.

</details>


### [204] [Improved Adaptive Estimation of Quantum Partition Functions with Heisenberg Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06197)
*Yufei Wang,Daniel Stilck França,Samuel Slezak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子算法，以Heisenberg标度估计n量子比特哈密顿量的对数配分函数，误差为ε，并最小化热态纯化制备的调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计量子哈密顿量的配分函数在量子多体物理与量子统计力学中有重要应用。已有非自适应策略的查询复杂度较高，需要设计自适应冷却调度方案以降低对热态纯化制备与块编码的调用次数。

Method: 输入为H的块编码、制备给定逆温度热场双态的酉算子及其逆和受控版本，以及自适应缓变冷却调度。证明常数逆温度下长度O(√n)的调度恒存在，可由重叠估计生成。采用递归倍增恒等式，将每个调度增量表示为热场双态重叠的对数与短虚时间步的加权和，结合量子奇异值变换与方差缩减技术。若热场双态可受控于温度寄存器，则使用量子逆二项对数估计器与量子均值估计。

Result: 在常数逆温度下，长度O(√n)的冷却调度恒存在，可由Õ(√n)次额外制备生成。给定该调度和经典选取的温度，以Õ(n^{5/4}/ε)期望调用次数估计对数配分函数，较非自适应策略改进n^{1/4}。若热场双态可受控制备，查询次数降至Õ(n/ε)，并证明该标度在块编码查询上至多差多对数因子是最优的。

Conclusion: 该工作通过自适应冷却调度与递归倍增恒等式，显著降低了量子配分函数估计的查询复杂度，并给出块编码查询的最优性分析及一维配分函数估计的端到端复杂度讨论。

Abstract: We give quantum algorithms that estimate the log partition function of an $n$-qubit quantum Hamiltonian to additive error $ε$ with Heisenberg scaling, while minimizing calls to thermal-state purification preparation. The input is a block encoding of $H$, unitaries preparing thermofield-double states at chosen inverse temperatures, with their inverses and controlled versions, and an adaptive slowly-varying cooling schedule. The system-size bounds below assume $0\preceq H\preceq hI$ with $h=O(n)$ and block-encoding normalization $α=Θ(h)$. For constant inverse temperature, we show that such a schedule of length $O(\sqrt n)$ always exists and can be generated from overlap estimates with $\widetilde O(\sqrt n)$ further preparations.
  Given such a schedule and classically chosen temperatures, we estimate the log partition function with $\widetilde O(n^{5/4}/ε)$ expected calls to state preparation and to the block encoding, improving by $n^{1/4}$ on non-adaptive strategies. To achieve our result, we use a recursive-doubling identity that expresses each schedule increment as a weighted sum of logarithms of thermofield-double overlaps and of short imaginary-time steps implemented by quantum singular value transformation combined with previously developed variance reduction techniques. If thermofield doubles can be prepared controlled on a temperature register, a quantum inverse-binomial log estimator combined with quantum mean estimation reduces both query counts to $\widetilde O(n/ε)$. We show that the latter scaling is optimal in block-encoding queries up to polylogarithmic factors and discuss end-to-end complexities for one-dimensional partition-function estimation.

</details>


### [205] [Unitary RQL Equals RQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06231)
*Quinten Tupker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在标准门集Γ={H,T,CNOT}下RQL_Γ = RQUL_Γ，保留多项式时间、对数空间与单边（零接受）错误；并将密度矩阵加倍方法推广到一般信道、更广有限门集及量子验证（QMAL1,G = QUMAL1,G）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中间测量可让量子计算丢弃信息、重用工作空间；若将所有测量推迟，则可能需存储完整测量历史，无法保持对数空间。Fefferman与Remscrim已证双边界误差下可消除测量，但其单边界误差（即no实例上接受概率恰为零）是否同样成立仍是开放问题。

Method: 将Girish、Raz与Zhan的密度矩阵加倍方法推广到一般量子信道，涵盖重置操作与经典记忆；对具有精确逆的更广有限门集（含适当的超越元门）证明测量消除；对特定代数门集族建立门集无关性；另用独立的历史检查构造处理量子验证情形。

Result: 对Γ={H,T,CNOT}得到RQL_Γ = RQUL_Γ，同时保持多项式时间、对数空间与no实例上恰好零接受；测量消除可扩展到更广有限门集；并证明QMAL1,G = QUMAL1,G，即在显式门假设下可从对数空间量子验证中消除测量且保持完美完备性。

Conclusion: 测量可在单边界误差、对数空间及完美完备性条件下被消除，回答了[FR21]的开放问题；密度矩阵加倍方法能推广至一般信道、经典记忆与更广门集，并适用于量子验证。

Abstract: Intermediate measurements let quantum computations discard information and reuse their workspace. Deferring all measurements can require storing their entire history, which need not preserve logarithmic space. Fefferman and Remscrim proved that measurements can nevertheless be eliminated with two-sided bounded error, and asked whether the same holds with one-sided error [FR21]. We prove RQLΓ = RQULΓ for the standard gate set Γ = {H, T, CNOT}, preserving polynomial time, logarithmic space, and exactly zero acceptance on no-instances. Our proof extends the density-matrix doubling method of Girish, Raz and Zhan [ GRZ21 ] to general channels, including resets and classical memory. We also prove measurement elimination for broader finite gate sets with exact inverses, including suitable gates with transcendental entries, and establish gate-set independence for a specified family of algebraic gate sets. A separate history-checking construction proves QMAL1,G = QUMAL1,G under explicit gate assumptions: measurements can also be eliminated from logarithmic-space quantum verification while preserving perfect completeness

</details>


### [206] [Probing Quantum Anomalous Hall Transport Under Microwave Irradiation Using a Topological Circulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06245)
*Athul Ashok,Frank Jin,Nick Du,Luis A. Martinez,Jenny Zhou,Sean O'Kelley,Zachary J. -R. Espley,Gang Qiu,Kang L. Wang,Dong-Xia Qu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用量子反常霍尔拓扑环行器研究微波光子对边缘磁等离激元输运的微扰，发现强频率选择性响应，并借助耦合系统的非厄米模式杂化在例外点附近获得约两倍增强的微波响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘磁等离激元（EMP）可用于探测拓扑量子材料中的手性电荷动力学与非互易微波输运，但其传播的微小扰动在传统微波散射测量中信号往往较弱，难以检测。

Method: 基于量子反常霍尔拓扑环行器，采用泵浦—探测（pump–probe）微波测量，考察微波辐照对EMP透射的影响；并通过耦合EMP—谐振腔系统的非厄米模式杂化，在例外点（EP）附近探测系统对微扰的响应。

Result: 响应表现出强频率选择性：若干泵浦频率下EMP透射被显著改变，而在包括4 GHz和7 GHz在内的特定频率下透射几乎不变。例外点附近泵浦引起的透射幅度变化约为远离EP处的两倍，表明微扰响应被增强。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了手性EMP输运、微波光子诱导微扰与非厄米动力学之间的相互作用，表明拓扑环行器可作为增强微波谱学与传感的潜在平台。

Abstract: Edge magnetoplasmons (EMPs) provide a platform for probing chiral charge dynamics and nonreciprocal microwave transport in topological quantum materials. However, detecting small perturbations to EMP propagation remains challenging because their signatures in conventional microwave scattering measurements can be weak. Here, we investigate microwave-photon-induced perturbations of EMP transport using a quantum anomalous Hall topological circulator. Pump--probe measurements reveal a strongly frequency-selective response: while microwave irradiation substantially modifies the EMP transmission at several pump frequencies, the transmission remains nearly unchanged at selected frequencies, including 4 and 7 GHz. We further exploit the non-Hermitian mode hybridization of the coupled EMP-resonator system to probe the response near an exceptional point (EP). The pump-induced change in transmission magnitude near the EP is approximately twice that observed away from the EP, demonstrating an enhanced microwave response to perturbations. These results reveal the interplay among chiral EMP transport, microwave-photon-induced perturbations, and non-Hermitian dynamics, and demonstrate the potential of topological circulators as a promising platform for enhanced microwave spectroscopy and sensing.

</details>


### [207] [High-Fidelity Remote Graph State Preparation for Blind Quantum Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06247)
*Jiawei Cai,Rex Fleur,Benedikt Tissot,Wolfgang Löffler,Tzula B. Propp*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出RGSP框架，用单个高维光子qudit直接制备任意图态，减少甚至消除服务器端纠缠操作，实现拓扑无关的容错性与盲量子计算资源节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有远程态制备(RSP)协议只能制备可分离态，需在远程服务器上执行后续纠缠门，导致资源开销大、依赖服务器纠缠能力。

Method: 将多个量子比特及其图连通性编码进光子的结构化相位轮廓，从单个高维光子qudit直接制备任意图态；针对累积光纤相位漂移，采用"最高权重优先"模式重排策略提升保真度。

Result: 在合理噪声模型下，所得态保真度与图拓扑无关；累积光纤相位漂移下，最高权重优先重排显著提升保真度；六比特量子傅里叶变换的RSP量子比特开销从标准砖墙拓扑的420降至48。

Conclusion: RGSP是资源高效、拓扑不变的量子安全云计算原语，支持任意拓扑上的减少SWAP通用盲量子计算。

Abstract: Measurement-based quantum computation (MBQC) relies on entangled graph states, yet existing remote state preparation (RSP) protocols prepare only separable states, requiring subsequent entangling gates on the remote server. Here, we introduce Remote Graph State Preparation (RGSP), a framework that prepares arbitrary graph states directly from a single high-dimensional photonic qudit. By encoding multiple qubits and their graph connectivity into the photon's structured phase profile, RGSP can reduce or, for small computations, completely eliminate the need for server-side entangling operations among qubits. We show that under a reasonable noise model the resulting state fidelity is independent of the graph topology. Under cumulative fiber phase drift, we demonstrate that this fidelity is significantly enhanced by a ``highest-weight-first'' mode reordering strategy. Finally, we show that RGSP enables reduced-SWAP universal blind quantum computing on arbitrary topologies, reducing qubit overhead e.g., for a six-qubit Quantum Fourier Transform from $420$ RSP qubits with a standard brickwork topology to $48$ RSP qubits. These results establish RGSP as a resource-efficient, topology-invariant primitive for quantum-secured cloud computing.

</details>


### [208] [Suppressing Frequency Collisions in a Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06264)
*Kuljeet Kaur,Xuexin Xu,Mohammad H. Ansari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于哈密顿量的频率分配框架，将量子比特两体/三体碰撞机制映射为Max-k-Cut问题，并用聚类拼接协议扩展到大规模格点，以缓解频率拥挤引起的串扰、泄漏与旁观者误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器中量子比特跃迁频率间距过近导致频率拥挤，进而引发串扰、泄漏和旁观者依赖误差，亟需面向硬件的频率规划方法。

Method: 建立哈密顿量框架，将两体与三体碰撞机制映射为量子比特频率分配上的Max-k-Cut问题；提出聚类拼接协议，将大格点分解为重叠子图并协调局部频率分配以形成全局一致配置；在多种量子比特架构上测试，并对比数字化反绝热QAOA（DC-QAOA）、标准QAOA与模拟退火。

Result: 该框架在多类架构上可抑制主导碰撞惩罚，并揭示硬件残余串扰；反绝热项在陡峭碰撞惩罚附近提升QAOA优化效果，模拟退火在测试实例上获得最低代价。

Conclusion: 该硬件感知优化将微观相互作用物理与处理器级频率规划相连接，为可扩展软硬件协同设计提供了系统化求解器与基准测试手段。

Abstract: Frequency crowding, the close spacing of qubit transitions, limits superconducting quantum processors through crosstalk, leakage, and spectator dependent errors. We introduce a Hamiltonian based framework that maps two- and three-body collision mechanisms onto a Max-k-Cut problem on qubit frequency allocation. In order to make larger lattices tractable, we introduce a cluster-stitching protocol that decomposes larger lattices into overlapping subgraphs and reconciles their local frequency assignments into globally consistent configurations. We tested our proposed framework across multiple qubit architectures to suppress dominant collision penalties by revealing a hardware residual crosstalk. We benchmark digitized counterdiabatic QAOA (DC-QAOA), standard QAOA, and simulated annealing. Counterdiabatic terms improve QAOA optimization near steep collision penalties, while simulated annealing achieves the lowest costs on the tested instances. These results indicate that our hardware-aware optimization connects microscopic interaction physics to processor-level frequency planning. These results enable a systematic solver for benchmarking of scalable software hardware co design.

</details>


### [209] [Adiabatic tracking and optimal schedules for classical and quantum Gibbs-state preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06268)
*Pedro C. S. Costa,Mauro E. S. Morales,Dong An,Kaur Kristjuhan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对经典与量子优化算法中的Gibbs态制备，提出离散绝热框架：对有限时间非齐次可逆Markov链建立跟踪定理，并给出基于谱隙与能量涨落的调度策略；应用于温度依赖Szegedy量子游走时，证明在适当谱隙适配调度下可实现从经典O(δ_min^{-1})到量子O(δ_min^{-1/2})的二次加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs态制备是经典与量子优化算法的核心，但有限时间、离散步骤下的绝热跟踪误差与调度设计缺乏统一分析；同时量子游走中特征相位隙与Markov链谱隙的平方根关系能否转化为实际量子加速，需要严格的离散绝热定理刻画。

Method: 1) 构建有限时间非齐次可逆Markov链的离散绝热跟踪定理，将当前分布到瞬时平稳态的距离分解为既往误差的收缩项与平稳分布逐步变化产生的新误差项；2) 对Gibbs路径给出显式步复杂度界及由Markov链谱隙δ和能量涨落尺度V(β)=sqrt(Var_{π_β}(E))控制的谱隙/涨落适配调度，弱阻尼下调度满足β_dot ∝ δ(β)/V(β)；3) 将离散量子绝热定理应用于温度依赖Szegedy量子游走，分析量子游走本征相位隙Δ_w=Θ(sqrt(δ))及有限差分项。

Result: 量子游走本征相位隙满足Δ_w=Θ(sqrt(δ))，即相对底层Markov链谱隙存在平方根谱放大；该放大仅在适当谱隙适配调度下才能转化为谱隙依赖的二次改进。在相应正则条件下，量子绝热代价为O(δ_min^{-1/2})，而经典松弛尺度为O(δ_min^{-1})。

Conclusion: 离散绝热框架统一刻画了Gibbs态制备的经典与量子跟踪误差与调度；平方根谱放大并不自动带来加速，需配合谱隙适配调度；在正则条件下Szegedy量子游走相对经典Markov链获得谱隙依赖的二次加速。

Abstract: We develop a discrete adiabatic framework for Gibbs-state preparation in classical and quantum optimisation algorithms. For finite time-inhomogeneous reversible Markov chains, we derive a tracking theorem that bounds the distance from the evolving distribution to the instantaneous stationary state by combining two effects: contraction of previously accumulated error by the Markov dynamics and the new error generated as the stationary distribution changes from one step to the next. For Gibbs paths, this leads to explicit step-complexity bounds and to gap- and fluctuation-adapted schedules controlled by the Markov-chain spectral gap and the energy-fluctuation scale $V(β)=\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}_{π_β}(E)}$. In the weak-damping regime, the resulting schedule takes the form $\dotβ\proptoδ(β)/V(β)$. We then apply the discrete quantum adiabatic theorem to temperature-dependent Szegedy quantum walks. The quantum-walk eigenphase gap satisfies $Δ_w=Θ(\sqrtδ)$, where $δ$ is the spectral gap of the underlying Markov chain. By analysing the finite-difference terms entering the theorem, we show that this square-root spectral amplification translates into a quadratic improvement in the gap dependence only for suitably gap-adapted schedules. Under the corresponding regularity conditions, the quantum adiabatic cost scales as $O(δ_{\min}^{-1/2})$, compared with the classical $O(δ_{\min}^{-1})$ relaxation scale.

</details>


### [210] [Two-Sided Bounds on Ground State Properties in Lattice Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06272)
*Eloïc Vallée,Ariel Kelman,Julius Mildenberger,Jordi Tura,Patrick Emonts*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于半定规划层级的方法，为格点规范理论基态能量提供可认证的上下界（并推广至Wilson圈、介子弦等观测量），在(1+1)维Z2理论(129格点)相对区间宽度0.04%，(2+1)维纯规范(7×8=97格点)为2.08%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点规范理论模拟困难：Monte Carlo在符号问题存在时失效；变分方法（量子或经典）仅能给基态能量上界，精度依赖拟设选择，缺乏严格下界与认证保证。

Method: 构建半定规划（SDP）层级（hierarchy）框架，逐级收紧基态能量下界，并将其传播/扩展至Wilson圈、介子弦等延展观测量，从而给出观测量的严格界限。

Result: 在含动态费米子物质的(1+1)维Z2理论及(2+1)维纯规范版本上验证数值能力：两者均获得基态能量的可认证区间。1维129格点系统相对区间宽度0.04%；2维7×8（97格点）为2.08%。同时对多格板Wilson圈、介子弦幅度等短程与长程观测量给出严格界。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为多体物理的计算“显微镜”，并为量子模拟器结果提供认证（certification），弥补变分方法仅得上界的不足。

Abstract: Gauge theories describe fundamental interactions in the standard model of particle physics and build effective theories in condensed matter physics. Being notoriously hard to simulate, these theories are commonly regularized as lattice gauge theories which can be evaluated with high precision through Monte Carlo algorithms or variational methods. However, Monte Carlo algorithms are not applicable in all regimes due to the sign problem; while variational methods, quantum and classical alike, only give upper-bounds on the ground-state energy. Their precision crucially depends on the chosen ansatz. Here, we present a framework based on a hierarchy of semidefinite programs to yield increasingly good lower-bounds on the ground-state energy and propagate these bounds to extended observables like Wilson loops and mesonic strings. We demonstrate the numerical capabilities by applying the algorithm to a (1+1)-dimensional $\mathbb{Z}_2$ theory with dynamic fermionic matter and its pure-gauge version in (2+1)-dimensions. In both cases, we obtain certified intervals for the ground-state energy. For a one-dimensional system with 129 sites, the certified interval has a relative spread of $0.04\%$. For a two-dimensional system with 97 sites (corresponding to a $7 \times 8$ lattice), the relative spread is $2.08\%$. Beyond energy estimates, the proposed method provides rigorous bounds on both short- and long-range observables such as Wilson loops over multiple plaquettes and the magnitude of the mesonic string. The method can be both used as a computational microscope into many-body physics and a way to certify results of quantum simulators.

</details>


### [211] [Robust subspace designs and the power of a unique small quantum witness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06302)
*Simon Apers,Roman Edenhofer,Benjamin Mathieu-Bloise,Partha Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"鲁棒子空间设计"概念，给出多项式规模的概率构造与超多项式规模的显式构造，并将其应用于空间有界量子Valiant-Vazirani定理及零点测试的随机化归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Valiant-Vazirani定理表明NP完全问题限制到至多一个接受见证仍保持难度，但对量子见证（对应接受见证子空间的维数）缺乏类似工具；已有子空间设计只能控制交集大小，无法刻画"方向接近"的量化需求。

Method: 定义鲁棒子空间设计：要求族中不能有过多子空间包含与给定固定维数子空间"接近"的方向，这是对Guruswami-Xing/Guruswami-Kopparty子空间设计的定量强化；给出多项式规模的概率构造和超多项式规模的显式构造；据此孤立空间有界量子Merlin-Arthur协议（完美完备性、接受间隙在其完美接受子空间之外）的唯一见证。

Result: 得到空间有界量子Valiant-Vazirani定理；将良态零点测试随机化归约到空间有界量子Merlin-Arthur协议；并利用普通子空间设计更简单地恢复Allender-Beals-Ogihara关于一般零点测试的C_=L包含关系。

Conclusion: 鲁棒子空间设计是子空间设计的有效定量推广，为量子见证隔离与零点测试提供了统一工具，兼具算法与复杂度类刻画意义。

Abstract: The concept of subspace designs was introduced by Guruswami and Xing (STOC'13), and explicit constructions were given by Guruswami and Kopparty (FOCS'13). These are families of subspaces that have small intersection with any given subspace of a fixed dimension. We introduce \emph{robust subspace designs}. Informally, these are a quantitative extension in which we demand that not too many subspaces of the family contain directions that lie close to any given subspace of a fixed dimension. We give a probabilistic construction of such a robust subspace design of polynomial size, as well as a non-trivial explicit construction of superpolynomial size.
  Our main application of this new concept is a quantum space-bounded variant of the Valiant-Vazirani theorem (Theor.Comput.Sci.'86), which shows that restricting $\mathsf{NP}$-complete problems to instances with at most one accepting witness preserves hardness under randomized reductions. For quantum witnesses, the analogous quantity is the dimension of an accepting witness subspace. We use our probabilistic construction of robust subspace designs to isolate a unique witness for space-bounded quantum Merlin-Arthur protocols with perfect completeness and an acceptance gap outside their perfectly accepting subspace.
  As further applications, we give a randomized reduction of well-conditioned nullity testing to space-bounded quantum Merlin-Arthur protocols with perfect completeness. Using a similar idea, we find that ordinary subspace designs allow us to recover the classical $\mathsf{C_= L}$ containment of Allender, Beals, and Ogihara (STOC'96) for general nullity testing through a simpler proof.

</details>


### [212] [Quantum Leakage from Shamir Sharing with Correlated Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06350)
*Isaac Barouch Essayag,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究Shamir秘密共享中，当各设备利用其份额从与秘密及共享随机性无关的初始量子资源产生一个量子比特时的信息泄露，证明在特定条件下任意两个秘密的泄露态之间具有指数级小的迹距离，从而给出安全性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子设备可能在与秘密无关的初始量子资源上产生泄露，需量化Shamir秘密共享在此类量子泄露下的安全性，明确其泄露上界。

Method: 将泄露刻画为设备边际态，比较任意两个秘密对应的泄露态。对满足上算子混合条件的固定平稳链，以及由固定有限量子记忆与原始转移信道生成的资源，证明迹距离上界；对独立设备-参考对确定最优指数底数，再通过比较所选边际与乘积态，将独立对的界转化为相关资源的安全保证。

Result: 当重建所需份额比例足够大时，任意两个秘密的泄露态之间迹距离指数级小；该保证允许设备边际任意扩展为敌方侧信息，并对大于份额数的素数域一致成立。在需全部份额重建时，独立设备-参考对的最优指数底数为：量子比特 2√2/π，经典比特 2/π，且均允许任意有限参考。

Conclusion: Shamir秘密共享在此类量子泄露模型下仍具指数级安全保证；结果覆盖平稳链与有限量子记忆资源，并可推广至相关资源，给出泄漏态的收敛性质与最优指数常数。

Abstract: We study leakage from Shamir secret sharing when each device uses its share to produce one qubit from an initial quantum resource independent of the secret and sharing randomness. We prove exponentially small trace distance between the leakage states of any two secrets, when reconstruction requires a sufficiently large fraction of the shares, for fixed stationary chains satisfying an upper operator mixing condition and for resources generated by fixed finite quantum memories with primitive transfer channels. These guarantees permit arbitrary extensions of the device marginal as adversary side information and hold uniformly over prime fields larger than the number of shares. For independent device--reference pairs, which provide the quantitative input to the argument, we determine the optimal exponential base when reconstruction requires all shares: $2\sqrt2/π$ for a qubit and $2/π$ for a classical bit, in both cases allowing arbitrary finite references. A comparison of selected marginals with product states converts the bounds for independent device--reference pairs into security guarantees for the correlated resources.

</details>


### [213] [Adaptive complete quantum search with unknown number of solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06364)
*Raj Alexandru Guţoiu,Bogdan-Călin Ciobanu,Pantelimon George Popescu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究解数目未知的无结构数据库搜索问题，提出统一经典搜索、Grover搜索与广义定点量子搜索的自适应贝叶斯方法，在单位执行时间内最大化期望成功概率，并证明其在特定条件下渐近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover算法在标记元素数目已知时可实现二次加速，但当解的数目未知且需要找出全部解时，问题更具挑战性。现有方法缺乏对经典搜索、Grover搜索和广义定点量子搜索等不同搜索模式组合的系统分析，也未明确何时应选择哪种策略。

Method: 作者系统分析经典实例、Grover实例与广义定点量子搜索实例的不同组合情形，将其统一为一个自适应贝叶斯框架，在每次搜索实例中最大化单位执行时间的期望成功概率；并利用解数目的概率分布积分近似，确定经典采样与量子搜索之间的最优切换点。

Result: 对于解数目远小于数据库规模且已知的情形，证明所提方法在所考虑的搜索模型内渐近最优；证明对任意刻画可能解数目的概率分布，当经典验证时间至多为量子振幅放大时间的25%时，混合经典-Grover方法优于广义定点方法；在该范围之外，给出定点搜索相对最佳Grover类得分可实现的最大改进上界。数值模拟及定点与Grover策略对比支持了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为解数目未知的无结构数据库搜索提供了统一的自适应贝叶斯优化框架，明确了经典、Grover与定点量子搜索策略的适用区间，并刻画了定点搜索相对Grover搜索的性能增益边界及最优切换条件。

Abstract: Unstructured database search is a fundamental problem that requires finding one or more elements satisfying a specific property within a set of candidates. While Grover's algorithm provides a quadratic speedup for finding a marked element when the number of solutions is known, finding all solutions when their number is unknown presents additional challenges. In this paper, we thoroughly analyze this problem by considering all the different regimes of combinations of classical, Grover and generalized fixed-point quantum search instances. We unify these into an adaptive Bayesian approach that maximizes the expected success probability per unit execution time at each searching instance. For a problem with a known number of solutions, much lower than the database size, we prove that our proposed approach is asymptotically optimal within the considered search model. Furthermore, we prove that for any probability distribution characterizing the likely number of solutions, in regimes where the classical verification time is at most 25% of the quantum amplitude amplification time, hybrid classical and Grover approaches are preferred to generalized fixed-point. Outside of this regime, we derive an upper bound on the maximal improvement achievable by fixed-point search compared to the best Grover-based score. Additionally, we determine the optimal transition point between classical sampling and quantum searching using an integral approximation of the probability distribution of the number of solutions. The analytical results are also supported by numerical simulations of the proposed algorithm and by comparisons between the generalized fixed-point and Grover-based search policies.

</details>


### [214] [Value of spatially multiplexed SNSPDs with photon-number-resolving capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06388)
*Fabio Auriemma,Mikkel Ejrnaes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立了组合与贝叶斯框架，量化多路复用SNSPD系统中提升局部光子数分辨能力所获得的信息增益，推导任意可分辨态数下的响应张量、广义N光子探测保真度与贝叶斯光子数重构及其保真度，并证明局部分辨提升可系统改善探测与重构性能、显著放宽二元阵列对探测器数目的二次标度要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究认为单个SNSPD可超越典型开/关探测机制而具备光子数分辨能力，但既有理论表述多针对二元器件。为评估"提升局部光子数分辨"这一设计选择的价值，需要把多路复用系统推广到任意可分辨状态数，并给出信息量的定量评估。

Method: 构建组合与贝叶斯框架：推导基于具有任意可分辨状态数SNSPD的多路复用系统的概率响应张量，将既有二元器件表述推广；通过二项式损耗卷积引入有限效率，并单独评估暗计数影响；由响应张量导出广义N光子探测保真度、贝叶斯光子数重构及相应重构保真度；并对不同探测器数目的2态、3态和4态多路复用系统进行具体化研究。

Result: 理论推导给出任意可分辨态数SNSPD多路复用系统的响应张量与广义保真度度量。数值研究表明，提高局部光子数分辨能力可系统性地同时改善探测保真度与贝叶斯光子数重构效果，并显著放宽纯二元多路复用阵列中探测器数目与可分辨光子数之间的二次标度约束。

Conclusion: 该工作为多状态光子数分辨探测器阵列提供了定量的信息价值评估，说明提升局部光子数分辨是缓解多路复用规模压力的有效途径。

Abstract: We develop a combinatorial and Bayesian framework to quantify the information gained by increasing the local photon-number resolution of multiplexed detection systems based on superconducting nanowire single-photon detectors (SNSPDs). Motivated by recent studies that attribute photon-number resolving capabilities to a single SNSPD overcoming the typical on/off detection mechanism, we generalize the existing formulation for the binary devices by deriving the probability response tensor of multiplexed systems based on SNSPDs with an arbitrary number of resolvable states. We include finite efficiency through binomial loss convolution and, separately, assess the impact of dark counts. From this response tensor we derive a generalized N-photon detector fidelity, a Bayesian photon-number reconstruction, and the corresponding reconstruction fidelity. We particularize this formulation to study 2-, 3- and 4-state multiplexed systems with different numbers of detectors and show that increasing local photon-number resolution systematically improves both detector fidelity and Bayesian photon-number reconstruction. This substantially relaxes the quadratic scaling between detector number and resolvable photon-number required by purely binary multiplexed arrays. These results provide a quantitative value-of-information assessment for multi-state photon-number-resolving detector arrays.

</details>


### [215] [When is the Clifford hierarchy generalized semi-Clifford?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06402)
*Rongbiao Thomas Wang,Bobby Zixuan Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Clifford层级中的semi-Clifford与generalized semi-Clifford结构随层级k与量子比特数n的变化，刻画了成立条件、构造了反例，并提出n_c-generalized semi-Clifford门的统一形式与相关猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifford层级是量子门结构性刻画与容错量子计算的核心对象。理解其中哪些门具有semi-Clifford或generalized semi-Clifford形式，有助于统一描述层级门的代数结构，并细化Zeng、Chen、Chuang的既有猜想，但此前缺乏对k与n依赖关系的系统结果。

Method: 以层级k与量子比特数n为参数，研究Clifford层级\mathcal{C}_k(n)的结构性质：判定semi-Clifford成立的充要条件；判断generalized semi-Clifford成立范围并构造k,n≥5的反例；对n=4、k≥5，证明定义在\mathbb{Q}(i)上的门（至标量）为generalized semi-Clifford并将一般情形归约到单一层级；引入n_c-generalized semi-Clifford门并给出其等价形式（至Clifford门）。

Result: \mathcal{C}_k(n)为semi-Clifford当且仅当k≤2、n≤2，或k=3且n≤6；当k≤4或n≤3时为generalized semi-Clifford，而k,n≥5时存在反例；n=4、k≥5时，\mathbb{Q}(i)上的门（至标量）为generalized semi-Clifford，并给出归约策略；n_c-generalized semi-Clifford门恰为\sum_x|π(x)⟩⟨x|⊗V_x（至两侧Clifford门），其中π置换n_c个控制比特的计算基态。

Conclusion: semi-Clifford与generalized semi-Clifford性质对k、n高度敏感，仅在低层级或低比特数时普遍成立。作者猜想\mathcal{C}_k(n)为generalized semi-Clifford当且仅当k≤4或n≤4；并进一步猜想k≥3时\mathcal{C}_k(n)中每个门均具n_c-generalized形式，且各V_x∈\mathcal{C}_{k-3}(n-n_c)，从而细化Zeng、Chen、Chuang的猜想。

Abstract: We study semi-Clifford and generalized semi-Clifford structures in the Clifford hierarchy as a function of hierarchy level $k$ and qubit number $n$. We show that $\mathcal{C}_k(n)$ is semi-Clifford if and only if $k\le2$, $n\le2$, or $k=3$ and $n\le6$. We also establish that $\mathcal{C}_k(n)$ is generalized semi-Clifford when $k\le4$ or $n\le3$ and construct counterexamples when $k,n \geq 5$. For $n=4$ and $k\ge5$, we show that every gate defined over $\mathbb{Q}(i)$ up to a scalar is generalized semi-Clifford, reduce the general case to a single level, and propose a strategy to prove our conjecture that $\mathcal{C}_k(n)$ is generalized semi-Clifford if and only if $k\le4$ or $n\le4$. Lastly, we go beyond this structure by introducing $n_c$-generalized semi-Clifford gates, which we show are exactly the gates of the form $\sum_x|π(x)\rangle\langle x|\otimes V_x$ up to Clifford gates on both sides, where $π$ permutes the computational basis states of $n_c$ control qubits. We conjecture that for $k\ge3$ every gate in $\mathcal{C}_k(n)$ has this form for some $n_c\ge1$ with every $V_x\in\mathcal{C}_{k-3}(n-n_c)$, refining the conjecture of Zeng, Chen, and Chuang.

</details>


### [216] [A programmable quantum photonic platform integrating coherent emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06451)
*Hanna Salamon,Mikkel T. Mikkelsen,Zhe Liu,Peter Lodahl,Severin Krüger,Arne Ludwig,Leonardo Midolo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一个可编程量子光子平台，可在低温下同时控制高相干量子点单光子发射器与可重构光学元件，实现片上系统级集成与多种量子光子功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子量子技术需要将高质量量子光源与可编程光子电路集成以实现片上量子信息处理；半导体量子点虽是领先的量子光源平台，但其与低温兼容、可编程光子电路的单片集成仍具挑战。

Method: 通过系统级集成，在单一芯片上实现对有源器件的独立电学与光学寻址，使其在保持相干非经典光发射的同时并发运行；并演示纯单光子产生、双光子干涉以及集成发射器的共振透射与反射测量等功能。

Result: 实现了低温下量子点单光子发射器与可重构光学元件的同步控制，并成功演示纯单光子产生、双光子干涉及共振透射/反射测量等多重量子光子功能。

Conclusion: 该成果标志着从孤立量子光学器件迈向可编程量子光子硬件平台，能够将相干量子发射器与大规模光子功能相结合。

Abstract: Photonic quantum technologies require the integration of high-quality quantum light sources with programmable photonic circuitry to enable on-chip quantum information processing. Semiconductor quantum dots have emerged as a leading platform for quantum light generation, yet their monolithic integration with cryogenic-compatible, programmable photonic circuitry remains challenging. Here we demonstrate a programmable quantum photonic platform that enables simultaneous control of highly coherent quantum dot-based single photon emitters and reconfigurable optical elements at cryogenic temperatures. System-level integration enables individual electrical and optical addressing of active components on a single chip, allowing their concurrent operation while preserving coherent non-classical light emission. We demonstrate multiple quantum-photonic functionalities, including pure single-photon generation, two-photon interference, and resonant transmission and reflection measurements of integrated emitters. These results mark a step from isolated quantum-optical devices towards programmable quantum-photonic hardware platforms capable of combining coherent quantum emitters with large-scale photonic functionality.

</details>


### [217] [The right entanglement for perfect nonlocality and zero-error communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06512)
*Laura Mančinska*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造Flagged Magic-Square博弈，证明完美策略要求并自检验Schmidt系数正比于(1,√2,√2,√2,√2)的非最大纠缠态，并给出博弈到信道的归约，得到经典信道中纠缠可实现单次十消息零误差传输而最大纠缠至多九消息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非局域博弈中完美策略是否必须使用特定非最大纠缠态，以及最大纠缠态是否为经典信道零误差通信的通用最优纠缠资源。

Method: 在标准Magic-Square博弈基础上增加"flag"结果与每人两个附加问题，构造Flagged Magic-Square博弈；给出代数证明最大纠缠策略胜率有界远离1；建立博弈到信道的通用归约。

Result: 该博弈存在使用Schmidt系数为(1,√2,√2,√2,√2)的态的完美策略；任意维度最大纠缠策略胜率有上界；博弈自检验该态；归约后得到经典信道，纠缠可单次零误差传十条消息，最大纠缠最多九条。

Conclusion: 最大纠缠态并非经典噪声信道零误差通信的通用最优纠缠资源；对刚性测量几何施加约束可强制特定Schmidt比例，并提供基于其他刚性伪心灵感应博弈的模块化自检验路径。

Abstract: We identify a structural mechanism by which perfect play in a nonlocal game requires and self-tests a specific non-maximally entangled state. To this end, we construct a Flagged Magic-Square game by extending the standard Magic-Square game with an additional ``flag'' outcome and two additional questions for each player. The game admits a perfect strategy using a state with Schmidt coefficients proportional to $(1,\sqrt{2},\sqrt{2},\sqrt{2},\sqrt{2})$. We give a direct algebraic proof that maximally entangled strategies have winning probability bounded away from one, independently of dimension. Moreover, the game self-tests this state, providing an example of a two-party pseudo-telepathy game that certifies a specific non-maximally entangled state. Our construction illustrates how adding suitable constraints to a rigid measurement geometry can enforce specific Schmidt ratios and provides a modular route to further self-tests based on other rigid pseudo-telepathy games.
  We establish a general game-to-channel reduction that transfers separations in the game model to one-shot zero-error communication over classical channels. Applied to the Flagged Magic-Square game, it yields a classical channel for which entanglement permits the zero-error transmission of ten messages in a single use, whereas any finite-dimensional maximally entangled resource permits at most nine. Thus, maximally entangled states are not a universally optimal entanglement resource for zero-error communication over classical noisy channels.

</details>


### [218] [Classical Simulation of Lossy Gaussian Boson Sampling beyond Square-Root Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06526)
*Youngrong Lim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 光子损耗使高斯玻色采样更容易被经典模拟。作者证明"用经典光替代损耗输入"的方法在存活光子数增长超过√N后必然失效，这一尺度是此类方法的极限；转而采用"近似探测而非输入"的新方法，可将误差控制为O(Nη³)，使平均存活光子数在固定精度下增长至N^{2/3}，等强度压缩时可达N^{3/4}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯玻色采样（GBS）被视为展示量子优势的候选方案，但光子损耗会削弱其量子特性，使其更易被经典算法模拟。此前的主流模拟策略是用经典光替代损耗后的输入态，但在存活光子数增长到一定规模后其精度无法保证。作者希望厘清这一替代策略的适用极限，并探索能否突破该极限，从而更精确地刻画损耗下GBS的经典可模拟边界。

Method: 首先从理论上证明"经典光替代输入"方法的尺度极限：对显式干涉仪，任何具有非负Glauber–Sudarshan P表示（无论高斯与否、无论是否含关联）的态，都无法在存活光子数均值超过√N（N为压缩输入数，固定压缩度与精度）时保持光子数分布误差足够小。随后提出新方法——近似探测而非近似输入：采样器在两次探测之间对量子态进行精确演化，每次探测后将条件态替换为具有相同协方差的高斯态。

Result: 新的探测近似方法对任意干涉仪，其全变差误差为O(Nη³)（η为光子存活概率），且期望代价为多项式级。由此，在固定精度下平均存活光子数可增长至N^{2/3}。此外，通过一套独立的配对构造，当每个输入模式被等量压缩时，存活光子数可进一步提升至N^{3/4}。

Conclusion: "用经典光替代损耗输入"存在固有尺度极限√N，无法通过引入关联或非高斯性突破。改以"近似探测"为核心的新方法突破了该极限，在多项式代价下将经典模拟的适用范围扩展至存活光子数N^{2/3}（等压缩时N^{3/4}），从而更精确地界定了损耗GBS的经典可模拟边界。

Abstract: Photon loss makes Gaussian boson sampling easier to simulate classically. A standard approach replaces the lossy input by classical light, and it stays accurate while the mean number of surviving photons grows no faster than the square root of the number $N$ of squeezed inputs at fixed squeezing and accuracy. We prove that this scale is a limit of replacement by classical light. For explicit interferometers, no state with a nonnegative Glauber--Sudarshan $P$ representation, Gaussian or not and however correlated, keeps the error in the photon count distribution small beyond this scale. We then surpass this limit by approximating the detection instead of the input. The sampler evolves the state exactly between detections and, after each detection, replaces the conditional state by a Gaussian with the same covariance. For every interferometer its total variation error is $O(Nη^3)$ at polynomial expected cost, where $η$ is the photon survival probability. The mean surviving photon number can therefore grow as $N^{2/3}$ at fixed accuracy. A separate pair construction reaches $N^{3/4}$ when every input mode is equally squeezed.

</details>


### [219] [Closed-Form Arbitrary-Gap Rényi Nonadditivity Above Any Positive Order via Direct Products of Free Groups and High-Girth Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06533)
*Guocheng Zhen,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了最小输出Rényi熵可加性被极大违反的量子信道的闭式有限维构造：对任意p0>0与目标间隙g>0，构造单一信道与Bell输入使所有p∈[p0,∞]上的可加性间隙同时大于g，使用15r个输出量子比特与O(r2^{30r}+r²(1+p0⁻¹))个输入量子比特（r=⌈2g⌉）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小输出Rényi熵的可加性问题是量子信息论中的核心问题，已知可被违反，但既有构造在参数（输出/输入量子比特数）上不够优。本文旨在给出更高效的有限维显式构造，降低实现可加性间隙所需的资源，并改进Shou与Gorshkov(2026)的参数。

Method: 基于自由群直积与固定域高围长图的不同有限实现，构造具有实代数元素的量子信道的闭式有限维表示。对每个p0>0、g>0构造单一信道与单一Bell输入，使间隙在p∈[p0,∞]上一致大于g；取r=⌈2g⌉，构造使用15r个输出量子比特与O(r2^{30r}+r²(1+p0⁻¹))个输入量子比特。在von Neumann序处另作专门化得到显式信道。

Result: 对每个目标间隙，构造使用的输出量子比特数均少于Shou-Gorshkov(2026)所述参数；当间隙增大而正截断固定时，输入量子比特的渐近上界也更小。在von Neumann序处，单独专门化给出一个显式信道，其间隙大于1/1024，使用37,608,898个输入量子比特与8个输出量子比特。

Conclusion: 该构造在参数效率上全面改进已有结果：输出量子比特数更少，渐近输入量子比特上界更小，并给出von Neumann序下间隙>1/1024的显式低输出维信道。

Abstract: We give a closed-form finite-dimensional construction of quantum channels with real algebraic entries exhibiting arbitrarily large violations of minimum-output Rényi entropy additivity uniformly above any prescribed positive Rényi order, using a different finite realization based on direct products of free groups and fixed-field high-girth graphs. More precisely, for every $p_0>0$ and $g>0$, we construct one channel and one Bell input for which the additivity gap is greater than $g$ simultaneously for all $p\in[p_0,\infty]$. With $r=\lceil2g\rceil$, the construction uses $15r$ output qubits and $O(r2^{30r}+r^2(1+p_0^{-1}))$ input qubits. Compared with the stated parameters of Shou and Gorshkov \cite{ShouGorshkov2026}, our construction uses fewer output qubits for every target gap and has a smaller asymptotic input-qubit bound as the gap grows with the positive cutoff fixed. At the von Neumann order, a separate specialization gives an explicit channel with gap greater than $1/1024$ using $37608898$ input qubits and $8$ output qubits.

</details>


### [220] [The Mermin-Peres Magic Square Game With Three or More Players](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06534)
*Itamar Lorian,J. E. Avron*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Mermin-Peres魔方博弈推广到N人N维超立方体版本：最优经典策略胜率恒为8/9，而N≥3时量子策略胜率仅介于1/3与3/4之间，量子优势因monogamy效应消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Mermin-Peres魔方博弈（N=2）展示了量子策略相对经典策略的显著优势（胜率1优于8/9）。作者希望考察这一量子优势在多玩家（N人）情形下能否保持，从而理解非局域性与纠缠共享的限制。

Method: 将魔方博弈中的方阵替换为N维超立方体，构造N玩家博弈；分析最优经典策略的胜率，并将N=2的量子策略推广到N≥3的情形，通过计算其量子胜率上界与下界来评估量子优势。

Result: 最优经典策略的胜率对所有N均为8/9；对于N≥3，推广的量子策略胜率介于1/3和3/4之间，显著低于经典策略的8/9，即量子策略不再占优。

Conclusion: Mermin-Peres魔方博弈的量子优势不能推广到N≥3的多玩家情形，其根源在于monogamy（纠缠的不可分享性）限制：一个量子比特无法与多个玩家同时保持强纠缠，导致量子关联的协同优势丧失。

Abstract: We present a generalization of the Mermin-Peres magic square game to $N$ players, in which the square is replaced by an $N$-dimensional hypercube. We show that the winning probability of the optimal classical strategy remains $8/9$ for all $N$, while for $N \ge 3$, a generalization of the quantum strategy for $N = 2$ achieves a winning probability between $1/3$ and $3/4$. This loss of quantum advantage for $N \ge 3$ is a consequence of monogamy.

</details>


### [221] [Symmetry and AI-assisted discovery of magic-state factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06535)
*Shubham P. Jain,Adam Wills,Shraddha Singh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出对称性与AI辅助搜索方法，统一二进制矩阵表述，在距离≥3的魔态蒸馏码搜索中获得699个工厂类（564个新），包含低开销纯T工厂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔态蒸馏是容错量子计算的主要资源开销，工厂成本强烈依赖失败率（随输入魔态数增长）。距离2搜索已因对称性受限方法而可行，但距离≥3、中等输入数量的搜索仍难以实现。

Method: 建立统一二进制矩阵表述，涵盖三正交码与直接电路搜索；证明距离≥3等价于非零且两两不同的校验子（syndrome）；将校验子选择与兼容输出门搜索解耦；用群对称性和语言模型智能体约束校验子搜索，再用确定性求解与独立验证。

Result: 搜索得到699个工厂类，其中564个为新的；包含纯T工厂及输出纠缠的T、CS、CCZ组合工厂。纯T工厂[[63,11,3]]（γ=1.589）和[[850,128,6]]（γ=1.057）在≤100和≤1000输入协议中取得已知最低开销指数；[[1715,287,6]]（γ=0.998）是已知最小γ<1的纯T工厂。

Conclusion: 提供含搜索简报与活动笔记的上下文目录，供读者训练自有智能体并定制搜索；据此开始构建面向量子社区的开源魔态蒸馏协议活跃仓库，附方法与数据，服务于实用容错量子计算场景。

Abstract: Magic-state distillation is a major resource cost in fault-tolerant quantum computing. The cost of a magic-state factory depends strongly on its failure rate, which grows with the number of input magic states. Although symmetry-restricted methods have recently made distance two searches tractable, distance three and above have remained elusive at moderate input counts. We develop symmetry- and AI-assisted methods to search this regime. We present a unified binary-matrix formulation encompassing both triorthogonal-code and direct circuit searches. We show that distance at least three is equivalent to nonzero, pairwise distinct syndromes, separating the choice of syndromes from the search for compatible output gates. We restrict the syndrome search using group symmetry and language-model agents, followed by deterministic solving and independent verification. Our searches yield 699 factory classes, including 564 new ones. These include factories for pure-T states and factories with entangled outputs comprising combinations of T, CS, and CCZ magic states. The pure-T factories [[63, 11, 3]] and [[850, 128, 6]] achieve the lowest overhead exponents we know among protocols with at most 100 and 1000 inputs, respectively, with $γ= 1.589$ and $γ= 1.057$. Our [[1715, 287, 6]] factory, with $γ= 0.998$, is the smallest known pure-T factory with $γ< 1$. We also provide a context directory of search briefs and campaign notes with which readers can train their own agents and tailor the search to their requirements. With these results, we begin constructing an active, open-source repository of magic-state distillation protocols for the quantum community, supplemented by our methods and data, for the practical fault-tolerant quantum computing regime.

</details>


### [222] [Products of unitaries as continuous evolutions at finite precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06541)
*Michael Jarret*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种离散化连续酉演化的方法，在连续酉演化与有限维酉乘积间建立双向对应，将离散绝热定理约化为连续绝热定理；算子范数误差为 O(1/T)，并应用于量子线性系统，得到泄漏 δ 时 O(κ/δ) 步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立连续酉演化与有限维酉乘积之间的双向对应，从而把离散绝热定理约化为连续绝热定理，避免单独依赖离散绝热定理，并将连续模拟与连续绝热界迁移到相位修正后的乘积上。

Method: 在合适有限分割上取受调控哈密顿量的截断样本得到逼近乘积；对时钟取整，将 T 个单位步样本的乘积与连续演化对应，相差两个半步步相位；利用每步内相消将算子范数距离界定为 O(1/T)。假设一阶、二阶导数有界、谱宽有界，且特征值差一致远离 2π 的非零倍数；对满足导数界的酉行走，用固定公共自由谱弧给出满足比较假设的 Hermitian 对数路径。

Result: 在所述假设下，得到算子范数距离 O(1/T) 的显式加性误差界；连续模拟保证可迁移到相位修正乘积，连续绝热界可迁移到乘积本身，并保留各绝热定理假设；对条件数 κ 的正定量子线性系统，该比较与连续绝热理论给出重标度量子化行走在泄漏 δ 时 O(κ/δ) 步，与既有离散绝热定理所得标度一致。

Conclusion: 该离散化对应可将离散绝热问题归约到连续绝热框架，误差 O(1/T)，并在量子线性系统上复现 O(κ/δ) 步的标度，且这些乘积构造无需离散绝热定理。

Abstract: I present a short approach to discretizing continuous unitary evolution. It gives a correspondence in both directions between continuous unitary evolutions and products of unitaries in finite dimensions, and I use it to reduce discrete adiabatic theorems to continuous ones. At fine steps, holding truncated samples of a regulated Hamiltonian on a suitable finite partition gives an approximating product. Conversely, rounding the clock identifies a product of $T$ unit-step samples with a continuous evolution, up to two half-step phases. I bound its operator-norm distance from the original evolution by $O(1/T)$ through cancellation within each time step. This assumes bounded first and second derivatives, bounded spectral width, and eigenvalue differences bounded away from nonzero multiples of $2π$, uniformly in $T$. For a unitary walk with these derivative bounds, a fixed common free spectral arc supplies a Hermitian logarithm path satisfying the comparison hypotheses. Continuous simulation guarantees then transfer to the phase-corrected product, and continuous adiabatic bounds to the product itself, with an explicit additive $O(1/T)$ error and each adiabatic theorem's hypotheses retained. For positive definite quantum linear systems of condition number $κ$, this comparison and continuous adiabatic theory give a rescaled qubitized walk with $O(κ/δ)$ steps for leakage $δ$, matching the scaling previously obtained through a discrete adiabatic theorem. These products need no discrete adiabatic theorem.

</details>


### [223] [Mid-circuit measurements and feedforward implement every unitary exactly in constant depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06551)
*Chenfeng Cao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明带前馈的中途测量可将精确n量子比特酉合成的最优最坏情况量子深度从Θ(n)降至O(1)，并给出确定性构造及深度-宽度权衡的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低精确n量子比特酉合成的最坏情况量子深度：利用带前馈的中途测量打破无前馈测量与重置所受的电路因果锥限制。

Method: 确定性构造：使用单量子比特测量、O(1)轮奇偶前馈及O(n4^n log(n+2))个总量子比特，辅助比特初末均为|0>；Teleport Nehoran-Yuen近似方案的矩阵误差，通过精确制备的辅助态编码该误差，以相干干涉加回；在算子范数误差2^{-n}下单步振幅放大足够；另用相干扇出电路及仅一、二量子比特门电路对比。

Result: 带前馈中途测量下精确合成达到常数深度；相干扇出电路同样达到常数深度界；仅用一、二量子比特门电路深度为Θ(n)且门数为O(4^n log^2(n+2))；深度h下通用目标需Ω(4^n/(h+1))量子比特（即使经典控制不受限）；所给深度-宽度权衡在1≤h≤2^n时与下界相差O(n^2)因子。

Conclusion: 带前馈的中途测量可实现常数深度精确酉合成，深度-宽度权衡接近通用下界（因子O(n^2)），经典处理不计入量子深度。

Abstract: Mid-circuit measurements with feedforward reduce the optimal worst-case quantum depth for exact $n$-qubit unitary synthesis from $Θ(n)$ to $O(1)$, using arbitrary one- and two-qubit gates and unrestricted ancillary space. Measurements and resets without feedforward remain subject to the circuit causal-cone bound. Our deterministic construction uses single-qubit measurements, $O(1)$ rounds of parity feedforward, and $O(n4^n\log(n+2))$ total qubits, with all ancillas starting and ending in $|0\rangle$. Classical processing is not counted in quantum depth. We teleport only the matrix error of an approximation by Nehoran and Yuen. An exactly prepared ancillary state encodes this error, whose action is added back by coherent interference. At operator-norm error $2^{-n}$, one step of amplitude amplification suffices. Coherent fanout circuits achieve the same constant-depth bound, while circuits using only one- and two-qubit gates attain depth $Θ(n)$ with $O(4^n\log^2(n+2))$ gates. Generic targets require $Ω(4^n/(h+1))$ qubits at depth $h$, even under unrestricted classical control. Our depth-width trade-offs meet this bound within a factor $O(n^2)$ for $1\le h\le2^n$.

</details>


### [224] [CT-bMPS: Approximate Tensor Network Contraction with Boundary MPS on Contraction Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06574)
*Hidetaka Manabe,Feng Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出收缩树边界矩阵乘积态（CT-bMPS），一种针对一般闭张量网络的可扩展近似收缩方法，将边界张量及其互补环境表示为矩阵乘积态，实现基于环境的局部压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般闭张量网络的精确收缩代价随网络规模指数增长，现有通用近似收缩方法在精度与效率上存在不足，且难以处理更大更复杂的网络，因此需要一种兼顾可扩展性与准确性的近似收缩框架。

Method: CT-bMPS 在收缩树的每条边上，将边界张量及其互补环境同时表示为矩阵乘积态；利用约化转移矩阵进行局部压缩，从而沿任意收缩树纳入环境信息，同时保持时间与内存开销在网络规模和键维数上为多项式级；并采用优化的收缩树与环境更新策略。

Result: 在Ising配分函数、随机张量网络、kicked Ising动力学以及量子纠错解码等多种模型与应用上进行了基准测试，相比现有通用近似收缩方法在精度和计算效率上均有提升，并能处理更大、更复杂的网络；优化收缩树与环境更新可在不增加键维数的情况下显著提高精度。

Conclusion: CT-bMPS 是一种通用且可扩展的近似收缩框架，具备灵活的收缩顺序与基于环境的压缩能力，适用于广泛的应用场景。

Abstract: We introduce contraction tree boundary matrix product states (CT-bMPS), a scalable approximate contraction method for general closed tensor networks. CT-bMPS represents the boundary tensor and its complementary environment at each edge of a contraction tree as matrix product states. This representation allows local compression based on a reduced transition matrix to incorporate environment information along arbitrary contraction trees, while keeping the time and memory costs polynomial in the network size and bond dimension. We benchmark the method on a variety of models and applications, including Ising partition functions, random tensor networks, kicked Ising dynamics, and quantum error correction decoding. These benchmarks demonstrate improved accuracy and computational efficiency over existing general-purpose approximate contraction methods, together with the ability to handle larger and more complex networks. Optimized contraction trees and environment updates substantially improve accuracy without increasing the bond dimension. These results establish CT-bMPS as a general and scalable approximation framework with flexible contraction orders and environment-based compression for a wide range of applications.

</details>


### [225] [Shaping double descent in quantum extreme learning through model design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06581)
*Sabri Meyer,Ege Yilmaz,Aurelien Lucchi,Francesco Tacchino,Francesco Scala*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文为相关特征下的双重下降曲线给出了经典的线性回归解析刻画，并将其应用于量子极端学习机（QELM），揭示了编码强度、输入重上传次数与测量次数三个设计旋钮通过正则化输出协方差稳定插值阈值，从而获得超越插值仍良好泛化的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双重下降现象在独立特征下已被充分理解，但相关特征情形下的风险曲线形态虽由特征协方差谱决定，现有分析通常只能给出隐式方程，缺乏显式的解析刻画；同时量子模型（如QELM）中设计选择如何影响双重下降尚不明确。

Method: 1) 对经典线性回归，用特征总体协方差的条件数以及一个刻画相关强度的谱测度这两个量，解析地刻画整条风险曲线，弥补已有工作仅给隐式方程的不足。2) 将该理论推广至量子模型，重点分析量子极端学习机（QELM）。3) 研究编码强度、输入重上传、测量次数三个可调设计旋钮对QELM输出协方差的正则化作用，并推导插值阈值保持稳定的条件；同时考察有限测量散粒噪声对稳定性的保障作用。4) 通过数值实验验证理论预测。

Result: 理论上给出了相关特征下线性回归整条风险曲线的显式解析形式；在QELM上证明了三个设计旋钮均对输出协方差起正则化作用，并给出插值阈值保持稳定的条件，且有限测量散粒噪声可用于保证该稳定性。数值实验逐一验证了各项预测。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅厘清了相关特征下双重下降的解析结构，还为QELM提供了具体的设计原则：通过调节编码强度、输入重上传次数与测量次数（含利用有限散粒噪声）来稳定插值阈值，使模型在超越插值后仍具有良好泛化性能。

Abstract: Increasing capacity can induce a machine learning model to generalize worse until it perfectly interpolates the training data, and better once it moves beyond interpolation. This inversion, known as \emph{double descent}, is well understood for independent features. For correlated features, its shape is governed by the spectrum of the feature covariance, but existing analyses typically yield only implicit equations. We first close this gap for classical linear regression, analytically characterizing the entire risk curve through two quantities, namely the condition number of the feature population covariance and a spectral measure of correlation strength. Then, we apply this theory on quantum models, focusing on quantum extreme learning machines (QELMs). We show that three tunable design knobs, \textit{i.e.,} encoding strength, input reuploads, and measurement shots, each regularize the QELM output covariance, and derive conditions under which the interpolation threshold remains stable. Finite measurement shot noise can be leveraged to guarantee this stability. Numerical experiments confirm each prediction, yielding concrete design principles for QELMs that generalize well beyond interpolation.

</details>


### [226] [Information causality does not single out the set of quantum correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06604)
*Marco Pompili,Mirjam Weilenmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明信息因果性（IC）不能刻画量子关联：对任意 n≥2 存在满足 IC 但含后量子关联的关联集，甚至在 3 输入 2 输出的 Bell 场景中亦是如此，从而否定了"IC 单独刻画量子关联"的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息因果性被提出作为一种物理原理，用以约束 Bell 场景中两方非局域关联，即 Bob 从 Alice 的 n 比特数据库获得的信息不超过她传达给他的信息。长期悬而未决的问题是：对所有 n，满足 IC 的关联集是否恰等于量子关联集。本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 构造满足 IC（对所有兼容协议）的双关联集，并借助为 GPT（广义概率论）建立的 IC 熵证明（可独立使用）。先对任意有限 n≥2 构造关联集，再针对 3 输入 2 输出场景构造对所有 n∈ℕ 满足 IC 且含后量子点的关联集。

Result: 对任意 n≥2，存在满足所有兼容协议 IC 的双方关联集，在 n+1 输入 2 输出的 Bell 场景中表现后量子关联；该关联集既不包含也不被包含于量子关联集。此外，在 3 输入 2 输出 Bell 场景中构造的关联集对所有 n 满足 IC 且含后量子点。

Conclusion: 所有已知形式的信息因果性表述均无法完整刻画双方量子关联集，即使在最简单的 3 输入 2 输出 Bell 场景中亦然。因此"IC 是量子关联的刻画原理"这一猜想被否定。

Abstract: Information causality (IC) was introduced as a physical principle that imposes constraints on the non-local correlations between two parties (Alice and Bob) in a Bell scenario. Specifically, it states that the information that Bob can gain about an $n$-bit database held by Alice is bounded by the information she communicates to him. To date, the question whether IC for any $n \in \mathbb{N}$ singles out the set of quantum correlations remains open, meaning that it is unknown whether for any number of inputs $m$ and outputs $d$ of the two parties the set of correlations that satisfy IC exactly coincides with the quantum correlations. In this work, we answer this question in the negative. Firstly, we show that for any finite database length $n \geq 2$, there is a bipartite set of correlations that satisfies IC for all compatible protocols and that exhibits post-quantum correlations in the Bell scenario with $n+1$ inputs and $2$ outcomes per party. This result relies on constructing an entropic proof of IC for GPTs that may be of independent interest. We further show that in the Bell scenario with $n+1$ inputs and $2$ outcomes per party, our constructed sets of correlations lead to neither a superset nor a subset of the quantum correlations. Secondly, we construct a set of correlations in the Bell scenario with $3$ inputs and $2$ outputs per party that satisfies IC (for all database sizes $n \in \mathbb{N}$) and that includes post-quantum points. Consequently, all known formulations of information causality fail to fully characterize the bipartite set of quantum correlations, already in the $3$-input, $2$-outcome Bell scenario.

</details>


### [227] [Staying Sober: A Shot-Free Predictor of Logical Error Rates for Concatenated Quantum Error Correcting Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06606)
*Sayam Sethi,Aditi Awasthi,Maxwell Poster,Joshua Viszlai,Jonathan Mark Baker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种无需采样的预测器，通过稳定子、最小权重逻辑算子（奇数d时含权重d+1的逻辑算子）计算级联码各级逻辑错误率多项式的主导单项式，时间多项式于码参数、线性于级联层数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡洛估计逻辑错误率需O(p^{-⌈d/2⌉}/ε²)采样，在大码距、低错误率下不可行，级联码码距随层数按d^ℓ增长更甚；fail fast方法仅适用于非级联码，且无法建模级联码层间传递的软信息。

Method: 构建预测器：输入稳定子、最小权重逻辑算子及奇数d时权重d+1的逻辑算子，无采样地逐层计算LER作为p的多项式的主导单项式c·p^o；并构造递归精确算法（指数时间）作为基准。

Result: 证明在预算未耗尽时，对任意非级联稳定子码、任意逻辑可观测量和任意逐量子比特错误率均精确；级联情形下预测器近似递归精确算法。在评估的每个码上第1层与精确枚举一致，冰山码可达三层；48核时内评估37种码配置、4种噪声偏置，其中148个案例里113个在p=10⁻³时蒙特卡洛需超10¹²次采样。

Conclusion: 该预测器可在不采样条件下高效估计级联码的逻辑错误率主导项，精度经与精确枚举对比验证，显著优于蒙特卡洛并可支撑大规模码配置比较。

Abstract: Estimating the logical error rate (LER) of a quantum error-correcting (QEC) code by Monte Carlo sampling takes $O(p^{-\lceil d/2 \rceil}/\varepsilon^2)$ samples at physical error rate $p$, code distance $d$ and relative error $\varepsilon$, which becomes intractable at large distances and low error rates, and especially for concatenated codes, whose distance grows as $d^{\ell}$ with the number of levels $\ell$. The fail fast method reduces this cost by sampling the failures of each weight separately, but it only works for unconcatenated codes, and does not model the soft information that the levels of a concatenated code pass to each other. We propose a predictor that takes the stabilizers, the minimum-weight logical operators and, for odd $d$, the logical operators of weight $d + 1$ of a code, and computes, without sampling, the leading monomial $c\,p^{o}$ of the LER when expressed as a polynomial in $p$, at every level of concatenation, in time polynomial in the code parameters and linear in $\ell$ for fixed budgets. We prove that our prediction is exact for any unconcatenated stabilizer code, for every logical observable and arbitrary per-qubit error rates, whenever no budget is exhausted. Furthermore, for concatenated codes, we construct a recursive approach that computes the leading monomial exactly in exponential time, and our predictor approximates this approach. Our predictions match exact enumeration at level 1 on every code we evaluate, and up to three levels for the iceberg code. Using our predictor, we evaluate and compare $37$ code configurations under four noise biases in $48$ core-hours, where Monte Carlo sampling would need more than $10^{12}$ shots at $p = 10^{-3}$ for $113$ of these $148$ cases.

</details>


### [228] [Quantum codes are quantized codes: Stabilizer codes from quantization over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06607)
*Michael Zurel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出有限域上几何量子化的离散类比，将其作用于特定经典纠错码可得到量子纠错码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码理论中已存在量子-经典对应关系，但缺乏物理解释；作者希望为这一对应提供物理视角。

Method: 构造有限域上几何量子化的离散版本，并将其应用于某些经典纠错码。

Result: 该离散几何量子化流程能从经典纠错码生成量子纠错码。

Conclusion: 所提框架为编码理论中已知的量子-经典对应提供了物理诠释。

Abstract: We present a discrete analogue of geometric quantization over finite fields which, when applied to certain classical error-correcting codes, yields quantum error-correcting codes. This provides a physical interpretation for a previously known quantum-classical correspondence in coding theory.

</details>


### [229] [Quantum Krylov Linear Solving Beyond Global Conditioning via Spectral Compression and Directional Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06620)
*Sijia Yu,Yifan Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究时间演化量子Krylov线性求解器（QKS）在"消失特征值仍与解相关"这一更难情形下的复杂度，指出归一化态的难度不由全局条件数决定，而取决于逆放大是否改变解方向；当病态沿与解对齐的"软方向"集中时，归一化可消除该方向的发散放大，仅留有限横向响应，从而获得不依赖所保留小特征值的端到端复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局条件数依赖往往会高估制备归一化量子线性系统解的难度。现有超越条件数的方法多在"全局病态方向与目标解相关性有限"时有效，但存在一类更难的情形：某个趋近于零的特征值仍与解相关且必须被保留，此时既有分析框架失效，需刻画该情形下归一化态的真实难度。

Method: 论文定位到一个特定的QKS工作区间，其中与解相关的谱被压缩到紧致的降维空间中，病态集中在一个与解对齐的软方向上。通过分析归一化过程对该软方向上放大因子的作用（归一化消去沿该方向的发散放大，仅保留有界的横向响应），并结合有限投影系统误差的扰动分析，推导归一化态复杂度的上界。

Result: （1）与解相关的信息具有紧致且稳定的QKS表示，随相关特征值趋于零，子空间维度、演化时间和重构开销均有界；（2）归一化态难度由"逆放大是否改变解方向"支配，而非仅由小特征值决定，即使任意病态的降维系统，只要放大与解对齐，仍可保持方向稳定；（3）该稳定性在有限投影系统误差下持续存在并可传递到实际QKS输出，给出对所保留小特征值无逆依赖的端到端复杂度界；一个显式的分离族进一步表明全逆放大判据可能发散，而相应QKS量保持有界。

Conclusion: 归一化量子线性系统求解的复杂度不应以全局条件数为判据；在解相关的软方向对齐情形下，QKS可绕过病态发散，实现端到端的常数级（对保留小特征值不敏感）复杂度。研究结论为"超越条件数"的量子线性求解提供了更精细的方向性稳定性判据。

Abstract: Global condition-number dependence can substantially overestimate the difficulty of preparing normalized quantum linear-system solutions. Many existing beyond-conditioning approaches are favorable when globally ill-conditioned directions have limited relevance to the target solution. Here we study a harder regime for time-evolution quantum Krylov linear solving (QKS), where a vanishing eigenvalue remains solution-relevant and must be retained. We identify a QKS regime in which the solution-relevant spectrum is compressed into a compact reduced space where the ill-conditioning is concentrated along a soft direction aligned with the solution, so that normalization removes the divergent amplification along that direction and leaves only a bounded transverse response. Consequently, the normalized-state complexity need not inherit the divergence of the global condition number. Our contributions are threefold: (1) We show that the solution-relevant information admits a compact and stable QKS representation, with bounded subspace dimension, evolution time, and reconstruction overhead as the relevant eigenvalue vanishes. (2) We show that normalized-state difficulty is governed by whether inverse amplification changes the solution direction, rather than by the small eigenvalue alone; even an arbitrarily ill-conditioned reduced system can remain directionally stable when the amplification is solution-aligned. (3) We prove that this stability persists under finite projected-system errors and transfers to the actual QKS output, yielding an end-to-end complexity bound without inverse dependence on the retained small eigenvalue. An explicit separation family further shows that a full inverse-amplification criterion can diverge while the corresponding QKS quantities remain bounded.

</details>


### [230] [Large Language Model-Guided Discovery of Weight-Five Bivariate Bicycle Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06623)
*Juan Cruz-Benito*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在LLM引导的程序演化工作流基础上研究权重为五的二元双循环(BB)与扰动二元双循环(PBB)码，构建了含1,142个不同候选码的目录，认证了连接型CSS实现并对候选码进行了结构与精确距离分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将此前由大语言模型(LLM)引导的程序演化工作流拓展到约束更强的码族（权重五的BB与PBB码），探索LLM引导发现方法在该场景下的有效性，并提供对其中最强候选码可复现的结构与精确距离刻画。

Method: 沿用LLM引导的程序演化工作流生成候选码，构建含1,142个不同码提案的目录（其中1,081个来自LLM生成的程序）；通过后搜索比较与导入的Lin–Pryadko存档构造进行对照；采用与基无关的连通性分析、代数分析（按阶-3、阶-7、阶-15分圆核分层）以及精确距离认证；并设置三个随机搜索对照组，每组以相同的每格与编码维度采样数均匀无放回抽样6,444个CSS码提案进行对比。

Result: 目录共含1,142个不同码提案（1,081个非基线提案源于LLM程序）；认证了连接型CSS实现[[96,4,10]]、[[140,6,10]]、[[180,4,14]]，并认证7个导入的Lin–Pryadko存档构造；基无关连通性分析显示1,142个条目中409个为不连通，且73.1%具有精确距离证书的类含重复连通分量；最强的精确连接型PBB参数点为[[216,4,10]]，由两个不同的分量类达到；LLM引导活动中有记录正距离的936个不同CSS提案中，816个(87.18%)在d≥5下被认证；对照组中为4,672–4,785个(72.50–74.26%)达到同一标准。LLM引导活动认证产出率更高，而随机对照组覆盖更多连通类。

Conclusion: 结果将LLM引导的发现扩展到一个约束更强的码族，并对其最强候选码给出了可复现的结构与精确距离分析；LLM引导在认证产出率上优于随机搜索，但随机对照在连通类覆盖上更广。

Abstract: Building on our earlier program-evolution workflow guided by large language models (LLMs), we study weight-five bivariate bicycle (BB) and perturbed bivariate bicycle (PBB) codes. The resulting catalogue contains 1,142 distinct code proposals, including 1,081 nonbaseline proposals attributable to LLM-generated programs. Across the catalogue, we certify connected Calderbank--Shor--Steane (CSS) realizations [[96,4,10]], [[140,6,10]], and [[180,4,14]]. A post-search comparison certifies seven imported Lin--Pryadko archive constructions. For leading parameter triples also represented in that archive, we provide exact distance evidence, explicit bivariate presentations, and verified component reductions. A basis-independent connectivity analysis identifies 409 of the 1,142 catalogue entries as disconnected and shows that 73.1\% of the classes with exact distance certificates contain repeated connected components. Algebraic analysis organizes the connected CSS classes into order-3, order-7, and order-15 cyclotomic-kernel strata. The strongest exact connected PBB parameter point is [[216,4,10]], attained by two distinct component classes. Among the 936 distinct CSS proposals from the LLM-guided campaign with a recorded positive distance, 816 (87.18\%) are certified at $d\geq5$. For comparison, three random-search controls each sample 6,444 CSS code proposals uniformly without replacement, using the same per-lattice and encoded-dimension sample counts as the LLM-guided campaign. In these controls, 4,672--4,785 proposals (72.50--74.26\%) meet the same criterion. The LLM-guided campaign has the higher certified yield, while the random controls cover more connected classes. Together, these results extend LLM-guided discovery to a more constrained code family and provide a reproducible structural and exact-distance account of its strongest candidates.

</details>


### [231] [Destroying and Preserving Measurement Incompatibility over a Quantum Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06642)
*Eric Chitambar,Yujie Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用zonotope与zonoid的数学框架完整刻画二分测量的兼容性，给出量子比特unital信道"破坏不兼容性"的充要条件，证明两个此类信道并行使用时出现超激活现象（对应两拷贝Werner态在噪声阈值1/2可被steering），并证明输入输出维数相同时保不兼容性信道必为酉变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道对量子测量不兼容性的作用存在两个极端：总是破坏不兼容性（incompatibility-breaking）与从不破坏（incompatibility-preserving）。理解这两类信道需要统一的几何刻画工具，而zonotope/zonoid框架为二分测量的兼容性提供了完整描述，并有望揭示并行使用信道时的非平凡现象。

Method: 采用zonotope和zonoid的数学框架刻画量子测量兼容性，特别是二分测量情形，并应用于qubit设置。基于zonoid语言推导qubit unital信道为incompatibility-breaking的充要条件，同时对非unital信道给出充分条件。通过构造并行信道分析超激活现象，并将结果转译为量子steering任务；对incompatibility-preserving问题，比较输入输出维数关系并给出一般保持信道的猜想形式。

Result: 给出了qubit unital信道为incompatibility-breaking的充要条件，该条件对非unital信道亦为充分条件；发现了两个incompatibility-breaking qubit信道并行使用时反而不再破坏不兼容性的超激活现象；转译为steering后表明两拷贝two-qubit Werner态在噪声阈值1/2下可被steering，而单拷贝不可；证明输入输出维数相同时，保一般不兼容性的信道当且仅当为酉变换。

Conclusion: zonoid框架能有效刻画量子信道对测量不兼容性的破坏与保持行为，揭示了incompatibility-breaking信道在并行使用时的超激活效应及其在量子steering中的对应表现。输入输出维数相同时保不兼容性信道等价于酉变换，而输出维数更大时该结论失效，作者据此提出了一般incompatibility-preserving信道的猜想形式。

Abstract: A quantum channel is called incompatibility-breaking if it always destroys the incompatibility of quantum measurements. On the other extreme are incompatibility-preserving channels, which never destroy incompatibility. This work studies both types of channels through the mathematical framework of zonotopes and zonoids, an approach that provides a complete characterization of compatibility for dichotomic measurements and is particularly powerful in the qubit setting. Using the language of zonoids, we present necessary and sufficient conditions for when a qubit unital channel is incompatibility-breaking, which are also sufficient for nonunital channels. As one of our main results, we demonstrate the superactivation phenomenon of two incompatibility-breaking qubit channels that are no longer incompatibility-breaking when used in parallel. Translating this result into the task of quantum steering, it says that two copies of a two-qubit Werner state can be steered at noise threshold $1/2$, even though this is impossible using just one copy.
