For the problem of incompatibility preservation, we prove that a quantum channel with matching input and output dimensions will preserve general incompatibility if and only if it is a unitary transformation. When the output dimension is larger, this result no longer holds, and we offer a conjectured form of a general incompatibility-preserving channel.

</details>


### [232] [Kramers' Theorem Leaves a Topological Footprint in Classical Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06661)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Kai-Wen Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出利用经典记录（而非平衡态）中时间反演算符的符号 Θ²=±1：设计能分辨手性的探测器，通过测量一段记录后接另一段记录时间反演的条件概率构造矩阵，其签名（拓扑整数）直接编码 Θ²，并可抵抗等量发射与吸收损耗；在量子处理器上用可编程发射器约 10³ 次采样即可读出该符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间反演 Θ 在半整数自旋上平方为 −1，使每个态与其时间反演正交，但这一符号历来只能在量子对象中被读出。平衡态会丢失该符号信息，因此需要一个存活于宏观、可重复测量的载体来读取 Θ²。作者旨在利用经典记录保留该符号的特性，把"时间反演的拓扑荷"变成可由实验直接照读的观测量。

Method: 引入对手性敏感的探测器，测量"一段记录之后紧接另一段记录的时间反演"这一事件的条件概率，并由这些概率组成矩阵；该矩阵的签名（signature）即携带 Θ² 的符号，是一个在等量发射与吸收损耗下仍保持不变的拓扑整数。实验上在量子处理器上实现可编程发射器，通过约 10³ 次测量采样读出该签名。

Result: 概率矩阵的签名被确认为拓扑整数，其正负对应 Θ²=+1 或 −1；该配号对发射与吸收的等量损耗具有鲁棒性；在量子处理器上以约 10³ 次采样即可稳定读出该符号。

Conclusion: 时间反演平方符号不仅属于量子对象，也可被经典记录所保存并通过手性探测统计读出；该方法把抽象的 Θ²=±1 转化为可实验访问的拓扑不变量，提供了一种抗损耗、低采样成本的符号读取方案。

Abstract: Time reversal $Θ$ squares to $-1$ on a half-integer spin, so every state is orthogonal to its time reverse. This sign has been read only from quantum objects. The equilibrium state loses it, but the classical record keeps it. For a detector that resolves handedness, the probabilities that one stretch of record is followed by the time reverse of another measure the cohomology class of time reversal, $Θ^2=\pm1$. Their matrix carries this sign in its signature, a topological integer that survives equal emission and absorption losses. A programmed emitter on quantum processors reads it in about $10^3$ shots.

</details>


### [233] [Quantum Gibbs State Preparation via Relative Decoding: From Fast-Mixing Sources to Broader Target Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06667)
*Zhong-Xia Shang,Yufei Wang,Daniel Stilck França*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于同态多项式转导（homomorphic polynomial transduction），推广了解码量子干涉（DQI/HDQI），提出“相对解码”（relative decoding）框架，可将源哈密顿量 \(H_A\) 的量子 Gibbs 态快速制备保证转移到目标哈密顿量 \(H_B\)，并证明可由相对距离 \(d_{\rm rel}\) 而非普通距离 \(d_{\rm ord}\) 决定可达逆温度，从而给出新一类可高效制备的 TFD/Gibbs 态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于制备量子 Gibbs 态的可证明保证（如快速混合、带隙相干制备路径）仅适用于受限的哈密顿量族，且通常需针对每个新族重新推导。作者希望建立一个可移植、可复用的保证转移机制，避免每次都从零分析。

Method: 将哈密顿量写成 \(m\) 个 Pauli 项之和；把乘积正比于恒等式的项子集（即关系）视为二元线性码，源为 \(K_A\)、目标为 \(K_B\)，以 Pauli 标签矩阵作为奇偶校验矩阵。从 \(H_A\) 的规范热场二重态（TFD）出发，通过 Bell 变换、可逆标签映射以及对商码 \(K_B/K_A\) 的相干解码器，制备 \(H_B\) 的近似 TFD，从而得到其 Gibbs 态。解码器只解析目标中存在而源中缺失的关系，因此可达逆温度由相对距离 \(d_{\rm rel}\)（此类关系中最少项数）控制。

Result: 可达逆温度由相对距离 \(d_{\rm rel}\) 决定，且 \(d_{\rm rel}\) 可远大于普通距离 \(d_{\rm ord}\)（后者界定 DQI 与 HDQI 的均匀精确解码半径）。作者证明：若 \(d_{\rm rel}\) 随规模线性增长且能在线性半径下高效解码，则可保证常数逆温度。以已知可在任意有限温度制备 TFD 的最近邻自旋链为源，通过稀疏经典奇偶校验矩阵构造出有界度数、几何非局域、非对易的目标哈密顿量，其 \(d_{\rm ord}=3\) 而 \(d_{\rm rel}=\Theta(m)\)。

Conclusion: 相对解码提供了一种将量子 Gibbs 态可制备性保证从源哈密顿量转移到目标哈密顿量的通用方法，突破了 DQI/HDQI 受 \(d_{\rm ord}\) 限制的瓶颈。所构造的新一类目标哈密顿量具有 \(d_{\rm rel}=\Theta(m)\)、\(d_{\rm ord}=3\)，据作者所知，这是一类新的可高效制备 TFD。

Abstract: Provable guarantees for preparing quantum Gibbs states, such as rapid mixing or gapped coherent preparation paths, are known only for restricted Hamiltonian families and usually must be re-derived for each new family. Building on homomorphic polynomial transduction, a generalization of decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) and Hamiltonian DQI (HDQI), we show how relative decoding transfers such a guarantee from a source Hamiltonian $H_A$ to a target $H_B$. Writing each Hamiltonian as a sum of $m$ Pauli terms, the subsets of terms whose product is proportional to the identity, its relations, form a binary linear code, $K_A$ for the source and $K_B$ for the target, with the Pauli-label matrix as parity-check matrix as in DQI. Starting from the canonical thermofield double (TFD) of $H_A$, a Bell transform, a reversible label map, and a coherent decoder for the quotient code $K_B/K_A$ prepare an approximate TFD of $H_B$, and hence its Gibbs state. Because this decoder only resolves target relations absent from the source, the reachable inverse temperature is set by the relative distance $d_{\rm rel}$, the fewest terms in any such relation. It can far exceed the ordinary distance $d_{\rm ord}$, the fewest terms in any target relation, which bounds the uniform exact decoding radius of DQI and HDQI. We show that linear $d_{\rm rel}$ with efficient decoding at linear radius certifies a constant inverse temperature. As an example, starting from a nearest-neighbor spin chain whose TFD is known to be preparable at every finite temperature, a sparse classical parity-check matrix yields bounded-degree, geometrically nonlocal, noncommuting targets with $d_{\rm ord}=3$ and $d_{\rm rel}=Θ(m)$. To our knowledge, this gives a new class of efficiently preparable TFDs.

</details>


### [234] [Measurement-free Preparation of Surface-Code States with Digital-Analog Counterdiabatic Drivings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06697)
*Shubham Kumar Balaganchi A. Bhargava,Paolo A. Erdman,Anne-Maria Visuri,Jose D. Martın-Guerrero,Yolanda Vives-Gilabert,Enrique Solano,Narendra N. Hegade*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出数字-模拟混合方法，利用绝热规范势结构特性制备表面码基态流形，显著提升短时基态制备保真度并大幅降低纠缠深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近中期超导量子纠错中，逻辑态初始化需要从原生单体与双体控制生成四体稳定子关联，浅电路制备稳定子码态是关键原语，现有方法在深度与保真度上受限。

Method: 基于Sels-Polkovnikov绝热规范势框架，利用规范势的结构性质，将稳定子局域的反绝热项映射为超导布局上的数字-模拟模块，使用固定角度双比特XY门、单比特旋转与模拟交换相互作用演化；由该"修饰模块"生成高阶算符，作为变分拟设确定反绝热项，并推广至n×m稳定子晶格。

Result: 在最高4×4棋盘格稳定子网格（方形对应旋转表面码，矩形用于检验可扩展性）上，方法均能生成主导四体反绝热基，短演化时间下基态制备显著优于纯绝热演化；数字-模拟合成可将纠缠深度降低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 建立了稳定子几何、反绝热规范势结构与数字-模拟控制之间的直接联系，为近中期超导架构上无测量制备稳定子码态提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Preparing stabilizer-code states with shallow circuits is an important primitive for near-term quantum error correction in superconducting circuits, where logical-state initialization requires four-body stabilizer correlations from native one- and two-body controls. We introduce a digital-analog method for the preparation of surface-code ground-state manifolds. The method exploits a structural property of the adiabatic gauge potential, mapping stabilizer-local counterdiabatic terms to digital-analog blocks on the superconducting layout using fixed-angle two-qubit \(XY\) gates, single-qubit rotations, and analog exchange-interaction evolutions. The dressed blocks generate higher-order operators used as the variational ansatz to determine the counterdiabatic terms within the Sels-Polkovnikov adiabatic-gauge-potential framework. We benchmark checkerboard plaquette-stabilizer grids up to \(4\times4\), the square-grid instances correspond to rotated surface codes, while the rectangular cases probe the scaling of the method. For all grids, the proposed method generates the dominant four-body counterdiabatic basis and, at short evolution times substantially improves the ground-state preparation as compared with bare adiabatic evolution. We further generalize the construction to \(n\times m\) stabilizer lattices and show that digital-analog synthesis can reduce the entangling depth by an order of magnitude. Together, these results establish a direct connection between stabilizer geometry, the structure of the counterdiabatic gauge potential, and digital-analog control for measurement-free preparation of stabilizer-code states on near-term superconducting architecture.

</details>


### [235] [Random-Oracle Unitary Synthesis is Impossible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06700)
*Andrew Huang,Akshar Ramkumar,John Wright*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出平均情形下的酉合成问题，证明在给定随机布尔函数的叠加访问下无法高效实现Haar随机酉，并给出超多项式查询下界；同时否定性地解决了ROM-PRU模型中可扩展PRU的开放问题，构造出新的下界与上界结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子复杂性中酉合成是核心开放问题：能否在可叠加评估任意经典函数的前提下高效实现任意酉操作。受Brakerski与Yuen（CRYPTO 2026）工作启发，作者希望从平均情形角度重新审视该问题，并厘清ROM-PRU模型下可扩展伪随机酉（PRU）的可能性。

Method: 提出"平均情形"酉合成问题：给定随机布尔函数的叠加访问，判断能否高效实现Haar随机酉；通过查询复杂性分析建立超多项式下界；在ROM-PRU模型下分析酉设计（unitary design）的查询复杂度，给出下界证明并构造新的上界算法。

Result: 证明平均情形酉合成在该模型下不可能，获得超多项式查询下界；证明ROM-PRU模型中不存在对任意γ>0可高效实现的Ω(N^{1+γ})酉设计（N=2^n），从而否定性回答Brakerski-Yuen的问题；同时构造出poly(n)查询内实现O(N)-设计的方案，超越此前O(√N)-设计的最优结果。

Conclusion: 平均情形酉合成在随机布尔函数叠加访问模型下不可行；ROM-PRU模型无法实现可扩展PRU，但通过新构造将酉设计查询复杂度从O(√N)提升至O(N)，深化了对量子伪随机性与酉合成极限的理解。

Abstract: A major open problem in quantum complexity is the question of unitary synthesis---namely, is it possible to efficiently implement any unitary, given the ability to evaluate any classical function in superposition? Inspired by a recent work by Brakerski and Yuen (CRYPTO 2026), we propose an ``average-case'' unitary synthesis problem, which asks whether it is possible to efficiently implement a Haar random unitary given access to a random Boolean function. We then demonstrate a superpolynomial query lower bound, establishing that unitary synthesis in this model is impossible.
  Along with our main result, we settle in the negative a question Brakerski and Yuen pose, of whether scalable pseudorandom unitaries (PRUs) can be implemented in the ROM-PRU model. In particular, we show that in the ROM-PRU model, no $Ω(N^{1+γ})$ unitary design on $n$ qubits, where $N=2^n$, can be efficiently achieved for any $γ> 0$. On the other hand, we construct an $O(N)$-design in $\text{poly}(n)$ queries in the same model, surpassing the previously best known results which constructed $O(\sqrt{N})$-designs.

</details>


### [236] [Optimal compression with quantum retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06702)
*Shyam Dhamapurkar,Mohit Garg,Manaswi Paraashar,Jaikumar Radhakrishnan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究将Hamming重量至多n的m比特串压缩为s比特串，使得能通过至多t次对标准oracle的量子查询无误地取回任意比特；自适应查询下可达对数因子内最优压缩，非自适应查询下给出空间复杂度方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究数据压缩在量子查询模型下的极限，即在允许对压缩串的标准oracle进行有限次量子查询时，如何最小化压缩长度s并保证任意原始比特可无误恢复；区分自适应与非自适应查询两种设定以刻画查询能力对压缩率的影响。

Method: 将串x∈{0,1}^m（Hamming重量≤n）编码为y∈{0,1}^s；恢复x_i时对y的标准oracle进行至多t次量子查询；分别设计自适应查询方案与非自适应查询方案。

Result: 自适应查询：实现至多对数因子意义下的最优压缩。非自适应查询：方案空间在m的依赖上达到最优（除对数因子），在n的依赖上至多比最优差平方级。

Conclusion: 量子查询可显著提升压缩效率；自适应查询在压缩率上接近信息论最优，非自适应查询虽受限但仍可在m维度近乎最优、在n维度有可控损失。

Abstract: We consider the following data compression problem. Given a string $x \in \{0,1\}^m$ of Hamming weight at most $n$, compress it into a shorter string $y \in \{0,1\}^s$ so that any bit $x_i$ of $x$ can be retrieved without any error using at most $t$ quantum queries to the standard oracle encoding of $y$. If queries are allowed to be adaptive we show how optimal compression up to a logarithmic factor can be achieved. If the queries are required to be made non-adaptively, we show schemes whose space is optimal in its dependence on $m$ except for a logarithmic factor, and is at most quadratically worse when compared to the optimum in its dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [237] [Out-of-control Hamiltonian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06709)
*Weiyuan Gong,Muzhou Ma,Sitan Chen,Jordan Cotler,Hsin-Yuan Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在近term模拟量子模拟器的最小访问模型下进行哈密顿量学习：在仅有均匀态制备与测量、以及仅限计算基制备与测量的两种受限设定下，分别证明可重构一般2-local哈密顿量（任意相互作用图）以及仅含Pauli X/Z相互作用的近邻哈密顿量（1D/2D矩形晶格，适用Rydberg原子平台）的全部参数（后者至多差不可避免的规范自由度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有最强可证明保证的哈密顿量学习算法通常假设存在快速任意单比特门与任意基测量等量子控制能力，而近term模拟量子模拟器（如原子、离子平台）并不具备这些能力。因此，需要研究在最小访问模型（受限的态制备与测量）下是否仍能学习多体哈密顿量。

Method: 提出两种受限访问模型下的学习协议：(1) 均匀态制备与测量——每次实验可将每个量子比特旋转到相同状态、进行短时演化、并在相同基下测量每个量子比特；(2) 计算基态制备与测量——态制备与测量均限制在计算基。协议引入求解大规模参数下结构化多项式系统的新技术，用于从动力学数据中重构哈密顿量参数。

Result: (1) 在均匀态制备与测量设定下，对于任意相互作用图上的通用2-local哈密顿量，所有参数均可从此类实验中被重构；(2) 在计算基设定下，对于仅含Pauli X/Z相互作用的近邻哈密顿量（涵盖当代Rydberg原子平台），在1D与2D矩形晶格上，所有参数均可从此类实验中重构，至多相差不可避免的规范自由度。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使在最严格的实验约束下，仍可从量子多体系统的动力学中学习到大量信息，为近term模拟量子模拟平台上的哈密顿量学习提供了理论与算法支持。

Abstract: Learning the Hamiltonian of a many-body system from its dynamics is a central task in quantum science, yet the algorithms with the strongest provable guarantees assume some level of quantum control--fast, arbitrary single-qubit gates interleaved with time evolution, and measurements in arbitrary bases--that is beyond the capabilities of near-term analog quantum simulators. Motivated by analog atom- and ion-based platforms, we study Hamiltonian learning under minimal access models.
  Uniform state preparation and measurements: We first consider the setting where in every experiment, one can rotate each qubit to the same state, perform short-time evolution, and measure every qubit in the same basis. Surprisingly, we show that for generic 2-local Hamiltonians on any interaction graph, all of the parameters can be reconstructed from such experiments.
  Computational basis state preparation and measurements: We then consider a similarly constrained setting, but where state preparation and measurement are restricted to the computational basis. For nearest-neighbor Hamiltonians with only Pauli $X/Z$ interactions, a class which captures contemporary Rydberg atom platforms, we show that over 1D and 2D rectangular lattices, all of the parameters can be reconstructed from such experiments up to unavoidable gauges.
  Our protocols introduce new techniques for solving structured polynomial systems over an extensive number of parameters. Taken together, our results suggest that one can learn a great deal from the dynamics of quantum many-body systems even under the most stringent experimental constraints.

</details>


### [238] [Tight Query Lower Bounds for Quantum Sampling, with an Application to Certified Randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06721)
*Keshav Bhateja,Mehdi Esmaili,Atul Mantri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究高XEB分数对不可信量子设备能证明什么。在量子查询模型中证明理想分数是高效设备与可认证随机性的Tsirelson界：超过理想分数常数需Ω(N^{1/3})次查询（Haar与Fourier oracle，且紧）；多项式查询且分数在理想分数o(1)内的设备输出具有n-O(log n)比特平滑最小熵（近乎最优）。副产品：碰撞分布查询复杂度为Θ(N^{1/3})，并给出近似制备量子Hadamard积的相干算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机电路采样是量子计算优势的主要实验演示，但其验证依赖线性交叉熵基准（XEB），按理想概率打分。核心动机是厘清：高XEB分数究竟能对不可信量子设备认证什么——即哪些计算任务或随机性来源是可靠设备难以伪造的。

Method: 采用量子查询模型。以“进展度量”（progress measure）刻画超出理想分数的上限，该度量每次查询仅小幅增长：对Fourier采样，度量是随机函数纯化中“移除配对”相干振幅的范数，结合多项式方法与压缩oracle技术；对Haar随机态，度量是Dirichlet正交多项式展开中平均能级的平方根，由单次观测量子效应的精确恒等式控制。据此建立下界并构造匹配算法。

Result: 1）超过理想分数一个常数需Ω(N^{1/3})次查询（Haar与Fourier oracle集合，N为输出数），该界紧，证实Haar随机态的猜想并推广至Fourier采样。2）Fourier采样与经规范态制备oracle访问的Haar随机态：多项式查询且分数落在理想分数o(1)内的设备，其n比特输出具有n-O(log n)比特平滑最小熵，即使对手与设备纠缠且事后获知oracle，此结果近乎最优。3）推论：碰撞分布（正比于理想分布平方）查询复杂度为Θ(N^{1/3})；同算法的相干版本可近似制备均匀振幅幅值的量子Hadamard积。

Conclusion: XEB中的理想分数在量子查询模型下充当高效设备与可认证随机性的Tsirelson界：高XEB分数不能被多项式查询的不可信设备以常数优势超越，且能保证近乎最优的平滑最小熵随机性。这为量子优势实验的验证提供了严格的复杂度与随机性认证基础，并揭示XEB分数作为认证量的内在极限。

Abstract: Random circuit sampling experiments, the leading demonstrations of quantum computational advantage, are tested with the linear cross-entropy benchmark (XEB), which scores outcomes by their ideal probabilities. We study what a high XEB score certifies about an untrusted quantum device. In the quantum query model, we show that the honest sampler's ideal score acts as a Tsirelson bound for efficient devices and certified randomness. First, exceeding the ideal score by a constant requires $Ω(N^{1/3})$ queries for the Haar and Fourier oracle ensembles with $N$ outcomes, and this bound is tight; this proves an earlier conjecture for Haar-random states and extends it to Fourier sampling. Second, for Fourier sampling and for Haar-random states accessed through the canonical state-preparation oracle, the $n$-bit output of a device that makes polynomially many queries and scores within $o(1)$ of the ideal score has $n-O(\log n)$ bits of smooth min-entropy, even against an adversary who is entangled with the device and later learns the oracle; this is nearly optimal. As a corollary, sampling the collision distribution, which is proportional to the square of the ideal distribution, has query complexity $Θ(N^{1/3})$, and a coherent version of the same algorithm approximately prepares quantum Hadamard products of states with uniform amplitude magnitudes. Both lower bounds rest on progress measures that bound the excess over the ideal score and increase only slightly with each query. For Fourier sampling the measure is the norm of the coherent amplitudes for removing pairs in a purification of the random function, combining the polynomial method with the compressed oracle technique; for Haar-random states it is the square root of the average level in an expansion in Dirichlet orthogonal polynomials, controlled by an exact identity for the effect of one observation.

</details>


### [239] [Certifying Measurement Incompatibility under Bounded Classical Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06722)
*Oxana Shaya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"并集最大泄露"(union maximal leakage)作为量化认证测量不相容性所需经典通信的信息度量,可通过线性规划从观测概率计算,并给出其在纠缠认证、Bell非局域性刻画及私密随机性提取中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典通信会影响接收方结果,因此在认证测量不相容性时必须纳入考量;现有方法缺乏一个能统一刻画所有观测概率之经典解释所需信息量的度量。

Method: 引入 union maximal leakage,并由观测概率通过线性规划计算;分析在最多 k 比特消息先于接收方独立测量选择的情形下该量的界,结合任意输入无关的非信号共享资源;在量子理论下建立与纠缠、Bell 非局域性的关系;对二元接收结果推导条件熵隐私界,并构造具有设备记忆的顺序协议中可组合安全的随机性提取。

Result: 若最多 k 比特消息先于接收方测量,则每条消息的相容测量给出的 union maximal leakage 至多为 k,且该界在任意输入无关非信号共享资源下仍成立;量子理论中违反亦意味着初始共享态纠缠;该量决定用相容测量与任意非信号辅助重现观测所需的最小经典通信量;固定共享量子态与接收方测量下,违反当且仅当对某消息值接收方测量在该态上对双结果发送方测量呈 Bell 非局域;二元接收结果时违反还可认证对发送方私密的随机性,即使接收方测量依赖消息且发送方保留量子边信息,并给出显式条件熵界与可组合安全提取。

Conclusion: union maximal leakage 提供了认证测量不相容性所需经典通信的统一操作化度量,将测量不相容性、Bell 非局域性、纠缠认证与设备无关隐私统一起来,并为带设备记忆的顺序协议给出可组合安全的随机性提取方案。

Abstract: Measurement incompatibility enables quantum advantages in communication and state discrimination. It is also necessary for Bell nonlocality, which is used in device-independent protocols for quantum key distribution and private randomness generation. Classical communication can influence the receiver's outcome and must therefore be accounted for when certifying measurement incompatibility. We introduce union maximal leakage, which quantifies the information needed for a common classical explanation of all observed probabilities and is computed from them by a linear program. If a message of at most $k$ bits precedes the receiver's independent measurement choice, compatible measurements for each message give union maximal leakage at most $k$. This bound holds with any input-independent nonsignalling shared resource. In quantum theory, a violation also implies that the initially shared state is entangled. Conversely, union maximal leakage determines the minimum classical communication needed to reproduce the observations with compatible measurements and arbitrary nonsignalling assistance. For a fixed shared quantum state and receiver measurements, a violation is possible exactly when, for some message value, the receiver's measurements are Bell-nonlocal against two-outcome sender measurements on that state. For binary receiver outcomes, we show that a violation also certifies randomness private from the sender, even when the receiver's measurement depends on the sender's message and the sender retains quantum side information. We quantify this privacy through an explicit conditional entropy bound and establish composably secure randomness extraction in sequential protocols with device memory.

</details>


### [240] [Approximate cloning of structured pure states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06723)
*Yaroslav Herasymenko,Junqiao Lin,Philip Verduyn Lunel,Dmitry Grinko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究结构化量子态族的近似 N→N+1 克隆样本复杂度，构建最优/显式克隆信道，将费米子与玻色子高斯态复杂度从指数降至多项式，并给出相位态最优保真度信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般纯态的近似克隆复杂度为 N≃dim H/ε，对高维系统天文数字般巨大。作者希望探索若输入被限制在物理与量子信息中具有结构性的态族时，克隆的样本复杂度能否显著降低。

Method: 建立统一的表示论框架，涵盖 n 模费米子高斯/ Slater 态与玻色子高斯态，构造最优或显式克隆信道并给出酉实现；通过 frame potential 的高 N 鞍点评估解释复杂度与族流形维数的线性关系；对 qudit 相位态给出独立的最优克隆信道构造。

Result: 费米子情形构造出最优克隆信道，复杂度从 n 的指数降为多项式；玻色子高斯态构造出无能量约束的显式克隆信道，证明多项式复杂度但非最优；统一框架推广了 Werner 及 Chiribella-Yang 的工作；相位态获得最优克隆保真度信道；同一计算也解释断层成像复杂度的类似标度。

Conclusion: 结构化态族的克隆样本复杂度可远低于一般纯态，且常与族流形维数线性相关；该工作补充了 stabilizer 态克隆的近期结果，并为多体物理与量子信息中的克隆与 tomography 提供统一视角。

Abstract: The no-cloning theorem is a cornerstone result in quantum mechanics that forbids copying of general quantum information. More quantitatively, even given $N$ copies of an unknown state, any channel producing an $(N+1)$-copy state incurs a nonzero trace-distance error on some inputs. Turning this around, one can ask about the sample complexity of approximate $N\rightarrow N+1$ cloning: for a desired small error $ε$, what $N$ suffices? For arbitrary pure states on a Hilbert space $H$, the answer is $N\simeq \dim H/ε$ - astronomically large for most $H$ of interest. What if the input is promised to lie in a structured family? We examine a range of families fundamental to many-body physics and quantum information: $n$-mode fermionic Gaussian and Slater states, $n$-mode bosonic Gaussian states, and qudit phase states. For fermions, we construct optimal cloning channels, reducing the complexity from exponential to polynomial in $n$. For bosonic Gaussian states, we construct an explicit cloner that certifies polynomial complexity, without any constraint on the energy of the state; this channel, however, is not optimal in general. The fermionic and bosonic results follow from a single representation-theoretic framework, which generalizes previous work by Werner and by Chiribella and Yang; it also yields the cloners' unitary implementation. Furthermore, we explain why for many families the cloning complexity is linear in the dimension of the family manifold, via a controlled saddle-point evaluation of the frame potential at high $N$. The same calculation accounts for the analogous scaling of tomography complexity. The above framework does not cover phase states; for these we give an independent construction of a channel with optimal cloning fidelity. Our work complements recent results on cloning stabilizer states.

</details>


### [241] [Random Order in Quantum Streaming: Replenishment and Robust Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06727)
*Nadezhda Voronova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究随机顺序如何改变量子内存在流式计算中的作用，提出"量子内存补充(replenishment)"现象，并构造人工问题揭示随机顺序下的量子优势及持续性空间下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式计算中量子态被先前查询消耗后通常失效，本文关注随机顺序是否能让后续经典输入"补充"已消耗的量子态，从而恢复其效用，并系统刻画随机顺序对量子内存作用的改变。

Method: 基于Hidden Matching构造含重复坐标数据与在线匹配请求的人工问题；强化Gilboa-Jain-McClean关于Multiple Hidden Matching的可消耗性下界，证明无预纠缠时支持r次独立匹配请求的量子编码需Ω(r)量子比特；改造Kallaugher三角计数算法以适配每条边等频重复的均匀随机流；将Assadi-Sundaresan的鲁棒Noisy Gap Cycle框架扩展到量子流式，并证明Block Hidden XOR的量子通信下界。

Result: 该人工问题存在单遍、多对数空间的量子算法（均匀随机顺序），但在随机顺序下经典算法及所有更新先于请求的量子算法均无条件需要多项式空间；对r≤N^{1/2-δ}（任意固定δ∈(0,1/2)）量子编码需Ω(r)量子比特，顺序请求下线性界延伸至r=Θ(√N)；改造后的三角计数算法在合适参数区间改进期望空间界；Block Hidden XOR的量子通信下界给出大环在随机边序下的Ω(n)空间下界。

Conclusion: 随机顺序可实现对小型量子表示的补充(replenishment)，使被消耗量子态重新有用；但对其他任务（如某些图问题）仍存在实质性空间需求，量子优势具有任务依赖性。

Abstract: How can random order change the role of quantum memory in streaming? Later classical input can restore the usefulness of a quantum state consumed by earlier queries. We call this replenishment.
  We construct an artificial problem based on Hidden Matching, with repeated coordinate data and online matching requests. It admits a one-pass quantum algorithm using polylogarithmic space in uniformly random order, but unconditionally requires polynomial space both classically in random order and quantumly when all updates precede the requests.
  To prove the quantum lower bound, we strengthen the consumability bounds of Gilboa, Jain, and McClean for Multiple Hidden Matching. Without prior entanglement, any quantum encoding supporting $r$ independent matching requests requires $Ω(r)$ qubits for $r\le N^{1/2-δ}$ and every fixed $δ\in(0,1/2)$, even with simultaneous revelation and arbitrary joint decoding. For sequential requests, the linear bound extends through $r=Θ(\sqrt N)$.
  We also adapt Kallaugher's triangle-counting algorithm to uniformly random streams in which every edge is repeated equally often. Rebuilding the quantum sketch and resampling the classical estimator improve its expected-space bound in suitable parameter regimes.
  Finally, we extend the robust Noisy Gap Cycle framework of Assadi and Sundaresan to quantum streaming. A quantum communication lower bound for Block Hidden XOR yields an $Ω(n)$ space lower bound in random edge order for large enough cycles, with consequences for several graph problems.
  Thus random order can enable replenishment of small quantum representations, while substantial space requirements persist for other tasks.

</details>


### [242] [Qubit CSS codes with Universal Transversal Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06730)
*Arpit Dua,Adam Holmes,Michael J. Gullans,Victor V. Albert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：若允许每一层深度一（depth-one）的少比特门各自遵循不同的量子比特划分，则这些层级可以共同生成纠错码上的通用逻辑门集合，从而绕过Eastin-Knill定理对单一划分的限制。作者构造了47个具有深度一生成集的编码，其中[[37,1,7]]和[[59,1,9]]码每层可容忍至少一个故障门，校验权重不超过8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eastin-Knill定理禁止在任何能检测单量子比特错误的编码上实现通用横向逻辑门集合，且该定理一次只约束一个量子比特划分。这导致在保持纠错能力的同时实现通用逻辑操作非常困难，通常需要魔法态蒸馏、码切换等复杂器件。作者希望突破这一限制，在不使用这些额外gadget的情况下实现通用逻辑门集合。

Method: 作者采用深度一（depth-one）层结构，每层由少比特门组成且各自尊重不同的量子比特划分，多个这样的层组合起来生成通用逻辑门集合。每个逻辑门发生器由单个深度一层加上同一稳定子码的纠错构成，无需魔法态蒸馏、码切换、规范固定、晶格手术或其他gadget。编码的构造方式为：对低距离的"种子"码（允许深度一非Clifford门）施加纠缠电路，并配合适当的"核"码来获得目标编码。

Result: 构造出47个具有深度一生成集的编码，这些编码每层至少能容忍一个故障门。其中包括[[37,1,7]]码和[[59,1,9]]码，两者的校验权重均不超过8。

Conclusion: 通过使用遵循不同比特划分的多个深度一层，可以规避Eastin-Knill定理的单一划分限制，在纠错码上实现通用逻辑门集合，且无需魔法态蒸馏等复杂器件。构造方法具有可行性，并给出了具体的编码实例。

Abstract: The Eastin-Knill theorem rules out a universal set of transversal logical gates on any code that detects single-qubit errors. The theorem constrains one partition of the qubits at a time. We show that depth-one layers of few-qubit gates, each respecting its own partition, can together generate a universal logical gate set on an error-correcting code. Each logical gate generator is then a single depth-one layer followed by error correction of the same stabilizer code, with no magic-state distillation, code switching, gauge fixing, lattice surgery, or other gadgets. We show that such codes can be found by applying an entangling circuit to a lower-distance "seed" code admitting a depth-one non-Clifford gate and an appropriate "kernel" code. We construct 47 codes with depth-one generating sets that tolerate at least one faulty gate per layer, including a [[37,1,7]] and a [[59,1,9]] code, both of check weight at most eight.

</details>


### [243] [Streaming Regev's Quantum Factoring Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06733)
*Katherine Van Kirk,Craig Gidney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了Regev因式分解算法中相位估计控制量子比特无法"回收流式"的问题，通过叠加掩码与斐波那契幂次相位估计两项改进，将空间降至n+M(n)+O(√n)，使用特定乘法器时仅需4n+O(√n)个量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Regev因式分解算法的主要实用障碍是空间开销过大，且此前被认为与"量子比特回收"（qubit recycling，即流式处理相位估计中的控制比特而非同时存储）不兼容。

Method: 基于Ragavan与Vaikuntanathan的构造（用斐波那契累加器将比特数从O(n^1.5)降至11.32n）做两点改进：一是用叠加掩码（superposition masking）阻止无用信息进入累加器，从而免除反计算；二是用酉算子的斐波那契幂而非2的幂进行相位估计，避免原构造所需的多种编码转换。改进后相位估计比特在电路中仅使用一次，因而可被流式处理。

Result: 改进将Regev算法空间降至n+M(n)+O(√n)，其中M(n)为原地乘法所需空间。例如采用Luo等人(2026)的原地乘法器，可用4n+O(√n)个量子比特分解n位整数。

Conclusion: 该构造虽仍不及Shor算法主流实现所需的n/2+εn量子比特，但已是迄今空间最低的Regev算法实例，表明Regev算法仍有较大优化空间。

Abstract: A major practical obstacle to Regev's factoring algorithm is its large space usage. In particular, the algorithm was believed to be incompatible with "qubit recycling," which streams the control qubits in phase estimation rather than storing them all simultaneously. We show how to fix this streaming problem. We build on the construction of Ragavan and Vaikuntanathan, which used Fibonacci accumulators to reduce the qubit count from $O(n^{1.5})$ to $11.32n$. We make two key improvements. First, we use superposition masking to prevent unwanted information from entering the accumulators, removing the need for uncomputation. Second, we show how to perform phase estimation using Fibonacci powers of a unitary operation rather than powers of two. This avoids various encoding conversions required by the construction of Ragavan and Vaikuntanathan. After these improvements, the phase-estimation qubits are used only once in the circuit, allowing them to be streamed.
  Our improvements reduce the space usage of Regev's factoring algorithm to $n+M(n)+O(\sqrt{n})$, where $M(n)$ is the space required for an in-place multiplication. For example, using the in-place multiplier of Luo et al. (2026), Regev's algorithm can factor an $n$-bit integer using $4n+O(\sqrt{n})$ qubits. Although our construction still falls short of the $n/2+εn$ qubits sufficient for leading implementations of Shor's algorithm, it is the lowest-space instantiation of Regev's algorithm to date and suggests that Regev's algorithm is still far from fully optimized.

</details>


### [244] [Characterizing Photonic Partial Distinguishability Through Gram Matrix Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06736)
*Marcin Kotowski,Marco Robbio,Oliver Reardon-Smith,Benoit Seron,Leonardo Novo,Ernesto F. Galvão,Michał Oszmaniec*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"Gram矩阵层析"方案，可从二次数量可测的Bargmann不变量中重建独立制备光子的Gram矩阵，实现与内态Hilbert空间维度无关、采样复杂度多项式化的部分可分辨性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可分辨性决定多光子干涉，是Boson Sampling等大规模光子实验与多光子量子技术应用的核心；但导致该行为的内态自由度往往无法直接访问，其维度甚至物理本质先验未知，因此难以表征。

Method: 针对内态为纯态或近纯态的独立制备光子引入Gram矩阵层析：证明纯态下Gram矩阵包含预测对内态不敏感实验统计所需的全部信息，并在物理无关相位选择意义下唯一重建该矩阵；数据来源为二次数量可测的Bargmann不变量（编码重叠模与集体相位），通过两种互补手段获取——定向Fourier干涉仪，以及单随机干涉仪中测量的两体与三体光子数关联。

Result: 给出显式误差界，控制任意后续内态不敏感实验中预测分布与真实分布的全变差距离，并涵盖有限采样与偏离纯态的影响；协议总采样复杂度与内态Hilbert空间维度无关，在内态重叠的温和假设下为多项式。

Conclusion: 该协议仅依赖标准干涉测量，将部分可分辨光子的刻画转化为稳健且具操作意义的表征方法。

Abstract: Partial distinguishability affects multiphoton interference, which is central to large-scale photonic experiments such as Boson Sampling and to applications of multiphoton states across quantum technologies. Characterizing the internal degrees of freedom responsible for this behavior is challenging: in many scenarios they cannot be accessed directly and even their dimension or physical nature may be unknown \emph{a priori}. Here we introduce Gram-matrix tomography for independently prepared photons with pure or nearly pure internal states. For pure states, the Gram matrix contains all information required to predict the statistics of experiments that are insensitive to the internal degrees of freedom. We reconstruct this matrix, up to physically irrelevant phase choices, from a quadratic number of measurable Bargmann invariants (quantities encoding overlap magnitudes and collective phases). We access these quantities using two complementary approaches: targeted Fourier interferometers and two- and three-body photon-number correlations measured in a single random interferometer. We derive explicit error bounds that control the total-variation distance between predicted and actual outcome distributions for any subsequent experiment that is insensitive to the internal states. These guarantees account for finite sampling and deviations from purity. Crucially, the total sample complexity of the protocol is independent of the internal Hilbert-space dimension and polynomial under mild assumptions on overlaps of internal states. Our protocol uses standard interferometric measurements and turns them into a robust and operationally grounded characterization of partially distinguishable photons.

</details>


### [245] [Anti-symmetrization is cheap and useful](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06737)
*Adam Bene Watts,John Bostanci,Matthias C. Caro,Daniel Grier,Sihan Liu,Gaurav Mahajan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究混合量子态的反对称化计算任务，提出流式与非流式两种算法，分别达到 $\widetilde{O}(r/\lambda_{\min})$ 和 $O(r/\lambda_{\min})$ 的样本复杂度，并应用于单向态生成器归约、Schur采样及纯度放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反对称化在量子信息处理中具有基础性作用，但针对未知混合态的高效反对称化算法尚不完善。作者希望设计样本复杂度低、操作简单且适用于流式场景的算法，并将其作为核心构件服务于单向态生成器归约、Schur采样与纯度放大等任务。

Method: 给定 $r$ 秩量子态 $\rho$ 的 iid 副本，目标是制备与 $\Pi_{\mathrm{anti}}^r(|\varphi_1\rangle\otimes\ldots\otimes|\varphi_r\rangle)$ 成比例的态。第一类算法采用流式处理，仅需少量工作内存，使用受控-SWAP和常数层单比特门等简单量子操作，样本复杂度为 $\widetilde{O}(r/\lambda_{\min})$；第二类非流式算法借助 Schur-Weyl 对偶工具，样本复杂度优化至 $O(r/\lambda_{\min})$。

Result: 流式算法实现了 $\widetilde{O}(r/\lambda_{\min})$ 副本复杂度并支持小内存运行，由此给出从带隙混合态单向态生成器到纯态单向态生成器的归约。非流式算法达到 $O(r/\lambda_{\min})$ 副本复杂度，作为新构件在弱 Schur 采样与酉 Schur 采样中于某些副本数 $n$ 和局部维度 $d$ 区间上优于现有方法，并用于量子态的最优纯度放大算法。

Conclusion: 该工作为未知混合态的反对称化提供了流式与非流式两套高效算法，显著降低了样本需求并简化了量子操作，同时在单向态生成器归约、Schur采样及纯度放大等下游任务中展现出性能优势，拓展了反对称化在量子信息处理中的应用范围。

Abstract: We consider the computational task of anti-symmetrization of an unknown mixed quantum state: Given iid copies of a rank-$r$ qudit state $ρ=\sum_{i=1}^r λ_i |\varphi_i\rangle\!\langle\varphi_i|$, prepare a copy of the state proportional to $Π_{\mathrm{anti}}^r (|\varphi_1\rangle\otimes \ldots\otimes|\varphi_r\rangle)$, with $Π_{\mathrm{anti}}^r$ the anti-symmetric subspace projector. We first give a simple algorithm for anti-symmetrization that uses $\widetilde{O}(r / λ_{\min})$ copies of $ρ$, where $λ_{\min}$ is the minimum non-zero eigenvalue of $ρ$. This algorithm works even in the streaming setting with small working memory, and it uses only simple quantum operations like controlled-SWAPs and constantly many layers of single-qubit gates. As an application of this streaming anti-symmetrization procedure, we give a reduction from gapped mixed-state one-way state generators to pure-state one-way state generators. Then, using tools from Schur-Weyl duality, we give and analyze a second (non-streaming) anti-symmetrization procedure that uses $O(r / λ_{\min})$ copies of $ρ$, which then serves as a building block in new algorithms for weak Schur sampling and unitary Schur sampling, outperforming existing approaches in some regimes of number of copies $n$ and local dimension $d$, as well as in an algorithm for optimal purity amplification for qudit states.

</details>


### [246] [Free entropy and quantum minimum description length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06741)
*Patrick Hayden,Alexander Maloney,Jinzhao Wang,Yuxiang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义了有限分辨率下的有限维物理自由熵，并证明其（至多相差一个显式常数）直接刻画了在已知特征值、未知特征基情形下编程多份量子态所需的最小量子记忆，即"量子最小描述长度"，从而赋予Voiculescu自由熵以量子信息论意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Voiculescu自由熵是Shannon熵的非交换类比，用于计数逼近算子的矩阵微观态，但长期以来在量子理论中没有物理意义，与任何密度算子的量子熵都不同。作者希望弥合这一空白，为自由熵寻找量子物理中的操作性解释。

Method: 作者定义了一个在有限分辨率下的有限维"物理自由熵"，并将其与一个具体的量子信息任务相联系：该任务是Schumacher压缩的变体，目标是在已知量子态的特征值、但特征基未知的条件下，编程（压缩）多份该量子态的副本。

Result: 证明该物理自由熵（至多相差一个显式常数）直接刻画了上述任务所需的最小量子记忆，作者将这一最优代价称为"量子最小描述长度"（quantum minimum description length）。

Conclusion: 自由熵可被赋予量子信息论中的操作性物理意义；文中还简要讨论了自由熵与各类量子熵的差异及其在量子物理中的潜在应用。

Abstract: In free probability, Voiculescu's free entropy counts matrix microstates that approximate an operator. It is a non-commutative analog of the Shannon entropy, distinct from any quantum entropy of a density operator, but it has had no physical meaning in quantum theory. We define a finite-dimensional physical free entropy at finite resolution and show that it directly characterizes, up to an explicit constant, the minimal quantum memory needed to program many copies of a state with known eigenvalues and unknown eigenbasis. This task is a variant of Schumacher compression, and we call its optimal cost the quantum minimum description length. We briefly discuss how free entropy differs from quantum entropies and its potential applications in quantum physics.

</details>


### [247] [Quantum minimum description of density matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06742)
*Patrick Hayden,Alexander Maloney,Jinzhao Wang,Yuxiang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一类 Schumacher 压缩变体：在已知谱、未知特征基且不要求保留纯化的条件下，压缩多个量子态副本的最小内存代价，并在固定维度且正特征值互异时通过加性常数刻画该代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典/量子压缩通常要求已知或可保留完整信息（如纯化），而本文关注更弱的信息条件：仅知谱、不知特征基，且无需保持纯化。此设定下需重新确定可达到的最小内存代价，并厘清其与通用无损编码开销及自由熵等概念的联系。

Method: 可达性方向：将 Werner 克隆映射推广到 GL(d,C) 的不可约表示，并利用由最高权差与行间隙控制的有限迹距离界，构造压缩方案。逆定理方向：在谱间隙假设下，对不可约群轨道建立 Koashi–Imoto 不可压缩性的定量形式，从而得到匹配的下界。此外，给出与量子最小描述长度、通用无损编码开销及自由熵之间关系的分析，并提供 Lean 证明证书。

Result: 在固定维度、正特征值互异的条件下，得到了该压缩任务的最小内存代价，并精确到加性常数；可达性与逆定理相匹配，形成紧凑刻画。同时阐明了量子最小描述长度与通用无损编码开销之间的关系，并指出自由熵联系在配套通信中论述；所有证明均附带 Lean 证书。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了在已知谱、未知特征基且不保纯化条件下量子态压缩的最小内存代价的加性常数级精确解，建立了基于不可约表示与定量 Koashi–Imoto 不可压缩性的匹配界，并连接了量子最小描述长度、通用无损编码开销与自由熵等概念，同时以形式化证书增强证明可靠性。

Abstract: We study a variant of Schumacher compression. The task asks for the minimal memory cost for compressing many copies of a density matrix with known spectrum and unknown eigenbasis, without preserving its purification. For fixed dimension and distinct positive eigenvalues, we obtain the cost through its additive constant. Achievability uses a generalization of Werner's cloning map to $\mathrm{GL}(d,\mathbb C)$ irreducible representations, with finite trace-distance bounds controlled by highest-weight differences and row gaps. The matching converse follows from a quantitative form of Koashi--Imoto incompressibility for irreducible group orbits under a spectral-gap assumption. We also identify the relation between this quantum minimum description length and universal lossless coding overhead, and its connection to free entropy is explained in a companion letter. We provide a Lean certificate for our proofs.

</details>


### [248] [Efficient Logic with Ultra-High-Rate Quantum Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06749)
*Nishad Maskara,Rohan Mehta,Zhiyang He,Varun Menon,J. Pablo Bonilla Ataides,Mikhail D. Lukin,Hengyun Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文面向超高码率量子LDPC码，提出利用码的群对称性将逻辑比特划分成轨道，并采用并行手术实现群不变的逻辑Pauli乘积测量；同时提出"链映射卷积"技术高效提升协议的时空距离。针对[[256,68,12]]、[[576,148,18]]及[[1152,580,12]]等码构造了保距并行测量小工具，空间开销约2.5×、每轮仅需3-4轮症状提取，并支持魔态并行注入，还通过子群限制揭示逻辑控制与时空资源之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC码有望降低容错的量子比特开销，近期超高码率构造已实现超过1/2的编码率。但超高码率码缺乏快速、并行的逻辑计算方法，逻辑操作的高时空开销成为其实际应用的主要瓶颈。因此需要为这类码设计低时空开销的逻辑操作方案。

Method: 1) 利用码的群对称性将逻辑量子比特划分为轨道；2) 采用并行手术方法实现群不变的逻辑Pauli乘积测量集合，测量具有高度可编程性，只要每个测量在整个轨道上重复即可任意耦合逻辑轨道；3) 提出"链映射卷积"(chain-map convolution)技术，利用群结构高效提升协议的时空距离；4) 通过子群限制扩展可编程性。

Result: 为[[256,68,12]]和[[576,148,18]]配对划分码以及[[1152,580,12]]（码率1/2）码构造了保距的并行测量小工具，空间开销仅约2.5×，每次仅需3-4轮症状提取。相同方法还可实现培养魔态向高码率码的并行注入。此外，子群限制揭示了逻辑控制能力与容错所需时空资源之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了设计低时空开销qLDPC逻辑运算的新方法，凸显了结构化并行性在降低开销中的关键作用，为更高效的容错量子架构开辟了道路。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes offer a promising route to reducing the qubit overhead of fault tolerance, with recent ultra-high-rate constructions achieving encoding rates above one half. In this work, we develop fast and parallel logical computation methods for ultra-high-rate codes. We first utilize the group symmetries of the codes to partition the logical qubits into orbits, and then apply parallel surgery methods to implement group-invariant collections of logical Pauli-product measurements. These measurements are highly programmable, as we may arbitrarily couple logical orbits as long as each measurement is repeated across the whole orbit(s). To reduce the space and time overheads, we develop a technique called chain-map convolution, which uses the group structure to boost both the spatial and temporal distances of our protocol in a highly efficient manner. As case studies, we construct distance-preserving parallel measurement gadgets for the [[256, 68, 12]], [[576, 148, 18]] pair-partition codes, and a [[1152, 580, 12]] rate-$1/2$ code, with only around $2.5\times$ space overhead and 3-4 rounds of syndrome extraction each. The same methods also enable parallel injection of cultivated magic states into high-rate codes. Finally, we extend programmability through subgroup restrictions, revealing a tradeoff between logical control and the spacetime resources required for fault tolerance and highlighting the crucial role structured parallelism plays in reducing overhead. These results provide new methods for designing qLDPC logical operations with low spacetime overhead, opening a route toward more efficient fault-tolerant quantum architectures.

</details>


### [249] [Discovering New Problems for Decoded Quantum Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06753)
*Rhik Mazumder,Pradeep Niroula*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"解码量子干涉"(DQI)可靠性与性能规则，用于筛选可能具有量子优势的优化问题；据此发现两类候选应用并在有限实例上观察到DQI相对经典求解器的质量优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI 利用经典纠错码求解优化问题，但缺乏判定哪些问题能带来量子优势的准则，导致候选问题的筛选缺乏理论依据。

Method: 建立基于码参数、解码保证与经典基线的可靠性(soundness)规则；针对计数满足约束的标准目标，在最坏情况Hamming解码下证明DQI保证相对Prange补全的差距上界为 (√2−1)/2，并用最优多项式交(OPI)渐近达到该上限；进一步推导实值目标的性能定律，指出目标函数同时决定解质量与解码器需纠正的错误；借助AI-agent搜索按规则寻找候选。

Result: 证明标准目标下的margin上限且OPI渐近达到；得到实值目标性能定律；AI搜索发现两个候选应用——McEliece型公钥上的alternant最大一致，以及基于离散对数的乘性多项式交(背包型)。二者均给出解码保证及与Prange重启的有限长比较，在较大测试实例上DQI期望质量超过固定预算内经典求解器的结果。

Conclusion: DQI的量子优势空间可由码参数与经典基线规则界定，其上限在标准目标下由OPI刻画；规则驱动的搜索能定位到具理论保证且实证占优的新候选问题。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) uses classical error correction to solve optimization problems, but it is hard to tell which problems could give it a quantum advantage. We develop soundness rules that screen candidate problems by their code parameters, decoding guarantees and classical baselines. For the standard objective of counting satisfied constraints, we prove that under worst-case Hamming decoding DQI's guarantee can exceed Prange completion by at most $(\sqrt{2}-1)/2$. Optimal polynomial intersection (OPI) attains this margin cap asymptotically, so no candidate in this setting can exceed OPI's margin. We also derive a performance law for real-valued objectives: the objective fixes both the solution quality and which errors the decoder must correct. An AI-agent search guided by these rules found two candidate applications. Alternant max-agreement on McEliece-type public keys tests what a secret decoder is worth to DQI against classical solvers that see only the public instance. Multiplicative polynomial intersection, a knapsack-type problem built from discrete logarithms, pairs a cosine objective with a public decoder for signed errors. For both we prove decoding guarantees and finite-length comparisons with Prange restarts, and on the larger instances we tested, DQI's expected quality exceeds what our classical solvers reach within fixed budgets.

</details>


### [250] [The Grothendieck Game and Self-Testing Continuous Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06755)
*Alexander Kulpe,Giulio Malavolta,Simon Schmidt,Michael Walter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了首个对 Maj orana 算符生成的连续测量族的鲁棒自检验，通过证明 Grothendieck 博弈可自检验 Majorana 可观测量，并进一步推广至 Majorana 可观测量之积及连续 pin 群的旋量表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自检验能从观测到的关联单独认证量子态与测量，而无需依赖对产生器件的描述。此前尚缺乏对由 Majorana 算符生成的整个连续测量族的鲁棒自检验，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用两方非局域博弈——Grothendieck 博弈：玩家收到球面上的方向输入并返回二元答案。通过证明该博弈是 Majorana 可观测量的自检验，将认证扩展到 Majorana 可观测量的乘积。

Result: 任何接近最优的表现都能认证整个连续参数化的量子测量族；进一步获得了对连续 pin 群旋量表示的鲁棒自检验。

Conclusion: Grothendieck 博弈可作为 Majorana 可观测量的自检验工具，从而实现对连续测量族及连续 pin 群旋量表示的鲁棒认证，拓展了自检验的适用范围。

Abstract: Self-testing certifies quantum states and measurements from observed correlations alone, without requiring a description of the devices that produce them. In this work we establish, to the best of our knowledge, the first robust self-test of the full continuous family of measurements generated by Majorana operators. We achieve this by showing that the Grothendieck game, a two-player nonlocal game in which the players receive directions on a sphere and return binary answers, is a self-test of Majorana observables. That is, any near-optimal performance certifies an entire continuously parameterized family of quantum measurements. We further extend this certification to products of Majorana observables, obtaining a robust self-test of the spin representation of the continuous pin group.

</details>


### [251] [Nonadditivity in fermionic quantum Shannon theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06758)
*Farzin Salek,Amir-Reza Negari,Graeme Smith,Jens Eisert,Debbie Leung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究费米子系统在奇偶宇称超选择规则与正则反对易关系下的集体态制备与信道编码，精确求解Shor-Hastings四类可加性问题的费米对应版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究费米子系统中由于超选择规则导致的纠缠度量与信道容量的非可加现象，为Shor与Hastings提出的可加性问题寻找精确的费米对应答案。

Method: 对双模高斯态τ计算精确的纠缠生成值E_F^phys；对关联高斯信道求各分组长度下的最小输出熵与Holevo信息；覆盖所有物理纯态分解与信道输入，采用高斯系综与经典编码。

Result: 得到E_F^phys(τ^{⊗m})=⌈m/2⌉log2，两份拷贝生成值与一份相同，违反可加性与强超可加性；证明最小输出熵与Holevo信息的非可加性；无助经典与零误差经典容量均为每模半比特；无助量子与零误差量子容量均为每模半逻辑量子比特；噪声高斯族在全秩下严格非可加；Bell违背首次出现于三份拷贝。

Conclusion: 精确回答了费米版四类可加性问题，生成值与最小输出熵公式对任意Rényi阶α∈(0,∞]成立，并给出可达容量的强逆定理。

Abstract: We study collective state preparation and channel encoding in fermionic systems under parity superselection and composition governed by the canonical anticommutation relations. For a two-mode Gaussian state $τ$, we obtain the exact entanglement of formation, $E_{\mathrm F}^{\mathrm{phys}}(τ^{\otimes m})=\lceil m/2\rceil\log2$ for every $m\geq1$. Two copies have the same formation value as one, violating additivity and strong superadditivity, while regularization halves the single-copy value. For an associated Gaussian channel, we determine the minimum output entropy and Holevo information at every blocklength and prove nonadditivity of both. Together, these results give exact answers to the fermionic counterparts of the four additivity questions associated with Shor and Hastings. One physical use carries no classical information, whereas two uses transmit one bit perfectly. The unassisted classical and zero-error classical capacities both equal one half bit per mode. A sharp finite-block bound gives a strong converse: the decoding success probability vanishes at every rate above capacity. These optimizations cover all physical pure-state decompositions and channel inputs, with Gaussian ensembles and classical codes attaining the optima. The formation and minimum output entropy formulas hold for every Rényi order $α\in(0,\infty]$. We also determine the unassisted quantum and zero-error quantum capacities, both equal to one half logical qubit per mode, and prove a strong converse: entanglement fidelity vanishes at every rate above capacity. For a noisy Gaussian family, we determine the one- and two-copy formation values and prove strict nonadditivity at full rank. For $τ$, Bell violation under local parity-preserving measurements first appears at three copies.

</details>


### [252] [A classical oracle separation between QMA and QMA(2)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06760)
*Sabee Grewal,Saachi Mutreja,Kabir Tomer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构造了一个经典预言机，使得相对于该预言机 QMA ≠ QMA(2)，即证明了允许两个纠缠但不可通信证明者的量子验证类 QMA(2) 在该预言机下严格强于标准 QMA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QMA(2) 与 QMA 是否相等是量子复杂性理论中的核心公开问题。已有证据（如无预言机下的复杂性含混）不足以判定二者关系，因此作者试图通过构造相对化的分离来给出结构性证据，说明 QMA(2) 在预言机模型下确实强于 QMA。

Method: 基于图上符号边约束定义的可分哈密顿量问题：YES 实例存在零能量乘积态，NO 实例（借助反对称子空间构造）有纠缠零能量态但无低能量乘积态。QMA 下界证明使用压缩预言机技术，追踪量子计算在少数查询 YES 实例时所获得的信息；关键步骤是把由此产生的部分预言机记录叠加映射到 NO 家族，同时不显著改变验证者的行为。

Result: 存在一个经典预言机，使得相对于该预言机 QMA ≠ QMA(2)。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了 QMA 与 QMA(2) 之间第一个相对化分离，表明在预言机模型下两个非通信纠缠证明者确实提供更强的验证能力，为理解 QMA(2) 的计算力提供了新的下界技术（压缩预言机与部分记录叠加映射）。

Abstract: We exhibit a classical oracle relative to which QMA \neq QMA(2). The separation is based on a separable Hamiltonian problem defined by signed edge constraints on a graph. The YES instances have zero-energy product states, while the NO instances, constructed using the antisymmetric subspace, have entangled zero-energy states but no low-energy product states. For the QMA lower bound, we use compressed-oracle techniques to track the information learned by a quantum computation making few queries to a YES instance. The key step is to map the resulting superpositions of partial oracle records to the NO family without significantly changing the verifier's behavior.

</details>


### [253] [Hypercontractivity and entropy decay for infinite-dimensional quantum Gibbs samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06764)
*Simon Becker,Cambyse Rouzé,Robert Salzmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限维量子Gibbs采样器的相对熵指数衰减速率，建立了超收缩估计与对数Sobolev不等式的微扰稳定性理论，并对多模Bose-Hubbard模型、量子Coulomb气体等证明了指数熵衰减，同时给出一个具有正谱隙却无有限时间L²→L^p估计的周期充电模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Gibbs采样器在无限维系统（如多模玻色子、量子气体）中的收敛速率分析缺乏通用工具，尤其在相互作用复杂或需截断时，超收缩性与对数Sobolev不等式等估计的稳定性问题亟待解决。

Method: 核心工具是超收缩估计与对数Sobolev不等式的微扰稳定性理论；通过选取合适的耗散耦合，将稳定性分析应用于多模Bose-Hubbard采样器（任意跳跃与排斥密度相互作用）以及谐振约束下的量子Coulomb气体与分子系统（对相互作用的固定有限秩低能截断）。

Result: 对适当选择的耗散耦合，证明了含任意跳跃和排斥密度相互作用的全多模Bose-Hubbard采样器具有指数熵衰减；对谐振约束量子Coulomb气体和分子系统，证明了每个固定有限秩低能相互作用截断下的指数熵衰减；并构造了一个周期充电模型，其具有正谱隙但对任意p>2均不存在有限时间的L²到L^p界。

Conclusion: 本文为无限维量子Gibbs采样器提供了相对熵指数衰减的通用理论框架，扩展了超收缩性与对数Sobolev不等式在微扰下的适用性；同时表明正谱隙不足以保证有限时间L²→L^p估计，揭示了谱隙与超收缩性之间的本质区别。

Abstract: We derive rates of exponential relative-entropy decay for a broad class of infinite-dimensional quantum Gibbs samplers. A key technical ingredient is a perturbative stability theory for hypercontractive estimates and logarithmic Sobolev inequalities. For suitably chosen dissipative couplings, we obtain exponential entropy decay for full multimode Bose-Hubbard samplers with arbitrary hopping and repulsive density interactions. For harmonically confined quantum Coulomb gases and molecular systems, we prove exponential entropy decay for every fixed finite-rank low-energy truncation of the interaction. Finally, we exhibit a periodic charging model with a positive spectral gap but no finite-time $L^2$-to-$L^p$ bound for any $p>2$.

</details>


### [254] [Fermions and the Tsirelson Problem in Real Quantum Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06767)
*Timothee Hoffreumon,Mischa P. Woods*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明实量子理论（RQT）在两类推广情形下仍与普通量子理论（QT）实验不可区分：允许分离测量由可交换而非张量积可观测量表示，以及引入超选择规则（如费米子信息论）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作表明RQT无法与QT实验区分；本文探究这种不可区分性在两类自然推广下是否依然成立——用可交换可观测量替代张量积表示分离测量，以及施加超选择规则。标准的复量子证明无法直接推广到实数情形，因此在超选择存在时该证明障碍尤为关键。

Method: 先建立RQT中可交换测量与张量积测量生成相同有限维关联（即RQT中Tsirelson问题的肯定回答），再将该框架扩展到超选择RQT，构造超选择理论的局域实数量子表述，并给出费米子版本。

Result: 第一，RQT中Tsirelson问题得到肯定回答：可交换与张量积测量生成相同的有限维关联；该结论对标准复证明并非平凡推论，因其无法移植到实数。第二，得到超选择理论的局域实数量子表述，其费米子版本的预测与费米子信息论实验不可区分。

Conclusion: RQT与QT的不可区分性在可交换测量和超选择规则两种推广下均得以保持；该构造可推广至所有超选择量子理论。

Abstract: In a previous work, we showed that real quantum theory (RQT) cannot be experimentally distinguished from ordinary quantum theory (QT). In this paper, we investigate whether this indistinguishability survives two natural extensions of the setting: allowing separated measurements to be represented by commuting rather than tensor-product observables, and imposing superselection rules, as in fermionic information theory. We show that it does in both cases.
  Our first result is a positive answer to the Tsirelson problem in RQT: commuting and tensor-product measurements generate the same finite-dimensional correlations. This is surprisingly not a trivial consequence of the standard complex proof, as it does not carry over to the reals. The proof obstruction becomes particularly relevant in the presence of superselection rules, which is why we first establish the equivalence in RQT before extending the framework to superselected RQT.
  Our second result is a local, real quantum formulation of superselected theories. We extend the RQT framework to accommodate a representation of superselection over the reals and show, in particular, that it admits a fermionic version whose predictions are experimentally indistinguishable from those of fermionic information theory. More generally, the construction extends to all superselected QTs.

</details>


### [255] [Complexity separations for optimal matchgate-Clifford synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06768)
*Berta Casas,Diego García-Martín,Yuxuan Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究matchgate-Clifford群（其伴随作用同构于2n个Majorana模的单位行列式带符号置换群）在给定门集下的最优精确合成问题。结果表明，该问题的复杂度强烈依赖门集的连通图结构：路径图与完全图情形可在O(n²)时间内经典求解，并给出显式的门数最优编译器；树形连通图情形下最优合成的判定版本为NP完全。在n=80链上，编译器相较Qiskit与Tket分别取得×2.57与×2.28的门数常数因子改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifford电路与matchgate电路是经典可模拟量子电路的典型代表，二者交集matchgate-Clifford群在随机化费米子协议与matchgate合成中具有重要作用。其伴随作用同构于2n个Majorana模的带符号置换群，因此在该群中研究最优精确合成（给定目标酉算子与门集，输出用门数最少的n-qubit电路）具有理论与实用价值。作者动机在于揭示该合成问题的复杂度如何随门集结构变化，并给出高效的最优编译器。

Method: 将matchgate-Clifford群的伴随作用建模为2n个Majorana模的单位行列式带符号置换群，在此群中研究精确最优合成。方法上，研究由Majorana编织（braids）构成的、具有不同连通图的门集：对路径图与完全图，设计多项式时间算法并构造显式的门数最优编译器，时间复杂度为O(n²)；对树形连通图，通过归约证明最优合成判定版本的NP完全性。此外，将最优编译器在最长n=80 qubit的链上与Qiskit、Tket的编译器进行基准对比。

Result: 最优精确合成的复杂度强烈依赖门集：路径图与完全图连通情形下，问题可在O(n²)时间内经典求解，并提供显式的门数最优编译器；树形连通图情形下，最优合成问题的判定版本为NP完全。在n=80 qubit链上的基准测试中，该最优编译器相比Qiskit与Tket分别取得×2.57与×2.28的总门数常数因子改进。

Conclusion: matchgate-Clifford群中的最优精确合成并非单一复杂度问题，而是由门集连通图决定：简单图结构（路径、完全图）可高效最优求解，而树形结构导致NP完全性，呈现出明显的复杂度相变。所提出的最优编译器在实际规模（n=80）上优于Qiskit与Tket，验证了最优合成算法在门数上的优势。该工作为费米子协议与matchgate合成中的电路优化提供了理论刻画与实用工具。

Abstract: Clifford and matchgate circuits are canonical families of classically simulable quantum circuits. Their intersection, the matchgate-Clifford group, plays an important role in randomized fermionic protocols and in matchgate synthesis. Its adjoint action is isomorphic to the group of unit-determinant signed permutations of $2n$ Majorana modes, and we study optimal exact synthesis in this group. That is, given a target unitary and a gate set, output an $n$-qubit circuit implementing the target using the fewest operations. We show that the complexity of this problem strongly depends on the gate set. In particular, we study gate sets consisting of Majorana braids with different connectivity graphs. For path and complete graphs, we prove that the problem is classically solvable in $\mathcal{O}\left(n^2\right)$ time, and we provide explicit gate-optimal compilers. In addition, we prove that when the connectivity graph is a tree, the decision version of the optimal synthesis problem becomes NP-complete. Finally, we benchmark our optimal compiler on chains of up to $n=80$ qubits against those of \texttt{Qiskit} and \texttt{Tket}, obtaining circuits with constant factor improvements $\times2.57$ and $\times2.28$ in the total number of gates, respectively.

</details>


### [256] [Fast convergence of propagation algorithms for open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06769)
*Giorgio Facelli,Hamza Fawzi,Omar Fawzi,Cambyse Rouzé,Sam Slezak,Daniel Stilck França*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了传播算法快速收敛的一般条件，统一处理自旋与费米子体系，并应用于含噪时间演化与Gibbs态局域可观测量的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望为量子多体系统局域可观测量的经典高效模拟提供一般机制：利用收缩性（contractivity）控制局域相互作用产生的时间增长，从而判定可高效模拟的时间尺度与温度范围，并改进已有结果。

Method: 对足够收缩的演化建立传播算法快速收敛的一般条件；分析任意相互作用图上局域哈密顿量生成的含噪时间演化，给出以噪声强度λ、相互作用强度u和相互作用结构为函数的可行时间上界t_max；并将同一框架用于Gibbs态，通过稳态为Gibbs态的pseudo-Lindbladian分析高温下的快速收敛。

Result: 含噪情形下，当噪声强度λ相对相互作用图度数足够大时动力学可被高效模拟；无噪声极限λ=0时，可模拟时间尺度从log(1/u)扩展到t_max~1/u；在Gibbs态应用中，高温下传播算法快速收敛，弱相互作用体系可达逆温度β~log(1/u)。

Conclusion: 收缩性可作为控制局域相互作用增长并实现高效经典模拟的一般原理，同时适用于含噪时间演化和高温Gibbs态局域可观测量的计算，并推广了近期关于可模拟时间尺度的结果。

Abstract: We establish general conditions for the rapid convergence of propagation algorithms for computing expectation values of local observables in quantum many-body systems, covering both spin and fermionic systems. Our results apply to evolutions that are sufficiently contractive, providing a general mechanism by which contractivity controls the growth generated by local interactions and enables efficient classical simulation. As a first application, we consider noisy time evolution generated by local Hamiltonians on arbitrary interaction graphs. We show that the dynamics can be efficiently simulated when the noise strength $λ$ is sufficiently large compared to the degree of the interaction graph. More generally, for systems with an interaction strength $u$, our bounds determine a time horizon $t_{\text{max}}$ as a function of the interaction strength $u$, the noise strength $λ$, and the interaction structure below which the algorithm is efficient. In the noiseless limit, $λ=0$, our analysis extends the efficiently simulable time scale from $\log(1/u)$ proved in Facelli, Fawzi, and Fawzi (2026) to $t_{\text{max}} \sim 1/u$ matching the recent improvement by Zhao, Marvian and Tong (2026). As a second application, we apply the same framework to the computation of local observables in Gibbs states of local Hamiltonians. We consider a pseudo-Lindbladian whose steady state is the Gibbs state and show that, at sufficiently high temperature, its propagation algorithm converges rapidly. In particular, for weakly-interacting systems with interaction strength $u$, our result applies up to inverse temperatures $β\sim\log(1/u)$.

</details>


### [257] [Informed Trading model with quantum walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06779)
*H. W. Shawn Liew,Patrick Rebentrost*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将经典Kyle模型中的单资产未来价值分别建模为经典随机游走与连续时间量子游走,解析近似并数值求解风险中性做市商与知情交易者之间的市场均衡。经典均衡下知情交易者采用线性策略、做市商定价规则近似logistic曲线、其利润随时间次线性增长;量子均衡下知情交易者策略呈多簇结构、定价规则随之出现多次跳跃,但利润随时间线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Kyle模型为理解知情交易者的价格冲击提供了基础框架,但原模型对资产未来价值的设定较为简化。作者希望将资产价值过程推广到经典随机游走与连续时间量子游走两种情形,考察不同价值动态下市场均衡结构(交易策略、定价规则)与知情交易者利润随时间增长规律的变化,从而拓展Kyle模型的应用范围。

Method: 在单资产框架下,将资产的未来价值分别建模为经典随机游走和连续时间量子游走;在此设定下,对风险中性做市商与知情交易者之间的市场均衡进行解析近似,并通过数值方法识别均衡。重点刻画两种情形下知情交易者的交易策略与做市商的定价规则,以及知情交易者利润随时间的增长规律。

Result: 经典均衡:知情交易者具有线性策略,做市商定价规则类似logistic曲线,知情交易者利润随时间t呈次线性增长。量子均衡:知情交易者策略形成多个簇(clusters),定价规则相应出现多次跳跃;尽管均衡的非线性程度增强,知情交易者利润随时间t呈线性增长。

Conclusion: 将Kyle模型中的资产价值动态替换为经典随机游走或连续时间量子游走会显著改变均衡的结构特征:量子情形下策略与定价规则的非线性更强、呈现多簇与多跳跃,但知情交易者利润的增长规律由经典情形的次线性转变为线性,表明资产价值动态的量子化对市场均衡与信息优势的获利能力具有实质影响。

Abstract: The seminal Kyle model offers a framework to understand the price impact of an informed trader in a market model for a single asset. We study variants in which the asset's future value is modeled by a classical random walk and by a continuous-time quantum walk, respectively. With these assumptions, we analytically approximate and numerically identify the market equilibrium between risk-neutral market maker and informed trader. In the classical equilibrium, the informed trader possesses a linear strategy, whereas the market maker's pricing rule resembles a logistic curve. The informed trader's profit is also identified to grow sub-linearly with time t. In the quantum equilibrium, the informed trader's strategy forms multiple clusters and the pricing rule exhibits multiple jumps correspondingly. Despite the increasing non-linearity of the equilibrium, the informed trader's profit grows linearly with time t.

</details>


### [258] [Pretty good shadow tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06781)
*Sabee Grewal,Angelos Pelecanos,Jack Spilecki,Ewin Tang,John Wright*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个使用 O(log(m) log(m/δ)/ε²) 份量子态副本的阴影层析算法，并指出 pretty good measurement 方案中存在的固有障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子阴影层析中，用尽量少的量子态副本估计多个可观测量期望值，量子复杂度与经典复杂度之间存在 log(m) 因子差距，本文旨在缩小该差距并分析其来源。

Method: 采用平均情形（average-case）框架：对已知先验随机抽取的量子态使用 pretty good measurement，在 O(log(m)/ε²) 副本上获得对先验绝大部分态常数误差的保证；通过 boosting 得到平均情形阴影层析算法，再借助极小极大原理非构造性地推出最坏情形下具有相同保证的测量方案。

Result: 给出仅需 O(log(m) log(m/δ)/ε²) 份输入态副本的阴影层析算法，对所有 m 个可观测量的估计误差不超过 ±ε，失败概率至多 δ；同时识别出阻碍进一步改进的障碍。

Conclusion: pretty good measurement 能放大关于先验随机性的成功概率，但不能放大关于测量随机性的成功概率，这一限制似乎是固有的，正是它导致了量子问题与经典问题之间残留的 log(m) 因子差距。

Abstract: We give a shadow tomography algorithm that uses $O(\log(m) \log(m/δ) / \varepsilon^2)$ copies of the input state. Specifically, given a quantum state $ρ$ and observables $O_1,\ldots,O_m$, the algorithm outputs estimates of $\mathrm{tr}(O_iρ)$ for all $i\in[m]$ that are all accurate to within $\pm\varepsilon$, except with probability at most $δ$. The key idea is to work in the average-case setting: for a random state drawn from a known prior, a pretty good measurement on $O(\log(m)/\varepsilon^2)$ copies gives a constant-error guarantee on all but a small fraction of the prior. Boosting this guarantee gives an average-case shadow tomography algorithm, and the minimax principle then (non-constructively) implies the existence of a measurement with the same worst-case guarantee.
  We also identify an obstruction to further improvement with this approach. While pretty good measurements amplify success over the randomness of the prior, they do not amplify success over the randomness of measurement. This issue appears to be inherent, and gives rise to the remaining $\log(m)$ factor separating the complexity of the quantum problem from its classical counterpart.

</details>


### [259] [Quantum-optimized phase closure for robust interferometric imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06786)
*Zhenning Liu,Emil T. Khabiboulline,Lorcan O. Conlon,Brittany McClinton,Aqil Sajjad,Saikat Guha,Jayadev Rajagopal,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Daniel Gottesman,Stephen T. Ridgway*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过多光子联合测量，可在更少光子下更精确测得闭合相位；大阵列中利用可见度物理约束，三光子联合方案以$\tilde{O}(m)$光子完成全部双谱估计，而顺序单光子方案需$\Omega(m^2)$光子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地基干涉成像中，闭合相位能消除望远镜相关的大气相位涨落，但传统单光子逐基线测量在光子效率和精度上受限，需探索更优测量策略。

Method: 先证明闭合相位信息需至少三个处于同一大气相位稳定窗口内的光子才能存活于未知望远镜相位噪声；在最小三望远镜情形下比较三光子联合测量与单光子测量；再针对大阵列，在全局均方误差准则下估计所有双谱，构建至多三光子联合测量的高效协议，并用多参数量子度量分析高精度区域。

Result: 三光子联合测量在最小三望远镜设置中已优于单光子测量；m望远镜阵列虽有$\Theta(m^2)$个独立三角双谱，但可见度物理约束使固定精度下总信息量近似线性增长；高效协议光子代价为$\tilde{O}(m)$，而任何顺序单光子协议需$\Omega(m^2)$；当每稳定窗口探测光子数随阵列规模线性增长时，所需曝光时间至多对数因子下与m无关；高精度区集体测量亦有类似优势。

Conclusion: 量子信息处理技术可显著提升光学干涉测量的灵敏度与鲁棒性，为地基干涉成像提供更高效的光子利用方案。

Abstract: Closure phase enables imaging with ground-based interferometric arrays by canceling telescope-dependent phase errors due to atmospheric fluctuations. It can be obtained by measuring fringe phases on individual baselines forming a triangle and summing them. We show that closure phases can be measured more accurately with fewer photons by performing joint measurements on multiple photons. We first establish that at least three photons from the same atmospheric temporal phase-stability window are required for closure-phase information to survive the unknown telescope-dependent phase noise, and show that joint three-photon measurements already provide an advantage over single-photon measurements in the minimal three-telescope setting. We then turn to large arrays and consider estimating all bispectra---complex quantities whose phases are the closure phases---under a global mean-square error criterion. Although an $m$-telescope array contains $Θ(m^2)$ independent triangle bispectra, we show that physical constraints on the visibilities make the total information content grow only nearly linearly with $m$ at fixed accuracy. Exploiting this structure, we construct an efficient protocol using at most three-photon joint measurements with photon cost $\tilde{O}(m)$, whereas any sequential single-photon protocol requires $Ω(m^2)$ photons. When the number of detected photons per temporal stability window scales linearly with the array size, this allows the required exposure time to remain independent of $m$ up to logarithmic factors. We further analyze the complementary high-precision regime using multiparameter quantum metrology and find a similar advantage for collective measurements. Overall, our results show that quantum information processing techniques can substantially improve the sensitivity and robustness of optical interferometry.

</details>


### [260] [Computational entanglement distillation beyond pure states: efficient protocols and fundamental limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06787)
*Takeru Utsumi,Jacopo Rizzo,Ryuji Takagi,Lorenzo Leone*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在受限资源条件下研究混合态纠缠蒸馏，证明Rényi相干信息在两个不同的资源约束情形下决定了最优速率，并赋予其新的操作意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠理论依赖任意多输入副本与无界复杂度的操作，而现实中资源是受限的。有限资源下的纠缠理论核心问题之一是哪些量刻画纠缠操纵，但目前仅有理想化的纯态情形得到较好探索，混合态情形尚待研究。

Method: 作者研究混合态纠缠蒸馏，分别考察两种资源约束：(1)样本高效机制，允许无界计算能力；(2)计算高效前沿。构造通用协议在多项式秩约束下无需已知输入态即可达到目标速率，并构造可蒸馏纠缠近乎广泛但通用协议无法超过Rényi相干信息的态以证明最优性。

Result: Rényi相干信息在两种计算设定下刻画了最优速率：样本高效情形下，通用协议在多项式秩约束下无需知道输入态即可达到该速率且最优；计算高效情形下，即使已知输入态，任何高效协议的速率也不超过该界，且在额外多项式边缘秩约束下另一通用协议可高效达到该速率。

Conclusion: 研究结果为Rényi相干信息在两种计算设定下赋予了新的操作意义：有限副本下的通用蒸馏，以及即便已知输入态下的高效蒸馏。

Abstract: The information-theoretic approach to entanglement typically relies on arbitrarily many input copies and unboundedly complex operations; however, the available resources are constrained in reality. This motivates the study of entanglement theory with limited resources. One of its central questions is which quantities characterize entanglement manipulations, and so far, only the idealized case of pure states has been well explored. In this work, we address entanglement distillation from mixed states and identify the Rényi coherent information as the quantity determining the optimal rate in the worst case under two distinct resource constraints. We first characterize the sample-efficient regime, in which unbounded computational power is allowed. Our universal protocol achieves this rate without knowledge of the input state under certain polynomial rank constraints. This rate is optimal among universal protocols: we construct states whose distillable entanglement is nearly extensive, yet from which no universal sample-efficient protocol attains a rate above their Rényi coherent information. We then push this characterization to the computationally efficient frontier: the same bound holds for any efficient protocol even when the input state is known, and another universal protocol achieves this rate efficiently under additional polynomial constraints on the marginal ranks. Overall, our results give the Rényi coherent information a novel operational meaning in two computational settings: universal distillation with limited copies and efficient distillation even with knowledge of the input state.

</details>


### [261] [Characterizing Quantum Advantage for Generalizations of the Boolean Hidden Matching Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06789)
*Mark Bun,Joao F. Doriguello,John Kallaugher,Nadezhda Voronova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究了 $f$-Boolean Hidden Partition 问题的单向通信复杂度，用 $f$ 的符号次数 $d$ 给出了经典与量子通信界的紧致刻画。经典随机通信复杂度为 $\Theta(n^{1-1/d})$，量子通信复杂度介于 $\Omega(n^{1-2/d})$ 与 $\tilde{O}(n^{1-1/\lceil d/2\rceil}\log n)$ 之间，证明了 Doriguello 与 Montanaro 的猜想，并完全刻画了存在多项式量子优势的函数族为 $d\ge 2$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题推广了 Boolean Hidden Matching 和 Hidden Hypermatching，后两者是 $f$ 为奇偶函数时的特例。先前工作仅对符号次数 $d\le1$（经典）与 $d\le2$（量子）给出对数上界，并对某些高符号次数的结构化函数给出多项式下界，未能完整刻画一般 $f$ 的复杂度。本文旨在解决 Doriguello 与 Montanaro (TQC 2020) 提出的猜想，统一刻画 $f$-Boolean Hidden Partition 的经典与量子复杂度。

Method: 以 $f$ 的符号次数 $d$ 为核心参数，分别构造经典随机协议与量子协议，并证明匹配的下界。上界方面给出经典随机通信 $\Theta(n^{1-1/d})$ 与量子通信 $\tilde{O}(n^{1-1/\lceil d/2\rceil}\log n)$ 的协议；下界方面通过（量子）通信下界技术证明经典 $\Omega(n^{1-1/d})$ 与量子 $\Omega(n^{1-2/d})$。

Result: 对每个符号次数 $d\ge2$ 的 $f$：经典随机通信复杂度为 $\Theta(n^{1-1/d})$；量子通信复杂度满足 $\Omega(n^{1-2/d}) \le Q(f) \le \tilde{O}(n^{1-1/\lceil d/2\rceil}\log n)$。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了存在多项式量子优势的函数 $f$ 恰为符号次数 $d\ge2$ 者，并在 $d=2$ 情形下给出新的无穷族指数级分离，验证了 Doriguello 与 Montanaro 的猜想。

Abstract: We study the one-way communication complexity of the $f$-Boolean Hidden Partition problem. Here, Alice is given an $n$-bit string and Bob is given $Ω(n)$ disjoint blocks of its indices together with a string of labels. Under the promise that the evaluations of $f$ on these blocks either agree with all of the labels or disagree with all of them, Bob must determine which is the case using a single message from Alice. This problem generalizes the Boolean Hidden Matching and Hidden Hypermatching problems, which capture the special case where $f$ is the parity function.
  We establish classical and quantum communication bounds for $f$-Boolean Hidden Partition in terms of the sign degree $d$ of $f$, proving a conjecture of Doriguello and Montanaro (TQC 2020). Their prior work gave logarithmic communication upper bounds for classical protocols when $d \le 1$ and quantum protocols when $d \le 2$, as well as polynomial lower bounds for certain structured functions with larger sign degree. We show that for every $f$ of sign degree $d \ge 2$, the randomized classical communication complexity of this problem is $Θ(n^{1-1/d})$ while its quantum communication complexity lies between $Ω(n^{1-2/d})$ and $\bO{n^{1-1/\lceil d/2 \rceil} \log n}$. This completely characterizes the functions $f$ admitting polynomial quantum advantage as those for which $d \ge 2$, with a new infinite family of exponential separations given by the case $d = 2$.

</details>


### [262] [Local universality of non-local quantum resources in rotationally invariant scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06791)
*Silas R. Beane,Roland C. Farrell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将弹性散射中纠缠与"非局域魔力"(non-local magic)的产生能力与S矩阵的几何距离联系起来，给出了任意自旋J下纠缠功率的闭式解，并证明一大类非局域量子资源的生成能力在领头阶仅由S矩阵到最近非纠缠门的投影Hilbert-Schmidt距离的平方决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅对J=1/2,1,3/2的旋转不变S矩阵，以散射相移的形式导出了纠缠功率的表达式，缺乏对任意自旋J的一般性刻画；同时，纠缠功率与非局域魔力功率等不同量子资源之间的关系及其共同起源尚未被统一理解。

Method: 对具有旋转不变性的S矩阵（作用于两个自旋J粒子的联合希尔伯特空间上的幺正量子门），推导任意J下纠缠功率的闭式解析解；并分析纠缠功率与non-local magic power等非局域量子资源生成量在领头阶的行为，将其与最近非纠缠门的投影Hilbert-Schmidt距离的平方进行关联。

Result: 得到了所有自旋J的纠缠功率闭式解，推广了此前J=1/2,1,3/2的结果；证明广泛类别的非局域量子资源在领头阶仅依赖于至最近非纠缠门的投影Hilbert-Schmidt距离的平方，该结论对纠缠功率与非局域魔力功率均成立，不同资源的差异仅表现为整体归一化因子的不同。

Conclusion: 散射的领头资源生成能力具有普适的几何起源，即由S矩阵到最近非纠缠门的投影Hilbert-Schmidt距离主导，与相互作用的具体细节无关；这种普适性将纠缠与非局域魔力等不同量子资源统一在同一几何框架下。

Abstract: Elastic scattering can generate entanglement and non-local magic between the spins of the two outgoing particles. The amount of entanglement and magic produced is encoded in the S-matrix; a unitary quantum gate that acts on the joint Hilbert space of the two spin-J particles. Previous work derived the entanglement power in terms of the scattering phase shifts for rotationally invariant S-matrices with J=1/2,1,3/2. In this paper, a closed form solution is derived for all J. We further prove that a broad class of non-local quantum resources depend to leading order only on the squared projective Hilbert-Schmidt distance from the nearest non-entangling gate. This holds for the entanglement power and non-local magic power, and the choice of resource simply changes the overall normalization. This universality establishes a common geometric origin for the leading resource-generating capacity of scattering, independent of the details of the interaction.

</details>


### [263] [Error Mitigation From a Single Unmodified Error-Corrected Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06792)
*Maor Shutman,Dorit Aharonov,Itai Arad,Yosi Atia,Eyal Bairey,Zvika Brakerski,Barak A. Katzir,Netanel H. Lindner,Omri Golan,Ilya Gurwich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出SOLEM框架，仅在后处理阶段利用综合征数据进行逻辑错误缓解，无需修改错误校正电路，兼具更优解码器、多估计器优化和降低硬件漂移敏感性等优势，运行时开销指数级低于外部LEM且与SALEM相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明错误校正量子计算中的综合征信息可用于逻辑错误缓解（LEM），相比不使用综合征的外部LEM方法可实现运行时开销的指数级降低。然而现有的综合征感知LEM方法（SALEM）通常需要基于综合征实时处理的自适应电路操作，实现复杂且对硬件敏感。因此作者希望开发一种完全在后处理阶段利用综合征数据的离线框架，以简化实现并提升灵活性。

Method: 提出SOLEM（基于综合征的离线逻辑错误缓解）框架，完全在后处理阶段利用综合征数据，不修改给定的错误校正电路。其核心思想是用自然发生的综合征条件逻辑错误信道替代刻意的电路修改。在该框架内开发了无偏和启发式估计器，包括概率误差消除（PEC）和零噪声外推（ZNE）的离线逻辑版本，以及全局回归估计器。

Result: SOLEM显著优势包括：(1)可用比实时解码器更强大的解码器对综合征进行后处理；(2)可从同一测量数据构造多个估计器，并利用经验统计误差及相关性能指标进行优化；(3)逻辑错误表征仅需在后处理中进行，允许在电路执行期间收集表征数据，降低对硬件漂移的敏感性。SOLEM估计器的运行时开销指数级低于外部LEM，并与相应的SALEM估计器相当。

Conclusion: SOLEM作为一种完全离线的逻辑错误缓解框架，通过在后处理中利用综合征数据实现错误缓解，无需修改电路，比外部LEM协议更易实现，同时具备更优解码、多估计器优化和抗硬件漂移三大优势，并在运行时开销上取得指数级降低，为错误校正量子计算中的逻辑错误缓解提供了高效实用的新途径。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that syndrome information obtained during error-corrected quantum computation can be utilized for logical error mitigation (LEM), yielding exponential reductions in runtime overhead compared to external'' LEM methods, which do not make use of syndrome data. However, these syndrome-aware LEM methods (SALEM) generally require adaptive circuit operations based on real-time processing of syndromes. Here, we introduce syndrome-based offline logical error mitigation (SOLEM), a framework that utilizes syndrome data entirely in post-processing, without any modification to the given error-corrected circuit. In this sense, SOLEM is built into'' error-corrected circuits, and is in fact simpler to implement than external LEM protocols. The basic idea of SOLEM is to use naturally occurring syndrome-conditioned logical error channels in place of deliberate circuit modifications. We develop unbiased and heuristic estimators within this framework, including offline logical versions of probabilistic error cancellation (PEC) and zero-noise extrapolation (ZNE), as well as a global-regression estimator. SOLEM's fully offline nature leads to three significant advantages. First, SOLEM can post-process syndromes with a more powerful decoder than the one used in real time. Second, multiple estimators can be constructed from the same measured data and optimized using empirical statistical errors and related performance metrics. Third, logical error characterization is needed only in post-processing, allowing characterization data to be collected during circuit execution and reducing sensitivity to hardware drift. Remarkably, SOLEM estimators can achieve these advantages while attaining a runtime overhead which is exponentially lower than that of external LEM and is comparable to that of corresponding SALEM estimators.

</details>


### [264] [Testing bosonic and fermionic Gaussian states with a constant number of copies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06794)
*Jop Briët,Davi Castro-Silva,Michael Walter,Freek Witteveen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究玻色与费米高斯量子态的检测问题，证明只需常数个态的副本即可判断给定态是否为高斯态或与任何高斯态重叠有界，改进了以往随模式数（系统规模）增长的副本数需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯量子态在量子物理与量子信息处理中具有核心地位。此前对该检测问题的分析所需的态副本数随模式数（系统规模）增长，作者希望降低这一资源需求，探究能否用常数个副本完成检测。

Method: 基于同种高斯态相同副本的对称性进行分析，并通过梯度流以及此前为球面上Kac随机游走研究过的谱界来控制到高斯态集合的距离。

Result: 证明了常数个态副本即足以完成检测，即判断给定态是否为高斯态，或与任何高斯态的重叠有界。

Conclusion: 对高斯态检测问题给出了副本数从随模式数增长到常数的改进，方法上借助副本对称性、梯度流与谱界。

Abstract: Bosonic and fermionic Gaussian quantum states are families of quantum states that play a central role in quantum physics and quantum information processing. We study the problem of testing whether a given state is either Gaussian, or has bounded overlap with any Gaussian state. Previous analyses of this testing problem led to tests that require a number of copies of the state that scales with the number of modes (the system size). In this work we show that a constant number of copies of the state suffices. We derive these results based on the symmetries of identical copies of Gaussian states, and by bounding the distance to the set of Gaussian states through a gradient flow and a spectral bound studied previously for the Kac random walk on a sphere.

</details>


### [265] [From Quantum Tunneling to Classical Optimization through Instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06797)
*Jiayu Shen,Dylan Herman,Guneykan Ozgul,Akshay Seshadri,Junhyung Lyle Kim,Shree Hari Sureshbabu,Shouvanik Chakrabarti,Niraj Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于瞬子分析证明，路径积分随机梯度下降（PISGD）可继承量子隧穿机制：在经典非凸优化中，其逃逸时间按反弹作用量（等于两势阱间Agmon距离的两倍）而非势垒高度指数缩放，从而在高而薄的势垒下快于SGD等经典噪声优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化问题的常见瓶颈是解被困于局部极小值，与更优解之间被势垒隔开。经典噪声驱动的优化器（如SGD）逃逸时间随势垒高度指数增长，而量子隧穿理论上可突破这一限制。作者希望探究：这种隧穿加速是否必须依赖真正的量子动力学，还是可被经典算法（如从路径积分蒙特卡洛实例化的PISGD）继承。

Method: 采用基于瞬子的分析框架，将Kramers逃逸率理论推广至路径空间，用以描述PISGD的逃逸行为；并在可分离与非可分离势上对PISGD进行数值实现，验证其逃逸时间缩放规律。

Result: PISGD在经典非凸全局优化中呈现与量子隧穿相同的指数缩放，逃逸时间由反弹作用量（即两势阱间Agmon距离的两倍）而非势垒高度决定；数值实验在可分离与非可分离势上均证实了该缩放规律。但PISGD仅保留非相干隧穿，缺乏多条隧穿路径之间的相干性。

Conclusion: 隧穿的指数加速并非真正的量子动力学算法所独有，经典算法亦可具备该特性。由于PISGD仅具非相干隧穿，本文分析并不能排除量子隧穿类量子算法相对于经典算法可能存在的一般性量子优势。

Abstract: We use an instanton-based analysis to show that Path Integral Stochastic Gradient Descent (PISGD), a classical optimization algorithm instantiated from path integral Monte Carlo (PIMC), can inherit the quantum tunneling mechanism. Quantum tunneling is the phenomenon in which a quantum particle passes through a potential barrier that a classical trajectory cannot cross in the same way. This mechanism can contribute to the performance of quantum optimization algorithms, since a common bottleneck in optimization is that the solution becomes trapped in a local minimum, separated from better solutions by a potential barrier. Classical noise-driven optimizers, such as stochastic gradient descent (SGD), have escape times that scale exponentially with the potential barrier height. For quantum tunneling, the escape time is instead exponential in the bounce action, which equals twice the Agmon distance between the two basins. When the barrier is tall but thin, quantum tunneling can allow escaping local minima faster than such classical methods. Remarkably, this scaling is not exclusive to algorithms with genuine quantum dynamics. For PISGD applied to classical nonconvex global optimization, we adapt Kramers' escape-rate theory to path space and obtain the same exponential scaling as quantum tunneling, in terms of the bounce action rather than the barrier height. We confirm this scaling numerically by implementing PISGD for both separable and nonseparable potentials. That said, PISGD retains only incoherent tunneling, since coherence among multiple tunneling paths is absent. Our analysis therefore does not rule out a potential quantum advantage for quantum-tunneling-based algorithms over classical ones in general.

</details>


### [266] [Bell nonlocality does not imply violation of nonlocal games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06799)
*Ricardo Faleiro,Lucas E. A. Porto,Marco Túlio Quintino,Flavien Hirsch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明确定性谓词定义的贝尔非局域游戏无法作为非局域性的完全见证：存在不违反任何此类游戏的局域性行为，且量子力学能够产生这种"游戏经典但非局域"的行为，该现象在所有 $X,Y\geq2$、$A,B\geq4$ 的双方情景中均存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局域游戏（贝尔非局域游戏）常被视为刻画贝尔非局域性的概念上清晰的方式。作者质疑其作为完全见证的能力：原本定义、且最常用的确定性谓词形式的游戏，是否能够刻画所有的非局域性行为。作者希望揭示这类游戏在概念上的局限。

Method: 作者在一般性的双方情景 $(X,Y,A,B)$ 中构造性地证明：存在非局域行为却不违反任何确定性谓词定义的非局域游戏。进一步，作者给出量子力学实现，即构造出量子可实现的"游戏经典非局域"行为，并证明该现象在 $X,Y\geq2$ 且 $A,B\geq4$ 的所有情景中普遍存在。

Result: 证明存在不违反任何确定性谓词非局域游戏的非局域行为，即确定性谓词游戏不是非局域性的完全见证；同时证明量子力学能够产生这类游戏经典非局域行为；并给出该现象在所有 $X,Y\geq2$、$A,B\geq4$ 双方情景中的普遍存在性定理。

Conclusion: 以确定性谓词定义的、最常用的非局域游戏无法完全刻画贝尔非局域性，存在被其遗漏的非局域行为，且该遗漏在量子力学中可实现并在广泛的双方情景中普遍出现，说明此类游戏作为非局域性见证存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Nonlocal games offer a conceptually clear way to reason about Bell nonlocality. However, we show that as originally defined, and most commonly used, i.e. with a deterministic predicate, they cannot be complete witnesses of nonlocality. Concretely, we prove the existence of nonlocal behaviours which do not violate any such nonlocal game. Furthermore, we also show that quantum mechanics can produce these game-classical nonlocal behaviours. We prove that this phenomenon exists in every bipartite scenario $(X,Y,A,B)$ with $X,Y\geq2$ and $A,B\geq4$.

</details>


### [267] [Tight bounds for hybrid quantum-classical query algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06803)
*Andris Ambainis,András Gilyén,Martins Kokainis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究查询模型下的混合量子-经典算法：计算由若干量子子程序组成，每个子程序至多进行 $q$ 次 oracle 查询，子程序之间所有量子比特被测量并丢弃。作者为该模型下的多个问题证明了紧的上界与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典算法在近期受到关注，但其下界证明方法此前非常零散（ad-hoc），缺乏系统框架。本文旨在建立通用方法，并为该模型下的若干基本问题给出最优复杂度刻画。

Method: 在查询模型中定义混合算法（子程序间测量并丢弃全部量子比特）。通过分析测量记录（measurement transcript）上的概率分布并界定一个刻画可区分性的"进展度量"（progress measure），证明前两个界；对 AND-OR 树问题，结合两个分别追踪算法经典与量子可用信息的进展度量进行更精细的分析。

Result: (i) 相位估计精度 $\varepsilon$ 需 $\Theta(\frac{1}{q\varepsilon^2})$ 次查询；(ii) 振幅估计精度 $\varepsilon$ 需 $\Theta(\frac{p(1-p)}{q\varepsilon^2})$ 次查询；(iii) $N$ 项搜索需 $\Theta(\frac{N}{q})$ 次查询；(iv) 两层 AND-OR 树（$m$ 个 OR 的 AND，每个 OR 有 $n$ 个输入）需 $\Theta(\frac{nm}{q})$ 次查询。以上界在常数因子上最优，且对所有 $q$（从 1 即经典复杂度，到无限制量子查询复杂度 $Q(f)$）均成立。此外，给出了区分由态制备酉算符指定的两个分布之混合下界，其紧至对数因子。

Conclusion: 本文为混合量子-经典算法提供了系统的下界证明方法（基于测量记录的进展度量），并对相位估计、振幅估计、搜索及 AND-OR 树问题给出了跨整个 $q$ 范围的最优复杂度界。这些结果统一并扩展了此前零散的下界工作。
